Method: 提出 EventSSM：以按时间顺序到达的事件包（event packets）驱动循环空间状态更新，保持持久空间上下文与物理时序状态；为解决异步预测与帧时刻监督的不匹配，用标定相机位姿把预测的事件深度几何搬运到参考时间戳，并通过可微渲染与之对齐监督。另做受控消融，考察跨模态初始化、时间递归、状态个体化、读出（readout）、监督分量与事件包细分。

Result: 基准评测中在多项深度误差指标上优于已有事件深度方法；消融验证了各组件（跨模态初始化、递归、状态与读出设计、监督项、包细分）的有效性贡献。

Conclusion: EventSSM 以因果递归空间状态结合几何搬运—可微渲染监督，为事件关联的度量深度估计提供了有效框架；摘要未提及真实机器人部署、开源代码或实时性能数据。

Abstract: Event cameras report asynchronous brightness changes, yet metric-depth supervision is typically available only at sparsely sampled reference timestamps. Aggregating events into frame-like representations simplifies learning but can obscure the fine-grained temporal structure of the original event stream. We introduce EventSSM, a causal spatial-state framework for event-associated metric-depth estimation using a recurrent memory updated by chronological event packets. To bridge asynchronous predictions and frame-time supervision, predicted event depths are geometrically transported using calibrated camera poses and differentiably rendered at reference timestamps. Benchmark evaluations demonstrate improvements over established event-depth methods across multiple depth-error measures. Controlled ablations further examine cross-modal initialization, temporal recurrence, individual state, readout, and supervision components, and packet subdivision. Together, these findings establish EventSSM as an effective framework for causal event-associated metric-depth estimation, combining persistent spatial context with physically timed state retention.

</details>


### [36] [BEARING: Who Is Talking to Whom? Grounding Acoustic Direction in Vision for Online Multi-Party Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31735)
*Nader Lobandi,Lombe Chileshe,Mohammad Mehdi Hosseini,Mohammad H. Mahoor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出几何优先的在线群组对话理解系统 BEARING：以麦克风阵列声源方向为单一核心信号，无标签在线标定方向到图像的配准，并用可验证奖励（说话人需位于声源方位）替代自评估奖励完成对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统把说话人识别与"对谁说"当作单通道离线任务，并用自评估奖励做对齐，缺乏可核查的物理依据；作者认为"声音来自哪个方向"这一几何线索足以支撑在线决策。

Method: （1）从数据流中自监督学习的方向-图像配准，无需人工标注；（2）在线注册人脸、声纹与方向，免注册；（3）方向作为VLM视觉提示（标记者/离屏箭头）；（4）方向与视觉主动说话人检测做可靠性加权融合；（5）以方向为可验证奖励（说话人须在声源方位、置信度须与校验一致、总答"group"不得分），用于 GRPO、reward-ranked fine-tuning 与 DPO。

Result: 在 AMI 三站点与自我中心 EasyCom 共 9,498 条带被指对象标注的语音上，方向提示使说话人准确率由 54% 升至 74%；被指对象准确率仅提升约 2 点（更依赖语言内容）。AVDIAR 同向说话人场景下视觉主动说话人检测保持 95%，纯方向降至 59%。可验证奖励优于自评估；前瞻从 5 s 缩至 0.5 s 损失 4.5 DER；在线学习配准前两分钟有性能下滑后反超纯视觉。

Conclusion: 声源方位这一"被测信号"可作为提示、融合权重与可验证奖励的统一载体，实现无需离线的在线群组对话理解；被指对象判别仍是瓶颈，需语言信息。代码与评测协议将开源。

Abstract: Understanding a group conversation means deciding, from video and audio, who is speaking and to whom. Leading systems treat these tasks offline on single-channel clips and align models with a self-evaluation reward. We propose BEARING, a geometry-first alternative built on one measured signal: the direction from which a microphone array beside the camera hears a sound. A label-free calibration learned from the stream registers this direction into the camera image, and an online registry of faces, voices and directions needs no enrollment. The registered direction serves as (i) a visual prompt for a vision-language model, a marker on the speaker or an arrow toward an off-screen source; (ii) one input of a reliability-weighted fusion with visual active-speaker detection, for speakers in the same direction from the array; and (iii) a verifiable reward for alignment: the named speaker must sit where the sound comes from, the confidence must match that check, and always answering "group" earns nothing. Every decision is online, with the lookahead as an explicit parameter. On 9,498 addressee-labelled utterances from three AMI sites and egocentric EasyCom, the direction prompt raises speaker accuracy from 54% to 74%; addressee accuracy moves by about two points, because telling one addressee from the group depends on the words more than on where people are. For co-located speakers on AVDIAR, visual active-speaker detection holds 95% where direction alone falls to 59%. The verifiable reward beats self-evaluation under GRPO, reward-ranked fine-tuning and DPO; shrinking the lookahead from 5 s to 0.5 s costs 4.5 DER points on AMI, and learning the registration online costs a dip in the first two minutes before the system overtakes vision alone. Code and evaluation protocols will be released.

</details>


### [37] [LeanWAM: Learning Latent World Action Models from Frozen Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31740)
*Shaolong Li,Hongze Fu,Joyce Chai,Yinpei Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LeanWAM 是一种轻量潜在世界动作模型：先用 Future Extractor 把观测转移压缩为 32 个预测 token，并通过冻结视频生成器在动作、RGB、语义、几何监督下对齐；第二阶段将世界表示蒸馏到 1.4B 策略网络中，实现高效动作块预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WAM 需微调大型预训练视频生成器以同时预测未来观测与动作，训练和部署成本高；且不清楚策略真正需要何种未来表征、能压缩到多紧凑。作者通过探测实验发现互补的未来表征可压缩到少量 token 仍保留大部分动作解码性能。

Method: 两阶段训练：(1) Future Extractor 将观测转移压缩为 32 个预测 token，条件化冻结视频生成器，受动作、RGB、语义、几何多任务监督，并从生成器隐状态读出紧凑 world token；(2) 将这些表征蒸馏进策略，使策略从当前观测预测未来表征并同时输出动作。总参数 1.4B，无大规模具身预训练。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 2.0 上超过强 VLA 与 WAM 基线；A100 上 25.1 ms 预测一个动作块，比代表性 WAM 快 12.3–41.5 倍。

Conclusion: 紧凑的预测性监督足以将视频先验迁移到鲁棒且高效的控制策略，表明潜在 WAM 可在不承担大模型推理成本的情况下获得世界模型收益。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) typically adapt large pretrained video generators to jointly predict future observations and robot actions, incurring substantial training and deployment costs. However, it remains unclear which future representations a policy actually needs and how compactly they can be represented. Our probing study shows that complementary future representations can be compressed into a few tokens while retaining most of their action-decoding performance. Motivated by this finding, we introduce LeanWAM, a latent WAM that transfers video priors from a frozen video generator to a lightweight policy via two-stage training. In the first stage, a Future Extractor compresses observed transitions into 32 predictive tokens. These tokens condition the frozen generator and are jointly shaped by action, RGB, semantic, and geometric supervision, while a compact readout of the generator's hidden states supplies complementary world tokens. The second stage distills both representations into the policy, which learns to predict them from current observations while generating actions. With 1.4B parameters and no large-scale embodied pretraining, LeanWAM outperforms strong vision-language-action (VLA) and WAM baselines on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin~2.0. It predicts an action chunk in 25.1\,ms on an A100 GPU, 12.3--41.5$\times$ faster than representative WAMs. These results demonstrate that compact predictive supervision can transfer video priors to robust and efficient control.

</details>


### [38] [RVLM-X: Towards a Universal VLM Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31755)
*Aadi Palnitkar,Dmytro Kurdydyk,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RVLM-X是一个任务通用的递归VLM框架：VLM在持有全分辨率图像的代码REPL中自主编写视觉搜索与递归放大的控制逻辑，代替固定的zoom-tree等流程；在7个公开基准上均优于单次调用，并发布MapGenBench（细节感知+多步推理地图问答），最高71.4% vs 基线55.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM高分辨率图像处理框架大多采用固定程序（如zoom tree）预设观察位置与证据融合方式；这类"脚手架"是为弥补旧模型能力不足而设计的任务特定路径，对能可靠规划与调用工具的前沿模型反而限制性能、降低精度。作者希望得到一个跨照片、图表、地图都不需改动、可任务泛化的通用harness。

Method: 借鉴Recursive Language Models的递归推理：把全分辨率图像放入代码REPL，VLM以程序方式控制自身视觉上下文（裁剪、缩放、改写子问题），并对自己选定的区域递归调用自身；所有对比方法统一使用同一低成本前沿模型作基座，以保证公平。

Result: 在7个公开基准上均优于单次调用，取得最高汇总准确率；在新提出的MapGenBench（程序化生成、需细粒度感知+多步推理的地图问答）上，留出题准确率71.4%，最强基线55.5%（+15.9点）。

Conclusion: 随着VLM规划与工具使用能力提升，让模型自行编写视觉搜索（递归、自适应）优于预先固定的流程；同时指出以"找单一小细节"为主的现有基准不足以衡量此类能力，需补充细节感知与多步推理耦合的评测（MapGenBench）。

Abstract: Harnesses for vision-language models (VLMs) have grown with the models' capabilities, notably for images far larger than those seen in training, where downscaling loses small details. Most of them wrap the VLM in a fixed procedure, such as a zoom tree, that decides in advance where it looks and how the evidence is combined. These procedures make up for the weaknesses of older models by keeping them on a task-specific path, but with frontier models that reliably plan and use tools, they can lower accuracy. We propose RVLM-X, a task-generalizable recursive VLM harness that runs unchanged across photographs, charts and maps. Following the recursive inference of Recursive Language Models, the VLM programmatically controls its own visual context in a code REPL that holds the full-resolution image and calls itself recursively on regions that it chooses. With a cost-efficient frontier model as the base of every method, RVLM-X is more accurate than a single call on all 7 public benchmarks and has the highest pooled accuracy. Because existing benchmarks mostly test whether a model finds one small detail, we also introduce MapGenBench, procedurally generated maps whose questions combine fine-detail perception with multi-step reasoning. There, RVLM-X answers 71.4% of held-out questions against 55.5% for the strongest baseline. Overall, our findings suggest that as VLMs plan and use tools more reliably, letting the model write its own visual search works better than fixing one in advance.

</details>


### [39] [Xtream: An Explicit Metric State for Large-Scale Streaming Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31757)
*Ernesto Lozano,Alberto Jaenal,Javier Civera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Xtream：以3DFM为几何前端、以关键帧因子图维持显式度量状态的混合流式SLAM管线，通过3DFM度量尺度预测或惯性测量注入尺度，在VBR、Oxford Spires、KITTI上实现3DFM在线重建SOTA，显著降低ATE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D基础模型（3DFM）前馈稠密重建的跨度受限于跨视角注意力；现有扩展手段（视频分块几何对齐+隐式记忆递归、位姿图）易漂移，根源是单目几何缺乏度量尺度可观性。

Method: 提出Xtream混合流式管线：(1) 3DFM作为几何前端做前馈稠密重建；(2) 后端在关键帧上构建因子图，维护显式度量状态；(3) 双通道度量注入——3DFM预测的度量尺度，或可用时的IMU测量。

Result: 在VBR、Oxford Spires、KITTI大规模基准上，3DFM在线重建性能达SOTA，平均绝对轨迹误差（ATE）较已发表基线与同期工作降低。

Conclusion: 将3DFM定位为几何前端、由因子图承载全局度量一致性，是突破前馈3DFM跨度限制并抑制漂移的有效范式，尺度可观性是关键。

Abstract: 3D foundation models (3DFMs) enable dense reconstruction in a feed-forward pass, yet their effective span is limited by cross-view attention. Local consistency has been expanded through geometric alignment of video chunks and recursive updates of an implicit memory, and large-scale scenes with pose graphs. However, both strategies are prone to drift, largely due to the lack of metric-scale observability from monocular geometry. We present Xtream,a hybrid streaming pipeline that maintains an explicit metric state through a factor graph over keyframes, while using a 3DFM as a geometric front end. Xtream incorporates metric information in two ways: through metric scale predictions from 3DFMs; or through inertial measurements if available. Across the large-scale benchmarks of VBR, Oxford Spires and KITTI, Xtream achieves state-of-the-art performance in 3DFM-based online reconstruction, reducing average absolute trajectory errors compared with published baselines and concurrent work. this https URL

</details>


### [40] [Dreamcatcher: Remembering What Surprises a World Model in Video Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31765)
*Jinxi He,Ce Zhang,Wenhe Liu,Jianshu Zhang,Katia P. Sycara,Yaqi Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Dreamcatcher：用冻结的视频世界模型的预测误差（surprisal）驱动流式视频问答的在线视觉记忆构建，无需训练，压缩80%+输入token并在OVO-Bench取得最高训练-free精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时直播视频流的全历史重复编码代价高，而未来问题未知，因此需要一次性前向构建视觉记忆；现有方法仅保留"与早期不同"的token或依赖预设代理查询，缺乏世界动态建模，难以区分意外细节与预期变化。

Method: 以近期历史为条件，用独立预训练且冻结的视频世界模型预测未来特征，取其归一化预测误差得到时间与空间surprisal信号，指导在线分段与冻结Video-LLM原生视觉token的保留（在随分段年龄衰减的预算下平衡显著细节与视觉覆盖）；查询时用证据门控，仅当Video-LLM判断某候选段在当前视野外提供额外证据时才纳入。

Result: 在3个流式+3个离线长视频基准上评测；OVO-Bench上对四个冻结Video-LLM均取得对比训练-free方法中最高总体准确率，Qwen3.8-27B达78.9%；三个Qwen Video-LLM上相对1 fps基线平均输入token减少80%以上。

Conclusion: 世界模型的预测surprisal是一种有效、免训练的流式视频记忆选择准则，可在显著降低token开销的同时提升长时流式VQA精度。

Abstract: As live video streams grow longer, repeatedly encoding and processing their full histories becomes increasingly costly. Answering questions as they arrive therefore calls for visual memory built in one pass before future questions are known. Existing methods mainly keep tokens that differ from earlier ones or match surrogate queries fixed in advance. Without modeling world dynamics, these criteria struggle to tell subtle surprising details from expected change. We introduce Dreamcatcher, a training-free framework and, to our knowledge, the first to use predictive signals from an independently pretrained, frozen video world model for online memory formation in streaming video question answering. Conditioned on recent history, the world model predicts incoming features using learned world priors. Its normalized prediction errors yield temporal and spatial surprisal signals that guide online segmentation and the retention of a frozen Video-LLM's native visual tokens, balancing surprising details with broad visual coverage under budgets that decay with segment age. At query time, an evidence gate admits a segment from a similarity-based shortlist only when the Video-LLM judges that it adds evidence for the question beyond the current view. We evaluate Dreamcatcher on three streaming and three offline long-video benchmarks. On OVO-Bench, Dreamcatcher achieves the highest overall accuracy among the compared training-free methods for each of four frozen Video-LLMs, reaching 78.9% with Qwen3.8-27B. With the three Qwen Video-LLMs, it also reduces average input tokens for answer generation by more than 80% relative to their base models at 1 fps.

</details>


### [41] [RoMo Hands: A Large Scale Richly Organized Text to Hand Motion Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31766)
*Yizhak Ben-Shabat,Jiahao Zhang,Joseph Liu,Seonghyeon Moon,Young-Yoon Lee,Haomiao Jiang,Oren Jacob,Mubbasir Kapadia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoMo Hands：约542K序列（884小时、95.5M帧@30fps）的野外双手文本-动作配对数据集，配层级化五级字幕与393维双手表示，并系统基准扩散/掩码token/自回归生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯手部动作生成受数据瓶颈制约：现有数据集把手当刚性末端、缺乏细粒度文本、规模不足以泛化。

Method: 构建大规模野外数据并加严格“灵巧度过滤”筛除低关节活动序列；每条剪辑配5级字幕（全局语义标签到原子手指状态），按层级分类体系组织；提出393维双手表示以迁移身体动作范式到高自由度手部；对扩散、掩码token、自回归三类生成器做统一基准评测。

Result: 规模比先前工作大一个数量级；基准显示不同生成范式在手部适配下权衡明显，语义对齐与运动学合理性之间出现显著分歧。

Conclusion: 数据集、表示与基准为灵巧手动作合成奠定基础；属数据/基准贡献，方法洞察有限。

Abstract: While text-driven body motion generation has matured, pure-hand synthesis remains bottlenecked by data. Existing datasets often treat hands as rigid end-effectors, lack fine-grained captions, or are too limited in scale to support robust generalization. We introduce RoMo Hands, an in-the-wild, text-paired dataset of ~542K sequences (884 hours, 95.5M frames at 30 fps) which is an order of magnitude larger than prior work. To ensure high-quality supervision, a stringent dexterity filter discards low-articulation sequences, focusing the distribution on complex bimanual movement. Each clip is paired with five captions at increasing granularities from global semantic tags to atomic finger states, organized under a hierarchical taxonomy. To bridge the gap between body and hand architectures, we propose a novel 393-D bimanual representation that adapts redundant body-motion paradigms to the high-DoF kinematics of hands. Systematic benchmarking of diffusion, masked-token, and autoregressive generators reveals distinct trade-offs under these hand-specific adaptations, including a sharp divergence between semantic alignment and kinematic plausibility. Our dataset, representation, and benchmark establish a rigorous foundation for the next generation of dexterous motion synthesis. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [42] [ET: Efficient Transfer from Softmax to Hybrid Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31770)
*Guangfu Guo,Xiaoqian Lu,Linsey Pang,Weiran Yao,Haolin Chen,Long Cheng,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ET框架：冻结预训练权重，用精确局部/共享键softmax注意力+均值锚定无扫描全局线性校正+自适应门控实现线性复杂度；仅通过交替最小二乘在少量无标注样本上校准新参数（无梯度更新），DeiT-S/B上ImageNet-1K top-1分别为79.64%/81.55%，与教师差距<0.25pp，DiT-XL/2 FID-50K 2.59（250次LoRA更新），SD3.5 Medium保持GenEval且1.39×推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练softmax Transformer转换为高效线性注意力通常导致显著性能下降，而恢复性能需昂贵微调；作者希望以极低优化成本实现高保真注意力转换。

Method: 混合注意力结构：精确局部注意力+共享键softmax注意力+均值锚定的无scan全局线性校正+自适应门控，序列长度线性复杂度；冻结预训练权重，仅通过交替最小二乘在少量无标签样本上校准新增参数，无梯度反传。

Result: ImageNet-1K上DeiT-S/DeiT-B top-1为79.64%/81.55%（差距<0.25pp）；768×768下比T^5高3.59pp且FLOPs相当；DiT-XL/2 FID-50K=2.59（仅250次LoRA更新）；SD3.5 Medium在1K分辨率匹配教师GenEval分数，仅0.73 A100 GPU时，推理1.39×加速。

Conclusion: 无梯度的最小化校准可实现接近教师的高保真线性注意力替换，适用于视觉分类、扩散生成等任务，显著降低转换成本并提供推理加速。

Abstract: Converting pretrained softmax Transformers to efficient attention often causes substantial performance degradation, while recovery typically requires costly fine-tuning. We propose ET (Efficient Transfer), a framework for high-fidelity attention conversion with minimal optimization cost. ET combines exact local and shared-key softmax attention with a mean-anchored, scan-free global linear correction and adaptive gating, achieving linear complexity in sequence length. To preserve pretrained knowledge, ET freezes pretrained weights and calibrates only newly introduced parameters through alternating least squares on a small set of unlabeled samples, without gradient updates. On ImageNet-1K, ET achieves 79.64% and 81.55% top-1 accuracy on DeiT-S and DeiT-B, respectively, within 0.25 percentage points of their softmax teachers. At $768\times768$, ET outperforms T$^5$ by 3.59 percentage points at comparable inference FLOPs, without gradient-based fine-tuning. On DiT-XL/2, ET achieves an FID-50K of 2.59 with only 250 LoRA updates. On Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, ET matches the teacher's GenEval score at 1K resolution with just 0.73 A100 GPU-hours of gradient-free conversion and a 1.39$\times$ inference speedup.

</details>


### [43] [BODYBENCH: Evaluating Adversarial Image Defenses Against AI Nudification Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31774)
*Li Qiwei,Salma Abdel Magid,Olga Russakovsky,Eric Gilbert,Sarita Schoenebeck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BODYBENCH基准（648个全合成穿衣成人主体，各配11种掩码变体）及三项与危害对齐的指标，审计四种对抗扰动保护方法（PhotoGuard、DiffusionGuard、AdvPaint、DiffVax），发现仅一种方法在一种配置下有效且需精确掩码对齐，现有评估严重高估防护效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗扰动图像保护方法从未针对"nudification"（AI生成非自愿私密图像，AIG-NCII）进行评估；作者认为这是问题表述失败：既有威胁模型（掩码形状、训练/测试提示分布、成功判据）与真实nudification编辑结构性错配，导致评估系统性高估防护有效性。

Method: 构建BODYBENCH：648个全合成成年主体图像，每个配11种掩码变体（身体、面部、上下文区域）；提出三项与危害对齐的指标——性化程度（sexualization）、自然度（naturalness）、可辨识度（recognizability），联合判定inpainting输出是否构成成功的nudification攻击；在此基准上审计PhotoGuard、DiffusionGuard、AdvPaint、DiffVax四种防护方法，并分析PSNR等常规指标与nudification防护的相关性。

Result: 四种方法仅在一种方法的一种配置下防护成功，且要求防御方与攻击方掩码完全对齐——真实防御方无法保证该条件，且实验表明该条件总可被轻易绕过；PSNR等标准指标与nudification防护基本不相关。

Conclusion: 既有图像保护范式的评估因威胁模型错配而失效，BODYBENCH使这种错配可度量；未来防御应基于已记录的危害重新定义成功判据与威胁模型。

Abstract: Adversarial perturbation defenses protect images from unauthorized edits by adding imperceptible noise. Yet none of these methods have been evaluated against nudification, despite AI-generated non-consensual intimate imagery (AIG-NCII) being among the most documented harms of generative AI. Moreover, nudification edits are readily carried out through inpainting, the primary setting these protection methods are designed to defend against. We argue this is a problem-formulation failure since prior threat models do not correspond to nudification edits (also called attacks). The misalignment of existing approaches is structural, spanning mask shape, training and testing prompt distributions, and success criterion which causes existing evaluations to systematically overestimate protection effectiveness. We reformulate image protection against unauthorized editing around this documented harm. We introduce BODYBENCH, a benchmark of 648 fully synthetic clothed adult subjects, each paired with 11 mask variants spanning body, face, and context regions. We propose three harm-aligned metrics: sexualization, naturalness, and recognizability that jointly determine whether an inpainted output constitutes a successful nudification attack. Auditing four recent protection methods (PhotoGuard, DiffusionGuard, AdvPaint, DiffVax) on BODYBENCH, we find that protection succeeds in only one configuration of one method, and only under exact mask alignment between defender and attacker---a condition no real defender can guarantee and one that we show is easily always bypassed. We also show that standard evaluation metrics such as peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) are essentially uncorrelated with nudification protection. BODYBENCH makes this misalignment measurable and provides a foundation for defenses grounded in documented harm.

</details>


### [44] [ReCAP: Sequential Concept Acquisition with Guarantees under Imperfect Concept Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31776)
*Md Nahiduzzaman,Steven Korevaar,Alireza Bab-Hadiashar,Ruwan Tennakoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReCAP：将概念预测的错误与弃权纳入序贯概念采集推理，用标签判别目标替代条件信息增益，并证明其自适应单调/子模性，配合弃权感知采集与保诊断剪枝，使解释更短且更可信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序贯概念诊断的可解释性依赖于概念证据正确，但现有方法默认预测概念正确，导致正确诊断可能建立在错误证据上；需在概念含错误与弃权时仍给出可靠、简短的诊断解释。

Method: 提出 ReCAP：在概念预测器输出（含错误与弃权）上推理；用标签判别目标替代条件信息增益以区分需不同诊断的病例；证明该目标具自适应单调性与自适应子模性，从而贪心定预算采集具 (1-1/e) 保证且在估计误差下有界退化；结合弃权感知采集与保诊断剪枝压缩解释长度。

Result: 在五个医学影像数据集上，相对最强基线，将"至少由一个错误预测概念支撑的正确诊断"占比从 27.1% 降至 9.6%，同时使用不到一半的概念数量，并取得更高诊断准确率。

Conclusion: ReCAP 表明在概念诊断中显式建模概念预测错误与弃权、并以判别性目标驱动采集，可在显著缩短解释的同时提升证据可靠性与诊断性能。

Abstract: Sequential concept acquisition enables interpretable diagnosis from a compact set of human-understandable findings. Such explanations are useful only if clinicians can trust them, yet most existing methods treat predicted concepts as correct, so a correct diagnosis can rest on incorrectly predicted evidence. We propose Reliable Concept Acquisition with Pruning (ReCAP), which reasons over what the concept predictor reports, including errors and abstentions, and replaces conditional information gain with a label-discriminating objective that separates cases requiring different diagnoses. We prove this objective adaptively monotone and adaptively submodular, yielding a (1-1/e) guarantee for greedy fixed-budget acquisition that degrades boundedly under learned estimates, and combine it with abstention-aware acquisition and diagnosis-preserving pruning to keep explanations short and reliable. Across five medical imaging datasets, ReCAP reduces the share of correct diagnoses supported by at least one incorrectly predicted concept from 27.1% to 9.6% relative to the strongest baseline, while using fewer than half as many concepts and achieving higher diagnostic accuracy.

</details>


### [45] [A Well-Posed Fourth-Order Navier-Stokes Model for Image Inpainting: Theory, Order Selection, and Stripe Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31777)
*Yiwen Dou,Yiqiang Ding,Zhongyun Bao,Senyan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文为Bertalmío–Bertozzi–Sapiro的Navier-Stokes图像修补模型给出首个系统性高阶扩展：证明直接的四阶修正是病态的，并构造了良态版本（门控前向四阶项），再以条带分解处理大块缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NS修补模型是PDE修补中唯一的输运型范式，但一直缺少高阶扩展；作者想提升其在结构主导型破损上的精度，并解决大块缺失时扩散门控失效的问题。

Method: 在涡量–流函数表述下引入门控四阶项，分析符号：后向形式属Perona-Malik型反扩散、Hadamard病态；前向形式为一致抛物、能量稳定。提出"阶–耗散原则"（奇阶色散、偶阶耗散），从而认定四阶为最低可接受阶数。针对大块缺失，提出条带分解：Telea基础填充 + 交错的窄缝式NS迭代，将块状损伤转化为结构性窄缝区域。整个方法免训练、与基础填充无关。

Result: 在BSDS500、DIV2K及一个中国古典绘画基准上，针对裂纹、块状、文字遮挡三类损伤，在结构主导损伤上逐样本优于全部经典基线；绘画测试集42张上较Telea提升+1.02 dB PSNR，逐样本胜率42/42；与最强近期PDE求解器差距在0.4 dB内，同时取得PDE族最佳感知质量；消费级GPU上4–16秒/图。

Conclusion: 高阶NS修补可行的关键在于四阶项符号（前向耗散）与门控机制；条带分解与门控修正的增益可叠加，构成免训练、基础填充无关的高效PDE修补框架。

Abstract: The Navier-Stokes (NS) inpainting model of Bertalmío, Bertozzi and Sapiro is the only transport-type paradigm among PDE-based methods, yet it has never received a systematic higher-order extension. We show that the obvious fourth-order correction is ill-posed, and we construct the corrected, well-posed model. In the vorticity-streamfunction formulation we add a gated fourth-order term whose sign is decisive: the backward form is a Perona-Malik-type anti-diffusion, Hadamard ill-posed and consistently harmful in experiments, whereas the forward form is uniformly parabolic, energy-stable and consistently beneficial. An order-dissipation principle, stating that odd orders are dispersive and even orders dissipative, singles out the fourth order as the lowest admissible one. Large missing blocks pose a separate problem, because the correction gate stays silent there; we answer it with stripe decomposition, a Telea base fill followed by interleaved slit-wise NS passes, which converts block damage into the structural-slit regime where the model excels. The device is base-agnostic, and its gains add to those of the forward correction. On three datasets, namely BSDS500, DIV2K and a classical Chinese painting benchmark, and on three damage types, namely cracks, blocks and text occlusion, the training-free model beats every classical baseline on every sample of structure-dominated damage, gains +1.02 dB PSNR over Telea on the full 42-image painting test set with a 42/42 per-sample win rate, matches the strongest recent PDE solvers within 0.4 dB while attaining the best perceptual quality of the entire PDE family, and runs at 4-16 s per image on a consumer GPU.

</details>


### [46] [VISOR: A Vision-Language Selector for Multimodal Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31780)
*Seunghan Lee,Jun Seo,Junhyeok Kang,Jaehoon Lee,Sangjun Han,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISOR proposes a multimodal time-series forecasting framework: a text-free stochastic predictor samples diverse candidate futures, and a vision-language model reads all candidates as one chart image and selects one plausible future based on textual context; a shape-aware target trains the selector.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型(TSFM)把预测分布压缩成中位数/均值，产生"平缓"轨迹；多模态TS预测依赖较弱的TS-语言对齐，远不如视觉-语言对齐成熟。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 无文本随机预测器采样多条候选未来；2) VLM选择器将候选序列渲染为单张折线图图像，结合文本信息选出最合适的一条；训练时采用shape-aware目标，同时考虑逐点误差与路径形状，避免过于平滑的预测。

Result: 在覆盖十个数据集的三个基准上，点误差与波动率误差的平均排名均优于TSFM及多模态TS预测方法。

Conclusion: 用"选择而非平均"的VLM选择器替代分布聚合，可保持预测多样性与波动结构，为多模态时序预测提供新范式。

Abstract: Recent time-series foundation models (TSFMs) forecast a distribution over futures, and multimodal time-series (TS) forecasting further exploits other modalities to improve forecasting accuracy. However, existing works have two limitations: 1) TSFMs reduce the predictive distribution to its median or mean, flattening diverse futures into a calm line, and 2) multimodal TS forecasting models incorporate text into the forecaster through TS-language alignment, which is substantially weaker than vision-language alignment. To address these limitations, we propose the VIsion-language SelectOR (VISOR), a multimodal TS forecasting framework that selects one plausible future instead of averaging across candidates, leveraging the alignment capabilities of vision-language models (VLMs) to select the most suitable candidate based on textual information. VISOR consists of two components: 1) a text-free stochastic predictor that samples diverse candidate futures, and 2) a VLM selector that reads the candidates as a single chart image and selects one based on the text. To avoid overly calm forecasting paths, we train the selector with a shape-aware target that evaluates each candidate based on both point-wise error and path shape. Across three benchmarks covering ten datasets, VISOR achieves the best average ranking in both point and volatility errors compared with TSFMs and multimodal TS forecasting models. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [47] [SceneMemBench: A Benchmark for Scene Memory in Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31803)
*Yuheng Si,Linye Lyu,Jiawei Zhou,Lingyi Du,Nanfei Ye,Aobo Liu,Yu Liu,Yu Li,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneMemBench：面向交互式视频世界模型场景记忆的基准，分离“已见视角回忆 vs 新视角恢复”与“受控诊断 vs 端到端交互”两个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多记忆评测集中于已见视角重访与完整 rollout，跨视角场景恢复（离开后再返回时世界是否可恢复）研究不足，且将记忆能力与轨迹跟随误差、生成退化缠绕在一起，难以定位真实失败模式。

Method: 构建含600条受控轨迹（33个Unreal Engine场景）与60个Full Rollout案例的基准，覆盖8个代表性开源/闭源模型；提出Continued Rollout——从共享的真实观测历史继续生成，与完整模型自生成轨迹的Full Rollout形成对照；沿主体一致性、外观保真度、几何一致性三个维度评估场景恢复。

Result: 新视角恢复整体难于已见视角回忆；受控历史下的模型画像与完整交互中观察到的画像可能显著不同，说明单一评测范式会掩盖互补的失败模式。

Conclusion: 仅靠已见视角重访或端到端 rollout 无法刻画视频世界模型的场景记忆；提供解耦的诊断协议，为长时一致、可重访世界模型提供评测基础。

Abstract: Interactive video world models generate navigable worlds frame by frame, but a persistent world requires previously observed scenes to remain recoverable as users move away and return. Existing memory evaluations, however, often focus on seen-view revisits and full rollouts, leaving cross-view scene recovery underexplored while entangling memory with trajectory-following errors and accumulated generation degradation. We introduce SceneMemBench, a benchmark for scene memory in interactive video world models. It evaluates two complementary dimensions: seen-view recall versus novel-view recovery, and controlled diagnosis versus end-to-end interaction. We introduce Continued Rollout from a shared ground-truth observation history, alongside Full Rollout over complete model-generated interactions. SceneMemBench contains 600 controlled trajectories across 33 Unreal Engine scenes and 60 Full Rollout cases, covering eight representative open- and closed-source models. We evaluate scene recovery along subject consistency, appearance fidelity, and geometric consistency. Results show that novel-view recovery is generally more challenging than seen-view recall, while model profiles under controlled histories can differ markedly from those observed in full interaction. Together, these findings reveal complementary failure modes obscured by seen-view revisits or end-to-end rollouts alone.

</details>


### [48] [Generalizable Feed-Forward 3D Hand-Object Reconstruction with Coarse-to-Fine Contact Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31812)
*Seonghwan Park,Yong-Hoon Kwon,Min-Gyu Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HORCRUX：统一前馈 Transformer，从单张图像联合重建手与物体 3D 姿态及其接触，采用粗到细接触估计，并借助冻结基础模型实现对未见物体的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合前馈估计器依赖预定义物体集合，难以泛化到未见物体；接触通常仅由图像特征直接回归，缺乏显式几何来精确定位接触点。

Method: 冻结的基础模型从给定或图像重建的网格编码物体表征（避免物体专属知识）；共享 Transformer 对物体与一到两只手联合推理以保持对齐；接触估计采用粗到细策略：先由隐特征给出逐关节粗预测，再利用重建几何在更精细的手部区域上解析精化。

Result: 在七个基准上，联合估计器中物体与手部姿态精度达 SOTA，且细粒度接触优于专用接触估计器。

Conclusion: 接触估计应与重建联合进行：重建得到的几何可直接用于接触定位，二者共享表示互为增益。

Abstract: Reconstructing the 3D poses of hands and objects from a single image, together with the contact between them, is fundamental to understanding manipulation. To be practical, the reconstruction must generalize to unseen objects, and the contact must be localized precisely. Yet joint feed-forward estimators often rely on predefined object sets, while contact is typically predicted from image features alone, without the explicit geometry needed to localize it. To address both limitations, we present HORCRUX, a unified feed-forward transformer with coarse-to-fine contact estimation. For unseen objects, a frozen foundation model encodes the object from a given or image-reconstructed mesh rather than from object-specific knowledge. A shared transformer then reasons jointly over the object and one or two hands to keep them aligned. For precise contact, coarse per-joint predictions from latent features are resolved over finer hand regions using the reconstructed geometry. Across seven benchmarks, HORCRUX achieves state-of-the-art object and hand pose accuracy among joint estimators and outperforms a dedicated contact estimator in fine-grained contact. These results suggest that contact is best estimated jointly with reconstruction, whose geometry directly localizes it.

</details>


### [49] [G0.7: Scaling Up a Fast World Action Model with Long Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31822)
*Tianyuan Yuan,Baijun Ye,Shicheng Cao,Xing Nie,Xiaopeng Yu,Ke Dong,Zijie Zhao,Shenling Qiu,Yizhao Xu,Feiyang Xue,Haonan Liu,Haoyu Zhang,Haobo Jia,Zheng Liu,Anqi Yang,Jiahui Niu,Yue Sun,Tailai Cheng,Jianpeng Yu,Yang Wen,Longhao Wei,Zibin Dong,Shiduo Zhang,Yicheng Liu,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G0.7 是一个沿"长上下文"与"大规模异构预训练数据"两轴扩展的通用 world action model；沿用 Fast-WAM 结论（视频预测在训练期塑造策略表征，部署时可跳过未来帧合成），以直接动作解码在单卡 RTX 5090 上 60 ms/16 步动作块完成实时闭环控制，并在仿真与真实机器人上验证性能随数据规模稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 world action model（WAM）在每次动作前生成未来帧，测试时推理过慢，难以支撑闭环控制；同时多数策略受限于极短的观测历史，缺乏长时程上下文与跨域泛化能力。

Method: 基于 Fast-WAM 的"训练期视频预测、部署期跳过帧生成"范式，提出 G0.7：上下文窗口从单帧扩展到最长约五分钟历史；预训练语料扩展至大规模机器人遥操作数据与人类第一人称（ego）视频；部署时用 Fast 模式直接解码动作，与同 checkpoint 的 imagine-then-act（IDM）共享权重，支持无停顿实时分块推理。

Result: Fast 模式与 IDM 性能相当，单张 RTX 5090 上每 16 步动作块 60 ms（比 IDM 快 7.6 倍）；五个仿真基准套件平均分 70.8；Galaxea R1 Pro / R1 Lite 零样本抓放成功率 48% / 54%；视频条件 in-context 学习任务 ID 96.7%、OOD 93.3%；七项真实世界后训练任务平均任务进度 86.9%（含多分钟折箱、饮品制备、商品装袋）。人类 ego 视频对 OOD 泛化贡献最大。

Conclusion: 长上下文与大规模异构（遥操作 + 人类 ego）预训练是提升通用 WAM 的有效两个缩放轴；训练期视频预测 + 部署期直接动作解码可兼顾世界模型表征收益与实时控制效率，且性能随预训练数据量单调提升。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) adapt pretrained video generators for robot control by jointly predicting future camera frames and actions, transferring physical priors learned from video into manipulation policies. However, generating future frames before every action makes test-time inference too slow for closed-loop control, while most existing policies remain restricted to short observation histories. Building on Fast-WAM, which showed that video prediction shapes policy representations during training so that future frame synthesis can be skipped at deployment, we present G0.7, a generalist world action model scaled along two axes: extending the context window from a single observation to up to five minutes of history, and scaling pretraining to a large-scale corpus of robot teleoperation and human egocentric (ego) video. Across public simulation suites and real-robot deployments, direct action decoding (Fast mode) matches imagine-then-act (IDM) inference from the same checkpoint while running in 60 ms per 16-step action chunk on a single RTX 5090 (7.6x faster than IDM) and supporting pause-free real-time chunking. Performance scales consistently with pretraining data, with human ego video driving the largest gains in out-of-distribution generalization: G0.7 achieves a 70.8 mean score across five simulation benchmark suites, 48% and 54% zero-shot pick-and-place success on Galaxea R1 Pro and R1 Lite, 96.7% in-distribution and 93.3% out-of-distribution success on video-conditioned in-context learning tasks, and 86.9% mean task progress across seven real-world post-training tasks including multi-minute box folding, drink preparation, and product bagging.

</details>


### [50] [Gaze World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31836)
*Jiawen Zhang,Houze Yang,Xu Cao,Yifan Shen,Zhongyi Zhou,Steve Wong,Ismini Lourentzou,Inki Kim,James M. Rehg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GWM 在冻结的 V-JEPA 2.1 编码器上联合训练潜在场景预测与人条件化注视监督，实现视频中的注视目标估计、注视转移检测及未来注视/转移预测，四类 AP 较最强基线高约 10 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注视目标估计方法基本逐帧处理，未建模注视目标之间的转移；而注视转移常由场景动态变化驱动，这正是世界模型擅长编码的信息，但二者关系隐蔽（远处事件可能只引起轻微头/眼运动）。

Method: 提出 Gaze World Model（GWM）：在冻结的 V-JEPA 2.1 编码器上，将潜在场景预测目标与人条件化注视监督协同训练；输入视频片段与人头框，输出当前片段内的注视位置与注视转移，并基于预测的场景特征外推未来注视与转移。同时为 VideoAttentionTarget 与 ChildPlay 增补事件级注视转移标注。

Result: 在增补基准上取得 SOTA 的转移检测（四类 AP 高约 10 点）；注视目标估计与最强图像方法相当；在未来注视定位与转移预测上优于对比基线。消融显示潜在预测目标同时提升转移检测与注视定位。

Conclusion: 将世界模型的潜在动态预测与注视监督结合有效建模注视转移，可作为注意力/交互意图预测的工具；但未涉及具身控制或 3D/机器人交互场景，属为具身代理提供感知先验的相邻工作。

Abstract: Where a person looks indicates their focus of attention, and how their gaze shifts between targets reveals how that attention responds to a changing scene. Existing gaze target estimation methods largely operate frame by frame and do not model these shifts. Gaze shifts are often driven by dynamic changes in the scene, which is the kind of information world models are designed to encode. This relationship is subtle, however, since an event far across the frame may produce only a slight movement of the person's head or eyes. We therefore introduce the Gaze World Model (GWM), which co-trains latent scene prediction with person-conditioned gaze supervision on a frozen V-JEPA 2.1 encoder; in our ablations, the latent prediction objective improves both shift detection and gaze localization. Given a video clip and the person's head box, GWM estimates gaze locations and detects gaze shifts in the observed clip, and forecasts future gaze and shifts from predicted scene features. To train and evaluate these tasks, we augment VideoAttentionTarget and ChildPlay with event-level gaze-shift annotations. GWM achieves state-of-the-art shift detection on these benchmarks, about 10 points higher in four-class AP than the strongest baseline, while remaining comparable to the strongest image-based models in gaze target estimation, and outperforms the compared baselines on future gaze localization and shift forecasting.

</details>


### [51] [JECT: Addressing the Context Shortcut in Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning for Chest CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31849)
*Hanbin Ko,Hong-Jun Choi,Jihun Kim,Young-Gon Kim,Dongheon Lee,Chang Min Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JECT：胸部CT自监督预训练框架，提出"上下文捷径"问题，让报告同时作为预测器条件与编码器预测目标


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA式掩码特征预测在胸部CT上失效：小结节等决定性发现无法由周围解剖推断，模型会倾向于预测成"正常解剖"，损失不再奖励编码器表征隐藏病灶。仅把报告作为预测器条件虽修复重建质量，但编码器学到的信息几乎不超出纯图像预训练，即知识被报告"外包"。

Method: 报告承担双重角色：(1) 作为预测器条件；(2) 通过掩码章节文本预测作为编码器目标，即由未掩码扫描预测报告的解剖章节文本。采用分层三维编码器，在各层级尺度上进行预测以保留临床语义。另外报告句子级条件预测器可零训练复用做句子-区域归因。

Result: 消融显示文本目标显著提升分类与定位，而条件项对编码器贡献甚微。173k 体积从头训练，在分类、检索、结节恶性度上达到现有CT基础模型水平，微调后在肺癌风险预测上达到任务专用模型水平；复用条件预测器可定位报告句子作用区域，且改动集中在句子所描述的发现处。

Conclusion: "上下文捷径"是掩码预测在医学影像中的结构性缺陷；将报告从条件转为编码器预测目标可有效注入临床语义，同时保留条件预测器的可解释复用价值。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures learn by predicting features of masked regions from visible context. In chest computed tomography, the findings that decide a diagnosis, such as a small nodule, are often not predictable from the surrounding anatomy. A hidden finding is then best predicted toward normal anatomy, and the prediction loss stops rewarding the encoder for representing it. We call this the context shortcut. Conditioning the predictor on the radiology report repairs the prediction, but in our setup the encoder gains little over image-only pretraining: the report supplies what the encoder never had to learn. JECT therefore gives the report two roles: a condition for the predictor and, through masked section-text prediction, a target for the encoder, which must predict the report's anatomical sections from the unmasked scan. A hierarchical three-dimensional encoder that predicts at every stage keeps this clinical content at every scale. In ablations, the text target improves classification and localization, while conditioning adds little to the encoder. Trained from scratch on 173k volumes, the recipe reaches the level of current computed tomography foundation models in classification, retrieval and nodule malignancy, and of task-specific models in lung-cancer risk prediction after fine-tuning. Conditioning still matters: its predictor can be reused, with no extra training, to show where a report sentence acts, changing most at the finding the sentence describes. Code and pretrained weights are available.

</details>


### [52] [Don't Miss the Forest for the Trees: Zero-Shot Skeleton Action Recognition with Hyperbolic Hierarchical Semantic Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31877)
*Zixiao Jin,Siyu Zhu,Feng Ding,Haisheng Fu,Xin Dong,Shankai Chen,Jie Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出零样本骨架动作识别仅在扁平叶子标签上评估，叶子对齐强并不代表能表征上层概念；提出双曲层级语义原型（H²SP），在Lorentz模型中用父子测地插值合成层级感知原型，并加入切空间对齐与软蕴含锥正则，显著提升全路径层级概念准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本骨架动作识别几乎只在扁平叶子标签上评测，无法判断表示是否同样能锚定包含该动作的更高层概念。实验发现叶子Top-1达57.86–73.29%的八种扁平方法，在独立解码下祖先概念的全路径准确率仅6.15–31.21%，说明叶子对齐不能迁移到层级概念。

Method: 提出H²SP框架，将动作分类树所有节点表示在双曲空间Lorentz模型中。1）不逐节点学习自由向量，而是在单一树级门控下通过递归父子测地插值合成层级感知原型；2）对可见类做切空间语义与分布对齐，将叶子原型锚定到骨架运动；3）设计软蕴含锥正则，鼓励有向的父子包含关系，将运动锚定传播到祖先概念。在独立解码、级联解码以及跨分类体系迁移三种设定下评测。

Result: 在NTU RGB+D四个划分上，独立与级联全路径准确率均取得更优（对比方法中最佳），叶子Top-1为72.40%（可比水平）。迁移到另一分类体系且不重训时，在每个划分和两种解码模式下均优于扁平基线及同树欧氏对照。

Conclusion: 扁平叶子对齐不足以支撑层级概念表征；双曲测地插值原型配合蕴含锥约束可将运动语义传播至祖先节点，且概念级对应关系可泛化到训练分类体系之外。

Abstract: Zero-shot skeleton-based action recognition is evaluated almost exclusively on flat leaf labels, which leaves open whether a representation also grounds the broader concepts that contain an action in skeleton motion. We find that strong leaf alignment does not carry over to them: when ancestor concepts are scored directly under independent decoding, eight flat methods with 57.86-73.29% mean leaf Top-1 reach only 6.15-31.21% mean full-path accuracy across four NTU RGB+D splits. We propose the Hyperbolic Hierarchical Semantic Prototype ($H^2SP$) framework, which represents every node of an action taxonomy in the Lorentz model of hyperbolic space. First, instead of learning a free vector per node, $H^2SP$ synthesizes hierarchy-aware prototypes by recursive parent-child geodesic interpolation under a single tree-wide gate. Second, tangent-space semantic and distributional alignment on seen classes anchor the leaf prototypes in skeleton motion. Third, a soft entailment-cone regularizer encourages directional parent-child inclusion, propagating this motion grounding to ancestor concepts. We evaluate hierarchical concept prediction under independent and cascade decoding, and transfer to an alternative taxonomy without retraining. Across the four splits, $H^2SP$ attains the best mean independent and cascade full-path accuracy among the evaluated methods, at a comparable leaf Top-1 of 72.40%. Transferred to the alternative taxonomy, it outperforms both flat baselines and the same-tree Euclidean control across every split and decoding mode, indicating that its concept-level correspondence extends beyond the training taxonomy.

</details>


### [53] [RELAY: Learning Compatible Representations One Encoder at a Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31882)
*Yu Wang,Chuan Qin,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RELAY通过"投影对齐-冻结-往返重编码"的师徒式流程，解决增量重编码图库时新旧文档嵌入共存导致检索不一致的问题，在COCO/Flickr30K的文本-图像检索升级中使未动图库Recall@1提升8.66-10.23个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 升级多模态检索系统时，图库需逐步重编码，期间新旧文档嵌入并存，查询向量与两代文档的兼容性不一致会造成检索性能下降（backfill期间掉分）。

Method: 先用学习到的投影，针对原始图库训练新查询编码器；再冻结查询编码器与原始图库，训练新文档编码器，使同一查询向量在单次重编码过程中同时兼容两代文档；回填完成后可选"查询精炼"：丢弃投影、直接复用已存文档向量。

Result: 三次COCO/Flickr30K检索升级中，未触碰图库的Recall@1较原系统提升8.66-10.23个百分点；直接重编码下，Recall@1曲线下面积在Qwen→Qwen升级中第一、CLIP→Qwen中第二（原生打分与校准打分均如此）；固定嵌入时对两代文档分别校准可减小回填期掉分并改变最优方法排名；查询精炼后最终Recall@1与常规联合训练差距不超过0.97个百分点。

Conclusion: 一种实用的增量重编码配方，兼顾回填期稳定性与最终精度，并可退化为标准联合训练的近似替代方案。

Abstract: Old and new document embeddings coexist during incremental gallery re-encoding. RELAY trains a new query encoder with a learned projection against the original gallery, then freezes both to train the new document encoder. The same query vector scores both document versions throughout a single re-encoding pass. After backfill, optional query refinement drops the projection and reuses the stored document vectors. Across three text-to-image retrieval upgrades on COCO and Flickr30K, RELAY improves Recall@1 on untouched galleries by 8.66-10.23 percentage points over the original systems. For direct re-encoding, RELAY ranks first in both Qwen-to-Qwen upgrades and second in CLIP-to-Qwen by area under the Recall@1 curve, under both native scoring and matched calibration. With embeddings held fixed, calibrating scores separately for each document version can reduce drops in Recall@1 during backfill and change which method achieves the highest backfill AUC. After query refinement, final Recall@1 is within 0.97 percentage points of conventional joint training in all three upgrades.

</details>


### [54] [Rethinking Video Agents: When and Why Video Harnesses Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31883)
*Luoyuan Zhang,Xuanyu Zheng,Yancheng Long,Haoyang Xu,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以 Video-Relook 为实验框架，让模型在统一默认配置基础上自适应调整时间与空间观测，分析不同模型与任务下交互式视频理解框架（harness）的收益差异及其成因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定观测策略预先决定模型可见证据，而 agentic harness 允许模型在理解推进中主动请求新观测，理论上可释放固定输入无法触及的能力；但"能请求观测"不等于"能有效利用观测"，本文探究 harness 何时、为何带来增益。

Method: 提出 Video-Relook 实验框架：模型从共同默认观测配置出发，自主决定时间（重看片段）与空间（区域/分辨率）观测调整；跨多个模型与任务采集性能与交互轨迹，分析证据寻求行为与答案修订可靠性。

Result: 自适应观测在细粒度动作理解上带来显著提升，收益延伸至 Video-MME-v2；同一观测接口下模型间增益差异明显，交互轨迹显示证据寻求方式与答案修订可靠性不同；同一模型在不同任务上从显著提升到有限增益甚至性能回退不等。

Conclusion: harness 有效性取决于"可获得信息性观测的机会"与"模型将交互转化为可靠修订的能力"之间的对齐；主张构建协调感知与推理、使观测自主性与任务需求和模型能力相匹配的视频 harness。

Abstract: Video understanding depends not only on how models reason about visual evidence, but also on which evidence they observe. Fixed observation policies determine this evidence in advance; agentic harnesses allow models to seek further observations as their understanding develops. This shift creates an opportunity to realize capabilities that fixed inputs leave untapped, yet the ability to request observations does not ensure their effective use. We investigate when and why video harnesses improve understanding, examining how task demands and models' capacity for interaction jointly shape their benefits. Using Video-Relook as an experimental framework, we let models adapt their temporal and spatial observations from a common default configuration, and analyze performance and interaction trajectories across models and tasks. Adaptive observation yields substantial gains on fine-grained action understanding, with benefits also extending to Video-MME-v2. Even under the same observation interface, gains differ substantially across models; interaction traces reveal corresponding differences in evidence seeking and the reliability of answer revision. The benefits also vary across tasks for the same model, ranging from substantial improvements to limited gains and regressions. Together, these findings support an account in which harness effectiveness depends on the alignment between opportunities for informative observation and the model's ability to turn interaction into reliable revisions. They motivate video harnesses that coordinate perception and reasoning, matching observational autonomy to task demands and model capabilities.

</details>


### [55] [Open-OmniCoach: Evidence-Enhanced Trajectory Learning for Omni Video Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31891)
*Weisong Liu,Xiangtai Li,Haochen Wang,Haiquan Wen,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Open OmniCoach 框架，包含 OmnCoach-240K 数据集（24.82 万条交互轨迹，含多跨度与反思轨迹）与步级证据引导的策略优化方法 SEPO，训练出 Open OmniAgent，在多模态视频理解基准上显著超越 Qwen3-Omni。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态视频智能体数据管线难以控制任务相关证据，且对"定位、关联、纠错证据"能力缺乏有效监督，导致全模态视频智能体可靠性受限。

Method: 构建三阶段数据管线，利用细粒度全模态证据与智能体自身错误生成 248.2K 轨迹（230.7K 多跨度 + 17.4K 反思轨迹）；提出 SEPO，基于证据覆盖率与时间定位改进量给出回合级信用分配，进行智能体强化学习。

Result: Open OmniAgent-30B 在九个基准上平均准确率较 Qwen3-Omni 提升 6.3 个百分点，LVOmniBench 与 LVBench 分别提升 12.2 与 13.3 点。

Conclusion: 证据寻求式的数据构建与步级信用 RL 联合设计可有效提升全模态视频智能体性能，框架具备可扩展性。

Abstract: Training capable omni video agents requires more than diverse question-answer pairs: agents must learn to reliably find, connect, and correct task-relevant evidence in omni-setting. However, we argue that existing data pipelines provide limited control over task-relevant evidence and weak supervision for these capabilities. In this work, we present Open OmniCoach, a new framework for evidence-seeking omni video agents. It contains a high-quality dataset, OmniCoach-240K, and a new agentic reinforcement learning (RL) method, Step-Level Evidence-Guided Policy Optimization (SEPO). For the former, OmniCoach-240K comprises 248.2K interaction trajectories, including 230.7K multi-span trajectories and 17.4K reflection trajectories. We design a three-stage data pipeline by leveraging both fine-grained omni-evidence and the agent's own errors. Meanwhile, compared with other RL baselines, SEPO provides turn-level credit based on improvements in evidence coverage and temporal localization. Applying both trajectory data and the SEPO method, we obtain Open OmniAgent models. The resulting models outperform the baselines across multiple video and audio-visual understanding tasks by significant margins, demonstrating the effectiveness of the Open OmniCoach framework. In particular, Open OmniAgent-30B improves average accuracy over Qwen3-Omni by 6.3 percentage points across nine benchmarks, with gains of 12.2 and 13.3 points on LVOmniBench and LVBench, respectively.

</details>


### [56] [Loupe: Reinforcing Knowledge-Intensive Video Generation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31895)
*Kai Zeng,Ruichuan An,Zhen Fang,Xi Fang,Han Lyu,Moran Li,Yingchen Yu,Yifan Dai,Bohan Zeng,Quanqing Xu,Zhengzhou Zhu,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Loupe：一个知识密集型视频生成智能体，通过检索并验证文本/视觉证据，构建带参考图的时间化视频提示；提出基于评分量表的分步信用（RBC），用于搜索规划步骤评分，并据此做SFT重加权与GRPO，RL阶段无需渲染视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识密集型视频生成中提示常缺失事实、外观与时间关系；搜索式流程依赖手工策略，缺乏自适应规划；视频级反馈代价高昂，轨迹级信用分配过于粗糙。

Method: 构建智能体框架 Loupe，检索并验证证据生成证据锚定的时序提示与参考图；提出 Rubric-Based Credit（RBC）按提示特定的事实/视觉/时序要求评估中间步骤；RBC-SFT 按步骤质量重加权 token 学习，RBC-GRPO 结合步骤级信用与最终规划奖励；构建 Search-VBench（150 条提示，7 类）。

Result: 三个视频生成器上四项评价维度均提升，MiniMax-H3 总分从 32.80 升至 70.67，优于未训练搜索流程；完整 RBC 训练比标准 SFT+轨迹级 GRPO 高 5.27 分；人类评估及 Sci-VBench、KIVI-Bench 显示科学性与事实精度提升。

Conclusion: 步骤级评分量表信用分配可有效替代昂贵的视频级反馈，使智能体式检索规划在知识密集型视频生成中显著优于手工流程与轨迹级 RL。

Abstract: Knowledge-intensive video generation remains challenging because prompts often omit the facts, appearances, and temporal relationships required for accurate generation. While search-based workflows can retrieve missing evidence, manually designed strategies limit adaptive planning, and conventional training suffers from costly video-level feedback and coarse trajectory-level credit assignment. In this paper, we present Loupe, a video generation agent that retrieves and verifies textual and visual evidence to construct evidence-grounded temporal video prompts with reference images. We further introduce Rubric-Based Credit (RBC), which leverages prompt-specific factual, visual, and temporal requirements to evaluate intermediate search and planning decisions before video rendering. Building on RBC, RBC-SFT reweights assistant-token learning by step quality, while RBC-GRPO incorporates step-level credits alongside a final planning reward, without rendering videos during RL. To address the lack of dedicated search-oriented evaluation, we construct Search-VBench, which contains 150 held-out prompts across seven categories. Experiments show that Loupe improves all four evaluation dimensions across three video generators, raising the overall score of MiniMax-H3 from 32.80 to 70.67 and outperforming an untrained search workflow. Ablation experiments show that full RBC training outperforms standard SFT followed by trajectory-level GRPO by 5.27 points. Human evaluations and results on Sci-VBench and KIVI-Bench further demonstrate improved generation quality, scientific grounding, factual precision, and helpfulness. Our project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [57] [Beyond Expert Replay: Cross-Instruction Anchoring for Continual Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31903)
*Ziyang Chen,Dehua Chen,Shaoguang Wang,Xu Yan,Hao Huang,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Cross-Instruction Anchoring (XIA)：在VLA持续学习中，每进入新任务时将旧策略冻结为教师，用旧指令+当前观测做蒸馏式响应对齐，弥补有限经验回放的监督缺口，抑制"旧指令触发新学竞争目标"的结构性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型需持续学习新操作技能且不遗忘旧指令。仅靠少量历史专家帧经验回放(ER)，大量旧指令响应缺乏直接监督，出现"旧指令被新学竞争目标劫持"的结构性失效模式，需在不增加回放数据预算的前提下扩展监督。

Method: 任务边界处冻结上一阶段策略作为教师；将旧指令与当前观测配对输入教师与学生，匹配其响应(蒸馏式锚定)，从而在存储的专家样本之外提供指令条件下的响应保持约束。可与ER组合，兼容多种VLA架构。

Result: 在多个VLA架构与三个LIBERO套件上，相同历史专家数据预算下成功率更高、遗忘更少，优于ER基线；RoboCasa与真实世界实验进一步验证有效性。

Conclusion: 指令条件下的响应保持是对经验回放的有效补充，可低成本缓解VLA持续学习中的指令—目标错配式遗忘；有真实机器人验证，但缺少开源代码与实际部署信息。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models need to continually acquire new manipulation skills while retaining the ability to follow previously learned instructions. Experience replay (ER) can mitigate catastrophic forgetting, but retaining only a small number of historical expert frames per task leaves many old-instruction responses without direct supervision. Under limited replay, we observe a structured failure mode in which an earlier instruction can trigger a subsequently learned, competing goal. To address this gap, we propose Cross-Instruction Anchoring (XIA), which supplements expert replay with instruction-conditioned response preservation. At each task boundary, XIA freezes the previous policy as a teacher and matches its responses to old instructions paired with current observations. This extends supervision beyond stored historical examples without requiring additional expert replay data. Experiments across multiple VLA architectures and three LIBERO suites demonstrate improved success rates and reduced forgetting compared with ER under identical historical expert-data budgets. Additional RoboCasa and real-world experiments further support the effectiveness of XIA.

</details>


### [58] [Your VLM Knows What to Inspect: Semantic Probe-Guided Adaptive Focusing for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31909)
*Ruixiao Shi,Fu Feng,Yucheng Xie,Jing Wang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPARK: 无训练的 GUI grounding 框架，用 VLM 的语义探针（exploration/verification probes）驱动自适应视窗观察，再以不确定性加权投票融合多视图预测，在 ScreenSpot-Pro 上提升 13.9–25.2 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI grounding 需在给定语义上下文中解析细微视觉细节；多视图/局部裁剪可提升定位，但其收益取决于所暴露的证据与保留的上下文，如何按需选择视图缺乏原则性方法。

Method: training-free 框架：利用 VLM 即使坐标预测错误也能描述可检查视觉证据的能力，让 exploration probes 描述相关上下文与区分性目标细节，verification probes 给出评估候选假设所需证据；用探针的空间注意力决定视图放置位置，用上下文性回答决定裁剪范围，用观测历史鼓励互补性检查；最终通过不确定性加权投票整合各视图预测。

Result: ScreenSpot-Pro 上较单视图推理分别提升 13.9（Qwen3-VL）、25.2（Qwen2.5-VL）、16.1（UI-TARS）个百分点，且在三个骨干上均优于所有对比的 test-time 方法；在另外四个基准上取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 语义探针是将冻结 VLM 的“该看哪里”的理解转化为更准确 GUI grounding 的有效机制，且无需训练、可直接套用于多种现有视觉语言模型。

Abstract: GUI grounding requires resolving fine visual details within the context that gives them meaning. Additional views can improve localization, but their value depends on what evidence they expose and how much context they preserve. We explore a capability of vision-language models (VLMs): even when their coordinate predictions are incorrect, they can describe visual evidence worth inspecting. We introduce \textbf{SPARK}, \textit{\textbf{S}emantic \textbf{P}robe-guided \textbf{A}daptive \textbf{R}egion see\textbf{K}ing}, a training-free framework that turns this semantic guidance into targeted visual observations. Exploration probes describe relevant context and distinguishing target details, while verification probes identify evidence needed to assess emerging hypotheses. Their spatial attention guides view placement, contextual responses determine crop extent, and observation history encourages complementary inspection. The resulting predictions are combined through uncertainty-weighted voting. On ScreenSpot-Pro, SPARK improves grounding accuracy over single-view inference by 13.9, 25.2, and 16.1 percentage points with Qwen3-VL, Qwen2.5-VL, and UI-TARS, outperforming all compared test-time methods for each backbone. Consistent gains across four additional benchmarks demonstrate its effectiveness across diverse interfaces and grounding tasks. These results establish semantic probing as an effective mechanism for turning a frozen VLM's understanding of what to inspect into more accurate GUI grounding.

</details>


### [59] [HiGS: A Hierarchical Architecture for Fast Differentiable 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31918)
*Dawid Pająk,Martin Bisson,Rodolfo Lima,François Trudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiGS 将 3DGS 的划分、光栅化与并行单元解耦：粗 macro-tile 划分高斯、细 render tile 做光栅化、按深度排序的有界批次并行处理，并把优化器融合进反向传播以降低训练显存与带宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 用同一 tile 同时充当划分单元、光栅化单元与并行单元：划分希望大 tile、光栅化希望小 tile，且每 tile 一个工作单元导致最稠密 tile 决定整帧耗时，负载不均视图性能差。

Method: 统一渲染与重建架构：高斯按粗 macro-tile 分区，在其内部细 render tile 上光栅化，并以有界、深度排序的批次处理；稠密区域发更多批次而非更长单元。相同批次支撑重建：合成过程提供可独立微分的透射率与颜色；将优化器融合进反向传播，直接存储/更新的参数与动量减半，并把外观更新推迟到视图之外。

Result: 在评测的所有已发表方法中渲染最快，负载不均视图上优势最大；重建速度更快、显存更少，且保持同等图像质量与精确前向 alpha 合成。

Conclusion: 通过角色分离的粒度设计与优化器-反向融合，HiGS 同时改善了 3DGS 的渲染负载均衡与训练开销，为统一渲染/重建管线提供了实用方案，代码尚未提及开源。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) makes one screen tile the cell of partitioning, the cell of rasterization, and the unit of parallel work. Partitioning favors large tiles and rasterization small ones, and with one unit per tile the densest tiles set the frame time. We present Hierarchically Tiled Gaussian Splatting (HiGS), a unified architecture for rendering and reconstruction that gives each role its own granularity: Gaussians are partitioned over coarse macro-tiles, rasterized over the fine render tiles within them, and processed in bounded depth-sorted batches. A dense region issues more batches instead of one longer unit, so frame time follows the work in a view rather than its densest tile. The same batches carry reconstruction: compositing supplies the transmittance and color needed to differentiate each one independently, leaving optimizer memory traffic as the dominant training cost. We fuse the optimizer into the backward pass, halve the parameters and moments it stores and updates directly, and defer appearance updates outside the view. HiGS renders scenes faster than every published method we evaluate, by the widest margin on views with uneven load, and reconstructs them faster and in less memory, at equal image quality and with exact front-to-back alpha compositing.

</details>


### [60] [DUALingo: A Dual-Hand Instruction-Guided Dataset for Affordance Grounding on 3D Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31919)
*Ming-Lun Lee,Yi-En Li,Feng-Dian Su,Ming-Han Lee,Kun-Ru Wu,Krzysztof Czarnecki,Yu-Chee Tseng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DUALingo：大规模任务条件化、角色感知的双臂3D可供性数据集与自动生成流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D可供性数据集只标注单一交互区域，而日常操作任务常需双手互补协作；人工标注双手协同接触点难以规模化。

Method: 将每个任务表示为两个协作且与左右手无关的角色，各自在物体表面以热图形式接地；用开放权重基础模型构建全自动流水线，在7.1万物体上生成27.1万自由形式任务标注，无需人工标注，并以专家评分与经验证的LLM裁判确认质量。

Result: 在DUALingo上训练的现有3D可供性模型可从自然语言指令同时接地两个协作角色，最佳模型aIoU由零样本10.3提升至37.8（超3倍）；机器人操作演示验证了预测的双臂可供性可执行。

Conclusion: DUALingo为学习日常双臂可供性提供了可扩展的基础数据资源，并展示了双臂可供性接地的可行性与可操作性。

Abstract: Affordance grounding localizes where an agent should interact with an object to perform a task. Many everyday manipulation tasks, however, require bimanual affordance, in which two hands assume complementary and cooperative roles to accomplish a shared goal. Existing 3D affordance datasets primarily ground single interaction regions, and manually annotating dual-hand, cooperative contacts does not scale. To address this gap, we introduce DUALingo, a large-scale dataset for task-conditioned, role-aware bimanual 3D affordance grounding on diverse everyday objects. DUALingo represents each task as two cooperative, hand-agnostic roles, each grounded as a heatmap on the object surface. It contains 271K free-form tasks on 71K objects, generated by a fully automated pipeline of open-weight foundation models without human labeling. Expert ratings and a validated LLM judge confirm the quality of the resulting annotations. Trained on DUALingo, existing 3D affordance models learn to ground both cooperative roles from natural-language instructions, with the best-performing model achieving a more than threefold improvement in aIoU over the zero-shot baseline, from 10.3 to 37.8. Robotic manipulation demonstrations illustrate the actionability of the predicted bimanual affordances in performing cooperative tasks. These results position DUALingo as a scalable foundation for learning everyday bimanual affordances. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [61] [TANDEM: Temporal Anchor Densification With Reliability-Aware Memory for Lightweight Sparse Depth Completion in Endoscopic Robotic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31922)
*Weikuan Qiu,Jiale Hou,Chongzhao Xu,Jinlian Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TANDEM：面向内窥镜手术视频的因果稀疏深度补全框架，用显式记忆将历史稀疏测距重投影到当前帧以补足度量证据，再用轻量网络做可靠性加权梯度积分与频率门控参考先验重建稠密深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜视频缺乏可靠度量深度，而跟踪、ToF、器械接触等获得的度量值每帧仅覆盖极少像素，单帧稀疏深度补全信息不足，属未解难题。

Method: 因果时序锚点致密化：历史测量重投影至当前视角恢复度量证据；显式记忆以解析的"重观测/时效/运动视差"三因子打分，且不覆盖当前测量以保证可解释、准确、高效；紧凑网络以可靠性加权梯度积分+频率门控参考先验输出稠密深度。

Result: 在C3VD结肠镜与StereoMIS腹腔镜数据集上，仅5.58M参数即相对最强基线将RMSE降低11.7%与32.0%，达到SOTA；在内窥镜位姿估计量级误差下仍保持精度。

Conclusion: 为内窥镜视频稀疏深度补全提供了轻量、可解释且对位姿误差鲁棒的时序方案；属医疗领域CV，与具身机器人重建主线关联有限。

Abstract: For endoscopic robotic surgery, accurate dense depth perception serves as a foundational capability, which enables navigation, instrument localization, and tissue interaction. However, endoscopic video lacks reliable metric depth, while metric measurements obtained via tracking, time-of-flight sensing, or instrument contact only cover a small fraction of pixels in each frame, rendering sparse depth completion an urgent and unsolved challenge. In this work, we propose Temporal Anchor Densification with Reliability-Aware Memory (TANDEM), a causal sparse depth completion framework for endoscopic video. It adopts a lightweight design and leverages the video stream: past measurements re-projected into the current view recover the metric evidence unavailable within a single sparse frame. To keep this evidence interpretable, accurate, and efficient, an explicit memory scores each re-projected measurement with analytic re-observation, age, and motion-parallax factors without overriding current measurements. A compact network then reconstructs depth via reliability-weighted gradient integration and a frequency-gated reference prior. We evaluate TANDEM on the C3VD colonoscopy and StereoMIS laparoscopy datasets. With only 5.58 M parameters, TANDEM reduces the root mean square error by 11.7% and 32.0%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline, and achieves state-of-the-art accuracy. It also remains accurate under pose errors of the magnitude reported for endoscopic pose estimation. Code will be released at this https URL upon acceptance.

</details>


### [62] [Decompose, Verify, Evolve: Physically Grounded Shape Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31924)
*Wenchao Ma,Rui Yu,Fan Zhu,Jiachen Liu,Sharon X. Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysDec: agentic framework using VLM semantics to predict material-aware primitive decompositions from images; PhysDec-Loop iteratively refines with point-cloud geometric feedback and physical verifiers, and PhysDec-Evolved distills the correction loop for point-cloud-free inference.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing shape decomposition methods optimize geometric fidelity only, lacking physical plausibility constraints, producing disconnected parts or physically unstable reconstructions.

Method: Agentic pipeline built on a pretrained VLM's semantic understanding and commonsense reasoning; predicts material-aware primitives from a few images. PhysDec-Loop reasons about part structure and materials with geometric feedback from a reference point cloud plus physical verifiers guiding iterative refinement; self-evolution yields PhysDec-Evolved, which learns from prior corrections to infer decompositions directly from images.

Result: PhysDec-Loop improves geometric fidelity, physical plausibility, and visual quality over geometry-focused baselines; PhysDec-Evolved approaches its performance at substantially lower inference cost.

Conclusion: Rendering decompositions agentically physical-aware is feasible; the correction loop can be internalized via self-evolution, enabling efficient image-only inference for simulation-ready assets.

Abstract: Shape decomposition represents 3D objects as a compact set of geometric primitives for interpretable modeling and simulation. However, existing decomposition methods are trained primarily for geometric fidelity, without explicit constraints on physical plausibility, and can therefore produce disconnected parts or physically unstable reconstructions. We present PhysDec, an agentic framework that draws on a pretrained vision-language model's semantic understanding and commonsense reasoning to predict material-aware primitive decompositions from a few images. Within this framework, PhysDec-Loop is an agentic pipeline that reasons about part structure and materials, using a reference 3D point cloud for geometric feedback and physical verifiers to guide iterative refinement. Through self-evolution, we further develop another agentic pipeline, PhysDec-Evolved, which learns from prior corrections to predict primitive decompositions from images without point-cloud feedback or a correction loop at inference. Experiments show that PhysDec-Loop improves geometric fidelity, physical plausibility and visual quality over geometry-focused baselines, while PhysDec-Evolved approaches its performance at substantially lower inference cost.

</details>


### [63] [DraftKV: Protecting Persistent Worlds through Hierarchical Streaming Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31944)
*Xiaoling Zhou,Haopeng Li,Yufa Zhou,Rui Ding,Yu Li,Jun Lin,Enze Xie,Chunhua Shen,Xuan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DraftKV：面向交互式视频世界模型的免训练历史KV缓存检索框架，用几何接地与query相关性的草稿检索+CPU卸载+tile级流式注意力，实现3.27×加速、42.2%显存峰值下降，并支持单张H100运行33B世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散世界模型在重访已探索区域时难以保持空间几何与外观一致性；历史KV缓存虽能提升长期一致性，但检索与注意力计算随历史增长带来显著延迟与显存开销。

Method: 提出DraftKV：(1) 基于几何接地与query条件相关性的历史KV缓存草稿检索；(2) CPU offload结合tile级流式注意力以降低显存与延迟；(3) 引入streaming CG-Attention，使CPU→GPU KV传输与注意力计算重叠，保持显存有界；(4) 理论分析：证明草稿相关性精确复现全分辨率块相关性均值分支，偏差仅限β加权max分支；以Top-K选择重叠度界定检索效用损失；指出池化描述子无法区分相似块，需几何接地先行。

Result: 跨多种模型家族实验显示，DraftKV在生成质量与一致性上优于其他CPU卸载KV检索方法，端到端加速3.27×，峰值GPU显存降低42.2%；可在单张H100上用SolarWM-33B进行高质量交互式世界生成。

Conclusion: DraftKV为交互式视频世界模型提供了一条免训练、可扩展的长时程一致性推理路径，兼具理论保证与实测效率收益。

Abstract: Interactive video world models enable action- and camera-controlled exploration, yet struggle to maintain consistent spatial geometry and appearance when revisiting previously explored locations. Historical KV caches help auto-regressive video diffusion models maintain long-term consistency, but their retrieval and attention computation introduce substantial latency as history grows. We propose DraftKV, a training-free framework that retrieves historical KV caches based on geometric grounding and query-conditioned relevance, while combining CPU offloading with tile-wise streaming attention to reduce GPU memory usage and inference latency. Theoretically, we show that draft relevance reproduces the mean branch of full-resolution block relevance exactly, so its deviation is confined to the $\beta$-weighted max branch, and we bound retrieval utility loss in terms of the measured Top-K selection overlap. We further show that draft relevance cannot distinguish blocks with similar pooled descriptors regardless of camera pose, motivating geometric grounding before refinement. Then, we introduce streaming CG-Attention, which overlaps CPU-to-GPU KV transfers with attention computation through tile-wise streaming, hiding transfer latency while maintaining bounded GPU memory usage. Experiments across diverse model families show that DraftKV achieves superior generation quality and consistency while achieving $3.27\times$ end-to-end speedup and 42.2% peak GPU memory reduction over other CPU-offloaded KV cache retrieval methods. Notably, DraftKV enables high-quality interactive world generation with historical KV retrieval on a single H100 GPU using SolarWM-33B.

</details>


### [64] [Beyond Rigid Hands: Deforming Skin into Contact-Consistent Hand Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31950)
*Seonghwan Park,Min-Gyu Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"抬升"(lift)框架：将刚体手网格通过联合优化（关节/腕微调 + 皮肤形变 + 时序一致性）转为接触一致网格，并用轻量预测头引导估计手的接触区域，使网格贴合物体表面而不显著改变姿态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手-物数据集与估计器把手建模为刚体关节网格，导致网格悬空或穿透物体；已有refiner只能调整姿态来换取物理合理性，牺牲姿态精度。作者希望在不改动手模型、不重训估计器的前提下，让刚体手像真实皮肤一样接触物体且保持原姿态。

Method: 三阶段优化：(i)微调关节与腕使指尖落到物体上；(ii)对穿透区域做皮肤形变使其贴合表面；(iii)约束形变在时间上一致。由于形变仅作用于靠近物体的皮肤，另训练一个轻量head在抬升后的标注上学习预测估计手应接触的位置，以引导形变。

Result: 对三个已发表估计器，抬升使落在物体表面的皮肤面积增加一倍以上，而姿态几乎不变；学习头引导进一步闭合估计残留的接触间隙，提升抓取稳定性。

Conclusion: 刚体手可在几乎不改变姿态的情况下获得接触一致的皮肤形变；该后处理模块可直接接入现有数据集与估计器，无需改动手模型或重训。

Abstract: Hands grasp objects with soft skin. A fingertip flattens against a mug, and a palm curves around a bottle. Yet hand-object datasets and estimators represent the hand as a rigid articulated mesh, which hovers above or penetrates the object where skin would press against it. Existing refiners keep the mesh rigid and adjust only the pose, trading pose accuracy for physical plausibility. We ask whether a rigid hand can instead touch the object as skin does while staying close to its original pose. We lift annotated or estimated rigid hands to contact-consistent meshes through an optimization that (i) slightly adjusts the joints and wrist to bring the fingers onto the object, (ii) deforms the skin so that penetrating regions settle onto the surface, and (iii) keeps this deformation consistent over time. Since deformation reaches only skin already near the object, we further train a lightweight head on the lifted annotations to predict where an estimated hand should touch and steer the deformation there. Unlike existing refiners, the lift more than doubles the skin lying on the object surface for three published estimators while barely changing the pose. Guidance from the learned head further improves grasp stability by closing contact gaps that the estimate leaves open. Existing hand-object datasets and estimators can thus adopt contact-consistent hands without changing the hand model or retraining the estimators.

</details>


### [65] [VLG-Drive: Unifying Vision-Language and Geometric Priors for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31952)
*Junge Zhang,Jie Zhu,Yanqi Jia,Xiangyu Xu,Zixun Huang,Wenbin He,Xin Li,Jiachen Li,Liu Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLG-Drive 冻结 VLM 与几何基础模型，仅训练一个几何锚定的逐点融合接口和指令条件轨迹规划器，在 NavHard/NavTest 与 HUGSIM 闭环驾驶中优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶规划既需要视觉语言模型的场景语义，也需要几何基础模型恢复的空间结构，而微调任一模型代价高且易破坏原能力，因此需要一种低成本的语义-几何融合方案。

Method: 保持 VLM 与几何基础模型全部冻结；在相机网格每个单元上，通过 geometry-anchored residual 将成对的 VLM 特征与几何特征融合为单一联合 token；命令条件化的规划器读取这些 token 并由轨迹标签监督预测自车轨迹，整体仅训练融合接口与规划器。

Result: 扰动初始状态下 NavHard 达 31.78 EPDMS（对比方法最高），NavTest 仅用相机达 88.27 PDMS；逐点融合在两个基准上均优于独立 VLM 流与几何流；在 420 个 HUGSIM 场景闭环驾驶中优于所有对比规划器。

Conclusion: 基于冻结视觉语言与几何基础模型融合特征的轻量规划器，是替代对任一模型做规划适配的有效方案。

Abstract: A driving planner needs both scene semantics, which vision-language models (VLMs) provide, and spatial structure, which geometric foundation models recover from visual history. We present VLG-Drive, which keeps both models frozen and trains only a fusion interface and a planner from trajectory labels. At each camera-grid cell, the interface fuses the paired VLM and geometry features into one joint token through a geometry-anchored residual, and a command-conditioned planner reads these tokens to predict the ego trajectory. VLG-Drive achieves 31.78 EPDMS on NavHard under perturbed initial states, the highest among the compared methods, and 88.27 PDMS on NavTest from cameras alone. Pointwise fusion outperforms independent VLM and geometry streams on both benchmarks. In closed-loop driving on 420 HUGSIM scenarios, VLG-Drive outperforms all compared planners. These results indicate that a lightweight planner trained on fused features of frozen vision-language and geometric foundation models is an effective alternative to adapting either model for planning.

</details>


### [66] [TACTUS: Unifying Surgical Action Understanding in Time and Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31956)
*Lurui Wang,Hao Ding,Chenhao Yu,Chenyan Jing,Jiru Xu,Ruoqi Zuo,Liwei Mei,Chang Liu,Lalithkumar Seenivasan,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TACTUS: a surgical dataset/benchmark jointly annotating tool & action identity, nested action/contact windows, landmark trajectories, and tissue-region tracks, built via a human+point-tracking+model-prompt pipeline; TactusNet reference network benchmarks semantic/temporal/spatial heads.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术基准多以任务特定目标定义监督，未完整刻画动作语义、器械运动与组织交互之间的关系，缺少对完整手术动作的时空统一标注。

Method: 提出统一标注框架（工具/动作身份、嵌套动作与交互时间窗、器械关键点轨迹、组织区域跟踪）；两级流水线结合人工标注、点跟踪与模型生成空间提示，重建141个视频共22,995片段（15,668 Gold + 7,327 Silver）；提出TactusNet，以共享视频特征多任务预测语义、时间与空间目标。

Result: 工具-动作准确率95.8%（逐头微调编码器）；时间召回46.6%@IoU 0.7，接触Dice仅0.351；加入Silver后默认TactusNet配对准确率93.8%→95.5%。

Conclusion: 为器械-组织交互的学习与评估提供统一基础，但时间定位与接触分割仍显著不足，空间交互建模是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Advancing surgical automation requires a comprehensive spatio-temporal understanding of how instruments act on tissue throughout an action. Existing surgical benchmarks, however, largely define supervision through task-specific targets, leaving the relationships between action semantics, instrument motion, and tissue interaction incompletely specified. To address this limitation, we introduce TACTUS (Tool, Action, Contact and Trajectory Understanding in Surgery), a new dataset and benchmark that establishes a unified annotation framework for complete surgical actions. The framework jointly specifies tool and action identities, nested action and interaction windows, instrument-specific landmark trajectories, and tissue-region tracks. To construct these annotations at scale, we develop a two-tier pipeline combining human annotation, point tracking, and model-generated spatial prompts. Using this pipeline, we re-annotate 141 videos from five public sources, producing 22,995 action clips spanning 2.9 million frames. The dataset comprises 15,668 Gold clips with human spatial prompts followed by review and re-checking, and 7,327 Silver clips combining human semantic and temporal annotations with model-generated spatial prompts. We further introduce TactusNet, the companion reference network, to benchmark semantic, temporal, and spatial prediction from shared video features. Across TactusNet and 13 published baselines, tool-action accuracy reaches 95.8% with per-head encoder fine-tuning, while the best temporal recall and contact Dice reach 46.6% at temporal IoU 0.7 and 0.351, respectively. Moreover, adding Silver improves the default TactusNet's pair accuracy from 93.8% to 95.5%. Together, these contributions establish a foundation for learning and evaluating the instrument-tissue interactions underlying future surgical automation.

</details>


### [67] [SPAR: Self-Referenced World-Model Rewards for Physical Alignment in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31958)
*Junyi Zhang,Qichao Wang,Wenzhe Qian,Yuanchuan Wang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPAR框架用潜世界模型的预测误差，并以"重复自身帧构成的静态片段"作为内容匹配参照，构造自参照物理对齐奖励，配合时空奖励精炼（空间聚焦局部预测偏差、时间平滑误差尖峰并引入特征变化一致性），在推理时引导视频生成更符合物理规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成视觉质量提升，但动力学物理合理性仍不足。用潜世界模型的预测/观测特征一致性作为物理评估信号存在混淆：高一致性可能只反映场景内容易预测，而非动力学更合理。需构造内容匹配的参照基线来解耦该偏差。

Method: 自参照物理对齐奖励（SPAR）：对每个视频计算世界模型预测误差，并与"由该视频自身帧重复拼接成的静态片段"的预测误差作对比，得到内容匹配的相对误差作为奖励。进一步提出统一的时空奖励精炼：空间精炼强化局部预测偏差；时间精炼抑制短暂误差峰，并用预测/观测特征变化量的一致性补充预测误差。整体用于推理时对齐（inference-time guidance）。

Result: 在多个基准上验证，SPAR提升文生视频与图生视频续写两类任务的物理合理性，使生成动力学更贴近真实世界事件的物理规律。摘要中未给出具体量化指标。

Conclusion: 推理时物理对齐的关键在于参照系设计：以内容自参照消除"可预测性"混淆，并针对物理交互的时空稀疏分布进行奖励精炼，可在不重训生成模型的情况下改善物理合理性，为世界模型作为评价/奖励信号提供更可靠的用法。

Abstract: Generating videos with physically plausible dynamics remains challenging despite advances in visual quality. Latent world models offer predictive signals for evaluating physical plausibility, but higher agreement between predicted and observed features may reflect easier-to-predict scene content rather than more plausible dynamics. We propose \textbf{S}elf-Referenced \textbf{P}hysical \textbf{A}lignment \textbf{R}ewards (\textbf{SPAR}), a framework that uses world-model predictions for inference-time physical alignment. SPAR compares each video's prediction error with that of static clips constructed by repeating its own frames, providing a content-matched reference for evaluating generated dynamics. To account for the uneven spatial and temporal distribution of physical interactions, we introduce a unified \textbf{spatiotemporal reward refinement} strategy. \textbf{Spatial refinement} emphasizes localized prediction discrepancies, while \textbf{temporal refinement} moderates brief error peaks and supplements prediction errors with agreement between predicted and observed feature changes. Evaluations across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that SPAR improves the physical plausibility of both \textbf{text-to-video generation} and \textbf{image-to-video continuation}, bringing generated dynamics into closer alignment with the physical regularities governing real-world events. Additional results and resources are available at this https URL

</details>


### [68] [Visual Thinking: Training-Free Active Perception for Counting and Localizing Hard-to-Find Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31973)
*Emil Kielar,Pratik Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Visual Thinking：免训练框架，将VLM与SAM 3通过迭代式主动感知闭环结合，VLM生成短名词短语提示，控制器按嵌入相似度/熵优先度/新颖性排序，SAM 3据此检测并跨轮关联置信度，同时用高置信检测作正例视觉提示并引入图像分块，显著提升小/遮挡目标的分割计数与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型（如SAM系列）依赖文本提示可精确分割，但对小目标、遮挡目标或原始文本描述不充分的目标召回差；VLM虽能理解图像上下文并提出更丰富描述，却缺乏精确定位与分割能力。作者希望融合二者优势。

Method: 免训练迭代主动感知闭环：1) VLM基于原图、候选裁剪与历史提出短名词短语；2) 控制器以原提示与候选短语的嵌入相似度、基于熵的验证优先度、提示新颖性三指标排序；3) SAM 3用短语检测；4) 跨轮检测关联，以SAM 3得分的加权均值更新目标置信度（更窄提示权重大）；5) 高置信检测作为正视觉示例回灌给SAM 3；6) 图像分块以回收杂乱场景中的小目标。

Result: 在CARPK、FSCD-147、PixMo-Count、OmniCount(Fruits)上评测计数与定位：相比SAM 3，FSC-147计数MAE降低约18、CARPK约8；FSCD-147与CARPK的box AP提升超过12。固定分块或不分块时性能与Claude Opus 5.5（extra-high reasoning effort）相当，单张桌面GPU约5.91秒/图，而Claude API约46.53秒/图。

Conclusion: 免训练的VLM+SAM 3主动感知闭环在计数与定位上稳健优于SAM 3，并以远低延迟达到与强VLM推理相当的效果；迭代提示精化、置信度加权与视觉正例回灌是有效机制，但实验限于计数/定位基准，未涉及机器人真实部署。

Abstract: Vision foundation models can precisely segment objects based solely on a text prompt. However, they struggle to recover objects that are small, occluded or whose appearance is poorly captured by the original textual description. Vision language models can examine the image and its context, they can propose richer descriptions of the objects to be detected. But these models struggle with precise segmentation and localization. We introduce Visual Thinking, a training-free framework that combines a vision-language model with Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) through an iterative active perception loop. At each iteration, the VLM proposes short noun phrases using the image, candidate crops and history from past iterations. A controller ranks these phrases using embedding similarity between the original prompt and the proposal, an entropy-based verification priority and the novelty of the prompt. Next, SAM 3 uses prompts to detect objects in the image. We associate detections across iterations and update each object's confidence score using a weighted mean of scores obtained from SAM 3 with a larger weight for detections using narrower prompts. We supply confident detections as positive visual exemplars for SAM 3, and incorporate image tiling to recover small objects in cluttered images. We evaluate counting and localization on CARPK, FSCD-147, PixMo-Count, and OmniCount (Fruits). Compared with SAM 3, Visual Thinking reduces the counting mean absolute error (MAE) by about 18 on FSC-147 and about 8 on CARPK. It improves box average precision (AP) by more than 12 on FSCD-147 and CARPK. Visual Thinking, with fixed or no tiling, performs on par with Claude Opus 5.5 at extra-high reasoning effort, while running on single desktop GPU in about 5.91 seconds per image, compared to about 46.53 seconds per image for Claude through its API.

</details>
