This work moves computational microscopy beyond standalone models and static scripts towards self-evolving platforms that accumulate human scientific expertise

</details>


### [136] [Longitudinal Characterization of Personalized Hydration Dynamics Using a Facial-Video Digital Hydration Biomarker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32326)
*Janindu Athukorala,D.M.J. Prathapa,Thanveer Ahamed,Yudara Kularathne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 一项小样本纵向研究用面部视频生物标志物跟踪水合状态变化：5名成年人完成等量2.0 L饮水日（250段视频，每30分钟一次）与500 mL电解质挑战（25段视频），发现晨起首测常为当日最低，起始分<50的摄入后平均升高+11.3分（显著），个体在≥50分时间占比从16%到100%差异巨大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水合状态通常按单时间点评估，但体内水分持续变化；面部视频水合生物标志物虽能捕捉状态转换，其纵向行为与个体差异尚不清楚，且需判断是否需要个性化校准。

Method: 5名成人、饮水日与电解质日采用相同2.0 L饮水计划，12小时内每30分钟采集面部视频（共250段）；另在独立早晨进行500 mL电解质挑战（基线+4次随访，共25段）。分析包括轨迹、饮后变化、变异性、≥50分时间占比，以及起始分数与饮料类型的探索性效应，并用时间打乱置换检验做对照。

Result: 10天中7天首测为当日最低；50次摄入的饮后平均变化+4.0分（优于打乱时间对照，p<0.001）；起始<50的21次摄入后无一人下降，平均+11.3分（置换期望+5.7，p<0.001）；起始≥50者无超随机上升。500 mL电解质后5人2小时平均变化均为正，30–90分钟峰值升幅7.2–22.6分；相同饮水计划下≥50分时间占比16%–100%；电解质日较饮水日晨起基线平均增幅+7.7分，但日期顺序固定。

Conclusion: 面部视频水合评分呈现与饮水相关的纵向变化及显著个体差异；这些探索性结果支持在更大规模对照研究中进一步验证个体校准与水合方案。

Abstract: Background. Hydration is often assessed at a single time point despite continuous changes in body water. A facial-video biomarker has tracked hydration-state transitions, but its longitudinal behavior is unclear. Objective. To assess within-person hydration score changes, individual differences in fluid response, and implications for personal calibration. Methods. Five adults completed water and electrolyte days with an identical 2.0 L fluid schedule, using facial videos every 30 minutes over 12 hours (250 videos). On a separate morning, each completed a 500 mL electrolyte challenge with baseline and four follow-up videos over 2 hours (25 videos). Analyses assessed trajectories, post-drink changes, variability, time at or above score 50, and exploratory effects of starting score and beverage day. Results. The first scan was the day's lowest on 7 of 10 days. Across 50 intakes, the mean post-drink change was +4.0 points, exceeding a shuffled-time comparison (p less than 0.001). For 21 intakes starting below 50, no subsequent score fell; the mean rise was +11.3 points versus +5.7 expected under permutation (p less than 0.001). Intakes starting at 50 or above showed no rise beyond chance. After 500 mL electrolytes, mean change over 2 hours was positive for all five participants, with peak increases of 7.2 to 22.6 points at 30 to 90 minutes. Despite identical fluid schedules, time at or above 50 ranged from 16% to 100%. The electrolyte day showed a greater mean increase from morning baseline than the water day (+7.7 points), although day order was fixed. Conclusion. Facial-video hydration scores showed longitudinal changes associated with drinking and marked individual differences. These exploratory findings support further research on personal calibration and hydration protocols in larger controlled studies.

</details>


### [137] [FlowVecFont: Scalable Vector Font Generation via Latent Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32327)
*Takumu Fujioka,Gouhei Tanaka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowVecFont：用缩放简单模型实现纯矢量字体生成，基于Transformer VAE + latent flow matching，仅用矢量轮廓训练，无需外部预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矢量字体生成依赖专用生成/细化机制、位图表示或外部预训练模型，导致系统复杂度高、额外计算与实现成本，并引入外部依赖。作者希望验证：简单模型规模化即可实现高质量纯矢量生成。

Method: Transformer-based VAE将字形序列编码为定长连续隐tokens，在潜空间上做标准条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）生成矢量字形；支持类别标签或参考字形条件，后者可泛化到未见字符与新字体。训练仅使用矢量轮廓数据。另发布TorchFont矢量字体机器学习库。

Result: 在Google Fonts采集的500万字形（含非拉丁字符）上，各条件策略下生成质量随模型规模提升；拉丁字形对比中，800M参数模型优于DeepVecFont-v2与DesigNet，并接近27B参数预训练模型VecGlypher的质量。

Conclusion: 验证了纯矢量、无外部预训练的简单模型可通过规模化达到高质量字体生成，且效率优于大预训练模型；同时提供TorchFont工具库。属于图形学字体生成工作，与具身智能/3D核心方向关联度低。

Abstract: Fonts are natively represented as vector outlines. Many existing studies on vector font generation have relied on specialized generation or refinement mechanisms, bitmap representations, or externally pretrained models to address the challenges of learning vector representations. These approaches often increase system complexity, incur additional computational and implementation costs, and introduce dependencies on external resources. We show that scaling a simple model enables high-quality pure vector font generation without external pretraining. We propose FlowVecFont, a scalable model for vector font generation based on latent flow matching and trained solely on vector outlines. A Transformer-based variational autoencoder maps glyph sequences into fixed-length continuous latent tokens, enabling standard conditional flow matching for vector glyph generation. The model supports conditioning on class labels or reference glyphs, with the latter enabling generalization to unseen characters and fonts. Experiments on 5M glyphs collected across Google Fonts, including non-Latin characters, show that generation quality improves with model scale across conditioning strategies. In a comparison on Latin glyphs, the 800M-parameter FlowVecFont outperforms DeepVecFont-v2 and DesigNet and achieves quality competitive with the pretrained 27B-parameter VecGlypher. We also provide TorchFont, a machine learning library for vector fonts.

</details>


### [138] [Cross-Version Compatibility of TESSERA Embeddings Through Lightweight Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32329)
*Yutong Yang,Zhengpeng Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 测试轻量转换器能否让基于某一 Tessera 嵌入版本训练的分类器跨版本复用：v1.1→v2 转换后精度从 0.061 恢复到 0.917，但跨站点泛化不佳，嵌入相似度高不等于下游可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入模型会持续迭代发布，而下游分类器往往长期服役于旧版本，重训成本高。作者希望验证轻量版本转换器能否解耦模型版本与下游模型，并检验嵌入相似度能否代表下游性能。

Method: 在地理上共位的 6,280 组三元组样本（25 个站点，2024）上拟合 v1、v1.1、v2 之间的转换器，训练/验证/测试站点完全不相交。以四类土地覆盖任务评测：v2 上训练的分类器直接用于原始 v1.1、转换后 v1.1、以及 v1→v2 直接转换与经 v1.1 复合路径的比较。另在 20 个新站点队列上对比"原站点训练转换器"与"新队列内训练转换器"，并补充 2021 WorldCover 标签与 24 个缺乏原生 v2 值站点的评估。

Result: v2 训练分类器在真 v2 上达到 0.937 精度 / 0.844 macro-F1；原始 v1.1 降至 0.061 / 0.050，转换后恢复至 0.917 / 0.795。直接 v1→v2 转换误差低于经 v1.1 复合。跨站迁移较弱：原站点转换器在新队列仅 0.597 精度，尽管余弦相似度达 0.902；新队列内训练可达 0.784。

Conclusion: 轻量转换器能在测试站点上大幅恢复冻结分类器性能，但跨地理/新站点迁移不可靠；嵌入余弦相似度不能替代下游任务评测，版本兼容性判断应以下游指标为准。

Abstract: Downstream models often outlive the embedding release on which they were trained. We test whether lightweight converters let a classifier trained on one Tessera release use embeddings from another. We fit converters between v1, v1.1, and v2 on 6,280 co-located triplets from 25 sites in 2024, with disjoint training, validation, and test sites. On a four-class land-cover test, a classifier trained on true v2 reaches 0.937 accuracy and 0.844 macro-F1. The same classifier scores 0.061 and 0.050 on raw v1.1, but 0.917 and 0.795 on converted v1.1. Direct v1-to-v2 translation has lower error than composition through v1.1. Geographic transfer is less reliable. On a separate cohort of 20 sites, a converter trained on the original sites reaches 0.597 accuracy despite 0.902 cosine similarity to v2, whereas a converter trained within the new cohort reaches 0.784. Tests with 2021 WorldCover labels and at 24 sampled sites without valid native v2 values extend the evaluation. Conversion recovers much of the frozen classifier's performance on the tested sites, but the new-site result shows that embedding similarity cannot stand in for downstream evaluation.

</details>


### [139] [GUI-WEdit and GUI-SkillEvo: Gradient-Free Adaptation of Small GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32332)
*Haixia Bi,Yingtian Chen,Sihan Yang,Zuzheng Kuang,Yuzhen Tong,Kai Fu,Zhengyong Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GUI-WEdit-4B 通过对晚期 MLP 施加受保护的闭式梯度更新（仅改动 0.535% 参数），GUI-SkillEvo-2B 则冻结权重、由助手迭代生成与精炼程序化技能，二者在 OSWorld 361 任务子集上分别取得 48.02% 和 45.47% 的最终聚合分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型 GUI 智能体虽能执行桌面工作流，但重复的局部错误和脆弱的过程性决策严重限制其可靠性，需要在不进行大规模重新训练的前提下提升其稳定性。

Method: 基于 GUI-Owl-1.5 骨干做两种无梯度适配：4B 模型用轨迹反馈构造对单个晚期 MLP 权重张量的保护性闭式更新（保留成功状态作为约束，共改动 23,739,095 个参数值，占 44.4 亿参数的 0.535%）；2B 模型保持权重冻结，由助手迭代提出并精炼程序化技能。

Result: 在排除 Google Drive 任务的 361 任务 OSWorld 子集上，两个系统最终聚合得分分别为 48.02% 与 45.47%。

Conclusion: 以极小参数改动或纯技能演化方式即可对小型 GUI 智能体做低成本适配并提升可靠性，为冻结骨干上的轻量代理优化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Small graphical user interface (GUI) agents can execute desktop workflows, but repeated local errors and fragile procedural decisions limit their reliability. We present GUI-WEdit-4B and GUI-SkillEvo-2B, two gradient-free adaptations of GUI-Owl-1.5 backbones. For the 4B model, we use trajectory feedback to construct guarded, closed-form updates to a late multilayer perceptron (MLP), retaining successful states as protection constraints. The final checkpoint changes 23,739,095 parameter values, or 0.535\% of the 4,437,815,808-parameter backbone, within one weight tensor. For the 2B model, an assistant iteratively proposes and refines procedural skills while the model weights remain fixed. On the 361-task OSWorld subset excluding Google Drive tasks, the frozen systems obtain final aggregate scores of 48.02\% and 45.47\%, respectively.

</details>


### [140] [GOLD-SLAM: Gaussian-splatting Omnidirectional LiDAR-Depth SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32335)
*Weicong Zhang,Yuan Huang,Yu Zheng,Lin Chenxiao,Yingying Li,Lilong Fan,Liangdong Zhuang,Yufan Chen,Ruiping Liu,Junwei Zheng,Jiaming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GOLD-SLAM：面向全景高斯泼溅SLAM的全向LiDAR深度先验系统，在球面域完成跟踪与建图并引入深度感知地图扩展与剪枝（DAEP），配合新采集的WHOLE UAV数据集；相比全景基线PSNR提升5.56–9.10 dB、Depth-L1降低71.9–83.9%，并在Jetson Orin NX上验证在线运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景相机单帧覆盖近全视场，但全景覆盖范围与LiDAR视场不匹配，导致依赖LiDAR深度先验的全景高斯泼溅SLAM在无LiDAR覆盖区域位姿估计不可靠；同时增量建图中新观测表面覆盖不足、易产生漂浮伪影。

Method: 在球面域进行跟踪与建图；利用LiDAR未覆盖区域的跟踪视觉特征约束位姿，弥补先验缺失；提出Depth-Aware Map Extension and Pruning（DAEP）处理新观测表面覆盖不足与漂浮伪影；构建UAV平台采集的WHOLE数据集（弱几何、高动态、全向、低光照、简单场景）；并在Jetson Orin NX（16GB）上评估嵌入式在线配置。

Result: 在公开基准与WHOLE上渲染质量具竞争力、几何精度优于透视式系统；相对现有全景基线平均PSNR提升5.56–9.10 dB，平均Depth-L1降低71.9–83.9%；嵌入式配置可实现实时在线运行，但重建质量有所下降。

Conclusion: 融合全向视觉与LiDAR深度先验的球面域高斯泼溅SLAM可兼顾渲染质量与几何精度，并在资源受限平台上具备部署可行性，但需在质量与实时性间权衡。

Abstract: Panoramic cameras capture nearly the entire spherical field of view in a single frame. However, the mismatch between this panoramic coverage and the field of view of LiDAR poses a substantial challenge for panoramic Gaussian splatting SLAM that relies on LiDAR depth priors. We introduce GOLD-SLAM, a Gaussian-splatting Omnidirectional LiDAR-Depth SLAM system that combines high-quality rendering with accurate geometric reconstruction. It performs tracking and mapping in the spherical domain and uses tracked visual features in regions without LiDAR coverage to improve pose estimation. To address insufficient coverage of newly observed surfaces and floating artifacts during incremental mapping, we introduce Depth-Aware Map Extension and Pruning (DAEP). To enable broader evaluation, we collect a new dataset, WHOLE, using a UAV platform across Weak-geometry, High-dynamic, Omnidirectional, Low-light, and Easy scenarios. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks and WHOLE demonstrate competitive rendering quality and improved geometric accuracy relative to perspective systems. Compared with existing panoramic baselines, GOLD-SLAM improves mean PSNR by 5.56-9.10 dB and reduces mean Depth-L1 by 71.9-83.9%. To assess deployment feasibility on resource-constrained platforms such as UAVs, we evaluate an embedded configuration of GOLD-SLAM on a Jetson Orin NX (16 GB), demonstrating online operation at the cost of reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [141] [Get the Next Moment Right: Short-Term Event Planning for Physically Plausible Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32337)
*Hao Liu,Xiang Tang,Ruotong Li,Xiaopeng Fan,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练闭环框架STEP，以短时事件规划逐段引导视频生成：每轮进行场景状态测量、下一物理事件预测与控制片段渲染、潜空间引导生成、事件验证与重采样，缓解全片段一次性轨迹预测的误差累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成方法预先为整段视频预测运动轨迹作为引导，预测误差会累积并导致后续帧物理不合理。作者希望只用可靠的可短程预测来控制生成。

Method: STEP（Short-Term Event Planning）为无需训练的闭环框架，逐短片段生成。每轮四步：(1) grounding，从最新生成帧测量场景状态；(2) event planning，预测下一物理事件并渲染为控制片段；(3) latent-guided generation，在该控制下生成下一短片段；(4) verification，检查事件是否发生，未发生则重采样。控制仅依赖短程预测。

Result: 在三个冻结的生成器上，于 Physics-IQ Verified 与 PhyGenBench 两个基准提升物理合理性；可无需训练迁移至新生成器。

Conclusion: 闭环、短程事件规划式引导是提升视频生成物理合理性的有效且通用途径，且具备免训练、可插拔的通用性。

Abstract: Video generation models synthesize realistic imagery yet still struggle to produce physically plausible evolution. Existing methods guide generation with a motion trajectory predicted for the entire clip in advance, so prediction errors accumulate and degrade later frames. We propose STEP(Short-Term Event Planning), a training-free closed-loop framework that guides generation one short clip at a time. Each round consists of four steps: grounding measures the scene state from the latest frames, event planning predicts the next physical event and renders it into a control clip, latent-guided generation produces the next short clip under this control, and verification checks whether the event occurs and resamples the clip if it does not. Because each round is grounded in the frames actually generated, control relies only on reliable short-range predictions. STEP improves the physical plausibility of three frozen generators on Physics-IQ Verified and PhyGenBench, and can be applied to new generators without training.

</details>


### [142] [Part-Set Prototypes for Explainable Mountain Gazelles Sex Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32343)
*Tali Boneh Shitrit,Amir Kedem,Ilan Shimshoni,Anna Zamansky*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"集合原型"(set prototypes)自解释细粒度识别架构，从数据中直接挖掘部件数量与无约束空间布局，用截断Sinkhorn竞争在冻结DINOv3 token网格上联合匹配，允许不支持的部件弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自解释视觉模型的刚性几何约束无法刻画野外图像中多样的语义形状；相机陷阱数据存在姿态不优、光照变化、遮挡、伪装和环境捷径等问题。

Method: 集合原型架构：部件基数与无约束布局均由数据学习；联合匹配公式将部件同时置于冻结DINOv3 token网格上，通过截断Sinkhorn竞争实现，支持部件弃权以避免空间坍缩。

Result: 在山羊性别分类相机陷阱基准上达到0.878 macro-F1，优于先前架构并与无约束线性探针相当；最强原型定位于角、颈、身体等生物特征区域。

Conclusion: 该表述可推广至单物种之外的多种生态监测任务，且提供可供生态学家视觉核验的可解释证据。

Abstract: Self-explainable visual models for fine-grained recognition are often hindered by rigid geometric constraints that cannot capture diverse semantic shapes in unconstrained "in-the-wild" imagery. Real-world camera-trap data introduces challenges, including non-optimal poses, lighting variations, occlusions, animal camouflage, and environmental shortcuts. To address this, we introduce set prototypes, a general architecture where both part cardinality and unconstrained spatial layouts are mined directly from data. Our joint-matching formulation places parts simultaneously on a frozen DINOv3 token grid via truncated Sinkhorn competition, allowing unsupported parts to abstain without spatial collapse. We evaluate our approach on Mountain Gazelle sex classification, a camera-trap benchmark, where our model achieves 0.878 macro-F1--outperforming prior architectures and at par with an unconstrained linear probe. The strongest prototypes localize to distinctive biological areas such as horns, neck and body, allowing ecologists to visually verify decisions before trusting the metric. This formulation generalizes naturally to diverse ecological monitoring challenges beyond a single species.

</details>


### [143] [DiCT: Teaching X-ray Vision-Language Models to Reason via Imagining CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32344)
*Zhenghao Lu,Xiaotian Zhang,Songtao Jiang,Yiqun Lin,Siming Fu,Hualiang Wang,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiCT：三阶段渐进式 CT→X 射线推理迁移框架，用 CT 视觉语言模型指导 X 射线 VLM，使其在推理时仅凭 2D X 光即可具备 CT 先验的 3D 空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X 光诊断需推理病灶的 3D 位置及其与解剖结构的空间关系，但 2D 投影丢失深度信息；推理阶段又难以获得 CT，因此需把 CT 的 3D 推理能力蒸馏进 2D X 光模型。

Method: 三阶段：① instruct the dreamer——先给 X 射线学生模型建立初始的 CT 导向推理能力与区分"X 光观测/CT 想象"的结构化接口；② visualize the dream——CT 教师通过 on-policy distillation（OPD）选择性引导学生的推理轨迹，迁移 3D 解剖知识；③ manifest the dream——把从数字重建影像（DRR）学到的能力适配到真实临床 X 光。推理时无需显式 CT 重建。

Result: 在 3D-RAD 与 ReXVQA 两个数据集、两种 VLM 骨干上验证有效；3D-RAD 异常识别任务上较基于 CT 重建的基线提升 1.66 个百分点，推理计算量降低 12.77×。

Conclusion: 通过"想象 CT + 策略蒸馏"的渐进迁移，可将 3D 解剖先验注入纯 2D X 光 VLM，以很小代价获得接近甚至优于显式 CT 重建的推理性能，为 2.5D/3D 感知的跨模态蒸馏提供了可复用范式。

Abstract: Diagnosing with X-rays often requires reasoning about the 3D locations of abnormalities and their spatial relationships to surrounding anatomical structures, yet such reasoning remains challenging due to the loss of spatial information in 2D projections. To address this challenge, we propose Dreaming in CT (DiCT), a three-stage progressive CT-to-X-ray reasoning transfer framework that leverages a CT vision-language model (VLM) to guide an X-ray VLM. Through this CT-guided transfer, the student acquires CT-informed 3D reasoning capabilities, enabling it to reason directly from 2D X-rays without explicit CT reconstruction at inference time. The framework begins with instruct the dreamer, which equips the X-ray student with initial CT-oriented reasoning capabilities and establishes a structured interface that distinguishes X-ray observations from CT imagination. Building on this structure, visualize the dream introduces CT-guided on-policy distillation (OPD), where the CT teacher selectively guides the X-ray student's reasoning trajectories to transfer 3D anatomical knowledge. Finally, manifest the dream adapts the acquired reasoning capabilities from digitally reconstructed radiographs (DRRs) to real clinical X-rays. Experiments across two VLM backbones on 3D-RAD and ReXVQA demonstrate DiCT's effectiveness across radiological reasoning tasks, achieving a 1.66 percentage point improvement over a CT reconstruction-based baseline on 3D-RAD Abnormality Identification with 12.77$\times$ lower inference computation. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [144] [LRQ-Cache: Compressing Feature Caches in Video Diffusion via Low-Rank Approximation and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32345)
*Ruizhe Yao,Zhen Huang,Songpan Gao,Danyi Liu,Zijian Cao,Weijie Zheng,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LRQ-Cache：免训练的特征缓存压缩方法，用低秩分解+量化将视频 DiT 时序缓存（TeaCache 等）的持久状态压缩 42–71 倍（109–346 GiB → 2.3–4.9 GiB），使原本需多卡或 CPU offload 的预测式缓存可放入单张 80 GB H100，生成速度提升 2.7–5.9 倍，VBench 质量基本持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散 Transformer 推理昂贵，时序特征缓存虽能跨去噪步复用中间特征，但需要长期保存稠密特征张量，其体积随视频长度、分辨率与缓存覆盖率增长（预测式缓存达 109–346 GiB/视频），超出单卡显存，只能 CPU offload（变慢）或多卡序列并行分片（显存需求主导 GPU 数量而非计算）。作者希望直接压缩缓存状态本身。

Method: 观察到每个缓存特征的能量高度集中于少量跨 token 共享的通道模式，据此提出免训练的 LRQ-Cache：对每个缓存特征做低秩近似（保存少量因子）+量化后存储，复用时在线重构，不改变现有缓存机制（TeaCache 等可即插即用）。

Result: 在 HunyuanVideo 与 Wan2.1 上的三种缓存机制中，预测式缓存的持久状态降低 42–71 倍（至 2.3–4.9 GiB/视频）；TeaCache 的 VBench 指标与原始缓存相差 ≤0.25 pp，Wan2.1 预测式缓存较大降幅（最高 3.5 pp）在更高 rank 下收窄；原本超单卡显存的缓存现可放入单张 80 GB H100，速度比 CPU offload 原生缓存快 2.7–5.9 倍。

Conclusion: 缓存冗余可被有效压缩，使时序缓存从「显存瓶颈」变为「计算友好」的加速手段，且无需训练、与缓存策略解耦；是高价值推理系统级工具，但与具身智能/物理 AI 核心方法关联较弱。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers (DiTs) achieve high-fidelity generation, but inference is expensive because every denoising step evaluates a large transformer over all spatiotemporal tokens of the video. Temporal caching accelerates them by reusing intermediate features across denoising steps. However, retaining dense feature tensors introduces a persistent storage cost that grows with video length, resolution, and cache coverage, reaching 109-346 GiB per video for the predictive caches we evaluate. Such states exceed the memory of a single GPU, so native caches must either be offloaded to the CPU, which slows generation, or be sharded across several GPUs, e.g., with sequence parallelism, so that the cache rather than the compute sets the number of GPUs. We instead shrink the cached state itself, exploiting its redundancy: a few channel patterns shared across tokens capture much of the energy of each cached feature. Based on this observation, we propose LRQ-Cache, a training-free method that combines low-rank approximation and quantization for feature-cache compression. LRQ-Cache represents each cached feature with a small set of factors, quantizes them before storage, and reconstructs the feature at reuse time, leaving cache mechanisms unchanged. Across three caching mechanisms on HunyuanVideo and Wan2.1, LRQ-Cache reduces the persistent state of predictive caches by 42-71x, to 2.3-4.9 GiB per video. VBench stays within 0.25 pp of the native cache for TeaCache, and the larger drops of the Wan2.1 predictive caches, up to 3.5 pp, shrink at higher ranks. As a result, predictive caches that exceed the memory of a single 80 GB H100 GPU fit on it with LRQ-Cache, making generation 2.7-5.9x faster than with CPU-offloaded native caches.

</details>


### [145] [LookAnything: A Foundation Model for Holistic Gaze Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32351)
*Jiayu Tang,Yuchen Zhou,Hongsheng Wu,Hanjing Ye,Ke Huang,Cheng Wang,Yuqin Dai,Yufei Huang,Yonggen Ling,Chao Gou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LookAnything：统一注视中心任务的 gaze foundation model，用可学习 gaze-prior queries 替代 head crop/depth 等辅助输入，输出点/框/热图/语言，并构建 HoliGaze（311K QA、5 能力维度、9 任务）大语料基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 gaze following 方法依赖高度定制架构与 head crop、深度图等辅助输入，造成级联误差和推理开销；且多在单一域训练、仅做热图输出的孤立注视跟随，缺乏灵活性与跨场景泛化。

Method: 提出 LookAnything 基础模型，大规模多任务、跨域训练实现整体注视跟随；核心是引入可学习 gaze-prior queries 促进注视感知表征学习，去除辅助外部输入；支持点、框、热图、语言多种输出；构建 HoliGaze 语料与基准（311K QA，5 能力维度，9 任务），覆盖定位、描述与推理。

Result: 在整体注视跟随上稳定优于 33 个领先视觉语言模型；在 5 个常规目标检测基准上与专用专家模型仍具强竞争力；未见数据集评估与真实机器人部署验证了域外泛化与灵活适配。

Conclusion: 注视理解可通过统一基础模型与内在 query 先验实现，无需辅助输入即可获得多任务、多输出、跨域泛化的整体注视跟随能力，并为社交信号分析与 HRI 提供可迁移范式。

Abstract: Human gaze provides a rich window into attention and intent, making its understanding essential for applications ranging from social signal analysis to human-robot interaction. Existing methods typically employ highly customized architectures relying on auxiliary inputs like head crops or depth maps, causing cascading errors and inference overhead. Trained on single domains and confined to isolated gaze following with heatmap outputs, they lack flexibility and generalization for diverse interactive scenarios. To bridge this gap, we present LookAnything, a foundation model that unifies diverse gaze-centric tasks for holistic gaze following. Benefiting from large-scale multi-task and cross-domain training, LookAnything flexibly adapts to various settings, generating diverse outputs (points, boxes, heatmaps, and language) and supporting broad gaze-centric localization, description, and reasoning. At its core, we introduce learnable gaze-prior queries to promote gaze-aware representation learning, eliminating the need for auxiliary external inputs. To facilitate this paradigm, we construct HoliGaze, a comprehensive corpus and benchmark comprising 311K question-answer pairs across 5 capability dimensions and 9 distinct tasks. Experiments demonstrate that LookAnything consistently outperforms 33 leading vision-language models on holistic gaze following, while remaining highly competitive with dedicated specialists across 5 conventional target detection benchmarks. Evaluation on unseen datasets and deployment on a real robot further validate its strong out-of-domain generalization and flexible adaptation in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [146] [Sub-Riemannian structure for Oriented Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32361)
*Thomas Besnier,Stefan Sommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出在位置-朝向空间 M=R^3×S^2 上定义 sub-Riemannian 接触度量，用非完整水平分布约束点云邻域，从而避免欧氏局部性把空间贴近但几何不同的表面错误合并；并给出可计算的局部距离近似 score，作为 PointNet++ 聚合的即插即用替换件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D（有向）点云框架在欧氏空间 R^3 中用 kNN 图和半径球定义局部性，会把空间中接近但几何上不同的采样表面（如薄叶片的两面）错误合并，导致特征跨边界泄漏、性能下降。

Method: 将点提升为 (x,n)（n 为表面法向），在位置-朝向空间上构造 sub-Riemannian 接触度量：运动被限制在非完整水平分布中——空间速度须位于垂直于法向的平面内，且改变朝向需付出代价。由此推导出显式的、可局部近似该距离的 score，用于比较有向点。实验中把该 score 作为 PointNet++ 聚合机制的 drop-in 替换，并用于在有向点上模拟热扩散以检验跨表面泄漏。

Result: 在接触/邻近边界区域，分割与对应（correspondence）精度有所提升；用作热扩散模拟时，跨边界的泄漏减少。

Conclusion: 在位置-朝向空间引入非完整接触度量是定义点云局部性的有效替代方案，可缓解薄结构两侧的特征串扰，且能以极小改动嵌入现有架构。

Abstract: Many frameworks dealing with 3D (oriented) point clouds define locality in the ambient Euclidean space: $k$-nearest-neighbor graphs and radius balls in $\mathbb{R}^3$ are critical to define which points are "close to one another" and should exchange information. This can unintentionally merge sampled surfaces that are close in space but geometrically distinct such as the two faces of a thin leaf. Then, features are more prone to leak across boundaries which hinder performance. To address this, we propose to define a sub-Riemannian contact metric on position-orientation space $\mathbb{M}=\mathbb{R}^3\times \mathbb{S}^2$: points are lifted to $(x,n)$ with $n$ a surface normal, and "motion" is constrained to a non-holonomic horizontal distribution in which spatial velocity must stay in the plane orthogonal to the normal which can reorient at a cost. From this, we derive an explicit score, which locally approximate the distance, to compare oriented points for downstream tasks. We report improved segmentation and correspondence accuracy near contact when using the score as a drop-in change inside PointNet++ aggregation mechanism as well as reduced leakage when using it to simulate heat diffusion on oriented points.

</details>


### [147] [Visual-State Graph Memory for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32369)
*Ailar Mahdizadeh,Pritam Sarkar,Xiangteng He,Leonid Sigal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VSG（Visual-State Graph）是一种免训练的长视频流式理解记忆机制：把视频流表示为对象及其关系的动态场景图加事件日志，而非原始帧序列，配合固定输入预算的检索实现回溯推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解中，回答问题所需的证据可能已滑出近期帧窗口；保留全部帧无法随视频长度扩展，丢弃旧帧又削弱对过去的推理能力，需要一种压缩但仍可回溯的长期记忆表示。

Method: 构建免训练的图式记忆：以持久对象节点+带时间戳的状态变化历史表示重复出现的对象，以事件日志记录变化；通过比较新提及对象所参与的关系与已存对象关系做实体关联归并；记忆在视频到达时即增量构建（问题未知），提问时在固定输入预算下联合检索相关对象、事件与近期帧。

Result: 在四个流式视频基准、七个开源视觉语言模型（7B–32B）上评测，匹配输入预算条件下，VSG 提升了对过去事件/对象的推理能力，同时保持实时性理解表现。

Conclusion: 以“流的状态”替代“帧的序列”是可行的长视频记忆范式，免训练、可增量构建、与模型规模无关，为流式视频理解提供可扩展的长期记忆方案。

Abstract: In streaming video understanding, the evidence needed to answer a question may have already left the recent-frame window. Keeping all frames does not scale with video length, while dropping older frames limits the model's ability to reason about the past. We propose Visual-State Graph (VSG), a training-free memory that stores the state of a stream rather than the raw sequence of frames. VSG represents the stream as a graph of objects and their relations, together with a log of events; similar to a dynamically evolving scene graph. An object observed repeatedly is represented by one persistent node, with a timestamped history of how its state changes. To determine whether a newly mentioned object already exists in the graph, we compare the relations it takes part in with those of stored objects. We build this memory as the video arrives, before the questions are known. When a question arrives, we retrieve relevant objects and events alongside recent frames under a fixed input budget. Our evaluation spans four streaming-video benchmarks and seven open-source vision-language models ranging from 7B to 32B parameters. Under matched input budgets, VSG improves reasoning about the past while preserving real-time understanding.

</details>


### [148] [Improving Character Identity Consistency in Diffusion-Based Visual Story Generation through Identity Memory and Automatic Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32383)
*Maria-Emilia Grigore,Stelian Spînu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出模块化流水线（SDXL + LoRA 角色个性化 + ControlNet 姿态控制 + IP-Adapter 身份迁移 + Best-of-K 重排序 + 持久身份记忆），并扩展 StoryDiffusion，以提升多帧视觉故事的跨帧角色身份一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型生成多帧视觉故事时，跨帧角色身份一致性难以保持，现有模型在人脸特征、服装、视觉风格与解剖比例上常出现显著漂移。

Method: 以 Stable Diffusion XL 为基座，组合 LoRA 角色个性化微调、ControlNet 姿态结构控制、IP-Adapter 视觉身份迁移；引入基于 Best-of-K 候选选择的重排序机制与持久身份记忆来自动挑选最一致的生成帧；并将 StoryDiffusion 集成扩展为可复现的本地工作流。评测使用 CLIP 图像相似度、DINO 相似度与 CLIP 图文对齐指标，在自建多角色、分难度（易/中/难）基准上进行，对比 SDXL 基线、Best-of-K、身份记忆与完整流水线等渐进变体。

Result: 重排序、身份记忆与视觉条件控制能降低跨帧身份漂移，提升生成视觉故事的整体连贯性与稳定性；作者以消融式渐进变体形式报告结果，未给出如具体数值的量化增益。

Conclusion: 模块化组合与候选重排序/身份记忆是缓解多帧角色身份漂移的有效工程手段，但方法依赖已有组件（SDXL、LoRA、ControlNet、IP-Adapter、StoryDiffusion）的集成，属应用层改进，缺少新方法层面的贡献与真实数据上的定量证据。

Abstract: Automatic visual story generation using diffusion-based models has become an increasingly important research direction in generative artificial intelligence. However, maintaining character identity consistency across consecutive frames remains an open challenge, as existing models often produce significant variations in facial traits, clothing, visual style, or anatomical proportions. This paper proposes a modular pipeline to improve character identity consistency in multi-frame visual story generation using Stable Diffusion XL alongside additional conditioning and automatic selection mechanisms. The proposed system integrates LoRA fine-tuning for character personalization, ControlNet for pose-based structural control, and IP-Adapter for visual identity transfer. Furthermore, a reranking mechanism based on Best-of-K candidate selection and persistent identity memory is introduced to automatically select the most visually consistent generated images. The work also includes integrating and extending the StoryDiffusion framework into a reproducible local workflow, along with improvements for generated frame selection. Performance evaluation uses automatic metrics such as CLIP image similarity, DINO similarity, and CLIP text-image alignment. Experimental evaluation was conducted on a custom benchmark of multiple characters and visual stories organized into difficulty levels (easy, medium, and difficult). Both progressive variants of the proposed pipeline and extended versions of StoryDiffusion were evaluated, including SDXL baseline, Best-of-K, Identity Memory, and the Full Pipeline. Results show that reranking, identity memory, and visual conditioning reduce identity drift across frames and improve the overall coherence and stability of generated visual stories.

</details>


### [149] [Adaptive and Detection-Aware Super-Resolution for Small Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32394)
*Cezica-Elena Luncanu,Stelian Spînu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在SRCNN单图超分（x4）基础上依次引入残差学习、Charbonnier损失与三区域自适应损失（按梯度划分平滑/纹理/边缘区），并用PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS/EdgeMAE评估；同时在COCO验证集上分析超分对YOLOv8检测的影响，发现重建质量提升不一定改善检测，但小目标上检测感知变体+残差缩放可超过双三次基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图超分在监控、遥感、医学影像与目标检测中有应用价值，但重建指标（PSNR/SSIM）与下游任务性能之间的不一致性缺乏系统分析，需要任务感知的评估视角。

Method: 以SRCNN为基线，逐步加入残差学习、Charbonnier损失和基于梯度的三区域（平滑/纹理/边缘）自适应损失；用PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS、EdgeMAE评估重建与结构保真；再将双三次与各SRCNN变体输出送入YOLOv8，在COCO验证集上统计检测性能。

Result: 重建质量提升并不总带来检测性能提升；在小目标场景下，结合残差缩放的检测感知变体相对双三次基线在F1上有改善。

Conclusion: 超分用于视觉流水线时应采用任务感知评估，而非仅依赖重建指标；区域自适应与残差缩放是可用的小目标改进手段。

Abstract: Image super-resolution aims to reconstruct a high-resolution image from a low-resolution input and is relevant for surveillance, satellite imagery, medical imaging, and object detection. This paper investigates SRCNN-based single-image super-resolution for a scale factor of x4, starting from a classical baseline and progressively introducing residual learning, Charbonnier loss, and adaptive loss functions. The proposed approach includes a three-region adaptive loss, where image areas are divided into smooth, texture, and edge regions using gradient information. The models are evaluated using PSNR, SSIM, LPIPS, and EdgeMAE in order to measure both reconstruction quality and structural detail preservation. In addition to reconstruction evaluation, the paper analyzes the impact of super-resolution on object detection. A YOLOv8 detector is applied to images processed through bicubic interpolation and SRCNN-based variants, using images from the COCO validation dataset. The results show that improved reconstruction quality does not always lead to better detection performance. However, for small objects, a detection-aware variant combined with residual scaling improves the F1 score compared to the bicubic baseline. These results highlight the importance of task-aware evaluation when super-resolution is used in computer vision pipelines.

</details>


### [150] [PRC4D: Physical Relation-Constrained Persistent 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32396)
*Junho Kim,Hyunyoung Choi,Jiseok Kim,Seongwon Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRC4D：用物理关系约束（3D 接触+相对运动一致性+序列级验证）识别持久刚体耦合，构建关系图引导可见→遮挡部件的运动传播与位姿精化，实现遮挡下忠实的4D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D重建靠局部空间/运动关联把历史几何搬运到遮挡期，误把短暂接触或共运动当作持久刚体证据，导致错误运动约束与整体重建扭曲。

Method: 构建物理关系约束框架：融合3D接触与相对运动一致性，并做序列级验证以区分持久耦合与瞬时交互；据此生成关系图，用于观测位姿精化及从可见部件向遮挡部件的运动传播，且不修改已重建几何。

Result: 在多样真实多物体序列与受控遮挡仿真上，重建精度提升、相对位姿误差降低，遮挡变化下物体配置更一致。

Conclusion: 以“关系可靠性验证”替代朴素运动外推是提升遮挡期4D一致性的关键；方法为后处理式约束，可叠加于现有重建管线。

Abstract: A faithful 4D reconstruction should capture both the shape and motion of objects, even when they disappear from view. Existing methods preserve previously observed geometry and extend motion through occlusion using local spatial and motion associations. However, treating short-term contact or shared motion as sufficient evidence of persistent rigidity can introduce incorrect motion constraints and distort overall reconstruction. To address this limitation, we present PRC4D, a physical relation-constrained framework that identifies reliable rigid relations and uses them to maintain spatial consistency through occlusion. PRC4D combines 3D contact and relative motion consistency with sequence-level verification to distinguish persistent couplings from transient interactions. The resulting graph guides observed-pose refinement and motion propagation from visible to occluded components without altering reconstructed geometry. Experiments on diverse real-world multi-object sequences and controlled occlusion simulations demonstrate improved reconstruction accuracy, reduced relative pose errors, and more consistent object configurations under changing visibility.

</details>


### [151] [Verify then Steer: Progress Hallucination-Aware Recovery for Continuous Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32400)
*Jiewen Hou,Guanxiang Qi,Meina Kan,Shiguang Shan,Xilin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对VLN智能体在测试时偏离路线后难以恢复的问题，提出"进度幻觉"（progress hallucination）概念，并设计测试时恢复框架PHAR：先用VLM残差流表征+分裂保形校准做跨模态证据失配检测，触发后再用上下文赌博机自适应选择指令进度的回退幅度，在R2R-CE/RxR-CE上提升多种SOTA VLN方法的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法在测试阶段出现路线偏移后难以从早期子指令中恢复；作者将其归因于智能体把偏离路线的观测误判为任务进度，导致内部指令进度超前于真实任务阶段（progress hallucination），从而阻断了回溯式纠错。

Method: 测试时"先验证后转向"（verify-then-steer）框架：Verifier读取VLM残差流表征，检测轨迹上跨模态证据的不一致，并用split conformal calibration控制误报率；触发警报时，Steering模块以contextual bandit自适应选择指令进度的回退范围（rewind extent）进行在线恢复。属无需重训练的test-time干预。

Result: 在R2R-CE与RxR-CE两个连续环境基准上，该框架作为即插模块提升了多种SOTA VLN方法的测试时可靠性（隐含成功率/导航误差改善），但摘要未给出具体数值增益。

Conclusion: 将VLN失败归因于内部指令进度状态失真（而非纯感知误差）是有解释力的分析视角；基于VLM内部表征的失配检测加自适应进度回退，可作为通用测试时纠错插件。局限在于指标提升幅度未量化、验证器依赖白盒残差流访问、评测仅限仿真CE基准，缺乏真实机器人部署验证。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to follow language instructions to reach a target in an unseen 3D environment. However, existing methods still struggle to recover from route deviations at test time. We investigate this limitation through the lens of instruction progress, and trace it to a phenomenon we term progress hallucination: agents misinterpret off-route observations as evidence of task progress, causing their internal instruction progress to advance beyond the actual task stage and hindering recovery based on earlier sub-instructions. To address this failure mode, we propose PHAR (Progress Hallucination-Aware Recovery), a test-time recovery framework following a verify-then-steer paradigm. The verifier examines residual-stream representations of the Vision-Language Model (VLM) to identify mismatch of cross-modal evidence along the trajectories, and uses split conformal calibration to control false alarms. When an alarm is raised, the steering module uses a contextual bandit to adaptively select the rewind extent of instruction progress for online recovery. Through extensive experiments on R2R-CE and RxR-CE, our framework enhances the reliability of multiple state-of-the-art vision-language navigation methods at test time.

</details>


### [152] [BEACON: Learning Observation-Conditioned Starting Points for Guided Conditional Flow Matching in Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32406)
*Anirban Ray,Florian Jug*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEACON提出一种观测条件化的flow matching学习耦合：训练时用轻量条件噪声模型为每个退化输入生成K个候选起点，选flow-matching损失最低者监督两个网络，并以KL项约束其接近高斯先验；推理时仅需单次采样即可复原高质量显微图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有guided conditional flow matching在显微去雾与计算超分辨中，为每个干净目标配对独立标准高斯噪声，未利用退化观测信息，导致起点与目标耦合任意、速度目标相互冲突、条件速度场难以学习。

Method: 学习观测条件化基分布（learned coupling）：轻量条件噪声模型对每个退化输入提议K个起点，以最低流匹配损失候选联合监督噪声模型与速度场，并加KL正则保持提议接近高斯先验；推理时从学习提议单次采样沿速度场传输，无需候选选择或真值。

Result: 在10个数据集（显微去雾与计算超分辨）上对比13类基线，BEACON在PSNR/MicroMS-SSIM与LPIPS/FID的每种组合下均取得最佳中位感知—失真排序。

Conclusion: 通过将flow matching的起始分布改为观测条件化并以最佳候选监督，可有效提升显微图像复原的感知—保真权衡；方法通用、推理简洁，代码与预训练模型将开源。

Abstract: Fluorescence microscopy drives much of modern life science, but accessible imaging setups often yield images degraded by out-of-focus light, limited resolution, and strong signal-dependent noise. Computational restoration can recover high-quality images from such data, enabling cheaper and faster imaging at lower light dose, provided biologically meaningful structures are preserved. Guided conditional flow matching has recently shown strong results for microscopy dehazing and computational super-resolution by learning distributions of plausible restorations. However, it pairs each clean target with an independent standard Gaussian sample, so the starting point of every training path does not leverage the degraded observation. This arbitrary noise--target coupling can produce conflicting velocity targets and makes the conditional velocity field harder to learn. We present BEACON, a $BE$st-of-$K$ $A$daptive learned coupling for guided $CON$ditional flow matching, which learns an observation-conditioned base distribution. During training, a lightweight conditional noise model proposes $K$ starting points for each degraded input, and the candidate with the lowest flow-matching loss supervises both networks, while a KL term keeps the proposal close to the Gaussian prior. At inference, a single draw from the learned proposal is transported along the velocity field, requiring neither candidate selection nor the target. We evaluate BEACON on ten datasets spanning microscopy dehazing and computational super-resolution against 13 baseline families. BEACON achieves the best median perception--distortion rank for every pairing of a fidelity metric (PSNR, MicroMS-SSIM) with a perceptual metric (LPIPS, FID). Code and pretrained models will be released under a permissive license.

</details>


### [153] [Every Edit Its Own Budget: Per-Instruction Token Freezing for Training-Free Acceleration of Multi-Edit Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32407)
*Jyotirmoy Nath,Neeraj Kumar,Brejesh Lall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TIDE：无需训练的多指令图像编辑加速器，用非平衡最优传输定位每条指令对应的区域，按"各指令各自"的相对漂移阈值冻结 token，并给出分指令误差预算无法由全局规则保证的理论证明。速度提升约3.5–4.9x，编辑丢失最少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: flow-matching 图像编辑器的免训练加速器通常用单一全局阈值跳过"预测已收敛"的 token；在多指令编辑场景下，为提速而调紧的阈值会优先冻结幅度小、收敛慢的编辑，导致用户请求的编辑消失。因此需要按指令部分分配算力预算。

Method: 1) 通过非平衡最优传输在编辑器自身注意力图上定位每条指令指令片段的区域，无需掩码或外部模型；2) 冻结判据改为 token 漂移相对于其所属指令区域的漂移（相对阈值），冻结后沿直线路径外推至端点估计；3) 稀疏计算引擎只计算剩余 token；理论上证明部分无关（全局）规则无法保证分指令误差预算，刻画最优分指令阈值策略（除每个部分一个并列类外唯一），并给出本方法达到该策略的条件。

Result: FLUX.2 Klein 上，MICE-Bench 中 89% 图像至少有一条编辑被全局阈值破坏预算；TIDE 在同等或更高速度下最多减少 77% 的编辑丢失（MICE-Bench 平均 48%），人类评审确认该差距。多步 Klein Base 上在六个基准均为最快（4.81–4.89x），TFLOPs 比原版少 5.3x；Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 上同样最快（3.46–3.52x）且编辑丢失最少。

Conclusion: 按指令部分分配误差预算、以注意力-最优传输做指令-区域关联的加速范式，可同时显著提速与保住多编辑语义；方法免训练、跨模型家族可迁移，并配有理论保证与人工评测。

Abstract: Accelerating instruction-based image editing when one instruction requests several edits is challenging. Many training-free accelerators for flow-matching image editors skip tokens whose prediction has stopped changing, but decide this with a single threshold for the whole image. With several edits, such a threshold, tuned for speed, freezes small and slowly settling edits first, and requested edits disappear. We propose TIDE (Training-free Instruction-aware Drift-based Editing), which gives every instruction part its own compute budget. First, TIDE finds the region of each part in the editor's own attention by unbalanced optimal transport, without masks or external models. Second, it freezes a token once its drift is small relative to its own part and moves it along the straight path to its endpoint estimate. Third, a sparse engine computes only the remaining tokens. We prove that part-agnostic rules cannot guarantee per-part error budgets, characterize the optimal per-part threshold policy up to one tie class per part, and derive when our rule attains it. On FLUX.2 Klein, one global threshold breaks at least one edit's budget in 89% of MICE-Bench images, and TIDE drops up to 77% fewer edits at matched or higher speed (48% on MICE-Bench), a gap human raters confirm. On the many-step Klein Base, TIDE is the fastest compared method on six benchmarks (4.81-4.89x) and needs 5.3x fewer TFLOPs than vanilla, the largest saving we measure. With the same settings, it is also the fastest compared method on Qwen-Image-Edit-2511, a second model family (3.46-3.52x), and drops the fewest edits on all six benchmarks. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [154] [TESS: Query-Guided Temporal Event-Structured Frame Selection for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32410)
*Qi Lu,Shiyan Tong,Shujie Li,Sen Li,Likun shi,Maofei Que,Xiaolong Jiang,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TESS：免训练的长视频查询引导时空事件结构化帧选择框架，联合选取同时包含参考事件与被问事件的帧并保持时间顺序，在固定帧预算下优于最强基线4.0–5.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频中与问题相关的关键事件稀疏、冗余内容多，有限视觉预算易漏掉事件及其时间上下文；现有帧选方法侧重语义相关性或时间覆盖，忽视定位相关事件并建立时间关系所需的互补证据。

Method: 免训练框架：基于冻结的视觉-语言表示，采用软时间门控（soft temporal gating）、角色感知的预算分配（role-aware budget allocation）与角色内冗余抑制（within-role redundancy suppression），使所选帧同时显示参考事件与目标事件并保持所需时序。

Result: 在4个长视频理解基准、3种多模态骨干上实验，固定帧预算下比最强基线高4.0–5.5个百分点；长视频提升更明显，紧预算下表现稳健；独立证据标注显示事件与时间关系覆盖更好。

Conclusion: 显式的时间证据组织是一种有效的免训练策略，可保留事件关系并支持长视频高效时序推理。属长视频理解/视频问答方向，与具身智能、VLA、3D重建等核心AI方向关联较弱。

Abstract: Temporal localization in long videos is challenging because brief, question-relevant events are dispersed among extensive redundant content. Limited visual budgets increase the risk of omitting these events or their temporal context, undermining reliable reasoning. Existing frame selection methods emphasize semantic relevance or temporal coverage, often overlooking the complementary evidence needed to locate related events and establish their temporal relationships. To address this limitation, we propose TESS, a training-free framework for query-guided temporal event-structured frame selection. TESS selects frames that jointly show the reference event and the event asked about, in the required temporal order. Leveraging frozen vision-language representations, TESS integrates soft temporal gating, role-aware budget allocation, and within-role redundancy suppression to preserve complementary event evidence. Extensive experiments across four long-video understanding benchmarks and three multimodal backbones demonstrate that TESS consistently outperforms the strongest compared baseline by 4.0-5.5 percentage points under a fixed frame budget. Further analyses reveal greater improvements on longer videos and robust performance under tighter visual budgets, while independent evidence annotations confirm enhanced event and temporal-relation coverage. These findings establish explicit temporal evidence organization as an effective training-free strategy for preserving event relationships and supporting efficient temporal reasoning over long videos. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [155] [Factorized Context Attention: From Emulated to Native Grid Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32423)
*Mohamed Eltahir,Manar Eyad,Yara Alzahrani,Fatimah Albesher,Duaa M. Tahir,Tanveer Hussain,Naeemullah Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的块稀疏原生注意力 FCA，将 grid probing 的"外部模拟注意力"改为模型内部一次 KV 编码后的批量掩码前向，用阶梯位置表保持 span 在训练位置范围内，在长视频、长文本与图像 grounding 上以极小成本匹配 grid probing 精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文单次前向会变慢、掉精度并受限于上下文窗口；稀疏注意力虽高效但仍继承这两点限制；grid probing 通过短局部/跨步 span 的重复提问恢复精度与覆盖范围，但每个 span 都要重新编码整段输入，计算重复、成本极高。

Method: 洞察 grid probing 本质是"由提示从外部复现的注意力模式"（每次 probe 只限制可见输入范围）。据此提出 Factorized Context Attention：训练无关的块稀疏注意力，在模型自身注意力中原生执行同一模式——输入仅编码一次为 KV cache，所有 span 以掩码序列形式在一次批量化前向中读取，并采用 stair position schedule 保证每个 span 落在模型训练过的位置区间内；冻结现有语言/视觉语言模型即可使用。

Result: 在长视频理解、长上下文文本推理和图像 grounding 上，FCA 以 grid probing 一小部分成本达到其精度与覆盖范围；相比单次前向，长视频约 +10 分，长篇小说 +9~14 分；可在超千帧视频、四倍于窗口长度的文本中检索"针"；对同一输入的后续提问耗时低至 grid probing 的 1/10。

Conclusion: 原生注意力可以保留"模拟注意力"带来的收益而免除其成本，全部基于现成冻结的语言与视觉语言模型实现，无需训练。

Abstract: Models read a book, an hour of video, or an image in one forward pass. As the context grows, this pass slows down, loses accuracy, and stops at the context window. Sparse attention is efficient, but keeps the other two limitations. Grid probing, a recent training-free method, recovers the accuracy and extends the reach by asking a frozen model the same question about short local and strided spans of the input, but every question repeats the whole computation, since each span is encoded again. Our insight is that grid probing is emulated attention, an attention pattern reproduced from outside the model through prompts, since each probe only restricts which part of the input the question can see. We introduce Factorized Context Attention (FCA), a training-free block-sparse attention that runs the same pattern natively, as the attention of the model itself. FCA encodes the input once into a KV cache, reads all spans as masked sequences in one batched forward, and uses a stair position schedule to keep every span inside the trained position range. Across long video understanding, long-context text inference and image grounding, FCA matches the accuracy and the reach of grid probing at a fraction of its cost. It gains about 10 points over the single pass on long video and 9 to 14 points on long novels, finds needles in videos of over a thousand frames and in texts four times the length of the window, and answers a later question about the same input in as little as a tenth of the time of grid probing. Native attention thus keeps what emulated attention gains without its cost, all with existing frozen language and vision-language models.

</details>


### [156] [One Subspace Is Not Enough: LocalSubspaceAD for Training-Free Few-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32424)
*Daniel Pröll,Sebastian Hegenbart*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LocalSubspaceAD：用 k-means 将基础模型 patch 特征聚类，每个簇拟合一个主子空间，patch 异常分数取所有子空间最小重投影残差，无需训练、无需 EM 精修，在多数据集 1/2/4-shot 设定下优于单子空间 SubspaceAD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业无缺陷样本在外观、纹理、几何上差异大，few-shot 下难以充分建模。SubspaceAD 的单一整体主子空间必须覆盖全部名义变异性，导致"在一个语义区域算作异常、在另一个区域却属正常"的偏差被淹没；需要更细粒度的局部变异性模型。

Method: 对冻结基础模型（DINOv2/DINOv3）提取的 patch 特征做 k-means 划分，每个簇独立拟合（SVD/子空间）基，测试 patch 分数取其在所有子空间中重投影残差的最小值；完全 training-free，不含 EM 迭代、不做簇中心偏移校正。

Result: MVTec AD、VisA、Real-IAD（1/2/4-shot）：DINOv2 上 image AUROC 提升 1.2–1.8 点；DINOv3 上 image AUROC、pixel AUPR、AUPRO 提升 1.4–4.4 点。多实例类别与 PCB 等名义外观异质物体增益最大。消融表明增益来自局部子空间更细粒度的变异性建模，而非簇均值偏移；亦非单纯容量增加——子空间捕获了特征空间中一致簇的结构，模型并非压缩记忆库。

Conclusion: 以"混合子空间 + 最小残差"替代单一主子空间，是一种低成本、可插拔的 few-shot 工业异常检测增强，验证了局部化变异性建模的必要性；属边缘相关（感知/工业检测）工作，与具身 3D 重建、VLA、世界模型主线关联较弱。

Abstract: Defect-free industrial samples can vary considerably in appearance, texture, and geometry. In few-shot anomaly detection, this variability has to be modeled from only a handful of images. SubspaceAD showed that a single principal subspace of foundation-model patch features is a strong training-free model of nominal appearance. However, a single subspace has to cover all of the nominal data's variability, so a deviation that is anomalous for one semantic region can be within the nominal variability of another. We propose LocalSubspaceAD, which extends SubspaceAD from a single subspace to a mixture of subspaces, assigning each group of patches with similar features its own subspace. The set of patch-features is partitioned using k-means, one subspace is fit per cluster, and each patch is scored by the minimum of re-projection residuals over all subspaces. The proposed method requires no training and no EM refinement. On MVTec AD, VisA and Real-IAD with 1, 2 and 4 shots, it improves over a single subspace (SubspaceAD) on both evaluated backbones: by 1.2 to 1.8 points image AUROC on DINOv2, and by 1.4 to 4.4 points in image AUROC, pixel AUPR and AUPRO on DINOv3. The gains are most pronounced on objects with heterogeneous nominal appearance, such as multi-instance categories and PCBs. Ablations show that the gains stem from a finer-grained model of variability through locally oriented subspaces, rather than from the subspace offsets (cluster means). We further show that the gains do not stem from added capacity alone: the local subspaces appear to capture the structure of coherent clusters in feature space, and the model does not merely act as a compressed memory bank.

</details>


### [157] [NRG-Twins: Pixel-Level Ground Truth for Rigidity Violations in Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32431)
*Chenrui Fan,Qiyang Hu,Luca Scharr,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 "真实-生成孪生" 数据构造法：用关键帧条件视频生成器重建真实视频相机轨迹，测量每像素偏离刚体场景的角度（度），发布 871 对 NRG-Twins 数据集与基准，并训练仅凭 RGB 预测逐像素非刚性角的检测器 NRG-PD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成视频存在细微的非3D一致性问题（静态内容漂移、变形、重建退化），人工难以标注；要修正生成器或构建奖励/引导，需要来自生成视频本身的、逐像素定位"何处、多大程度"破坏刚性的真值数据。

Method: 构造真实-生成孪生对：真实片段 + 关键帧条件生成器的重建版本；插入真实关键帧使孪生相机轨迹近似跟随真实轨迹；在孪生自身相机路径下逐像素测量其相对刚性场景的偏离角（度）。发布 NRG-Twins（871 对）、可迁移到新生成器的构建代码，以及含官方测试集的基准。训练 NRG-PD 从 RGB 回归逐像素角度。

Result: NRG-PD 在区分一致/不一致像素上达 AUROC 0.85，七种现有一致性/运动分割方法为 0.40–0.74；其预测可提升含生成内容视频的 3DGS 重建质量。

Conclusion: 该工作为生成视频的3D一致性提供了自动化的逐像素真值来源与可用的检测器，可服务于生成器改进、奖励建模与3DGS优化。

Abstract: Video generators now produce realistic clips of scenes and objects, which makes generated video an attractive source of 3D content. Yet generated video is not 3D consistent: static content slowly deforms or slides, 3D reconstruction degrades, and the errors are too subtle to label by hand. To make generators 3D consistent, or to build rewards and guidance that push them there, we need data that say where and by how much a generated video breaks rigidity, and these data must come from generated video itself. We obtain per-pixel ground-truth labels from real-generated twins: a real clip paired with its re-creation by a keyframe-conditioned video generator. After inserting real keyframes so that the twin camera roughly follows the real one, we measure per pixel, in degrees, how far the twin departs from a rigid scene seen along its own camera path. We release Non-Rigidly Generated Twins (NRG-Twins), 871 twins, the code that builds them for new generators, and a benchmark with an official test split. A detector trained on these data, the Non-Rigidly Generated Pixel Detector (NRG-PD), predicts the per-pixel angle from RGB alone. It separates inconsistent from consistent pixels with AUROC 0.85, against 0.40-0.74 for seven existing consistency and motion-segmentation methods, and its predictions improve 3DGS of video that contains generated content.

</details>


### [158] [Video Models are Excellent Motion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32432)
*Kian Izadpanah,Ali Mahdavi Amiri,Daniel Cohen-Or,Yiorgos Chrysanthou,Andreas Aristidou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个统一评测框架，通过三种设定（纯文本生成动作、从视频生成动作的稀疏关键帧重建、用视频模型增强动作模型输出）将视频基础模型与专用动作生成模型统一转换为3D骨架表示进行比较，发现视频基础模型的动作先验在自然性与语义范围（尤其复杂/复合动作）上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者主张视频基础模型已具备比专用动作模型更丰富、更通用的动作先验，其优势不仅在于视觉真实感，还体现在动作自然性（身体协调、物理合理性、与物体/环境交互）和动作范围（更广的语义与复杂复合动作）两方面，需要系统性的实证比较来验证这一立场。

Method: 构建通用评测框架，设计三种互补设定：1) 纯文本驱动动作生成；2) 从视频生成动作的稀疏关键帧重建；3) 基于视频对动作模型输出进行增强。所有输出统一转换为共享3D骨架表示，并评估动作自然性、语义对齐、动作覆盖度、物理运动特性及对动作约束的保真度；另通过宽松骨架控制研究动作条件生成。

Result: 视频基础模型能生成更自然、语义更合理的动作，尤其在复杂与复合动作上表现更好；专用动作模型并不能稳定改善视频生成的动作，而视频模型可利用稀疏动作引导增强骨架动画。

Conclusion: 专用动作模型的角色应发生转变：从生成完整动画转向引导与控制已嵌入视频基础模型中的更丰富动作能力。

Abstract: Video foundation models have emerged as powerful generators of dynamic visual content, exhibiting remarkable capabilities in synthesizing complex actions and interactions. We argue that these models have also become excellent motion generators, with motion priors that are richer and more general than those learned by dedicated motion models. Their advantage extends beyond visual realism and is manifested in two important aspects: motion naturalness, including coherent body coordination, physical plausibility, and realistic interactions with objects and the environment; and motion scope, encompassing a much broader range of activities and semantic coherence, particularly uncommon, complex, and compound actions. To investigate this position, we introduce a common evaluation framework that compares video and dedicated motion models through three complementary settings: text-only motion generation, reconstruction of video-generated motion from sparse keyframes, and video-based enrichment of motion-model outputs. By converting all outputs into a shared 3D skeletal representation, we evaluate motion naturalness, semantic alignment, action coverage, physical motion characteristics, and fidelity to motion constraints. Our results suggest that video foundation models can generate more natural and semantically meaningful movements, particularly for complex and compound actions. Moreover, we investigate motion-conditioned generation through loose skeletal control, finding that dedicated motion models do not consistently improve video-generated motion, while video models can enrich skeletal animations using sparse motion guidance. Our findings suggest a shift in the role of specialized motion models, from generating complete animations to guiding and controlling the richer motion capabilities already embedded in video foundation models.

</details>


### [159] [PVWander: 3D Memory-guided Panoramic Video Wandering with Path-Facing Canonicalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32443)
*Chuanxin Song,Xiaodong Wang,Shijie Chen,Junliang Xie,Zhanmi Zhong,Suiying Wu,Peixi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PVWander：以在线点云记忆+重投影引导长时程 360° 全景视频漫游生成，含路径朝向规范化与时间步感知门控两个设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程全景视频漫游难以稳定：运动-朝向关系多样，且累积生成伪影与深度误差使记忆引导不可靠。

Method: 将已生成观测累积为在线点云记忆并重投影引导后续生成；Path-Facing canonicalization 利用 ERP 水平循环对称性将每段朝向对齐到主平移方向；Timestep-aware gating modulation 用时间步相关的通道级门控调节重投影引导强度。

Result: 实验显示长时程生成质量提升，消融验证两个模块均有效（摘要未给出量化指标）。

Conclusion: 3D 记忆引导+几何/时间步自适应调制是稳定长时程全景漫游的有效路径。

Abstract: Long-horizon panoramic video wandering aims to continuously synthesize scene-consistent $360^\circ$ observations along user-specified camera trajectories from an initial equirectangular projection (ERP) panorama. Existing panoramic video models have achieved promising results in camera-controlled generation, while recent 3D-guided approaches further provide geometric guidance for improving scene consistency. However, stable long-horizon wandering remains challenging due to diverse motion--orientation relationships and imperfect memory guidance induced by accumulated generation artifacts and depth inaccuracies. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{PVWander}, a 3D-memory-guided framework that accumulates generated observations into an online point-cloud memory and reprojects it to guide subsequent generation, with two complementary designs: \textit{Path-Facing canonicalization} for motion--orientation alignment and \textit{Timestep-aware gating modulation} for adaptive memory guidance. \textit{Path-Facing canonicalization} exploits the horizontal cyclic symmetry of ERP panoramas to uniformly align each clip's ERP orientation with its principal translation direction, yielding more consistent, forward-like relative motions while preserving the prescribed camera path. \textit{Timestep-aware gating modulation} exploits the coarse-to-fine nature of diffusion denoising to adaptively regulate reprojected memory guidance through timestep-dependent channel-wise gates, preserving its structural benefits while mitigating visual artifacts from imperfect projections. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PVWander achieves improved long-horizon generation quality, while ablations validate the effectiveness of both designs.

</details>


### [160] [A Mesh to Remember: Persistent Appearance for Interactive Video Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32445)
*Joonghyuk Shin,Alex Trevithick,Katarína Tóthová,Liyuan Zhu,Shengyu Huang,Jaesik Park,Shalini De Mello,Laura Leal-Taixé,Riccardo de Lutio,Haithem Turki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: M2R 是一个少步因果视频渲染器，把特征存到网格表面（在物体坐标系中）并按需栅格化检索，实现持久、跨视角/跨智能体一致的世界渲染，720p 实时 40 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型外观逼真但长时交互中一致性差；显式 3D 世界（编码智能体、text-to-3D）几何与状态天然持久但外观不真实。已有"粗代理引导视频模型"的方法中，渲染器需自行"发明"表面外观，表面离开视野后外观被遗忘、再现时重造，导致不一致。

Method: 关键洞察：网格在跨帧跨视角（即使物体运动）唯一确定每个像素对应哪个表面，可作为生成内容的"地址"。M2R 在每帧将自身特征写入所描绘表面、存于各物体自身坐标系，当表面重新入视野时通过栅格化网格取回特征，从而让外观随物体移动、跨重访持久并被多智能体共享；内存随可见表面数增长而非帧数。仅用合成数据训练。

Result: 零样本迁移到真实室内扫描与编码智能体构建的世界；在静态、动态、多智能体基准上一致性超越 SOTA，物体持久性提升一倍以上、跨智能体一致性接近翻倍；720p 实时渲染 40 FPS。

Conclusion: 用网格作为表面记忆地址，把几何持久性与视频生成的真实感结合，为长时交互、多智能体共享的世界模型提供可扩展记忆机制。

Abstract: Video world models synthesize photorealistic observations in response to user actions but struggle to remain consistent over long interactions. Coding agents and text-to-3D models instead generate explicit 3D worlds whose geometry and state persist by construction, but whose appearance falls far short of photorealism. Recent methods combine the strengths of both by rendering such worlds into coarse proxies that guide a video model. However, since these proxies only specify where things are, the renderer must invent what they look like as the world is explored, and anything it forgets is reinvented when a surface reappears. Our key insight is that a mesh identifies which surface each pixel shows, across frames and views, even as objects move, allowing it to serve as an address for what the renderer generates. We propose M2R (Mesh to Remember), a few-step causal video renderer that stores its own features on the surfaces they depict, in each object's frame, and retrieves them by rasterizing the mesh whenever those surfaces come back into view. Appearance thus travels with objects wherever they move, persists across revisits, and is shared by every agent that observes it, while memory grows with the surfaces seen instead of the number of frames. Trained only on synthetic data, M2R transfers zero-shot to real indoor scans and to worlds authored by coding agents. It outperforms state-of-the-art methods in consistency across static, dynamic, and multi-agent benchmarks, more than doubling object permanence and nearly doubling cross-agent consistency, while rendering 720p video in real time (40 FPS).

</details>


### [161] [ObsBridge: Observation-Grounded Conditioning for Ego Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32447)
*Zhenhao Wang,Shaofeng Liang,Jiemin Wu,Sijia Chen,Keni Shi,Nan Li,Wenshuo Chen,Bowen Tian,Runwei Guan,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ObsBridge，一种基于 flow 匹配的轨迹预测框架，通过“观测锚定的历史桥接”与智能体级渐进式课程训练，使预测器同时以恢复的历史状态与原始观测证据为条件，在 ego-view 行人预测基准上超过 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ego-view 行人预测中，遮挡、有限视野与跟踪误差会污染历史运动观测；现有方法要么先插补/重建干净历史再预测（部署时残余误差敏感），要么用辅助重建分支联合训练（但仍在原始含噪坐标上计算智能体交互）。作者希望让预测显式条件于“恢复历史 + 原始观测证据”两者，并容忍不完美恢复。

Method: 1) 用冻结的估计器从 agent-relative / ego-relative 位置、图像平面坐标与可见性状态恢复历史运动；2) 训练期引入 agent-wise curriculum，按智能体逐步用完整估计历史替换干净 teacher 历史，再送入上下文编码；3) 设计 residual geometry adapter，将编码后的运动表示与原始观测证据融合；4) 经 social attention 聚合表示，条件化 flow-based 轨迹生成器。

Result: 多基准上优于现有 SOTA；在 EgoTraj-TBD 上 minADE@20 降低 6.5%、minFDE@20 降低 8.7%。为 ego-view 行人轨迹预测的对比实验级提升，未涉及真实机器人部署验证。

Conclusion: 将历史恢复与未来预测解耦但通过渐进课程和观测残差融合桥接，是缓解部署期恢复误差的有效范式；代码将开源。

Abstract: Pedestrian forecasting from ego-view observations is challenging because occlusion, limited visibility, and tracking errors corrupt observed motion histories. Such errors are particularly consequential for autonomous robot navigation. Existing paradigms typically either impute noisy history positions before forecasting or jointly train an auxiliary history reconstruction branch. However, predictors trained on clean histories may be sensitive to residual errors in recovered histories at deployment. Meanwhile, auxiliary reconstruction still computes inter-agent interactions over raw, corrupted coordinates. We investigate conditioning future prediction on both recovered historical states and raw observation evidence, with progressive training to accommodate imperfect recovery. Building on this insight, we propose ObsBridge, a flow-based trajectory forecasting framework with observation-grounded historical bridging. Specifically, ObsBridge first employs a frozen estimator to recover historical motion from agent-relative and ego-relative positions, image-plane locations, and visibility states. During training, we introduce an agent-wise curriculum that gradually replaces clean teacher histories with complete estimated histories before context encoding. This progressively exposes the predictor to estimated histories during training. Furthermore, we design a residual geometry adapter to fuse the encoded motion with original observation evidence. We aggregate the resulting representations through social attention to condition the flow-based trajectory generator. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that ObsBridge outperforms state-of-the-art methods. On EgoTraj-TBD, it reduces minADE@20 by 6.5\% and minFDE@20 by 8.7\%. The code will be provided at this https URL .

</details>


### [162] [Which Parameters to Update? An Instance-Specific Adaptation Framework for Real-World Pansharpening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32450)
*Jin-Liang Xiao,Liang-Jian Deng,Yu-Xuan Zhang,Hua Meng,Zhengchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出实例级测试时自适应框架：用交叉打分从预训练泛锐化网络中筛选参数组，并以光谱一致性+新设计空间一致性损失进行更新，提升真实场景泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练泛锐化网络在训练/测试分布偏移下性能下降，限制真实场景泛化能力。

Method: 利用单个观测的PAN-LRMS图像对做测试时自适应：交叉打分（cross-scoring）按图像块识别能改善重建的参数组，仅更新选中子集；损失为光谱一致性项加新提出的空间一致性项。

Result: 自适应后的网络优于其预训练版本，并在GaoFen-2、QuickBird、WorldView系列数据集上超过代表性SOTA泛锐化方法。

Conclusion: 参数子集选择的测试时自适应是缓解泛锐化分布偏移、提升实际部署泛化的有效途径。

Abstract: Pansharpening aims to fuse a single observed image pair, comprising a low-resolution multispectral (LRMS) image and a panchromatic (PAN) image, to obtain a high-resolution multispectral (HRMS) image. Recently, deep learning-based methods have achieved significant performance due to their strong capability of extracting nonlinear features. However, distribution shifts between training data and test images can degrade the performance of pretrained pansharpening networks, limiting their generalization in real-world scenarios. In this work, we propose an instance-specific test-time adaptation framework that uses the observed PAN-LRMS pair to select and update only a subset of the pretrained network's parameters. Specifically, the framework uses cross-scoring to identify parameter groups whose updates improve reconstruction across image patches. It then updates the selected groups by minimizing a loss that combines a spectral consistency term with a newly designed spatial consistency term. Experiments show that the adapted networks perform better than their pretrained counterparts and outperform representative state-of-the-art pansharpening methods on GaoFen-2, QuickBird, and WorldView series datasets.

</details>


### [163] [ViDM: Efficient and Order-Robust Parallel Visual Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32461)
*Xudong Tan,Peng Ye,Chenyu Huang,Yaoxin Yang,Huafeng Qin,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViDM 将多问题视觉推理改造为单次前向的并行决策：把共享视觉上下文、多个问题与各自决策槽组织进一个序列，复用 LM head 同时输出全部答案分布，并配合 Decision-Query Mask 与 case-balanced 多顺序训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归 MLLM 对同一视觉输入的多问题逐 token 生成，解码开销随问题数线性累积，且答案随问题顺序变化。目标是在保持或提升精度的同时消除顺序解码瓶颈与顺序敏感性。

Method: 统一序列化输入（视觉上下文+多问题+问题专属决策槽）；复用原生语言模型 head 并行得到所有答案分布；Decision-Query Mask 约束每个决策 query 的注意力仅覆盖共享上下文与对应问题；case-balanced 多问题顺序训练提升重排鲁棒性。在 Qwen3.5-4B、LLaVA-OneVision-7B、InternVL3.5-4B 上验证。

Result: 9 个基准（图像/多图/视频）：Qwen3.5-4B 平均准确率 73.49%→77.55%，端到端平均吞吐 8.4x；GQA（平均 11.4 问题/图）达 20.3x 加速；平均与最差顺序准确率提升、顺序引起的波动下降。

Conclusion: 多问题视觉决策可并行化而不损精度，注意力掩码设计是关键，方法具备跨模型族可迁移性；属高效推理/多任务 VQA 方向，可用于具身智能体高频视觉查询场景，但未涉及机器人实体部署或 3D/世界模型。

Abstract: Visual decision-making demands accurate judgments and timely responses. Yet for multiple questions sharing the same visual input, autoregressive multimodal large language models generate answers token by token, accumulating sequential decoding costs as question count grows and delaying completion of the decision set. Their predictions can also vary with question order. We introduce ViDM, which transforms multi-question visual inference into parallel decisions in a single forward pass, improving both accuracy and inference efficiency. ViDM organizes shared visual context, multiple questions, and question-specific decision slots into one sequence, reusing the native language-model head to produce all answer distributions simultaneously. A Decision-Query Mask aligns each decision query's direct attention with shared context and its corresponding question, while case-balanced multi-order training strengthens robustness to question reordering. Across nine benchmarks spanning image, multi-image, and video tasks, ViDM improves Qwen3.5-4B's average accuracy from 73.49% to 77.55% while achieving 8.4x the baseline's average end-to-end throughput. On GQA, where each evaluated image has 11.4 questions on average, the end-to-end speedup reaches 20.3x. ViDM also improves mean and worst-order accuracy while reducing order-induced variation across multiple benchmarks, with further validation on LLaVA-OneVision-7B and InternVL3.5-4B.

</details>


### [164] [MAGPIE: Agentic Multi-Reference Image Generation via Executable Scene Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32462)
*Yifan Dai,Daili Hua,Bohan Zeng,Yuran Wang,Jialing Liu,Bozhou Li,Chengzhuo Tong,Yang Shi,Kai Zeng,Liu Yang,Xinyu Liu,Shujie Li,Yiyan Ji,Bingyin Mei,Yushuo Guan,Yuanxing Zhang,Fangcheng Fu,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAGPIE将多参考图像生成中的构图意图表示为由编码代理生成并迭代修正的可执行Blender 3D程序，用其渲染结果作为视觉蓝图约束扩散生成器，从而显式检查与精修场景关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参考生成要求保持指定主体外观并按指令排布，但现有组合控制方法给出的关系指导是隐式的，无法在合成前被显式实例化、检查与修正。

Method: 引入MAGPIE：以简化的3D场景（可执行Blender程序）表示目标构图；编码代理将每个场景实例链接到对应参考图，对照指令检查几何与渲染视角，并修订程序以消除偏差；最终代理渲染作为图像生成器的视觉计划。基于MAGPIE-Instruct-3K训练生成器，使其遵循该计划同时从参考图提取主体外观。在Qwen与FLUX上验证。

Result: 在DreamOmni2与SconeEval上均带来提升；配合FLUX在DreamOmni2上整体成功率较原始骨干提升28.21个百分点，并在SconeEval上取得参评模型中最高的总体得分。

Conclusion: 将构图目标外化为可执行、可检查、可迭代修正的3D代理场景，是提升多参考图像生成构图可靠性的有效范式，且可跨骨干模型迁移。

Abstract: Multi-reference image generation requires a model to preserve the appearance of selected subjects while arranging them according to an instruction. Despite recent progress in reference-based generation and compositional control, reliably organizing selected subjects into the intended scene remains challenging. Although existing methods provide various forms of compositional guidance, the planned relationships are not always explicitly instantiated for inspection and refinement before image synthesis. To address this limitation, we introduce MAGPIE, which represents the intended composition as a simplified 3D scene defined by an executable Blender program. A coding agent links each scene instance to its reference, checks the geometry and rendered view against the instruction, and revises the program to correct discrepancies. The final proxy rendering serves as a visual plan for the image generator. Using MAGPIE-Instruct-3K, we train the generator to follow this plan while drawing each subject's appearance from its reference. MAGPIE improves both Qwen and FLUX on DreamOmni2 and SconeEval. With FLUX, it improves overall success on DreamOmni2 by 28.21 percentage points over the original backbone and achieves the highest overall score among the models evaluated on SconeEval.

</details>


### [165] [Describe, Don't Ask: Caption-Driven On-Policy Distillation for Fine-Grained Visual Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32463)
*Haozhan Shen,Liangbo He,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Tiancheng Zhao,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CaptionOPD用一组与图像无关的固定caption提示替代定制QA，实现基于无标注图像与区域框的多方面自描述—特权视角蒸馏，使crop视角教师对区域内多类视觉要素进行监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有multimodal on-policy distillation依赖为每个区域精心构建的区域专属QA对，存在三大缺陷：需为每区域单独编写QA、难以扩展到无标注图像；同一区域所有rollout回答同一问题，导致只监督被提问的细节；问题预设目标必然存在，使学生在目标缺失时仍倾向作答。

Method: 提出CaptionOPD：以固定、图像无关的caption提示集合（通用描述、局部、颜色、计数、文字、布局等）替代定制问题；同一区域的不同rollout接收不同提示，教师可监督多方面的视觉要素；caption提示要求模型描述可见内容而非查询特定目标，从而避免预设。以模型自身作为crop视角教师，仅需图像与区域框，无需问题、答案或更大模型标注，属on-policy特权视角蒸馏。

Result: 在相同图像与区域训练条件下，CaptionOPD在多个细粒度与通用感知基准上优于先前的OPD方法。

Conclusion: 通过多视角自描述与特权视角蒸馏，可将无标注图像转化为广泛的视觉监督，摆脱昂贵QA构建，提升VLM细粒度感知，且具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often recognize fine-grained details more accurately in a relevant crop than in the full image. Multimodal on-policy distillation (OPD) methods exploit this by transferring the advantage of a crop-view teacher to a full-image student. However, their supervision relies on region-specific questions curated through an elaborate synthesis pipeline, which brings three weaknesses: (1) every region needs a question--answer pair written exclusively for it, locking training into costly QA curation that is hard to scale to unlabeled images; (2) all rollouts of a region answer the same question, so the teacher supervises only the details it asks about, leaving other visual elements in the region unsupervised; and (3) every question presupposes that its target exists, so the student becomes prone to answering even when the target is absent. In this paper, we study how to turn unlabeled images into broad visual supervision through multi-aspect self-description and privileged-view distillation, and propose CaptionOPD, which replaces tailored questions with a fixed set of image-agnostic caption prompts, ranging from general descriptions to focused captions of parts, colors, counts, text, and layout. The rollouts of a region receive different prompts, so the teacher supervises various aspects of the region. Moreover, because caption prompts mask the model to describe what is visible instead of querying a specific target, they avoid presupposing what the region contains. Using the model itself as the crop-view teacher, CaptionOPD requires no questions, no answers, and no larger model annotations: only images and region boxes. Experiments show that, trained on the same images and regions, CaptionOPD outperforms prior OPD methods across multiple fine-grained and general perception benchmarks.

</details>


### [166] [TDD: Target-Discrepancy Distillation for Underwater Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32467)
*Zefan Feng,Zhaocheng Yu,Laibin Chang,Kui Jiang,Junjun Jiang,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出目标-差异知识蒸馏（TDD）：用水下视觉语言教师同时监督"目标外观"与"相对目标的观测差异"两类表征，联合蒸馏进轻量引导网络，并在多尺度增强网络中按"先校正差异、后按目标细化"的顺序渐进融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下退化改变图像外观并削弱语义线索；仅识别场景内容无法明确"要恢复成什么样"以及"当前观测与目标之间的差异"，因而缺少可操作的复原监督信号。

Method: 对每个退化-参考训练对，用教师提取参考目标外观特征，并计算配对特征残差作为观测-目标差异；两者联合蒸馏到轻量引导网络（推理时无需教师与参考图）。在多尺度增强网络中引入渐进知识融合：差异估计先调制空间特征更新，再由目标外观引导细化更新后特征，最后以通道交互整合响应。

Result: 在六个水下基准上，全参考与无参考指标均具竞争力；消融与特征可视化验证了知识来源、联合蒸馏与渐进融合各自的贡献。

Conclusion: 将复原问题拆解为"目标外观"与"输入相关差异"两类引导并以校正-再细化顺序融合，是有效的蒸馏式水下图像增强范式。

Abstract: Underwater degradation changes image appearance and can compromise the semantic cues used to guide enhancement. Identifying scene content alone does not explicitly specify the appearance to recover or the input-dependent changes associated with that target. We propose target-discrepancy knowledge distillation (TDD) for underwater image enhancement, which organizes guidance around these two aspects of the same restoration problem. For each degraded-reference training pair, reference features from an underwater vision-language teacher provide target-appearance supervision, while paired feature residuals describe the observation's discrepancy relative to the same target. Both representations are jointly distilled into a lightweight guidance network that predicts them from the degraded image alone; neither the teacher nor reference images are required at inference. To align their use with their supervisory roles, we introduce progressive knowledge fusion within a multi-scale enhancement network. The estimated discrepancy first conditions spatial feature updates, after which target-appearance guidance refines the updated features and channel interactions consolidate their responses. This correction-before-refinement design lets target-guided refinement build on discrepancy-conditioned features. Experiments on six underwater benchmarks demonstrate competitive performance across full-reference and no-reference metrics. Ablations and feature visualizations analyze the contributions of the knowledge source, joint distillation, and progressive fusion.

</details>


### [167] [STAR: Counterfactual Dual-World Rollouts and Spatial Target Adaptive Reconstruction for Visual On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32472)
*Yi Zou,Zhuang Yan,Yudong Zhang,Xu Wang,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STAR：面向视觉语言模型的视觉on-policy自蒸馏框架，通过独立反事实"双世界"rollout与空间注意力引导的目标重构，显式迫使模型依赖局部答案决定性证据而非语言先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉on-policy自蒸馏（如特权图像裁剪监督）仅以单图正确性为信号，跨尺度诊断表明模型在局部证据完全被遮挡时仍保留超过一半的全图准确率，说明正确性不能保证对局部证据的依赖；同时不同token对视觉修正的需求随其空间注意力状态而异。

Method: (1) 局部图像编辑构造成对"世界"：问题与背景一致、答案决定性事实不同的双图，共享学生模型各自独立采样轨迹，在图像一致前缀下暴露上下文捷径；(2) 在每个世界内，教师对比真实裁剪与均值色裁剪以确定视觉修正方向；(3) 以学生图像注意力与教师质心空间参考的散度自适应地做token级目标对数概率重构。无需辅助注意力损失，也无测试时工具开销。

Result: 在六个细粒度基准上，Qwen3.5各规模平均准确率较初始化最多提升8.04个百分点；以4.7倍速度达到OPSD的最终准确率；并可泛化到域外推理任务。

Conclusion: 以反事实双世界rollout替换单图监督、以空间注意力散度做token级目标重构，可在无额外损失与推理开销下有效提升VLM对局部证据的依赖与细粒度准确率。

Abstract: Vision-language models often rely on global context and language priors while overlooking answer-determining local details. Privileged visual on-policy self-distillation supervises full-image student rollouts using a frozen teacher that views high-resolution evidence crops. Yet our cross-scale diagnostics show that models retain over half of their full-image accuracy when local evidence is completely masked, indicating that single-image correctness does not guarantee reliance on local evidence, while the need for visual correction also varies across tokens with their spatial attention states. We propose STAR, a visual on-policy self-distillation framework combining independent counterfactual dual-world rollouts with spatial-attention-guided target reconstruction. Local image editing constructs paired worlds with identical questions and backgrounds but distinct answer-determining facts, where a shared student samples trajectories independently to expose contextual shortcuts under image-consistent prefixes. Within each world, the teacher contrasts real and mean-color crops to determine the visual correction direction, while the divergence between student image attention and a teacher-centroid spatial reference adaptively controls token-wise target log-probability reconstruction. Without auxiliary attention losses or test-time tool overhead, STAR improves Qwen3.5 across scales by up to 8.04 percentage points in average accuracy over initialization across six fine-grained benchmarks, reaches OPSD's final accuracy $4.7\times$ faster, and generalizes to out-of-domain reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [168] [OmniPersist: Persistent Dynamic World Modeling via Coupled Panoramic Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32473)
*Zhilu Zhang,Xiaoyu Liu,Wenxi Liu,Shengfeng He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出单阶段扩散框架 OmniPersist，将透视视频生成与全景演化耦合，用几何锚定与时序补偿解决相机离开视野时动态物体消失/畸变问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界模型中，相机转向再返回时视野外运动实体因透视视野有界而无法在潜空间中合理演化，常发生形变或彻底消失，破坏动态物体持续性。

Method: 单阶段扩散模型中同时建模透视流与低频全景流；无需辅助模型或初始全景输入。通过几何锚点 (GeoAnch) 建立跨视图空间对应，时序补偿 (TemComp) 聚合时间信息；与相机控制架构无关，兼容 latent 级与 attention 级条件注入。

Result: 在新构建的动态基准上，图像到视频与视频续写任务中，视觉质量、相机可控性、重访一致性、动态持续性均一致提升。

Conclusion: 全景流作为低频"记忆通道"可有效弥补透视视野的观测缺失，为动作条件世界模型提供无辅助模型、可插拔的持续性解决方案。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models play a vital role in robotics and autonomous driving, yet existing video diffusion models struggle to maintain dynamic object persistence. When the camera turns away and returns, out-of-view moving entities fail to evolve plausibly in latent space, often mutating or vanishing entirely due to bounded perspective views. To resolve this issue, we propose OmniPersist, a unified framework that couples perspective video generation with panoramic evolution within a single-stage diffusion model, without relying on auxiliary models or initial panoramic inputs. To maintain efficiency, we model the panoramic stream at a lower frame rate, bridging both views across space and time via Geometric Anchoring (GeoAnch) for spatial correspondence and Temporal Compensation (TemComp) for temporal aggregation. OmniPersist is agnostic to camera-control architectures, supporting both latent-level and attention-level conditioning. Extensive experiments on a newly constructed dynamic benchmark across image-to-video and video continuation tasks demonstrate consistent gains in visual quality, camera controllability, revisit consistency, and dynamic persistence.

</details>


### [169] [Progressive Reliable Supervision for Black-Box Universal Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32474)
*Jing Li,Zhe Zhang,Huateng Sun,Yuhui Yang,Wanli Xue,Xu Cheng,Fan Shi,Jianhua Zhang,Qinghua Hu,Shengyong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PReS：在仅能访问黑盒源模型的通用域适应中，利用扩散扰动语义一致性、CLIP与源模型的交叉共识筛选可靠已知样本，并按可靠度加权融合伪标签，结合非对称监督与迭代闭环更新，实现可靠的渐进式目标监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B²-UniDA设定下源数据与源模型参数均不可见，缺乏可靠目标监督；黑盒模型预测受源偏置与域偏移影响不可靠，需要构造可信伪标签并区分已知/未知类别。

Method: 1) 用扩散扰动下的语义一致性和黑盒源模型与CLIP的跨模型共识识别可靠已知样本；2) 估计两个预测器的样本级可靠度并自适应融合互补预测得到伪标签；3) 对可靠已知样本施加强置信、判别性监督，对潜在未知样本保持低置信但局部一致性约束；4) 随适应进展用新获得的目标域知识反向精炼伪标签和已知/未知划分，形成监督构建与适应的闭环。

Result: 在多种标签空间关系下的多个基准数据集上取得SOTA或极具竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 通过协调黑盒源模型、基础模型与演化目标模型的互补知识，可渐进构建可靠监督，为黑盒通用域适应提供有效范式。

Abstract: Black-Box Universal Domain Adaptation (B$^2$-UniDA) aims to adapt a target model to an unlabeled target domain under arbitrary label-space relationships, while neither source data nor source-model parameters are accessible during adaptation. Under such restrictive information access, reliable target supervision is unavailable, while predictions from the black-box source model can be unreliable due to source bias and domain shifts. To address this challenge, we propose Progressive Reliable Supervision (PReS), which progressively establishes and refines reliable target supervision by coordinating complementary knowledge from the black-box source model, foundation models, and the evolving target model. PReS first identifies reliable known samples by exploiting semantic consistency under diffusion-based perturbations and cross-model consensus between the black-box source model and CLIP. It then estimates the sample-wise reliability of the two predictors and adaptively integrates their complementary predictions to construct reliable pseudo labels.
