We then render views of the scene by splatting, with a view selection strategy that maximizes scene coverage with as few views as possible. Next, we apply text-prompted segmentation on the rendered views, giving each 3D point a set of multi-view semantic cues, fused to produce a 3D segmentation of the scene. We evaluate SplatSeg3D on semantic segmentation and novel-class instance segmentation on 4 standard benchmarks: Scannet, Scannet++, Replica, and S3DIS. Our pipeline not only performs better or on-par with the state-of-the-art, but we also achieve these results efficiently across runtime and compute requirements. Code will be published.

</details>


### [204] [Diving Deeper into Dense Keypoint Localization in Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32728)
*Jiahong Chen,Jinghao Wang,Yueneng Yang,Banglei Guan,Zi Wang,Dewen Hu,Qifeng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GHLAPose: 用混合Gray码-二进制编码(HGB)改善全局关键点定位的不连续性问题,再用局部感知精化(LAR)估计不确定性并细化定位,实现6D位姿的全局到局部框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集语义关键点方法用二进制编码做高效全局关键点定位,但二进制编码的不连续性使高分辨率训练困难,且固有离散化/量化误差导致显著定位不确定性。

Method: HGB编码缓解不连续性以获得精确的初始全局关键点定位;LAR模块估计不确定性并对关键点定位进行精化,二者协同聚合全局与局部特征,形成精确的全局到局部策略。

Result: 在LM、LM-O、YCB-V、TUD-L上全面超越SOTA;LM-O上ADD(-S) 0.02d/0.05d分别领先次优1.4%和1.9%,YCB-V上ADD(-S) 0.1d领先1%。

Conclusion: 混合编码+不确定性驱动的局部精化是提升密集关键点6D位姿精度的有效组合,但方法仍局限于实例级/数据集内评估,未涉及类别级泛化、真实机器人闭环或开源可用性验证。

Abstract: 6D pose estimation of rigid objects is a fundamental and challenging task in computer vision. Recent dense semantic keypoint-based methods employ binary encoding for efficient global keypoint localization, delivering strong performance in object pose estimation. Nevertheless, the discontinuous nature of binary encoding hinders effective training at high resolutions, while its inherent discrete and quantization errors lead to significant localization uncertainty. We propose GHLAPose, a global-to-local framework that synergizes hybrid Gray-binary (HGB) encoding with local-aware refinement (LAR). Specifically, HGB encoding first alleviates discontinuity to achieve accurate initial global keypoint localization. To further address intrinsic encoding errors, the LAR module estimates uncertainty and refines keypoint localization. This synergy enables a precise global-to-local strategy by aggregating global and local features. Extensive experiments on LM, LM-O, YCB-V, and TUD-L datasets demonstrate that GHLAPose surpasses state-of-the-art methods, particularly on fine-grained metrics. On LM-O, it outperforms the second-best method by 1.4% and 1.9% on ADD(-S) 0.02d and 0.05d respectively, and on YCB-V by 1% on ADD(-S) 0.1d. The code will be available at GHLAPose.

</details>


### [205] [AnyPrune: Optimization-Free Pruning for Any Feedforward 3D Gaussian Splatting Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32735)
*Nicola Leonetti,Federico Stella,Aoxiang Fan,Saqib Javed,Pascal Fua,Luigi Di Stefano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnyPrune：一种基于 Transformer 的免优化剪枝模块，附加于任意前馈 3DGS 管线，通过单 GPU 短训练学习该管线的特有冗余模式，实现高效高斯点剪枝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈 3DGS 单次前向即重建可实时渲染的高斯表示，但输入视图增多时高斯数量远超实际所需，导致显存与存储开销剧增；现有剪枝法或需昂贵的重优化（丧失单次前馈优势），或仅用简单启发式策略而未充分利用信息。

Method: 在冻结的目标前馈 3DGS 管线之上，用短时间单 GPU 训练一个 Transformer 剪枝模块，学习并利用该管线特有的高斯冗余模式，无需重优化即可直接剔除冗余高斯。

Result: 在多数设置下渲染质量优于现有免优化剪枝方法，并可泛化到未见数据集；在消费级 GPU 上约 5s 可剪枝 160 万高斯场，速度快于所有高斯合并或场重优化方法；可与前馈管线内置剪枝叠加，在中等剪枝比下保持或提升质量；还能给出场景特定剪枝比建议。

Conclusion: AnyPrune 提供了一个通用、快速、免优化的前馈 3DGS 剪枝方案，兼顾压缩效率与渲染质量，并可即插即用地适配多种前馈管线、数据集与视图数量。

Abstract: Feedforward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) pipelines reconstruct a scene from images in a single forward pass, representing it as real-time-renderable 3D Gaussians. However, the resulting representations contain far more Gaussians than are needed to render the scene faithfully, especially when there are many input views. This drastically increases memory and storage requirements. Existing pruning methods either require costly re-optimization, which defeats the purpose of a single forward pass, or rely on simple strategies that do not exploit all the available information. We present AnyPrune, a transformer-based, optimization-free pruning module designed to work on top of any feedforward 3DGS pipeline. To remove unnecessary Gaussians, AnyPrune learns and exploits pipeline-specific redundancy patterns through a short single-GPU training on the frozen target feedforward pipeline. AnyPrune achieves better rendering quality than existing optimization-free pruning methods in most settings and generalizes to unseen datasets, as shown through extensive experiments across multiple feedforward pipelines, datasets, and input view counts. It prunes a 1.6M-Gaussian field in about $5\,\mathrm{s}$ on a consumer GPU, faster than every method that merges Gaussians or re-optimizes the field. It can also be combined with the pruning built into feedforward pipelines, pruning further at moderate ratios while preserving or improving rendering quality. Finally, it can even suggest a scene-specific pruning ratio that retains most of the quality.

</details>


### [206] [Qwen-Image-HD: Autoregressive Diffusion for High Definition Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32736)
*Zihao Liu,Zekai Zhang,Shengming Yin,Zikai Zhou,Donghao Zhou,Chenyang Zhu,Lihan Jiang,Ningyuan Tang,Bingxuan Zhao,Yanran Zhang,Yan Shu,Zhening Liu,Jiahao Li,Jie Zhang,Kaiyuan Gao,Kun Yan,Qihang Ai,Rui Song,Shuo Cao,Xiao Xu,Xiaoyue Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Zhendong Wang,Zhouyang Li,Bo Zheng,Jun Zhou,Xiu Li,Chenfei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Qwen-Image-HD 通过局部训练与分块自回归生成，把 Qwen-Image 2.0 适配到 4K 高分辨率合成，并用 Wavefront-AutoRegressive 推理调度降低单图延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接在高分辨率完整画布上微调生成器需要超长序列与巨大算力，且在训练资源受限时局部区域容易出现不自然的重复条纹；直接 4K 生成也难以兼顾质量与效率。

Method: 采用局部（tile 级 1K 尺度）训练 + 分块自回归生成：每次预测一个目标 tile，条件为高分辨率的上方与左侧邻块以及压缩的场景历史缩略图；将历史线索与目标映射到统一画布坐标系以表达空间关系与尺度差异；推理端提出 Wavefront-AutoRegressive 调度，在多 GPU 上并行生成同一反对角线上的 tile。

Result: 在 Qwen-Image-Bench 4K 设置下整体评测优于 FLUX-3-Image-Klein 直接 4K 生成；相比同主干的全图 4K 监督微调，生成质量更好、训练算力更低、推理效率更高，原始分辨率对比中重复条纹更少。

Conclusion: 以局部训练+分块自回归+坐标统一条件的方式，可在有限算力下实现高质量、低延迟的高分辨率图像生成，为高分辨率视觉生成提供可扩展范式。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a generator on a complete high-resolution canvas requires long image sequences and substantial computation, yet local regions can still exhibit unnatural repetitive stripes when training resources are limited. We introduce Qwen-Image-HD, which adapts Qwen-Image 2.0 to high-resolution synthesis through local training and blockwise autoregressive generation. Each prediction covers a target tile at 1K scale, conditioned on high-resolution upper and left neighbors and a compressed historical thumbnail. These conditions provide adjacent fine-scale structures and an overview of the scene generated so far. To integrate these complementary cues, we map both forms of history and the target to a shared canvas coordinate system to express their spatial relationships and scale differences. On Qwen-Image-Bench at 4K, Qwen-Image-HD achieves stronger overall evaluation performance than direct 4K generation with FLUX-3-Image-Klein. Compared with full-image 4K supervised fine-tuning of the same backbone, it achieves better generation quality with lower training compute and higher inference efficiency, while exhibiting fewer unnatural repetitive stripes in original-resolution comparisons. We further introduce a Wavefront-AutoRegressive inference schedule that generates tiles on the same anti-diagonal concurrently across GPUs to reduce single-image latency.

</details>


### [207] [A Morphological Approach to Inverse Lithography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32742)
*Gijs Bellaard,Bart M.N. Smets,Tim van der Leek,Alexandru Onose,Remco Duits*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出制造约束的数学刻画与评分函数（次可微），作为正则项嵌入梯度型逆光刻求解器，得到MorphILT算法，可在满足可制造约束的同时保持接近无约束ILT的成像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆光刻技术（ILT）需要在满足可制造性约束的前提下求解掩模，但约束难以在梯度优化中直接表达，因此需要一个可微的约束度量。

Method: 对制造约束进行数学建模；构造可（次）微分的评分函数衡量约束满足程度及违反严重度；将其作为正则项加入基于梯度的逆问题求解框架，形成MorphILT算法。

Result: MorphILT生成的掩模满足制造约束，且打印质量与不可制造的无约束ILT掩模几乎相同。

Conclusion: 可微约束正则化是解决ILT可制造性问题的有效途径，约束惩罚与成像保真度可良好兼顾。

Abstract: Lithography is a critical step in the manufacturing of chips that works by projecting light through a patterned mask and subsequently focusing it onto a photosensitive layer on top of a wafer. The task of finding a mask that prints a desired pattern on the wafer is called inverse lithography technology (ILT). The design of the mask is complicated by the fact that it must satisfy a set of constraints in order to be manufacturable. In this paper we provide a mathematical characterization of these constraints. Next, we develop a scoring function that allows us to measure whether a given mask satisfies the manufacturing constraints and if not, how badly the constraints are violated. Since our scoring function is (sub-)differentiable it can be used as the regularizer in a gradient-based inverse problem solver. We show that the resulting MorphILT algorithm that we developed can generate manufacturable masks that have nearly the same print quality as the (unmanufacturable) masks obtained from unconstrained inverse lithography.

</details>


### [208] [MemAnchorWorld: A Sparse World Model with Horizon-Conditioned Memory Retrieval for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32745)
*Xinyang Jia,Yuqi Zhang,Jiakai Wang,Yahu Yang,Guandong Pan,Chao Tong,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemAnchorWorld：通过显式检索历史几何-语义证据的分层双路径记忆锚点机制，缓解稀疏4D语义占据预测中的误差累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏世界模型仅靠场景状态隐式携带历史信息，长时程预测中误差逐步传播累积，导致几何与语义漂移。

Method: 受von Restorff效应启发，提出预测时刻条件化的分层双路径记忆锚点检索：基于历史信息的"有效年龄"与语义兼容性，结合query级候选选择与点级记忆锚点检索；并引入点级残差适配器，由任务与预测时刻双门控，分别控制对语义、占据置信度与空间位置的残差修正。

Result: 在Occ3D-nuScenes上，1-3秒预测范围内平均mIoU 13.55%、平均IoU 22.84%，达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 显式时序证据检索优于隐式状态记忆，可有效抑制长时程稀疏占据预测的误差累积。

Abstract: Sparse world models for 4D semantic occupancy forecasting represent scenes with a small set of evolving queries to efficiently predict future geometry and semantics for autonomous driving. However, existing sparse world models face a bottleneck in using historical temporal evidence for long-horizon forecasting. They rely on scene states to carry historical information implicitly, allowing errors to propagate and accumulate across successive forecasting steps and causing geometric and semantic deviations. To address this limitation, we present MemAnchorWorld, a sparse occupancy world model that explicitly retrieves relevant historical geometric and semantic evidence for 4D semantic occupancy forecasting. Inspired by the von Restorff effect, we introduce a forecast-time-conditioned hierarchical dual-path memory-anchor retrieval mechanism. Guided by the effective age and semantic compatibility of historical information, it combines query-level candidate selection with point-level memory-anchor retrieval to provide historical evidence for sparse points at different forecast times. To better use memory anchors and existing features, a point-level residual adapter jointly gated by the task and forecast time separately controls residual corrections to semantics, occupancy confidence, and spatial positions. On Occ3D-nuScenes, MemAnchorWorld achieves an average mIoU of 13.55% and an average IoU of 22.84% across the 1-3 s forecasting horizons, attaining state-of-the-art performance on both this http URL page: this https URL

</details>


### [209] [Beyond Static Semantics: Instance-Adaptive Vision-Language Alignment for Building Damage Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32777)
*Hang Yin,Jiepan Li,Hongruixuan Chen,Guangyi Yang,Wei He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一视觉-语言框架LAMIA-IBDA，将共享的损坏语义逐步接地到局部双时相观测与单体建筑，实现实例级建筑损坏评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一损坏等级在不同单体建筑上视觉表征差异大；文本可提供显式物理损坏描述作为类别标签之外的互补语义，但现有视觉-语言方法未解决“语义如何适配局部双时相证据”以及“如何作为实例级参考用于严重程度判别”。

Method: 自适应文本-视觉对齐（ATVA）：在可靠性感知注意力监督下，联合上下文化多种损坏描述与双时相视觉特征，实现证据自适应语义交互；动态实例-原型接地（DIPG）：将上下文化语义与单体建筑区域关联，构建实例自适应原型用于细粒度损坏分类；实例级描述偏好额外监督融合阶段注意力，耦合语义适配与实例判别。

Result: 在xBD与BRIGHT数据集上，光学-光学与光学-SAR两种设置均取得SOTA性能；代码开源。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言语义的分层接地（全局语义→局部双时相证据→实例原型）是提升实例级建筑损坏评估的有效路径，并可跨模态（光学/SAR）推广。

Abstract: Instance-level building damage assessment (IBDA) from bi-temporal remote sensing imagery remains challenging, as the same damage grade may exhibit diverse visual manifestations across individual buildings. Natural language offers explicit descriptions of physical damage, providing complementary semantic cues beyond discrete category labels. However, existing vision--language approaches have not fully addressed how damage semantics should adapt to local bi-temporal evidence and subsequently serve as instance-specific references for severity discrimination. To bridge this gap, we propose LAMIA-IBDA, a unified vision--language framework that progressively grounds shared damage semantics in local observations and individual buildings. Specifically, Adaptive Text-Visual Alignment (ATVA) enables evidence-adaptive semantic interaction by jointly contextualizing multiple damage descriptions and bi-temporal visual features under reliability-aware attention supervision. Dynamic Instance-Prototype Grounding (DIPG) further associates these contextualized semantics with individual building regions to construct instance-adaptive prototypes for fine-grained damage classification. Instance-level description preferences additionally supervise fusion-stage attention, linking semantic adaptation with instance discrimination. Extensive experiments on xBD and BRIGHT demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across optical--optical and optical--SAR settings. The source code is publicly available this https URL .

</details>


### [210] [Back to Raw Motion: Latent-Free Diffusion Transformers for Text-to-Motion and Exact Conditional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32793)
*Yun Chen,Jeonghyeok Do,Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RawMo 提出在原始运动空间（而非学习型 codec 隐空间）中做文本到动作生成的像素扩散式框架，含 Motion Diffusion Transformer (MiT) 与高噪声文本-动作对齐损失 HiNCE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型运动 codec 虽能压缩潜表示提升生成效率，但会丢失细节且无法严格保持给定条件；直接在原始数据空间生成可避免 codec 引入的误差。受像素扩散在图像生成中取得高质量、可接受算力的启发，作者探索将像素扩散思想迁移到动作生成。

Method: 先在像素扩散上做预研以验证可行性，再提出 RawMo：直接在 raw motion space 上做文本条件扩散。核心为 Motion Diffusion Transformer (MiT)，建模局部骨骼结构与全身动力学，并跨帧细化以提升时序一致性；另设计高噪声文本-动作对齐损失 HiNCE，在无需中间运动解码的情况下增强文本一致性；单一条件模型支持多类运动条件任务，并可精确保持给定运动条件。

Result: 在 HumanML3D 与 KIT-ML 上，RawMo 在对比方法中取得最佳文本对齐效果，运动质量具竞争力；消融实验验证了 MiT 与 HiNCE 的有效性。

Conclusion: 将文本到动作生成迁移至原始运动空间的扩散范式可行，避免 codec 带来的细节损失与条件不精确问题，且单一模型可统一支持多种运动条件任务，为运动生成提供新范式。

Abstract: As generative models tackle larger and more complex datasets, learned codecs enable efficient generation through compact latent representations, but may lose details and cannot guarantee exact preservation of given conditions. Generating directly in the original data space avoids errors introduced by the codec. Recent advances in image generation demonstrate that pixel diffusion can produce high-quality samples at practical computational costs. Inspired by these advances, we conduct a preliminary study of pixel diffusion, demonstrating its potential for adaptation to motion generation. Building on these findings, we present RawMo, a framework that brings text-to-motion generation to raw motion space. Our Motion Diffusion Transformer (MiT) models local skeletal structures and whole-body dynamics, while refining motion across frames to improve temporal consistency. Working directly in raw motion space further allows our high-noise text-motion alignment loss (HiNCE) to improve text consistency without intermediate motion decoding. RawMo supports multiple motion-conditioning tasks with a single conditional model while preserving given motion conditions exactly. Experiments on HumanML3D and KIT-ML show the best text alignment among compared methods and competitive motion quality, with ablations confirming the benefits of MiT and HiNCE.

</details>


### [211] [SpatialArena: Where Do Geometric Foundation Models Stand?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32816)
*Yu Chen,Haosen Yang,Su Ee Tan,Zhensong Zhang,Jifei Song,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialArena 是一个统一的开放评测平台，用一致的指标、稳健且确定性的对齐方式评测几何基础模型（单目深度、前馈 3D/4D 重建、SLAM 等 5 类任务），覆盖 23 个公开数据集共 418,929 张图像及自采数据，评测 52 个模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何基础模型评测不可靠：各论文的采样帧、分辨率、测试划分不同导致数字不可比；上尺度预测所需的对齐方式差异可能超过模型间差距（错误空间拟合深度使误差近 3 倍，标准轨迹对齐低估失败方法误差达 2.6 倍）；且评测少覆盖长序列、动态场景与航拍视角。

Method: 构建 SpatialArena 评测竞技场：统一指标与规则，采用稳健、确定性的对齐协议；涵盖单目深度到 SLAM 五个任务，聚合 23 个公开数据集（室内、室外、航拍、物体中心、动态场景）及自有带精确位姿与深度采集的数据。

Result: 评测 52 个模型发现：单目深度在公开基准上接近饱和但在大规模非受控场景远未饱和；进展主要来自跨帧跨视角一致性，前馈 3D/4D 模型已匹配或超过单目专家模型，并超越视频深度模型。

Conclusion: SpatialArena 提供了统一、稳健、可复现的几何模型评测标准，揭示当前 3D 感知的真实进展方向在于多视图一致性而非单帧精度；基准、工具箱与排行榜将公开。

Abstract: 3D perception is shifting from task-specific pipelines to geometric foundation models: monocular depth networks, feed-forward 3D/4D reconstruction models, and vision--language models that predict geometry. However, their progress cannot be measured reliably. Published numbers are not comparable, because every paper samples frames, sets resolutions, and selects test splits differently. They are not robust because the alignment required for every up-to-scale prediction can outweigh the gap between models: fitting depth in the wrong space nearly triples its error, and standard trajectory alignment understates the error of failing methods by up to $2.6\times$. And they rarely cover long recordings, dynamic scenes, or aerial views. We present SpatialArena, an open arena that scores geometric models with the same metrics and rules, with robust and deterministic alignment. SpatialArena spans five tasks, from monocular depth to SLAM. It covers 23 public datasets with 418,929 images across indoor, outdoor, aerial, object-centric, and dynamic scenes, together with Spatial Arena, our own captures with accurate poses and depth. Evaluating 52 models, we find that single-image depth is close to saturation on public benchmarks but not on large, uncontrolled scenes, and that progress now comes from consistency across frames and views, where feed-forward 3D/4D models match or surpass monocular specialists and overtake video depth models. The benchmark, toolbox, and leaderboard will be publicly released.

</details>


### [212] [Beyond Image-Text Pairs: Aligning Semantic Changes for Fine-Grained Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32817)
*Yujie Sun,Zeqing Wang,Shuang Liang,Zhenlin Zhao,Kunyu Zhang,Xiaowu Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语义差异对齐(SDA)后训练损失，对齐图文表示变化方向，并构建VarFamily-40K受控变化数据集，提升组合理解与下游分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图文对齐训练范式对"跨模态语义变化如何对应"监督不足，表征缺乏细粒度判别性，限制VLM的组合理解能力。

Method: 构造受控视觉/文本变化的图文对，提出SDA损失使对应视觉与文本表示的变化方向对齐；与成对sigmoid匹配联合训练，兼顾原始对应关系与语义变化；配套VarFamily-40K数据集。

Result: 实验显示组合理解与下游分类性能稳定提升；将适配后的视觉编码器接入VLM后，多模态任务进一步获益。

Conclusion: 以表示差分方向为监督信号是提升跨模态语义敏感性的有效后训练手段，可迁移至VLM视觉编码器。

Abstract: Image-text alignment serves as a foundation for modern vision-language models (VLMs), yet prevailing training paradigms provide limited supervision for learning how semantic changes correspond across modalities. To address this limitation, we propose a post-training method that learns cross-modal semantic change correspondences through controlled visual and textual variations. Specifically, we introduce a Semantic Difference Alignment (SDA) loss which can align the directions of corresponding visual and textual representation changes. We combine our proposed SDA with pairwise sigmoid matching to jointly learn image-text correspondences and cross-modal semantic changes, encouraging more fine-grained and discriminative visual representations. To support this learning, we construct VarFamily-40K, a dataset of image-text pairs with controlled cross-modal semantic variations. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in compositional understanding and downstream classification performance, alongside further gains on multimodal tasks when the adapted visual encoder is integrated into VLMs.

</details>


### [213] [PhysMend: Verification-Guided Program Refinement for Physics-Aware Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32821)
*Meilong Xu,Ziyi Zhao,Bhagyashree Puranik,Shayda Moezzi,Yixiao Zhang,Kushan Thakkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysMend：免训练框架，将目标事件表示为可编辑物理程序（物体/事件/关系+可调参数），在仿真rollout与深度控制（depth control）上验证与修复，而非在生成视频上打分；混合验证器检查语义一致性、物理合理性、求解器稳定性与可见性，VLM仅判语义，失败项定位可修字段并按违规度量做有界参数更新，仅通过的深度控制送入生成器（每输入仅一次生成）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型的交互常违反物理（球穿过沙面、沙面不留坑）。事后VLM打分无法定位该改哪个物体/参数且每次修正都要重新生成；仿真流水线又用固定阶段，仿真误差会传递到视频。

Method: 可编辑物理程序（grounded对象、事件、必需关系）+可调执行参数；在仿真rollout与条件深度视频上做定量违规测量与定向编辑，合成前快速复验；混合验证器覆盖语义事件一致性、物理合理性、求解器稳定性、深度可见性；VLM判断限定于语义，不能覆盖物理测量失败；每项失败检查定位可修字段，违规量缩放参数的受限更新；仅接受的深度控制进入生成器，一次生成。

Result: 在Physics-IQ、PhyGenBench、VBench-2.0上跨多种生成器backbone均有显著提升；如Wan-VACE的Physics-IQ总分由27.33升至39.40。

Conclusion: 在生成前于仿真与深度控制层面做验证引导的程序精修，可低成本、可解释地缓解幻觉与物理违规，且不依赖重新训练或多次合成，具备跨backbone的可移植性。

Abstract: Video generative models synthesize realistic scenes, yet their interactions often violate physics. A ball dropped onto sand should displace grains and settle into a crater, whereas a generated video may show it passing through the surface or leaving the sand flat. Post-hoc methods typically score generated videos with a vision-language model (VLM), but such scores offer limited guidance on which object or parameter to modify, and each correction costs another synthesis. Simulation-based pipelines instead run fixed stages before synthesis, so simulation errors reach the video. To address these limitations, we present PhysMend, a training-free framework for verification-guided program refinement. PhysMend describes the target event as an editable physical program of grounded objects, events, and required relations, paired with tunable execution parameters. Our key insight is to verify and repair the event on its simulated rollout and on the depth video that conditions the generator (depth control) rather than on generated videos, enabling quantitative violation measurement, targeted editing, and fast re-verification before synthesis. A hybrid verifier evaluates semantic event consistency, physical plausibility, solver stability, and visibility in the depth control. VLM judgments are confined to event semantics and cannot override failed physical measurements, which limits the impact of VLM hallucinations. Each failed check localizes the program fields eligible for repair, and its measured violation scales a bounded update of their parameters. Only accepted depth control reaches the generator, which runs once per input. Experiments on the commonly used benchmarks Physics-IQ, PhyGenBench, and VBench-2.0 demonstrate substantial gains across different generator backbones; for example, PhysMend raises the overall Physics-IQ score of Wan-VACE from 27.33 to 39.40.

</details>


### [214] [Reliable and grounded reporting of any computed tomography scan with FORA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32823)
*Sezgin Er,Utku Turkbey,Alperen Gur,Omer Faruk Durugol,Suprosanna Shit,Murong Xu,Julia Wolleb,Jiayi Wang,Xiaoliang Wang,Hadrien Reynaud,Teodora Spasojevic,Dong Yang,Andriy Myronenko,Pengfei Guo,Yufan He,Can Zhao,Marc Edgar,Andreas Maier,Michael Moor,Mehmet Kemal Ozdemir,Daguang Xu,Bernhard Kainz,Bjoern Menze,Ibrahim Ethem Hamamci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FORA：以报告弱监督训练的通用CT大模型，可识别并空间定位1,028个层级化放射学概念，覆盖28种CT协议并生成报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CT人工智能系统多局限于特定解剖区域、检查类型或预定义异常，缺乏全面且具有空间依据的解读能力，因此需要单一模型覆盖多协议、多异常并具备定位与报告生成能力。

Method: 在304,962例常规CT检查上以伴随报告为弱监督，无需病灶级标注；架构为多实例学习＋可解释概念瓶颈，串联空间证据、放射学发现与报告生成；先在胸片上验证架构，再训练三维CT模型。

Result: 36,619例留出CT上概念识别macro-AUROC达0.918，比18个现有CT模型中最强的高0.21；三个公开基准排名第一（自有测试集AUROC 0.907 vs Merlin 0.826，且未用该机构数据训练）；肺栓塞、肾脏肿块、肋骨骨折定位基准中，最强空间证据落在标注病灶内的比例最高为随机水平的348倍；报告生成macro平均发现召回率超过最强现有生成器三倍以上。

Conclusion: 单一模型可在12类CT检查上同时实现广谱异常识别、空间定位与报告生成，仅依赖常规报告而无病灶级监督，展示了弱监督概念瓶颈范式的通用性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems for computed tomography (CT) remain largely restricted to specific anatomical regions, examination types or predefined abnormalities, limiting their ability to provide comprehensive and spatially grounded interpretations. Here we present FORA, a general-purpose CT model that recognizes and spatially localizes 1,028 hierarchically organized radiological concepts and generates reports across the 28 CT protocols represented in our cohort. Trained on 304,962 routine CT examinations supervised by their accompanying reports, FORA learns to localize abnormalities without manually annotated lesions. Its architecture combines multiple-instance learning with an interpretable concept bottleneck that connects spatial evidence, radiological findings and report generation. We first developed and benchmarked the architecture on chest radiographs, achieving state-of-the-art performance, before training a separate model on three-dimensional CT examinations. On 36,619 held-out CT examinations, FORA achieved a macro area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.918 for radiological concept recognition, exceeding the strongest of 18 existing CT models by 0.21. FORA also ranked first on three public benchmarks, outperforming Merlin on its own test set without training on that institution's data (AUROC 0.907 versus 0.826). On independent localization benchmarks for pulmonary emboli, renal masses and rib fractures, FORA's strongest spatial evidence fell within annotated lesions at rates up to 348 times greater than chance. FORA achieved more than threefold higher macro-averaged finding recall than the strongest existing report generator. These results show that a single model can combine broad abnormality recognition, spatial localization and report generation across 12 CT examination types, using routine reports without lesion-level supervision.

</details>


### [215] [EEG-World: Context-Conditioned Dynamics of the Watching Brain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32825)
*Junxiang Zhang,Jiayu Zuo,Wenhao Ye,Junfeng Xia,Xiang Chen,Mo Wang,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EEG-World: 循环世界模型用6秒历史EEG+后续视听刺激预测10秒EEG频带功率动态，可迁移到未见个体


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑编码模型只从视听输入预测神经响应，忽略ongoing brain activity对个体响应的影响

Method: 基于连续EEG预训练的循环世界模型，学习共享神经动力学，通过轻量subject-specific readout适配个体；结合视频特征(CLIP/DINOv2/V-JEPA 2)做刺激条件预测与状态/刺激可预测性分解

Result: 78名被试2584小时EEG上，预测相关0.261 vs 最强history+video线性基线0.233，预测时域长3.3秒；24人中23人迁移优于基线；V-JEPA 2使clip-locked预测较CLIP+DINOv2提升35%

Conclusion: 可迁移神经动力学对个体EEG预测有价值，需区分状态驱动与刺激驱动的可预测性

Abstract: Brain encoding models predict neural responses from audiovisual input, but typically overlook how ongoing brain activity shapes an individual's response. We introduce EEG-World, a recurrent world model that forecasts 10 seconds of EEG band-power dynamics from 6 seconds of preceding EEG and the subsequent audiovisual stimulus. Pretrained on continuous EEG, EEG-World learns shared neural dynamics and adapts to unseen individuals through lightweight subject-specific readouts. Using 2,584 hours of EEG from 78 participants, we show that nonlinear recurrent forecasting surpasses strong history-aware linear baselines at scale. EEG-World achieves a forecasting correlation of 0.261 versus 0.233 for the strongest history-and-video linear model, with a 3.3-second forecasting-horizon advantage. Its learned dynamics transfer to unseen participants, outperforming the linear baseline in 23 of 24 individuals after calibration. While ongoing EEG history accounts for most predictive performance, controlled stimulus analyses reveal an additional video-dependent component. This component also enables evaluation of visual representations, with V-JEPA 2 improving clip-locked prediction by 35% over combined CLIP and DINOv2 features. Our results demonstrate the value of transferable neural dynamics for individual EEG forecasting and highlight the importance of separating state-driven from stimulus-driven predictability.

</details>


### [216] [SAVERA: Bridging Frozen Vision and Language Models via Sparse Alignment and Visual Evidence Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32828)
*Jiajun Shen,Chuancheng Shi,Jiayi Lyu,Yanpeng Sun,Fei Shen,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAVERA 提出一种在冻结视觉与语言模型之间进行“共享稀疏空间双向对齐 + 提示引导残差融合”的框架，通过 SPARE、BEAM、PRIVA 三模块缓解跨模态对齐中视觉信息丢失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立预训练的视觉与语言模型可能共享对同一世界的结构化知识，但如何在对齐跨模态特征的同时，保留语言适配前后视觉信息的可访问性，仍是一个关键难题。

Method: （1）SPARE：共享空间投影、激活重建与编码，用共享软门控稀疏编码表示原始视觉、适配后视觉与文本特征，并以分支专属重建保留各自信息；（2）BEAM：双向跨模态对齐模块，在样本级与神经元级同时对齐适配视觉特征与文本特征，建立细粒度对应；（3）PRIVA：提示条件化的视觉与适配特征残差融合，借助门控与注意力建模视觉—语言交互，并以残差方式整合原始与适配表示的互补信息。整体视觉与语言骨干保持冻结。

Result: 在图像描述与视觉问答任务上取得有竞争力的性能，同时降低物体幻觉。

Conclusion: 冻结多模态骨干 + 稀疏共享空间对齐 + 提示引导残差融合是提升跨模态对齐质量、抑制幻觉的有效轻量路线；但实验局限于图像描述与VQA，尚缺机器人与具身场景验证。

Abstract: Independently pretrained vision and language models may learn shared structures of the same underlying world, providing a foundation for combining them to build multimodal capabilities. However, a key challenge remains in aligning cross-modal feature representations while preserving access to visual information before and after language adaptation. To this end, we propose sparse alignment with visual evidence-guided residual adaptation (SAVERA), a framework that combines frozen vision and language models through bidirectional alignment in a shared sparse space and prompt-guided residual fusion. Specifically, we first present shared-space projection, activation reconstruction and encoding (SPARE), which represents original visual, adapted visual, and textual features through shared soft-gated sparse encoding, with branch-specific reconstruction to preserve their respective information. Second, we devise bridge--text embedding and activation matching (BEAM), a bidirectional cross-modal alignment module that aligns adapted visual and textual features at both the sample and neuron levels, establishing fine-grained cross-modal correspondence. Finally, we introduce prompt-conditioned residual integration of vision and adapted features (PRIVA), a prompt-guided residual fusion module that models vision--language interactions through gating and attention and integrates complementary information from original and adapted representations through residual fusion. Extensive experiments show that SAVERA achieves competitive performance on image captioning and visual question answering while reducing object hallucination.

</details>


### [217] [Aqua2CAD : Observation-Guided Point Cloud Correction and Completion for CAD Reconstruction from Underwater Stereo Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32835)
*Weizhao Huang,Jiayi Zhang,Pangkit Fong,Chongrong Fang,Jianping He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Aqua2CAD 利用水下双目多视角射线证据做视角条件校正与质量过滤，并以其条件化学习形状先验，在原始度量坐标系中补全缺失曲面，从而恢复可编辑 CAD 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下双目重建得到的点云存在位移点与缺失区域；而原始视角中仍保留跨视角分歧与射线证据，这些信息在点云融合阶段被丢弃，导致难以恢复准确、完整且可编辑的 CAD 几何。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段做视角条件校正（view-conditioned correction）与质量过滤，建立可靠几何支撑；第二阶段以多视角观测条件化学习形状先验，在原始度量坐标系中推断缺失表面。恢复后点云用于曲面拟合与实体装配，面组经人工修订完成可编辑 CAD。

Result: 在基于 OceanSim 的合成基准上，点云 Chamfer 距离较双目重建降低 60%；相较匹配的纯点云补全骨干，5 mm 阈值下缺失区域恢复率由 16.27% 提升至 24.16%；共享 CAD 后端下外部接受率从 6/19 提升至 14/19。真实水下拍摄上定性恢复出六件 3D 打印物体的主体结构。

Conclusion: 保留并利用多视角射线证据（而非仅依赖融合点云）是提升水下 CAD 反演完整性与可编辑性的关键；合成基准上定量优势明显，但真实场景仅给出定性结果，缺乏真实标注定量评估。

Abstract: Recovering editable computer-aided design (CAD) models from underwater stereo images requires accurate and complete geometry, yet stereo reconstruction produces displaced points and missing regions. The original views retain cross-view disagreement and ray evidence lost during point-cloud fusion. Aqua2CAD exploits this evidence in two stages: view-conditioned correction and quality filtering establish reliable geometric support, while multi-view observations condition a learned shape prior to infer missing surfaces in the original metric frame. The recovered points support surface fitting and solid assembly with manually revised face groups. On an OceanSim-based synthetic benchmark, Aqua2CAD reduces point-cloud Chamfer distance by 60% relative to the stereo reconstruction. Compared with a matched point-only completion backbone, it improves missing-region recovery at 5,mm from 16.27% to 24.16%. With a shared CAD backend, exterior acceptance increases from 6/19 to 14/19. Qualitative results on selected real underwater captures demonstrate recovery of the main structures of six 3D-printed objects.

</details>


### [218] [DOPER: Deep Object Pose Estimation Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32846)
*Hugo Hadfield,Alperen Degirmenci,Jonathan Tremblay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DOPER：共享视觉骨干的RGB单目6D位姿估计框架，统一检测、分割与关键点预测，经PnP求解位姿；自合成训练过渡到真实数据弱监督+多视图几何约束，推理时用热图细化拟合刚体位姿，在多个数据集上取得SOTA检测AP并实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-only 6D位姿估计需兼顾实时性与对已知物体的泛化，同时避免依赖深度输入或额外的学习式位姿精化网络；合成到真实的域差异与欠约束CAD模型也是关键障碍。

Method: 多任务共享骨干（检测、实例分割、关键点预测）+PnP；合成数据预训练后用校准多视图观测的几何约束做弱监督适配；推理端可选关键点热图分布上的刚体位姿拟合以消解对应歧义；并逐步细化物体表示与位姿估计器。

Result: HANDAL、HOPEv2、HOT3D、T-LESS上单/多视图配置取得SOTA检测AP；SPEED+与SHIRT上验证光照变化适应与时序位姿精度；单GB300单模型35 Hz实时。

Conclusion: 联合学习目标检测与位姿关键点，结合真实观测与多视图几何约束，可在纯RGB条件下实现实时且SOTA的6D位姿估计。

Abstract: We present DOPER, an RGB pose estimation framework that shares a visual backbone across object detection, segmentation, and keypoint prediction to recover the 6D pose of known objects through Perspective-n-Point. Starting from synthetic training, DOPER adapts to real observations using partial supervision while retaining synthetic keypoint labels. Calibrated multi-view observations provide geometric constraints for keypoint supervision. During inference, optional heatmap refinement fits a rigid pose directly to the predicted keypoint distributions, resolving ambiguous correspondences without depth input or a learned pose refiner. Starting from underspecified CAD models, DOPER progressively refines the object representation and pose estimator. Results on SPEED+ and SHIRT further demonstrate effective adaptation to illumination changes and accurate temporal pose estimation. Across the evaluated single-view and multi-view configurations, DOPER achieves state-of-the-art detection AP results on HANDAL, HOPEv2, HOT3D, and T-LESS while using only RGB and running in real time (35 Hz per object model on one GB300). These results demonstrate the effectiveness of jointly learning detection and pose keypoints, with real observations and geometric constraints.

</details>


### [219] [CityRecast: Suppressing Persistent Artifacts in Generative Satellite 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32863)
*Shuo Cui,Yingyan Hou,Wanxuan Lu,Hongfeng Yu,Daobing Zhang,Xian Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CityRecast：通过高斯场景修复与监督校准，抑制卫星3D重建在低仰角渲染中的鬼影/条纹/模糊伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星影像观测稀疏、跨时相变化大，现有生成先验方法虽能补全细节，但在高斯场景中残留鬼影、条纹与模糊，低仰角渲染尤为明显。

Method: (1) 多角度预热约束高斯空间范围以减少初始鬼影；(2) 表面引导替换在保持已有外观的前提下继续修复高斯分布；(3) 观测校准缓解多时相观测干扰，提供更可靠监督；(4) 联合精修减少生成视角间冲突。

Result: 在DFC2019与IARPA上低仰角渲染伪影减少且保持卫星视角保真度；相较Skyfall-GS，FID-CLIP降低25.3%/51.8%，MUSIQ提升14.54/24.95分。

Conclusion: 面向卫星斜视重建的伪影修复与监督校准流程有效，可用于城市级3D高斯地表重建，但缺少开源与实时性、跨城泛化证据。

Abstract: Satellite-based 3D reconstruction is limited by sparse observations and changes across acquisition dates. Existing methods use generative priors to recover missing visual detail, but ghosting, streaking, and blur persist in their Gaussian scenes. These artifacts become particularly apparent in low-elevation renderings. We introduce CityRecast to suppress these artifacts through Gaussian scene repair and supervision calibration. To correct the Gaussian distributions that produce these artifacts, we first use multi-angle warm-up to constrain their spatial extent. This reduces the initial ghosting carried into progressive refinement. Surface-guided replacement then continues to repair the Gaussian distribution while preserving existing appearance. To provide more reliable supervision for this process, observation calibration mitigates interference from multi-temporal observations. Joint refinement further reduces conflicts among generated views. Experiments on DFC2019 and IARPA demonstrate reduced artifacts in low-elevation renderings while maintaining satellite-view fidelity. Compared with Skyfall-GS, CityRecast reduces FID-CLIP by 25.3% on DFC2019 and 51.8% on IARPA. MUSIQ increases by 14.54 and 24.95 points, respectively.

</details>


### [220] [MemHand: Memory-Enhanced World-Space Hand Motion Reconstruction from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32876)
*Junyi Ma,Zhangshuo Qi,Erhang Zhang,Mingjie Li,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemHand 提出记忆增强的自我中心视频世界坐标双手运动重建框架，用短期事件记忆补充遮挡/出视野帧的时序上下文，用长期尺度记忆在有限状态内维护全片最可靠观测作为度量尺度锚点，在 ARCTIC/HOT3D 上显著超越 StableHand。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自我中心视频恢复连续世界坐标双手运动对机器人策略迁移、AR 等应用至关重要。仅逐帧重建不足：遮挡或手离开视野时，重建所需上下文跨时间分布；现有方法依赖固定长度时序窗口，无法保证每帧都获得足以恢复准确位姿与度量尺度的上下文。

Method: MemHand 为世界坐标手部运动重建框架，维护两种互补记忆：(1) 短期事件记忆，在每个遮挡/出视野事件附近保留观测良好手部的上下文，并供给视觉证据弱或缺失的帧；(2) 长期尺度记忆，利用度量尺度满足全片一致（尺度不变）的先验，在有限状态中保留全视频最可靠的观测作为尺度锚点。二者协同为每帧提供适配的时序与尺度上下文。

Result: 在含严重遮挡与频繁出视野区间的基准上取得 SOTA 精度，并对未见域泛化良好；相对 StableHand，ARCTIC 上 W-MPJPE 降低 57%，HOT3D 上降低 70%，输出连续且度量准确的世界坐标手部位姿与轨迹。代码与模型将公开。

Conclusion: 以事件级短期记忆与有界长期尺度记忆替代固定窗口时序上下文，是提升自我中心双手世界坐标重建在遮挡与出视野场景下鲁棒性与度量精度的有效方案。

Abstract: Recovering the continuous world-space motion of both hands from egocentric video is essential for applications such as human-to-robot policy transfer and augmented reality. Achieving this goes beyond reconstructing the hands from each individual frame, as the context that a frame needs is often spread over time, especially around occlusions or when a hand moves out of sight. Recent works have explored how to exploit temporal context, yet they mostly draw it from fixed-length windows. Such windows cannot guarantee that every frame has the context required to reconstruct the hands in world space with accurate poses and metric scale. To address this limitation, we introduce MemHand, a memory-enhanced framework for world-space hand motion reconstruction. MemHand maintains two complementary forms of memory. The Short-Term Event Memory keeps the context of the well-observed hand around each occlusion or out-of-sight event and supplies it to frames with weak or missing visual evidence. In addition, since the metric scale holds over the whole recording, the Long-Term Scale Memory retains the most reliable observations across the video as scale anchors within a bounded state. Across benchmarks featuring severe occlusions and frequent out-of-sight intervals, MemHand achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and generalizes well to unseen domains. Benefiting from the synergy of both memories, it cuts W-MPJPE by 57\% on ARCTIC and 70\% on HOT3D relative to StableHand, delivering continuous and metrically accurate world-space hand poses and trajectories. We will release our code and models at the project page: this https URL

</details>


### [221] [Simultaneously Boosting Efficiency and Capability for Linear Attention via Adaptive Channel Compression and Activation Purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32877)
*Senqi Guan,Xianchao Zhang,Moyan Tian,Linlin Zong,Wenxin Liang,Xinyue Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ACCAP-LA轻量线性注意力：在QKV投影中嵌入自适应通道压缩与激活纯化(ACCAP)，并融合聚焦核函数与自门控模块，以缓解ViT效率-能力权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力二次复杂度是ViT核心瓶颈；现有softmax注意力与线性注意力优化往往牺牲表征能力，导致性能必然下降，形成效率-能力两难。

Method: 首次将ACCAP机制嵌入线性注意力的QKV投影，使模型在降维通道计算时仍保留高维特征信息；进一步在ACCAP中融合聚焦核函数与自门控模块，构成轻量线性注意力模块ACCAP-LA。

Result: 理论分析显示相比标准线性注意力参数减少79.17%、FLOPs减少77.5%；应用于ViT在ImageNet-1K取得79.4% Top-1、仅5.8M参数；跨架构兼容性强，使EfficientViT-b1与PVT-T分别提升3.3%和3.4%。

Conclusion: 通过通道压缩-激活纯化与核函数、自门控的协同设计，可在显著降低注意力计算与参数开销的同时保持甚至提升分类性能，并具备良好的跨架构迁移性。

Abstract: Self-attention's quadratic complexity constitutes a core bottleneck for Vision Transformers (ViTs). Existing efficiency-oriented optimizations, including softmax-based and linear attention (LA) variants, generally compromise model representational capacity, leading to inevitable performance degradation. We propose a novel linear attention which alleviates this efficiency-capability dilemma. For the first time, we embed an Adaptive Channel Compression and Activation Purification (ACCAP) mechanism into the QKV projection of linear attention, which enables the model to retain high-dimensional feature information while performing computations with reduced channels. We further fuse a focused kernel function and a self-gating module into ACCAP to construct a lightweight linear attention module (ACCAP-LA). Our theoretical analysis shows that, compared to standard linear attention, ACCAP-LA reduces the attention module's parameter count by 79.17% and FLOPs by 77.5%. Extensive experiments demonstrate ACCAP-LA's effectiveness: when applied to ViT, it achieves 79.4% Top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1K with only 5.8M parameters, and more notably, it boasts strong cross-architecture compatibility, boosting EfficientViT-b1 and PVT-T by 3.3% and 3.4% respectively.

</details>


### [222] [3D Tracking Any Point with Events and Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32883)
*Roberto Pellerito,Zijia Dai,Laurent Kneip,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrackAnyMotion 融合单目 RGB 帧与事件相机数据，在无 RGB 帧的时刻也能跟踪任意点的 2D/3D 轨迹，并显式解码独立运动物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB 帧模糊或帧间隔过大时，快速运动点难以在 3D 中被跟踪；事件相机记录帧间亮度变化，可填补帧间时间空档。

Method: 以给定场景几何与相机运动计算静态点的预期运动作为参考，识别独立运动物体；独立运动解码器结合几何运动线索、事件引导与 2D 轨迹，并将预测回馈到迭代的 2D/3D 精化中；同时构建合成 RGB-事件数据集 HyperEvent，含高频 3D 轨迹、几何与独立运动标签。

Result: 在 HyperEvent 基准的 RGB 时间戳处，3D 与 2D 端点误差分别降低 70% 和 32%。

Conclusion: 事件-RGB 融合为高速点跟踪与独立运动分割提供了可行路径，但主要证据来自合成数据集，真实场景鲁棒性仍需验证。

Abstract: Tracking a fast-moving point in 3D is prohibitive when RGB frames are blurred or too far apart to reveal its motion. Event cameras record brightness changes between frames, providing measurements inside these temporal gaps. We introduce TrackAnyMotion, which combines monocular RGB frames and events to track arbitrary points in 2D and 3D, including at times when no RGB frame is available. The motion expected for a static point, given scene geometry and camera motion, provides a reference for identifying independently moving objects. An independent-motion decoder combines geometric motion cues with event guidance and 2D tracks, feeding its predictions back into iterative 2D and 3D refinement. We also introduce HyperEvent, a synthetic RGB--Event dataset with high-frequency 3D trajectories, geometry, and independent-motion labels. At RGB frame timestamps on this benchmark, TrackAnyMotion reduces 3D and 2D endpoint errors by 70\% and 32\%, respectively.

</details>


### [223] [Biomechanically Grounded Prediction of Selective Motor Control and Clinical Outcomes from Infant Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32890)
*Divya Joshi,Ruize Yang,Colleen Peyton,R. James Cotton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BB3D：将单目婴儿视频转换为三维关节角轨迹的生物力学管线，并用时空Transformer从轨迹预测BabyOSCAR选择性运动控制评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑瘫（CP）早期识别依赖GMA、BabyOSCAR等人工观察评分，需专业评分员且耗时；现有自动化方法基于二维关键点手工特征，只描述像平面运动而非各关节的三维行为。

Method: 适配婴儿的生物力学管线，从单摄像机视频估计关节中心与标记点三维位置并导出关节角轨迹；以17名婴儿的多视角无标记动捕为参考做Procrustes对齐评估；用1k+单目录制的关节角轨迹预训练时空Transformer，在350名婴儿的550段视频上微调，逐项回归BabyOSCAR条目与总分。

Result: 对照多视角参考，关节中心中位误差17.5 mm、标记点20.4 mm；留出集上各条目平均站点内AUC 0.79，总分Spearman 0.86；未用诊断标签训练，预测总分区分CP的AUC 0.86，优于复现的二维特征分类器（0.67），低于专家评分员（0.97）；预测分数对运动功能与身体分布携带信息，但弱于专家评分。

Conclusion: 生物力学基础的单目三维表征可支撑可扩展、关节级别的婴儿期选择性运动控制评估，为CP早期筛查提供可自动化的量化路径。

Abstract: Early identification of cerebral palsy (CP) relies on observational assessments of spontaneous infant movement, such as the General Movement Assessment (GMA) and the Baby Observational Selective Control AppRaisal (BabyOSCAR), which require trained raters and substantial scoring time. Existing automated approaches derive hand-engineered features from two-dimensional keypoints, which describe image-plane motion rather than the three-dimensional behavior of individual joints. We present Baby Biomechanical 3D Body (BB3D), an infant-adapted, biomechanical pipeline that estimates joint-angle trajectories from single-camera video. Against a multi-view markerless motion capture reference in 17 infants, BB3D's median Procrustes-aligned error was 17.5~mm for joint centers and 20.4~mm for marker sites. A spatio-temporal transformer, pretrained on BB3D joint-angle trajectories from 1k+ single-camera recordings, and fine-tuned on 550 videos from 350 infants, predicted each individual BabyOSCAR item with a mean within-site AUC of 0.79 and the BabyOSCAR total with a Spearman correlation of 0.86 on held-out recordings. Without being trained on diagnosis, the predicted total separated infants with CP from those without CP with an AUC of 0.86, exceeding a replicated two-dimensional feature classifier (0.67) but below the expert clinician rater's total (0.97). Predicted scores also carried information about functional ability and body distribution, though less than expert clinician scores. These results show that biomechanically grounded representations of monocular video can support scalable, joint-level assessment of selective motor control in infancy

</details>


### [224] [DualSpec: Spectral Reconstruction with Optimized Stereo Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32896)
*Luca Cogo,Marco Buzzelli,Simone Bianco,Raimondo Schettini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DualSpec: 双滤光片立体RGB采集框架，联合优化两个外部光学滤光片与光谱重建网络，从6通道响应中恢复密集光谱，降低光谱角误差36-72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB去光谱存在严重不适定性：不同光谱可产生相同相机响应，纯后处理算法难以消解歧义，因此需在采集端增加互补光谱信息。

Method: 在两台普通RGB相机前各放置一个可学习/可优化的外部光学滤光片，与重建网络端到端联合优化，使六通道响应在光谱上互补；并进行薄膜近似分析验证光学可实现性。

Result: 跨重建架构与光谱范围实验：相较单RGB相机平均光谱角误差降低36-72%，相较商用滤光片立体组合降低11-29%；跨数据集及新建立的视点偏移光谱图像基准上验证泛化性并具备视点失配鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将光谱信息获取前移到采集端（滤光片-网络联合设计）是缓解RGB-to-spectrum歧义的有效途径，且滤光片在物理上可实现、方案具跨数据集泛化性。

Abstract: Spectral imaging captures information that ordinary RGB sensors cannot, but dedicated spectral cameras can be expensive and bulky. Recovering dense spectra from RGB images offers an accessible alternative, but distinct spectra can produce the same camera response, generating an ambiguity that even more advanced recovery algorithms cannot always resolve. Therefore, increasing the spectral information captured at acquisition is essential. We propose DualSpec, a stereo acquisition framework that jointly optimizes two external optical filters and a reconstruction network. Each filter is placed in front of a standard RGB camera, and the two are designed to produce six responses that provide complementary information for the network. Experiments across reconstruction architectures and spectral ranges show that DualSpec reduces mean spectral angle error by 36-72% relative to a single RGB camera and by 11-29% relative to stereo pairs selected from commercial filters. Cross-dataset evaluations, including a new benchmark of spectral images captured from displaced viewpoints, show that the method generalizes and remains robust to viewpoint misalignment, while analyses of thin-film approximations support optical feasibility.

</details>


### [225] [Label leakage through file headers: geometry and batch confounds in public mammography datasets, and a pre-training acceptance gate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32899)
*Achraf Toufah,Rana Saad,Moulay Youssef El Hafidi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对6个公开乳腺X线数据集，用仅含图像几何、全局纹理、子集标识符或单一协变量的预训练对照模型检验诊断标签是否可由非诊断信息恢复，发现KAU-BCMD的几何信息可完美预测BI-RADS、CMMD的子集前缀可预测恶性，说明部分数据集存在捷径泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像数据集的"诊断标签"若能从非诊断信息（图像尺寸、纹理、子集标识、协变量）中被恢复，模型会获得虚高但不可复现、无临床意义的性能，需系统性排查这类捷径泄漏。

Method: 对6个公开乳腺摄影数据集施加仅可见单一非诊断信息的预训练对照：仅图像几何、仅全局纹理、仅子集标识符或仅单一协变量；设定AUC 0.60为停止阈值，并做收缩、重采样及事后加入标识符顺序对照等检验。

Result: KAU-BCMD：几何单独预测BI-RADS在本地副本AUC=1.0000、Kaggle公开档案均值0.979，重采样后仅纹理0.847；CMMD：子集前缀预测恶性0.7753，受限子集过滤了几何(0.5000)与纹理(0.5810)但未通过事后标识符顺序对照(测试AUC 0.9926)；CBIS-DDSM几何超过阈值(20次划分池化均值0.654，但该统计量为事后选定)，官方mass与calcification划分未超阈值；M-SYNTH、INbreast及部分VinDr-Mammo副本通过少量对照。

Conclusion: 若干公开乳腺数据集存在显著的非诊断捷径泄漏，报告的高AUC可能无法反映真实诊断能力；建议将此类"几何/纹理/标识符"对照作为标准数据审计步骤，并公开代码与派生表格以支持可复现评估。

Abstract: A dataset whose diagnostic label can be recovered from non-diagnostic information yields high, reproducible and uninformative model performance. We applied pre-training controls that see only image geometry, global texture, a sub-collection identifier or one covariate to six public mammography datasets, against a stop threshold of AUC 0.60. In KAU-BCMD, geometry alone predicted the BI-RADS class at AUC 1.0000 in a local copy and at a mean of 0.979 in a public Kaggle archive; after resizing, texture alone gave 0.847. In CMMD, the sub-collection prefix predicted malignancy at 0.7753; the restricted sub-collection passed geometry (0.5000) and texture (0.5810) but failed an identifier-order control added post hoc (test AUC 0.9926). CBIS-DDSM exceeded the stop on geometry (pooled mean 0.654 over 20 splits), a verdict statistic chosen post hoc; its official mass and calcification splits did not. M-SYNTH, INbreast and a partial VinDr-Mammo copy passed the few controls run. We release the code and derived tables. There is no quantum component in the inference path.

</details>


### [226] [Motion Conditioned World Modeling for Embodied Visual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32900)
*Jiahao Zhang,Xuan Yao,Junyu Gao,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出运动条件世界模型（MCWM），在隐状态转移中耦合目标运动分布预测与机器人自运动，用Geometry–Progress模块联合建模路径选择与单步行进距离，并以未来视觉特征重建监督运动预测，支撑轻量0.5B骨干的具身视觉跟踪策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视觉跟踪中目标运动不确定且机器人视角变化引起观测变化，二者共同作用使未来状态预测困难；现有世界模型难以显式刻画目标运动的路径不确定性及其与行进进度的依赖关系，且缺乏几何落地的条件输入。

Method: 1）Motion-Conditioned World Modeling：将预测的目标运动分布与机器人ego-motion注入潜在状态转移；2）Geometry–Progress模块：以共享运动模式+路径条件化的progress分布联合建模几何路径与单步行进距离，保留路径选择与进度的相关性并刻画不确定性；3）动作诱导坐标变换：把预测路径表达在下一时刻机器人坐标系，为未来视觉状态预测提供几何条件；4）以条件化转移分支上的未来视觉特征重建监督运动预测器；5）联合优化运动似然、特征重建与专家动作监督。

Result: 在三个具身跟踪基准上取得更高跟踪性能，并具备更好的跨环境泛化；模型采用0.5B轻量骨干。摘要未报告具体仿真或实机部署细节与量化提升幅度。

Conclusion: 显式运动表示与未来观测预测的闭环联结是具身世界模型的有效设计范式；但证据以仿真基准为主，缺少实机验证与开源信息，实用性待进一步确认。

Abstract: World models support embodied decision-making by predicting future environment states. In embodied visual tracking, uncertain target motion and robot-induced viewpoint changes jointly affect visual observations, making future-state prediction challenging. We propose Motion-Conditioned World Modeling, which incorporates predicted target-motion distributions and robot ego-motion into latent state transitions. A Geometry--Progress module jointly models geometric paths and single-step travel distances through shared motion modes and path-conditioned progress distributions. This formulation captures uncertainty in route selection and motion progress while preserving their dependence. Action-induced coordinate transformations express predicted paths in the next robot frame, providing geometrically grounded conditions for future visual-state prediction. Future visual feature reconstruction supervises the motion predictor through the conditioned transition branch, linking explicit motion representations to subsequent observations. The model is jointly optimized with motion likelihood, feature reconstruction, and expert-action supervision, enabling the tracking policy to combine visual context with predictive motion this http URL on three embodied tracking benchmarks demonstrate improved tracking performance and cross-environment generalization with a lightweight $0.5$B backbone.

</details>


### [227] [IMPACT-Seg: Consistent Anatomical Segmentation and Representation Learning Across CT, CBCT, and MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32903)
*Cédric Hémon,Valentin Boussot,Matthew McCormick,Lukas Zimmermann,Christopher Kurz,Guillaume Landry*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IMPACT-Seg：用共享参数网络在CT/CBCT/MRI上做126类密集解剖分割，无需显式特征对齐即可获得跨模态一致表征，并显著提升分割与多模态配准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT、CBCT、MRI是同一解剖结构互补成像，但外观差异大，导致自适应放疗中的一致分割与可变形配准困难；作者想验证密集解剖分割监督本身能否诱导跨模态的解剖对应关系。

Method: 构建8,290例体数据集（公开队列标签统一、模型辅助伪标签补全、迭代精修、2,864例基于物理的CBCT仿真并与CT体素对齐），用非配对图像联合训练2.5D与3D残差编码器网络，共享参数分割126个结构。

Result: 真实CBCT上3D模型平均Dice 0.842（最强对比方法0.671）；497对体素对齐CT-仿真CBCT上Dice仅下降0.022（对比方法0.153–0.178）；胰腺放疗CBCT胃肠道Dice 0.735 vs TotalSegmentator 0.634；配准CT-CBCT预测一致性0.881、CT-MRI 0.790；加入MRI训练使CT-MRI编码器特征检索误差由36.8 mm降至3.2 mm；配准流程在两个Learn2Reg多模态基准上排名第一；全身CT推理15秒。

Conclusion: 密集解剖分割监督可支撑跨模态一致的解剖表征，可直接服务分割与配准两类任务；代码、数据与预训练模型已开源。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT), cone-beam CT (CBCT), and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) provide complementary representations of the same underlying anatomy, yet substantial differences in image appearance challenge consistent segmentation and deformable registration in adaptive radiotherapy. We investigate whether dense anatomical segmentation supervision can promote cross-modality correspondence without explicit feature alignment. We introduce IMPACT-Seg, which segments 126 structures across CT, CBCT, and MRI using shared network parameters. A collection of 8,290 volumes was assembled from public cohorts through label harmonization, model-assisted pseudo-label completion, iterative refinement, and 2,864 physics-based CBCT simulations with voxel-aligned CT labels. Unpaired images were used to jointly train 2.5D and 3D residual-encoder networks. On 139 real CBCTs, IMPACT-Seg 3D achieved a mean Dice of 0.842 versus 0.671 for the strongest comparator. On 497 voxel-aligned CT-simulated-CBCT pairs, the CT-to-CBCT Dice decrease was 0.022 versus 0.153-0.178 for competing methods. On independent pancreatic radiotherapy CBCTs, gastrointestinal Dice was 0.735 versus 0.634 for TotalSegmentator. Prediction agreement reached 0.881 on registered CT-CBCT and 0.790 on CT-MRI pairs. Adding MRI training data reduced CT-MRI encoder feature retrieval error from 36.8 to 3.2 mm. Registration pipelines incorporating IMPACT-Seg features ranked first on two multimodal Learn2Reg benchmarks. The 3D model segmented a whole-body CT in 15 s. These findings show that dense anatomical segmentation supervision can support consistent anatomical representations across modalities, with applications to both segmentation and registration. Code, datasets, and pretrained models are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [228] [HARI4D: Hand-Articulation Aware 4D Reconstruction of Human-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32932)
*Semin Kim,Xianghui Xie,Chuqiao Li,Jongyoo Kim,Gerard Pons-Moll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HARI4D：从单目RGB视频重建手部关节感知的4D人-物交互，采用历史感知物体跟踪器 + ReachNet/TouchNet 粗到细优化，显著降低物体误差并提升手-物接触F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有全身4D重建方法忽略手部关节建模，导致重建的手会穿透物体或悬空于物体表面，缺乏空间与时间一致性；游戏、VR与机器人学习需要准确的单目人-物交互重建。

Method: (1) 历史感知（history-aware）物体跟踪器，提供鲁棒稳定的物体轨迹；(2) 基于轨迹的粗到细交互配置推理，按操作时序分解：ReachNet 在接触阶段将全身与手臂对齐到物体，TouchNet 依据时序上下文与局部物体几何精化手部关节姿态。

Result: 在训练未见过的ARCTIC数据集上，物体重建误差降低56%，手-物接触F1由0.866提升至0.921；可恢复一致的全身-物体交互与连贯手部关节，并支持在线视频。

Conclusion: 显式建模手部关节与接触几何对单目4D人-物交互重建至关重要；分阶段（Reach→Touch）设计兼顾时序一致性与局部几何精度，具备泛化性与在线可用性。

Abstract: Reasoning about human-object interaction from RGB cameras is important for gaming, VR environments, and robot learning. Yet 4D reconstruction from a monocular RGB camera is challenging, especially when articulated hands are involved. Prior full-body works neglect hands during reconstruction, causing them to penetrate into, or float from the object. We present HARI4D, a hand-articulation-aware method for reconstructing spatially and temporally consistent 4D human-object interaction from monocular RGB video. We introduce a history-aware object tracker that provides robust and stable tracking. Based on these tracks, we improve the interaction configuration through coarse-to-fine reasoning that follows the order of object manipulation: ReachNet first brings the full body and arms to the object during contact, and TouchNet then finalizes hand articulation according to temporal context and local object geometry. On ARCTIC, unseen in training, our method reduces the object error by 56% and improves hand contact F1 from 0.866 to 0.921 over prior art. Our method recovers accurate full body-object interaction with coherent hand articulations and applies to online videos. Our code and pretrained models will be released.

</details>


### [229] [Efficient Video Retrieval Augmented Generation via Token Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32938)
*Ayush Barik,Fan Lai,Francis Y. Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lembas将长视频RAG重构为在帧与字幕间共享预算的token分配问题，用迭代注水算法求解，并在多轮对话中复用已处理token，提升精度并大幅降低算力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频RAG仅做帧选择与token压缩，未显式建模"每个额外token的价值"及其边际递减与冗余证据竞争，导致共享预算分配低效。

Method: 提出即插即用框架Lembas：将视频RAG建模为帧与字幕在统一预算下的token分配优化问题，目标函数同时刻画item内部token边际价值递减与相似证据间冗余衰减；用带收敛保证的高效迭代注水（water-filling）算法求解。多轮对话中复用前轮已处理token，并借助VLM注意力以零额外开销精化相关性。

Result: 同算力下准确率较SOTA基线最高提升7.2个百分点；多轮视频理解任务中准确率最高提升1.5个百分点，同时TFLOPs减少1.7–17.3倍。

Conclusion: 显式token价值建模与预算分配是长视频理解的有效杠杆，Lembas可即插即用地兼容现有VLM，并兼具精度与效率优势。

Abstract: Efficient long-video understanding requires identifying relevant evidence while minimizing the tokens processed by vision-language models (VLMs). Existing video retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods rely on frame selection and token compression, but overlook how much each additional token is worth. We introduce Lembas, a plug-and-play framework that formulates video RAG as a token allocation problem over frames and subtitles under a shared budget. Our key insight is that the value of a token diminishes within each item and decreases further as similar evidence receives more tokens. Lembas captures both effects in a single objective and solves it with an efficient iterative water-filling algorithm that has a provable convergence guarantee. For multi-turn conversations, Lembas reuses the tokens processed in earlier turns and refines relevance with the VLM's attention at no extra cost. Our evaluations show that Lembas improves accuracy by up to 7.2 percentage points over state-of-the-art baselines at comparable compute. On multi-turn video understanding, Lembas achieves up to 1.5 percentage points higher accuracy with 1.7-17.3x fewer TFLOPs.

</details>


### [230] [Do Vision-Language Models Know Their Own Visual Weaknesses? Probing Capability-Level Metacognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32940)
*Dohoon Kim,Taesup Moon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉语言模型（VLM）无法在解题前预判自己擅长或薄弱的视觉任务类型与图像退化条件；但在实际作答时，其答案概率分布已隐含了自身特有的弱点信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元认知不仅是判断单个答案对错，还包括对自身在不同任务类型上强弱的先验知识。作者希望检验VLM是否具备这种跨任务的自我认知，并将"所有模型都难"的共享难度与"仅本模型难"的特有弱点区分开，从而提供可检验VLM自我认知的方法与信号。

Method: 在大量开源与闭源VLM上，跨多种视觉任务类型与图像条件（如退化图像）评测其事前准确性预测；通过分解共同难度与模型特有难度进行归因分析；再训练VLM预测自身准确率（准确性自预测微调），并对比训练后在未见任务类型上的泛化；同时分析模型在少量无标签同类问题上的答案概率是否反映其特有弱点。

Result: VLM事前几乎不知道自身的视觉弱点：整体准确率预测偏差大，无法识别所有模型共同困难的题型，多数模型也误判图像退化的影响。训练自预测可让模型学会自身准确率水平（可泛化到训练中未见类型），但学不到"哪些类型难"；然而一旦开始解题，答案概率即可反映跨任务类型与图像条件的模型特有弱点。

Conclusion: VLM内部持有自身弱点的信息，但未体现在其显式预判中；区分模型特有难度与共享难度为评估"教会VLM认识自身弱点"的方法提供了测试，答案概率可作为起点信号。

Abstract: Metacognition includes knowing one's own strengths and weaknesses across kinds of tasks, not only judging whether a single answer is correct. We test whether vision-language models (VLMs) know in advance which kinds of visual tasks and image conditions are hard for them, across a wide range of open and proprietary models. Because some tasks are hard for every model, we separate what a model knows about this shared difficulty from what it knows about itself. We find that VLMs know little in advance about their visual weaknesses. They misjudge their overall accuracy and do not even know which kinds of tasks are hard for every model, and most misjudge how degraded images affect them. We further train VLMs to predict their own accuracy, which teaches them their own level of accuracy on kinds unseen in training but not which kinds are hard for them. However, information about these weaknesses appears once a model starts solving. On a few unlabeled questions of a kind, the probability a model assigns to its own answers follows weaknesses particular to the model, across kinds of tasks and image conditions. VLMs thus hold information about their own weaknesses that their stated predictions do not yet reflect. By separating what is particular to a model from what holds for every model, our evaluation offers a test for methods that aim to teach VLMs their own weaknesses, and the answer probability offers them a signal to start from.

</details>


### [231] [ProX-MAD: Aggregating Spatial Context into Representative Proxies for Few-shot Medical Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32941)
*Seunghyun Oh,Seongsu Lee,Seunghye Chae,Youngmin Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProX-MAD：用可学习的空间代理（proxy）聚合参考图像块特征，实现统一的跨数据集少样本医学异常检测，从"一模型一数据集"转向"一模型多数据集"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本医学异常检测长期采用数据集特定的"一对一"范式，统一"一对多"建模研究不足；直接把现有方法扩展到该设定会导致显著性能下降，尤其像素级。原因在于其检测机制：共享决策边界在异构病灶定义下不可靠，而仅建模正常样本的记忆库在少量参考下无法覆盖多样的正常分布。

Method: 提出 ProX-MAD 代理框架：将空间上下文聚合为紧凑的、可学习的、按状态（正常/异常）区分的代理。核心为 Masked Proxy Infusion Attention (MPIA)：代理 token 对参考 patch 特征做交叉注意力，并施加按代理的掩码以保留高置信对应，缓解代理坍塌，促使代理多样化并同时概括正常与异常参考。

Result: 在六个医学数据集上，从一对一迁移到一对多训练时性能仅轻微下降；仅训练一次的单一统一模型，平均而言仍优于此前 SOTA 方法（即使在后者更有利的一对一协议下），验证了统一多数据集 MAD 的实用性。

Conclusion: 代理式、状态感知的表示是缓解跨数据集异质性、实现可扩展统一少样本医学异常检测的有效途径。

Abstract: Few-shot medical anomaly detection (MAD) has largely followed a dataset-specific "one-for-one" paradigm, leaving unified "one-for-many" modeling underexplored. Naively extending existing methods to this setting causes substantial performance degradation, especially at the pixel level. We attribute this to their detection mechanisms: a shared decision boundary becomes unreliable across heterogeneous lesion definitions, whereas a normal-only memory bank cannot adequately cover diverse normal distributions with few references. We propose ProX-MAD, a proxy-based framework that aggregates spatial context into compact, learnable, state-specific proxies for unified few-shot MAD. Its Masked Proxy Infusion Attention (MPIA) module cross-attends proxy tokens to reference patch features and applies proxy-wise masking to retain high-confidence correspondences, mitigating proxy collapse and encouraging diverse proxies that summarize both normal and abnormal references. Across six medical datasets, ProX-MAD exhibits only a marginal performance drop from one-for-one to one-for-many training. Despite being trained only once as a unified model, it also outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods on average even when they use their more favorable one-for-one protocol, demonstrating the practicality of unified multi-dataset MAD.

</details>


### [232] [OmniHarness-RSI: Modality-Aware Harness Evolution for Omnimodal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32951)
*Shilin Ma,Xingming Shui,Jinglin Chen,Zetong Zhao,Jingqi Tian,Deyi Zhu,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniHarness-RSI 将基于 harness 的递归自我改进（RSI）扩展到全模态理解：把进化空间分解为模态路由与协作、模态感知证据定位、模态特定感知三个维度，构建含专家与音视频检索工具的 Factorized-Harness Agent，并用模态引导的代码修订+执行反馈进化 harness，在 WorldSense/Daily-Omni/OmniVideoBench 上分别提升 8.86/8.13/6.23 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频理解中无需更新基座参数的 harness-based RSI 已见效，但推广到全模态理解困难：多模态感知与推理异构且相互依赖，进化空间复杂，难以做针对性 harness 优化。为此需要模态感知的因子化进化框架。

Method: 1) 将进化空间因子化为三个能力维度（模态路由与协作、模态感知证据定位、模态特定感知）；2) 据此构建 Factorized-Harness Agent，集成专门感知专家与音视频检索工具；3) 模态引导的 harness 进化：通过结构化的"模态-能力"归因定位缺陷，再用交互式代码修订与执行反馈精炼 harness 策略；4) 用验证引导的选择与 held-out 评测检验数据集内与跨数据集的泛化。

Result: WorldSense、Daily-Omni、OmniVideoBench 测试准确率分别提升 8.86、8.13、6.23 个百分点，稳定优于模态无关的 RSI；进化后的 harness 无需适配即可跨数据集迁移，同时减少 planner 调用次数与查看帧数。

Conclusion: 模态因子化 + 归因驱动的 harness 进化能有效降低全模态 RSI 的搜索复杂度，在提升准确率的同时降低推理开销，并具备跨数据集泛化性；为不改基座模型的 agent workflow 自我进化提供了可复用范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in harness-based recursive self-improvement (RSI) have improved video understanding by evolving executable agent workflows without updating foundation model parameters. However, extending this paradigm to omnimodal understanding remains challenging, as the heterogeneous and interdependent nature of multimodal perception and reasoning creates a complex evolution space, making targeted harness optimization difficult. To address these challenges, we propose OmniHarness-RSI, a modality-aware framework for evolving agent harnesses. Specifically, we factorize the evolution space into three capability dimensions: modality routing and collaboration, modality-aware evidence localization, and modality-specific perception. Based on this factorization, we construct a Factorized-Harness Agent integrating specialized perception experts and audio-video retrieval tools. We further introduce modality-guided harness evolution, which identifies deficiencies through structured modality-capability attribution and refines corresponding harness strategies through interactive code revisions and execution feedback. Validation-guided selection and held-out evaluation assess generalization within and across datasets. Experiments on WorldSense, Daily-Omni, and OmniVideoBench demonstrate test accuracy improvements of 8.86, 8.13, and 6.23 percentage points, respectively, consistently outperforming modality-agnostic RSI. The evolved harnesses also demonstrate cross-dataset transfer without adaptation, while reducing planner calls and viewed frames.

</details>


### [233] [FedTumor-IoMT: Federated Learning-Aided Brain Tumor Diagnosis on Resource-Constrained Edge IoMT Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32952)
*Pranshu Jena,Prajnyajit Mohanty,Umesh C. Pati*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级多任务联邦学习框架，在资源受限边缘设备本地训练，联合完成脑肿瘤分割与分类：共享预训练LiteMedSAM编码器（TinyViT）驱动坐标注意力分割解码器与分割引导分类头，无需独立特征提取器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合分割+分类模型计算量大，只能在GPU工作站训练或仅在边缘端做分类，无法支撑IoMT中隐私保护的联邦学习；需填补"边缘端本地训练+多任务"的空白。

Method: 共享LiteMedSAM/TinyViT编码器提取特征，接坐标注意力分割解码器与分割引导分类头；采用选择性编码器冻结（保留95%可训练参数？原文表述存疑，实际为冻结部分参数以降低端上训练开销）；在5个资源受限边缘客户端的实验床、1200例T1加权MRI上进行5轮FL验证，并做注意力/特征细化模块消融。

Result: 分类准确率94.20%、DSC 88.53%、IoU 83.68%、ROC-AUC 99.87%；较GPU基线95.21%仅差0.6%；注意力与特征细化模块使DSC、IoU分别提升3.82%、4.60%，仅增加0.74 GFLOPs；边缘端推理延迟46.54 s、CPU利用率52.8%、峰值温度65°C无热降频，延迟为现有框架的1/6.4–1/10.5。

Conclusion: 证明在资源受限边缘平台上实现隐私保护的脑肿瘤联合分割与分类具备可行性；但属医学影像联邦学习领域，与Physical AI/机器人核心方向无直接关联，方法洞察（选择性冻结、共享编码器）具参考价值。

Abstract: Joint brain tumor segmentation and classification models are computationally intensive, limiting their training on resource-constrained edge platforms and restricting privacy-preserving Federated Learning FL in the Internet of Medical Things IoMT. Existing approaches either perform both tasks on GPU-equipped workstations or support only classification on edge devices. To address this gap, this work proposes a lightweight multitask FL framework for joint segmentation and classification through local edge training. A shared pretrained LiteMedSAM encoder with TinyViT extracts features for a coordinate-attention-based segmentation decoder and a segmentation-guided classification head, eliminating a separate feature extractor. Selective encoder freezing retains 95\% trainable parameters while reducing on-device training costs. The proposed framework is experimentally validated on a laboratory testbed of five resource-constrained edge clients using 1,200 T1-weighted MRI samples. It achieves 94.20\% classification accuracy, 88.53\% DSC, 83.68\% IoU, and 99.87\% ROC-AUC. Its classification accuracy remains within 0.6\% of the GPU-trained baseline of 95.21\% across five FL rounds. The attention and feature refinement modules improve DSC and IoU by 3.82\% and 4.60\%, respectively, with 0.74 GFLOPs of additional computation. On edge hardware, the framework achieves 46.54 s inference latency, 52.8\% CPU utilization, and a peak temperature of 65$^\circ$C without thermal throttling. Its inference latency is observed to be 6.4--10.5 times lower than that of existing frameworks. These results demonstrate the feasibility of privacy-preserving joint brain tumor segmentation and classification on resource-constrained edge platforms.

</details>


### [234] [Benchmarking Evidence Sufficiency in Vision-Language Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32964)
*Nhat Ha Nguyen,Sudipta Chowdhury,Lokesh Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveAnswer是一个含20,151个问题、7类推理族的驾驶VLM基准，通过配对强/弱证据场景，把"答案正确"、"证据充分性（是否应弃答）"与"定位（grounding）"作为联合能力来评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测把grounding与answerability割裂评估，无法区分"定位到相关但证据不足"的预测与"证据充分支撑"的预测；可靠驾驶VLM需判断视觉证据是否足以支持回答，因此需要能操作性区分二者的基准。

Method: 构建20,151题、7个推理族的基准；每对强/弱证据配置保持底层场景事实与语义答案不变，仅改变观测是否足以回答（弱证据下弃答为正确行为）；在六族评估上测零样本基线并做受控适配（Gemma-4-12B、Qwen3-VL-8B）。

Result: 最强零样本基线强证据准确率67.31%，但仅21.55%的配对问题同时满足"答—弃答"行为与完整grounding；受控适配后Gemma-4-12B在该联合能力上达63.29%，约为零样本的三倍；Qwen3-VL-8B表现出同向提升。

Conclusion: 语义正确性、证据充分性与grounding是三种不同能力，应联合评估；当前驾驶VLM在证据敏感型回答与定位的联合维度上仍显著不足。

Abstract: Reliable driving VLMs must do more than answer correctly or localize relevant scene actors: they must determine whether the available visual evidence is sufficient to support an answer. Existing evaluations largely treat grounding and answerability as separate measurements, making it difficult to distinguish well-supported predictions from those localized to relevant but insufficient evidence. We introduce DriveAnswer, a benchmark of 20,151 questions across seven reasoning families, that operationalizes this distinction using matched strong- and weak-evidence configurations. Each pair preserves the underlying scene fact and semantic answer while changing whether the observations support answering, making abstention the appropriate response under weak evidence. On the six-family evaluation, the strongest zero-shot baseline reaches 67.31% strong-evidence accuracy, yet only 21.55% of matched questions satisfy both answer-abstain behavior and complete grounding. With controlled adaptation, Gemma-4-12B reaches 63.29% on this joint capability, nearly tripling its zero-shot performance. Qwen3-VL-8B exhibits the same broad improvement in evidence-sensitive answering and grounding. These results show that semantic correctness, evidence sufficiency, and grounding are distinct capabilities that should be evaluated jointly.

</details>


### [235] [VidForce: Predicting Force-Aware Articulation by Observing Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32967)
*Rafay Mohiuddin,Jiaqi Chen,Marc Pollefeys,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VidForce从第三人称RGB视频中联合估计交互轨迹与力曲线，以少量仪器化测量学习“物理原型”作为先验，并在Spot机器人上用于真实铰接物体操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可通过观察他人推断物体运动与交互所需力度，而具身智能体缺乏此类能力；直接力测量需专用设备且数据量有限，因此希望从视频中获得力与轨迹监督弱、可扩展的力感知。

Method: 构建共享学习到的力水平与时间轮廓的物理原型作为先验，由视觉与语义线索决定各原型贡献；利用几何与视觉-语言特征做自适应调整；用联合条件流模型同时精化初始跟踪轨迹与力曲线。以配对的人类视频与仪器化夹爪记录提供实测力参考进行监督，并训练独立力矩模型验证方法可扩展至其他感知量。

Result: 在Hoi!数据集留出交互上，ATE 6.6 cm，峰值力MAE 4.81 N、RMSE 5.92 N，力曲线RMSE 5.52 N；真实视频中推断稳定；在Spot上以人类示范估计的轨迹与力曲线驱动铰接物体操作并报告多次试验成功率。

Conclusion: 该方法将少量仪器化力测量转化为可泛化的视频力估计先验，实现视频到力与轨迹的联合推断，并能迁移到真实机器人操作，验证了原型先验+联合流建模的有效性与跨感知量扩展性。

Abstract: People can anticipate how an object moves and the effort an interaction may require by watching others. How can embodied agents acquire this understanding from video? Force measurements provide direct sensory supervision, but require specialized equipment and remain limited in quantity. We introduce VidForce, which estimates an interaction trajectory together with a force profile from a third-person RGB video; 2D contact clicks and textual object-action prompts are optional. Our approach makes limited instrumented measurements useful for interpreting human interactions through learned physical prototypes as a prior. The prototypes share learned force levels and temporal profiles across interactions of comparable effort, with visual and semantic cues determining their contribution to each observation. Geometric and vision-language features adapt this prior to the observed interaction, while a joint conditional flow refines an initial tracked trajectory together with the force profile. Paired human and instrumented-gripper recordings provide measured force references for supervising human video, and a separately trained torque model demonstrates that the formulation extends to other sensory quantities. We evaluate trajectory recovery and force estimation through baseline comparisons and controlled ablations on held-out interactions from the Hoi! dataset. On this evaluation subset, VidForce achieves 6.6 cm ATE, peak-force MAE and RMSE of 4.81 N and 5.92 N, respectively, and force-profile RMSE of 5.52 N. On real-world videos, VidForce infers motion and force reliably and consistently despite the variability in human execution. We further deploy VidForce on a Spot robot, where trajectories and force profiles estimated from human demonstrations drive real-world articulated-object manipulation, and report success rates across repeated trials. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [236] [FocusAnchor: Zero-Shot Metric Depth by Anchoring a Depth Foundation Prior to Focus Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32970)
*Ashkan Ganj,Hang Su,Tian Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FocusAnchor：用冻结基础模型的相对深度先验反复对齐到当前度量深度估计的循环式 depth-from-focus 框架，配合聚焦体查找、随机相机参数合成训练栈、逐切片独立噪声与可变栈训练，实现跨相机泛化与少切片鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 depth-from-focus 方法在焦点线索微弱、新相机、焦点切片较少时性能下降；需要跨相机的度量深度估计方法，并能利用相对深度基础模型的场景结构先验。

Method: 循环网络 FocusAnchor：(1) 聚焦体查找（focus-volume lookup）细化度量深度；(2) 将冻结基础模型的相对深度先验反复对齐到当前深度估计，对齐后的先验以反馈回路引导后续细化，把纹理区域恢复的度量尺度传播到焦点线索弱的区域并保持场景结构；(3) 随机相机参数与焦距的合成训练栈以支持跨相机迁移；(4) 添加逐切片独立噪声以抑制栈合成产生的伪深度线索（因模糊对噪声作用不一致）；(5) 可变栈训练（variable-stack）提升少切片鲁棒性，支持移动端采集，对应变体 FocusAnchor-flex。

Result: 相对 FOSSA ViT-B：NYU Depth V2 上 AbsRel 降低 36%，DDFF-12 微调后降低 9%；零样本评测中 FocusAnchor 与 FocusAnchor-flex 在全部五个基准上取得最低 AbsRel 的前两名；ZEDD 测试集上 AbsRel 比最佳对比方法 FOSSA ViT-B 低 9%；ZEDD 验证集上 FocusAnchor-flex 用少 40% 的焦点切片取得更低 AbsRel。

Conclusion: 将基础模型相对深度先验与焦点线索在反馈循环中结合，可有效把度量尺度传播至弱焦点区域；逐切片噪声注入与可变栈训练是抑制合成伪线索、提升跨相机与少切片泛化的关键设计。

Abstract: Existing depth-from-focus methods can struggle with weak focus cues, new cameras, or fewer focal slices. We present FocusAnchor, a recurrent model that uses focus-volume lookups to refine metric depth and repeatedly aligns a relative depth prior from a frozen foundation model to the current depth estimate. The aligned prior guides subsequent refinement in a feedback loop, carrying metric scale recovered from textured regions into regions where focus cues are weak while preserving scene structure. To support transfer across cameras, we synthesize training stacks with randomized camera parameters and focus distances. However, stack synthesis blurs existing image noise differently across slices, creating artificial depth cues even on textureless surfaces. We add independent per-slice noise to suppress these cues. We also introduce variable-stack training to improve robustness with fewer focal slices and support more practical mobile capture. Compared with FOSSA ViT-B, FocusAnchor reduces AbsRel by 36% on NYU Depth V2 and by 9% on DDFF-12 after fine-tuning. In zero-shot evaluation, FocusAnchor and its variable-stack variant, FocusAnchor-flex, achieve the two lowest AbsRel values on all five benchmarks. On ZEDD test, FocusAnchor achieves 9% lower AbsRel than the best competing method, FOSSA ViT-B, while FocusAnchor-flex achieves lower AbsRel with 40% fewer focal slices on ZEDD validation.

</details>


### [237] [Teaching Time Without Losing General Retrieval: Adapting Multimodal Embeddings for Time-Sensitive Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32971)
*Toya Oyama,Rainer Lienhart,Shin'ichi Satoh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三个简单组件（Keep loss、Pair loss、Middle-layer LoRA）微调MLLM视频嵌入模型，用单一模型、每段视频一个向量同时实现强通用检索与时间敏感检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM嵌入模型在场景描述式文本-视频检索上表现好，但对动作及其方向（时间敏感）检索差；TARA用时间硬负样本三元组微调虽提升时间敏感检索，却显著损害通用检索（R@1 58.4→44.8），且需与基座模型集成才能保住通用能力。

Method: (1) Keep loss：约束输出嵌入不偏离基座模型，而非约束权重；(2) Pair loss：视频与反转副本的嵌入距离过近、文本嵌入却已分离，故沿两文本嵌入差的方向拉开视频嵌入间距；(3) Middle-layer LoRA：仅在帧融合所在的中层训练LoRA（0.15%参数），因时间信息在该层附近达到峰值。

Result: 通用R@1保持58.4，反转副本入库的R@1由55.5升至63.2；增益可迁移到未见基准与InternVL2-8B-RA；Keep loss作为TARA配方的补救使通用R@1从44.8升至57.8；与TARA集成后在RTime和未见CiA上取得对比模型最高分。

Conclusion: 单一嵌入模型可在不牺牲通用检索的前提下显著提升时间敏感检索，关键洞察在于约束输出漂移、对齐文本方向拉开视频嵌入、并只训练帧融合中层。

Abstract: In text-to-video retrieval, MLLM-based embedding models do well when the text describes the scene (general retrieval), but poorly when it describes actions and their direction (time-sensitive retrieval). TARA, a pioneering work, fine-tunes such a model on text triplets with temporal hard negatives; it improves time-sensitive retrieval but lowers general retrieval (mean R@1 over six benchmarks in our rerun on Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B: 58.4->44.8), and its released model keeps general retrieval only when ensembled with Qwen3-VL-Embedding. Our goal is strong general and time-sensitive retrieval with one model and one vector per clip. Three findings lead to three simple components. Keep loss: general retrieval follows how far the output embeddings drift from the base model, not how far the weights move; we therefore keep them near the base model, which keeps most general retrieval even without general-domain data. Pair loss: the text embeddings of an action description and its reversed-direction version are well separated, but the video embeddings of a clip and its reversed copy are close; we therefore widen the gap between the video embeddings of each clip and its reversed copy, in the direction of the difference between their two text embeddings. Middle-layer LoRA: frames are combined in the middle layers, and temporal information peaks in or just after them; we therefore train LoRA only on these layers, which nearly matches training all weights on most time-sensitive benchmarks with 0.15% of the parameters and keeps general retrieval better. General R@1 stays at 58.4, while R@1 with each clip's reversed copy in the gallery rises from 55.5 to 63.2; the gains carry over to unseen benchmarks and InternVL2-8B-RA. The keep loss is also a remedy for the TARA recipe (general R@1: 44.8->57.8), and ensembling TARA with Ours gives the highest scores on RTime and unseen CiA among the compared models.

</details>


### [238] [NEST: Uncovering and Nesting Unknown Objects in Open-World Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32981)
*Yuchen Zhang,Yuan Gao,Sebastian Schmidt,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文针对开放世界目标检测，提出类别无关排序器（class-agnostic ranker）解决未知目标在top-k选择中被背景挤压丢弃的问题，并用欧拉图启发的分类器将未知目标归入给定类taxonomy的宽泛类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放世界检测器只把训练未覆盖类别的目标标为"unknown"，只告知"有物体"而不告知"是什么"，无法支撑更明智的下游决策；同时发现基础检测器其实已定位出多数未知目标，但因背景分数更高而在最终top-k筛选中被丢弃。

Method: 1）类别无关排序器：评估每个预测框与物体的贴合程度，作为类别无关打分替代原有分类分数来完成最终预测筛选；2）欧拉图式分类器：类别区域包含其成员区域，用已知类别构建每个类别的区域，并学习为未见过的成员预留多大的空间，从而把未知目标归入taxonomy的宽泛类别。

Result: 在三个benchmark上，未知类召回相对最强基线提升3.8%–195.1%（相对值），同时保持有竞争力的已知类检测性能；在相同检测结果下，未知目标分类准确率优于现有层次化方法。

Conclusion: 关键问题不在"没检测到"，而在"排序筛选阶段被背景抑制"，用类别无关的物体性排序即可显著回收未知召回；进一步以欧拉图包含关系显式建模宽泛类别为未知成员留白，可将"unknown"升级为taxonomy级别的语义归属。

Abstract: Open-world object detectors aim to localize objects of classes not covered by their training annotations, but most label them only as "unknown". This label tells a system that an object is present but not what it is, while its broader category would allow a more informed decision. We therefore address both uncovering unknown objects and assigning them to the broader categories of a given class taxonomy. For uncovering, we find that most unknown objects are already localized and labeled "unknown" in our base detector, but are discarded during final top-k selection because background receives higher scores. To mitigate this, we propose a class-agnostic ranker that estimates how well each predicted box fits an object and uses these scores to select the final predictions.
