Across three benchmarks, our detector improves unknown recall by 3.8% to 195.1% relative to the strongest baseline in each task, while maintaining competitive known-class detection. For categorizing, we draw on Euler diagrams, in which the region of a category contains the regions of its members. Our categorizer builds each category's region from the known classes and learns how much room to leave for members it has never seen. On the same detections, it categorizes unknown objects more accurately than existing hierarchical methods. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [239] [When Repair Accuracy Misleads: Training a VLM on Its Own Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32982)
*Ang Qi,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出verified failure-driven repair (VFR)：通过类型化最小对（minimal-pair）故障挖掘、三LLM评判员盲标签验证、答案优先纠正目标与带replay的LoRA微调修复VLM，并揭示"held-out故障准确率"与"无条件下准确率"两类评估陷阱（标签错误率37%、仅凭问题措辞即可答对73.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接用模型自身失败样本微调VLM是直观的修复手段，但以held-out故障集准确率作为成功指标具有误导性：人工审计Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct在COCO/GQA生成问题上的100个"失败"发现37个是标签错误；且挖掘出的失败存在措辞规律，仅用问题文本的分类器在73.2%的held-out失败对上两问全对，说明模型可能学到措辞捷径而非真实能力。

Method: VFR流程：(1) 类型化最小对故障挖掘（同一图像/情境下的对照问题对）；(2) 由三个LLM评判员在不看存储标签的条件下作答进行验证，剔除标签错误；(3) 采用答案优先（answer-first）的纠正性监督目标；(4) 以replay数据混合进行LoRA微调。评估上对比held-out故障集、独立标注的无条件对照对、以及改写/独立数据集迁移，并做去掉验证、去掉失败选择、去掉multi-image replay等消融。

Result: held-out故障修复率82.8%（基座10.0%）；独立标注无条件下对照对整体准确率87.4%→92.4%，空间关系66.7%→96.8%（即使措辞指向错误答案）。但否定类：held-out修复88.9%，无条件下对准确率88.7%→79.7%（对"缺失物体"的否定被破坏）。无验证时无条件下对降至80.8%（低于基座87.4%，源于开发基准未暴露的否定捷径）；无失败选择时修复率降至73.4%（准确率92.0%，单seed）。双图像关系准确率74.3%→49.6%（仅replay为48.4%），multi-image replay部分且不均衡地缓解。空间问题改写后仍保留大部分增益（87.3% vs 基座62.0%），但改写使held-out空间修复从92.9%降至76.8%。What'sUp独立图像上caption选择保持满分，而自建yes/no对准确率84.5%→26.4%。

Conclusion: 以held-out失败准确率衡量修复会系统性高估模型能力；需盲标签验证、失败选择与无条件下/改写后的独立评估。失败驱动的窄域修复会带来跨能力负迁移（否定、多图关系、分布外场景），提示"修复即引入捷径"的风险，需在目标类型与replay设计上做更细粒度控制。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a vision-language model on its own failures is a direct way to repair it, but accuracy on held-out failures, the obvious measure of success, misleads. Many mined failures are not model errors: a human audit of 100 failures of Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct on questions generated from COCO and GQA found label errors in 37. And mined failures share wording regularities: question-only classifiers get both answers right on 73.2% of held-out failure pairs. We study both traps with verified failure-driven repair (VFR): typed minimal-pair failure mining, verification by three LLM judges that answer without seeing the stored labels, answer-first corrective targets, and LoRA fine-tuning with replay. VFR repairs 82.8% of held-out failures (base model: 10.0%), and on independently labeled, unconditioned pairs it raises overall accuracy from 87.4% to 92.4% through spatial relations, whose accuracy rises from 66.7% to 96.8% even where the wording points to the wrong answer. Yet on negation, of which it repairs 88.9% of held-out failures, its accuracy on unconditioned pairs falls from 88.7% to 79.7%: it breaks negations about absent objects, rare in its training set. Without verification, accuracy on unconditioned pairs falls to 80.8%, below the base model's 87.4%, through a negation shortcut our development benchmarks miss; without failure selection, it stays at 92.0% (one seed) while repair falls to 73.4%. Training also lowers two-image relation accuracy (74.3% to 49.6%; replay data alone: 48.4%); multi-image replay partly and unevenly reverses this. Paraphrasing the spatial questions keeps most of the gain (87.3% vs. the base model's 62.0%), though rewording lowers spatial repair on held-out failures from 92.9% to 76.8%. On What'sUp's independent photos of objects under others, caption choice stays perfect while accuracy on our yes/no pairs falls from 84.5% to 26.4%.

</details>


### [240] [Decision-Native Multimodal Clinical Modeling via Anatomical Grounding and Confidence Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32993)
*Juntao Jiang,Wenqi Zhong,Guangyu Guo,Dexia Chen,Yilong Hu,Yunhao Bai,Zhenyu Yi,Jianwei Xu,Tony C. W. Mok,Muxuan Gao,Ling Zhang,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang,Zhongwei Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Radev：解剖引导视觉压缩+单次前向候选预测+反事实校准训练的决策原生3D医学多模态模型，并构建CT-Verdict基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D医学多模态模型存在两个问题：自回归解码导致置信度未校准；依赖与决策无关解剖区域的捷径特征。目标是在体数据CT上给出解剖学有据的预测与校准置信度。

Method: 1）冻结3D编码器抽取可复用器官级特征，由查询动态选择，并在预测的解剖边界内关注2D切片特征，实现全局上下文与细粒度切片融合的紧凑视觉token；2）语言骨干单次前向估计候选概率，取消自回归解码；3）反事实校准训练（CCT）：对比完整与干预器官表示，移除目标器官证据导致决策质量下降时应降低置信度，移除无关器官时过度置信下降受惩罚。

Result: 在自建CT-Verdict基准（二分类征象存在性、多选问答、有序严重度评估，含分布内与外部队列）上，决策准确率与置信度校准均显著优于SOTA，并在分布偏移下保持稳健泛化。

Conclusion: 将解剖先验、非自回归候选判别与反事实置信监督结合，可同时提升3D医学决策准确性与校准性，并具有跨队列泛化能力。

Abstract: Structured clinical decisions from volumetric computed tomography (CT) require anatomically grounded predictions and calibrated confidence estimates. However, existing 3D medical multimodal models often suffer from uncalibrated autoregressive decoding and shortcut reliance on irrelevant anatomical regions. We introduce Radev, a decision-native multimodal model that integrates anatomy-guided visual compression, direct candidate prediction, and counterfactual confidence supervision. To bridge global context with fine-grained slice details, a frozen 3D encoder extracts reusable organ-level features, which are dynamically selected by the query to attend to 2D slice features within predicted anatomical bounds. The resulting compact visual tokens enable the language backbone to estimate candidate probabilities in a single forward pass without autoregressive decoding. We further propose Counterfactual-Calibrated Training (CCT) to compare predictions under intact and intervened organ representations. CCT encourages confidence to decrease when removing target-organ evidence degrades decision quality, while penalizing excessive confidence drops under unrelated-organ removal. To facilitate rigorous evaluation, we construct CT-Verdict, a comprehensive benchmark spanning binary finding presence, multiple-choice questions, and ordinal severity assessment across both in-distribution and external cohorts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Radev significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both decision accuracy and confidence calibration, while generalizing robustly under distribution shifts. Project page at this https URL

</details>


### [241] [PAVE: Preference-Aligned Visual Egocentric Learning for the Blind and Low-Vision Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32996)
*Nan Li,Shaofeng Liang,Shiyun Zhao,Keni Shi,Zhencheng Huang,Bowen Tian,Jiemin Wu,Wenshuo Chen,Kaishen Yuan,Runwei Guan,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建偏好对齐的自视角问答数据集PA-EgoQA，并提出PAVE框架（结构化答案策略微调 + 问题条件路由 + Filter-GRPO），使BLV用户的视觉问答输出在事实准确之外兼顾空间方位、可操作性与简洁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自视角VQA基准与模型仅优化与参考答案的语义一致性，把视觉辅助简化为事实匹配；而真实BLV场景中，事实正确但缺乏空间方位、可操作细节或冗余啰嗦的回答实际无用，缺乏以人为中心的响应标准监督。

Method: 基于EgoBlind真实BLV自视角视频与用户查询重建参考答案构建PA-EgoQA；提出PAVE统一学习与推理框架，将结构化答案策略微调与确定性的、以问题为条件的路由结合，在推理时按查询表层形式适配答案组织方式；进一步用Filter-GRPO后训练，奖励为分层"正确性-质量"奖励，并配合基于对比的候选组过滤，从事实正确性对比与正确答案间的细粒度质量差异中提取学习信号。

Result: 在三个视觉-语言主干上一致提升事实准确率，并在辅助性回答质量上取得显著增益。

Conclusion: 为将人本响应需求转化为自视角监督数据、生成策略与后训练目标提供了可操作范式；属数据集+对齐方法工作，非机器人/3D核心方向。

Abstract: Egocentric video question answering enables blind and low-vision (BLV) users to query their immediate surroundings through natural language. However, existing benchmarks and models primarily optimize for semantic agreement with reference answers, reducing visual assistance to a purely factual matching task. In real-world scenarios, a factually correct response remains practically unhelpful if it lacks the spatial orientation, actionable detail, or conciseness required by the immediate task of the user. Drawing upon real BLV egocentric videos and authentic user queries from EgoBlind, we construct the PA-EgoQA, a preference-aligned dataset that reconstructs reference answers according to empirical BLV communication criteria. Based on this dataset, we introduce Preference-Aligned Visual Egocentric Learning (PAVE), a unified learning and inference framework that couples structured answer-policy fine-tuning with deterministic question-conditioned routing to adapt response formulation directly to the surface form of the query at inference time. We further align the model via Filter-GRPO, which pairs a hierarchical correctness-quality reward with contrast-based candidate-group filtering to extract learning signals from both factual accuracy contrasts and fine-grained quality variations among correct answers. Extensive experiments across three vision-language backbones demonstrate consistent improvements in factual accuracy alongside substantial gains in assistive response quality. These results establish a concrete paradigm for translating human-centered response requirements into egocentric supervision, generation policies, and post-training objectives. Code will be provided at this https URL .

</details>


### [242] [First-Hit Photoconsistency for Neural Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32998)
*Gabriel Lenain,Edmond Boyer,Jean Ponce*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 仅用多视角patch光度一致性+几何可见性做神经隐式表面重建，球追踪求首个表面交点，避免体渲染与参数化外观模型，在BMVS上Chamfer最低、DTU上与主流方法持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式/高斯方法联合优化几何与外观会引入歧义：参数化外观模型可"吸收"几何误差，仅靠图像重建损失无法保证表面准确；同时依赖体渲染、前景掩码与几何正则。

Method: 1-Lipschitz MLP表示表面；对每条参考光线做球追踪取首次交点，由交点位置与法向定义局部patch；仅在可见邻域视角下用直接从输入图像采样的颜色计算单一光度一致性损失（几何可见性剔除遮挡）；无体渲染、无外观参数模型、无掩码与辅助正则。

Result: BlendedMVS上平均Chamfer距离在所有对比方法中最低；DTU上几何精度与领先重建方法相当。

Conclusion: 以几何可见性约束的patch光度一致性单独驱动1-Lipschitz隐式表面，即可获得高精度表面，说明外观模型与体渲染并非必需，可作为前馈/优化式重建的几何规范化思路。

Abstract: Recent neural implicit and Gaussian splatting approaches have achieved impressive results in multi-view surface reconstruction. However, jointly optimizing geometry and appearance introduces ambiguities. A parametric appearance model can compensate for geometric errors, reproducing the input images accurately without an accurate surface. We demonstrate accurate neural implicit surface reconstruction using only multi-view patch photoconsistency with geometry-based visibility, without volumetric rendering or parametric appearance models. For each reference ray, sphere tracing computes the first surface intersection. Its position and normal define a local patch whose photoconsistency is evaluated across visible neighboring views using colors sampled directly from the input images. For efficient sphere tracing, we use a 1-Lipschitz multi-layer perceptron. We use a single photoconsistency loss, without foreground masks or auxiliary geometric regularization. Experiments show that our method achieves the lowest mean Chamfer distance on BlendedMVS (BMVS) among compared methods and matches the geometric accuracy of leading reconstruction methods on DTU.

</details>


### [243] [One, No One and One Hundred Thousand CTs: Testing Anatomical Control in Text-to-CT Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33003)
*Francesca Pia Panaccione,Eugenio Lomurno,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CtrlCT 评测工具包，用"可信度"与"可控性"两类测试统一评估 Text-to-CT 生成器，并用"报告卡"汇总结果，揭示可信度与可控性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Text-to-CT 生成器在预处理与指标上各自为政，难以横向比较；且几乎没有在固定种子下检验"编辑报告是否会按预期改变 CT"的协议，导致"报告控制解剖结构"这一假设缺乏证据。

Method: 设计两类测试：可信度测试将生成 CT 群体与真实 CT 及报告比较；可控性测试基于蜕变关系、无需参考 CT——固定种子下编辑单一发现应产生对应变化、被否定的发现应保持缺失、远处解剖变化不应超过换种子的影响，且该效应需跨种子稳定。以 Report Card 汇总各生成器的全部指标与结论。在 5 个公开生成器、940 例胸部 CT、687 次胸腔积液与心脏扩大编辑上评测，并留出第 6 个生成器做工具包泛化验证。

Result: 最可信的生成器对新增积液的响应极弱；响应最强的生成器生成的真实性与多样性较差。文献中已有的分析无法揭示这一权衡关系。发布 CtrlCT、编辑数据、种子及 30,525 张合成 CT。

Conclusion: Text-to-CT 生成器的可信度与可控性存在显著取舍，评测必须同时覆盖两者；CtrlCT 提供了可复现、可跨模型比较的标准化协议。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning and the public availability of computed tomography (CT) datasets with paired reports have enabled many Text-to-CT generators. However, their evaluations almost always differ in preprocessing and metrics, which hinders comparison. Moreover, their protocols almost never test how editing the report changes the CT, not even at the same seed, so the report is often assumed to control the anatomy without evidence of how much and how consistently it does. We therefore propose CtrlCT, a toolkit that tests two properties of a generator, Credibility and Control. Credibility tests compare generated CTs, as a population, with real CTs and reports; Control tests are metamorphic and need no reference CT: at a fixed seed, editing one finding should change it as requested, keep negated findings absent and shift distant anatomy no more than a new seed, and the change should hold across seeds. A Report Card summarises all measures and insights for each generator. We benchmark five public generators on 940 chest CTs and 687 edits of pleural effusion and cardiomegaly, holding out a sixth to test the toolkit. Among them, the most credible generators barely respond to an added effusion, while the most responsive produce less realistic and less diverse CTs. The analyses proposed in the literature do not reveal this. We release CtrlCT, its edits, seeds and 30,525 synthetic CTs after acceptance.

</details>


### [244] [Train What You Run: Probabilistic Convolutions for Learning Token Compaction in State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33009)
*Piotr Kubaty,Mikhail Saladkou,Jacek Tabor,Łukasz Struski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProbConv 用"保留概率"下激活前的精确期望卷积替代硬性 token 剪枝，使可微的 token 压缩在局部卷积序列模型中正确考虑邻域变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉 token 压缩（只把部分图像 patch 或视频 token 传向后续层）可降低推理成本，但在含局部卷积的序列模型（如 Mamba）中，每个被删除的 token 都会改变其邻居关系，因此"该保留哪些 token"的学习信号必须对新邻域结构建模；现有硬性门控/剪枝训练忽略了这一邻域漂移。

Method: 提出 ProbConv：在独立保留决策假设下，于被保留位置计算激活前的精确期望卷积；该算子对 keep 概率可微，因此可用概率门控端到端训练；在二值决策下 ProbConv 与 step gating 退化为压缩后的 Mamba block。评估覆盖图像、视觉-语言、视频骨干网络，并延伸到视频帧选择与语音帧丢弃。

Result: 在强压缩设置下优势最明显，且相对匹配训练对照的优势随删除 token 比例增加而扩大；在 Cobra 上于同等推理预算下优于已发表视觉-语言剪枝方法的适配版本；在视频帧选择与语音帧丢弃任务上优于匹配的 zero-mask 训练。

Conclusion: 将邻域变化纳入期望意义下的可微卷积，是提升局部卷积序列模型 token 压缩鲁棒性与压缩率的关键；该算子可作为通用、模型无关的压缩训练目标替换硬门控。

Abstract: Visual token compaction reduces inference cost by passing only selected image patches or video tokens to later layers. In sequence models with local convolutions, each removal changes which tokens are neighbors. Learning which tokens to keep therefore requires a training signal that accounts for the new neighborhoods. We introduce ProbConv, which computes the exact expected convolution before activation at a kept position under independent keep decisions. The operator is differentiable in the keep probabilities. With binary decisions, ProbConv and step gating reproduce the compacted Mamba block. Across image, vision--language and video backbones, ProbConv is strongest under strong compaction: its advantage over matched training controls grows as more tokens are removed. In Cobra, it also outperforms adaptations of published vision--language pruning methods at matched inference budgets. ProbConv also improves over matched zero-mask training in video frame selection and speech frame dropping. Our code is available at: this https URL

</details>


### [245] [Embodied AI Fails the Mirror Test: How a Simple Mirror Breaks Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33010)
*Xuanchen Liu,Hejun Zhang,Boxuan Dong,Ying Wu,Heshuai Li,Wen Gao,Yonghong Tian,Weihao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RoboDojo-Mirror 基准，考察含镜面反射场景下机器人操作策略失效现象，发现多种 VLA/WAM/agentic 策略成功率显著下降，且非碰撞所致，归因于"幻影目标"与空间定位混乱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受动物镜像自我识别测试启发，检验当前机器人操作策略能否在有自身及物体反射的场景中可靠操作物理物体，揭示反射环境这一被忽视的鲁棒性盲区。

Method: 基于 RoboDojo 构建 RoboDojo-Mirror，保持任务目标、成功判据与策略权重不变，设置无镜、单镜、双镜、镜面桌面四种条件；用几何匹配的非反射面板作对照，结合轨迹与接触分析及针对性诊断（近反射抓取、推动进展、主相机遮挡下的真实目标交互）；并在真机上用 π0.5 与 FastWAM 验证。

Result: 五种策略（VLA、World Action Model、agentic）在所有镜面条件下成功率均下降，三条件平均下降 10.8–56.7 个百分点，镜面桌面条件最高达 62.5 点；50–88% 的镜面特有失败无机器人—面板接触记录，排除单纯碰撞解释；轨迹/接触分析显示幻影目标瞄准与真实目标空间错定位；真机实验同样显示成功率下降。

Conclusion: 多样机器人策略在反射环境中存在共性脆弱性，凸显具身智能评测需引入反射感知维度；该基准可作为诊断工具推动反射鲁棒策略研究。

Abstract: Can a simple mirror expose the limitations of today's robotic manipulation policies? Inspired by the mirror self-recognition test in animal cognition, we investigate whether robot policies can reliably manipulate physical objects in scenes containing their reflections. We introduce RoboDojo-Mirror, a benchmark built on RoboDojo that preserves task objectives, success criteria, and policy checkpoints while comparing a mirror-free baseline with single-mirror, dual-mirror, and mirrored-tabletop conditions. Across five policies spanning Vision-Language-Action models, World Action Models, and agentic policies, every mirror condition reduces aggregate success rates: averaged over the three conditions, the drop ranges from 10.8 to 56.7 percentage points across policies, and it reaches 62.5 points on the mirrored tabletop. Because a mirror is also a physical obstacle, we compare it against geometrically matched non-reflective panels; 50--88\% of mirror-specific failures occur without recorded robot--panel contact, so direct collisions alone do not explain the degradation. Trajectory and contact analyses instead reveal recurring behaviors consistent with phantom targeting of reflected objects and spatial mislocalization of real ones. Targeted diagnostics further characterize grasping near reflections, policy-dependent pushing progress, and physical-target interaction under main-camera occlusion. Real-robot experiments with $\pi_{0.5}$ and FastWAM also show lower overall success rates under the upright-mirror and mirrored-tabletop conditions. These findings reveal a shared vulnerability to reflective environments across diverse robot policies and motivate reflection-aware evaluation of embodied intelligence. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [246] [MESSI: Multi-Expert Soccer Skill Integration for Physics-Based Character Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33011)
*Xiaogang Peng,Jiaqi Zou,Lei Zhong,Sirui Xu,Huaizu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MESSI：先训练8个足球技能专家并蒸馏为技能条件Transformer学生，再在冻结学生上训练上下文条件LoRA适配器，仅在技能切换时激活并在习得退出机制后交还控制权，实现技能平滑衔接且不损害原技能策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理仿真足球角色需连续执行多种运动技能，单项技能虽在孤立条件下表现良好，但前序行为会把角色与球带入分布外状态，导致顺序执行失败；为每种切换单独重训专家代价过高。

Method: 八技能专家由动作先验与任务奖励训练；蒸馏为统一技能条件Transformer学生；在冻结学生上以在线RL训练共享的上下文条件低秩适配器（技能切换时激活，退出机制学习判定何时归还控制），原生技能执行时禁用适配器以结构性保留原策略。

Result: 单技能、技能切换与连续技能链实验均显著优于直接切换，带球转射门成功率由19.8%提升至61.8%。

Conclusion: 以“状态进入修复+退出判定”的轻量共享适配器替代逐切换重训，是物理角色多技能组合的可扩展方案，且通过构造保证原技能能力不退化。

Abstract: Physics-based soccer characters must perform diverse motor skills and transition smoothly between them, such as dribbling into a shot. However, skills that perform well in isolation may fail when executed sequentially, as preceding behaviors can leave the character and ball in unfamiliar states. We present MESSI (Multi-Expert Soccer Skill Integration), a framework that enables multiple soccer skill transitions through shared adaptation while preserving the original skill policy outside transitions. MESSI first trains eight task-competent experts using motion priors and task rewards, then distills them into a unified, skill-conditioned Transformer. Rather than retraining individual experts for different transitions, we train a shared, context-conditioned low-rank adapter on the frozen student through online reinforcement learning. Activated upon skill switching, the adapter helps the target skill handle unfamiliar entry states, while a learned exit mechanism determines when to return control to the original student. By disabling the adapter during native skill execution, MESSI preserves the original student policy by construction. Experiments across individual skills, skill transitions, and continuous skill chains demonstrate substantial improvements over direct switching, including an increase in dribbling-to-shooting success from 19.8% to 61.8%.

</details>


### [247] [RFW-W: Scaling Physical Asset Universes for Generative Embodied Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33013)
*Jiawei Weng,Rui Zhang,Saining Zhang,Changjian Pan,Yuanbo Lyu,Jiarui Guo,Yongzhi Li,Guolin Liu,Jialu Liu,Jiankun Wang,Yi Yang,Peishuo Li,Zhiheng Li,Jiaying Wan,Junhao Chen,Weiqing Xiao,Jingrun Du,Wenyi Li,Weiyi Lin,Zenan Meng,Hu Wei,Lin Qu,Zongzheng Zhang,Hao Zhao,Chaojian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RFW-W是一个统一刚体、铰接体与可变形体的百万级物理资产库（1,010,386条资产记录，2,228个数据集-类别条目），提供按资产类型保留物理约束的通用接口，并支持任务条件化场景组装；在RoboTwin五个任务上以RFW-R训练的策略宏成功率71.2%（干净）/41.8%（随机化），分别超最强基线15.8与10.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身仿真规模化需要多样化且带可执行物理表示的物体，而现有资产数据集在类别覆盖、物理属性（碰撞几何、关节结构、可变形表示）与任务条件化场景组装之间存在割裂。

Method: 构建三层资产体系：RFW-R（类别引导生成+碰撞几何、组合物理属性、交互标注，1,212类共524,611件）、RFW-A（可复用的agent编写程序化生成器，显式部件与关节结构，531类共402,410件）、RFW-S（结构感知仿真表示、标定与独立测试，485类共83,365件经联合验证的可变形体）；以统一接口按类型保留物理需求，用于检索与场景拼装。

Result: RFW-R在资产覆盖度与场景满足能力上优于现有数据集；RoboTwin五任务、每源每任务100条示教下，RFW-R策略宏成功率71.2%（干净）/41.8%（随机化），相对最强基线提升15.8/10.6个百分点。

Conclusion: RFW-W为生成物理落地、任务条件化的具身世界提供了可扩展的统一资产基础，可作为仿真数据与场景构建的底层设施。

Abstract: Scaling embodied simulation requires diverse objects with executable physical representations. We present RFW-W, a physical asset universe connecting rigid, articulated, and deformable asset sources to task-conditioned scene composition. Its reported inventories comprise 1,010,386 asset records: 524,611 rigid assets in 1,212 categories (RFW-R), 402,410 articulated assets in 531 categories (RFW-A), and 83,365 jointly verified deformables in 485 categories (RFW-S). These taxonomies contribute 2,228 dataset--category entries. RFW-R couples category-guided generation with collision geometry, composed physical properties, and interaction annotations; RFW-A uses reusable agent-authored procedural generators with explicit part and joint structure; RFW-S uses structure-aware simulation representations, calibration, and independent testing. A common interface preserves physical requirements specific to each asset type for retrieval and scene assembly. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate that RFW-R provides broader asset coverage and stronger scene-fulfillment capabilities than existing datasets. Across five RoboTwin tasks, using 100 demonstrations per task for each asset source, policies trained on RFW-R achieve macro success rates of 71.2% in clean settings and 41.8% under randomization, exceeding the strongest baseline by 15.8 and 10.6 percentage points, respectively. RFW-W provides a scalable and unified asset foundation for generating physically grounded, task-conditioned embodied worlds.

</details>


### [248] [Training Lightweight Gaze Models without Target Gaze Annotation: What Makes a Good Teacher?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33014)
*Scott J. Lewis,Di Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在注视目标估计任务中，通过11种监督条件的受控对比研究，发现未在目标数据集标注上训练的专家模型（Gaze-LLE）生成的标签可替代人工标注训练轻量学生模型，但部分大模型教师存在"抄坐标"捷径；且验证集标签误差可在训练学生前预测教师质量（Spearman ρ=0.91）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互机器人需要既准确又高效的注视估计模型；模型生成标签提供了可扩展的轻量视觉模型训练路径，但"什么样的模型是好教师"以及"能否在学生训练前预判教师质量"尚不清楚。

Method: 控制变量实验：固定学生模型、训练协议与361k训练样本，仅改变注视点标签来源（11种监督条件），覆盖3个视频数据集、2种学生规模（ViT-Tiny/ViT-Small）、3个随机种子，均以人工标注评估；并分析大模型教师对提示中人脸中心坐标的依赖（copy-the-coordinate基线）。

Result: (1) Gaze-LLE 生成的标签训练的 ViT-Tiny 学生与人标注训练持平，ViT-Small 学生显著更优（平均距离误差0.187 vs 0.196），ViT-Tiny 学生比教师快32倍；(2) InternVL3/Qwen3-VL在提示含他人脸部中心坐标时，分别有93.4%/81.1%的样本直接返回该坐标，复制坐标基线即可匹配InternVL3的标签与学生性能，去除坐标后两者远落后于专家模型；(3) 验证集标签误差可排序学生测试误差（ρ=0.91），仅10个标注片段在95%的抽样中挑出两个专家教师。

Conclusion: 提出实用原则：先评估监督信号质量、识别并排除捷径行为、在训练前筛教师；模型生成标签可作为人工标注的有效替代，但表面能力可能掩盖不可靠监督。

Abstract: Model-generated labels offer a scalable route to training lightweight vision models, but what makes a model a good teacher, and can we tell before training a student? We study this in gaze target estimation, where interactive robots need models that are both accurate and efficient. In a controlled comparison of eleven supervision conditions, we vary only the source of gaze-point labels, keeping the student, training protocol and 361k training samples fixed, across three video datasets, two student sizes and three seeds; every student is evaluated against human annotation. Three findings emerge. First, imperfect model-generated labels can replace human annotation: Gaze-LLE, never trained on the target datasets' gaze annotation, trains students that match annotation-trained ones on ViT-Tiny and are significantly more accurate on ViT-Small (average distance error 0.187 vs 0.196), and the ViT-Tiny student runs 32$\times$ faster than its teacher on a laptop CPU. Second, apparent competence can conceal unreliable supervision: given another person's face centre in the prompt, InternVL3 and Qwen3-VL return it on 93.4% and 81.1% of test samples, and a copy-the-coordinate baseline matches InternVL3's labels and students. Without that coordinate, both models fall far behind the specialist. Third, good teachers can be identified before training: validation label error ranks student test error (Spearman's $\rho = 0.91$), and ten annotated clips single out the two specialist teachers in 95% of draws. These findings give practical principles for assessing supervision, exposing shortcuts and selecting teachers for lightweight models.

</details>


### [249] [RePix-VFI: Re-rendering Observed Pixels for Video Frame Interpolation with Generative Priors from One-Step Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33016)
*Dayeon Kim,Jeonghyeok Do,Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RePix-VFI 将"单步草稿条件扩散"与"观测像素回读重渲染"耦合：扩散模型一次前向精修流渲染的草稿，再用光流把生成帧与两侧关键帧匹配，在 VAE 外回投原始 RGB 像素，轻量外观融合头合成最终帧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多帧 VFI 在大时间间隔下，流方法纹理保真但遮挡/歧义运动处错位；迭代隐扩散方法能合成合理内容，但采样代价高且 VAE 重建会改变细节。需要同时兼顾准确中间运动与忠实外观。

Method: 1) 预训练视频扩散模型单次前向精修整窗流渲染草稿；2) 生成式回读：用光流估计器把目标帧的两个边界关键帧与该预测帧建立对应；3) 依据对应关系在 VAE 之外重渲染原始关键帧 RGB 像素；4) 轻量外观融合头将重渲染结果与生成外观结合。

Result: 在 SNU-FILM ×8 与 ×16 上取得最高 PSNR 与最低 LPIPS、FloLPIPS、tOF，PSNR 分别超过最佳扩散基线 1.71 dB 与 1.48 dB；相比最强多帧 VFI 扩散模型快 32–42×。

Conclusion: "扩散精修草稿 + VAE 外像素回投"是缓解扩散 VFI 细节漂移与高采样成本的有效路径，说明观测像素保真与生成先验可低成本互补。

Abstract: Multi-frame video frame interpolation (VFI) aims to reconstruct dense video from sparse keyframes, requiring accurate intermediate motion and faithful appearance. As temporal gaps grow, flow-based methods can retain observed textures but misplace them under ambiguous motion, whereas iterative latent-diffusion models can synthesize plausible content but incur substantial sampling costs and may alter details during reconstruction with a variational autoencoder (VAE). We present RePix-VFI, named for Re-rendering observed Pixels, a multi-frame VFI framework that couples draft-conditioned one-step video diffusion with reconstruction from observed keyframes. A pretrained video diffusion model refines a flow-rendered draft of the whole window in a single forward pass. For each target frame, our generative readback matches its two bounding keyframes to the generated prediction using an optical-flow estimator. The resulting correspondences guide re-rendering of original keyframe RGB pixels outside the VAE. A lightweight appearance fusion head combines these renderings with generated appearance. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate significant improvements in reconstruction and perceptual quality. On SNU-FILM at $\times$8 and $\times$16, RePix-VFI achieves the highest PSNR and the lowest LPIPS, FloLPIPS, and tOF among the compared methods, exceeding the best evaluated diffusion-based baseline by 1.71 and 1.48dB in PSNR, respectively. It also runs 32--42$\times$ faster than the state-of-the-art multi-frame VFI diffusion model.

</details>


### [250] [E-MAPS: Event-Based Promptable Video Segmentation with Memory-as-Prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33017)
*Adonay Demewez Gebremedhin,Mohamad Alansari,Wessam Shehieb,Muzammal Naseer,Hasan AlMarzouqi,Sajid Javed,Naoufel Werghi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: E-MAPS将预训练可提示分割器迁移到事件流，通过Event-to-Token对齐与Memory-Aware Prompting把对象历史转为稀疏提示，统一记忆与用户输入于单解码器，形式化事件式可提示视频分割ePVS并构建EViM数据集与三个基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB视频目标分割在快速运动与极端光照下失效，事件相机虽抗模糊/曝光但慢速运动信息不足；交互可恢复对象状态，但现有可提示事件方法仅处理单一窗口，缺乏跨窗口初始化、修正与引入对象的统一形式化。

Method: 提出ePVS任务形式（任意窗口提示可初始化/修正/引入对象与预测）；E-MAPS将预训练可提示分割器迁移至事件流并学习传播与修正；Event-to-Token（E2T）对齐循环事件特征与预训练表征并保留提示条件先验；Memory-Aware Prompting（MAP）把对象条件历史转成稀疏提示，使记忆与用户输入共享同一解码路径；基于206K对齐RGB-事件对与620K掩码构建Event-View Masklets（EViM）用于两阶段表征迁移与原生事件视频学习。

Result: 三个训练不相交基准上，E-MAPS在prompt-once与交互式ePVS均领先且更高效，并在零样本目标跟踪基准上表现良好。

Conclusion: 事件相机+可提示记忆机制为VOS提供了鲁棒且高效的跨窗口交互范式，可作为下游具身感知与事件驱动跟踪的基础组件，但缺少机器人平台实机验证。

Abstract: RGB-based video object segmentation (VOS) remains brittle under fast motion and adverse illumination. Event cameras preserve signal under blur and challenging exposure, but provide little evidence when motion slows. Interaction can restore object state for subsequent propagation, yet promptable event methods merely address single windows. We formalize event-based Promptable Video Segmentation (ePVS), where prompts at arbitrary windows initialize, correct, or introduce objects and predictions. We present Event Memory-As-Prompt Segmentation (E-MAPS), which transfers a pretrained promptable segmenter to event streams and learns propagation and correction. Event-to-Token (E2T) aligns recurrent event features with pretrained representations while preserving prompt-conditioning priors. Memory-Aware Prompting (MAP) converts object-conditioned history into sparse prompts, allowing memory and user inputs to share one decoder pathway efficiently. We construct Event-View Masklets (EViM) from 206K aligned RGB-event pairs and 620K masks for two-stage representation transfer and native event-video learning, and establish three training-disjoint benchmarks. E-MAPS leads prompt-once and interactive ePVS across all three benchmarks while being more efficient. It further demonstrates strong performance on zero-shot object tracking benchmarks. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [251] [GRAPE-Bench: When Vision Degrades, Does Video Reasoning Hold?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33020)
*Ce Zhang,Junyi He,Tianshi Ming,Jinxi He,Jianshu Zhang,Xiyuan Yang,Jianuo Zhu,Yicheng Tao,Xiyu Luo,Minghao Fu,Jiahui Zhu,Kehan Guo,Yunzhong Xiao,Jingyang Lin,Wei Chow,Lingdong Kong,Katia Sycara,Yaqi Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRAPE-Bench: a unified benchmark jointly evaluating video reasoning under visual degradation (12 synthetic corruption types + real night/rain/snow/fog driving footage) and question difficulty (3 progressive levels), revealing ranking reversals between synthetic and real degradation and level-specific weaknesses; also shows fine-tuning and preference-steering routes to improve and control this trade-off.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Video reasoning models degrade under visual corruption, but existing benchmarks either simulate degradation synthetically or probe only hard reasoning questions — leaving unclear how models handle naturally degraded video and whether they can make the simple, safety-critical judgments that matter for driving.

Method: Constructs GRAPE-Bench by combining (1) a synthetic set with twelve corruption types over daily-life and driving videos and (2) real-world driving footage at night or in rain, snow, fog; each video is paired with three progressively harder questions (from basic safety-relevant judgments to reasoning stress tests). Evaluates open-weight and frontier models, then explores three interventions: synthetic-data fine-tuning (Qwen3.5-9B), clean-privileged on-policy distillation, and preference steering over a frozen model.

Result: Finds model ranking reversals between synthetic and real degradation conditions and difficulty-level-specific weaknesses hidden by aggregate accuracy. Synthetic-data fine-tuning lifts Qwen3.5-9B real-world accuracy 76.7%→81.1%; clean-privileged on-policy distillation reaches 80.4% in a reasoning-enabled setting; preference steering gives continuous control over the safety-judgment vs. hard-reasoning trade-off with little overall accuracy change.

Conclusion: GRAPE-Bench serves as a unified testbed for evaluating, improving, and controlling video reasoning under degraded visual conditions, and shows that aggregate accuracy masks robustness and difficulty-level failures that decompose evaluation exposes.

Abstract: Despite strong performance on standard benchmarks, video reasoning models remain vulnerable to visual degradation, raising concerns about their reliability in safety-critical scenarios. Existing benchmarks address this concern in part by simulating visual degradation or probing model reasoning with challenging questions, leaving open how well models handle naturally degraded video and whether they can make the simpler judgments that matter for safe driving. To this end, we introduce GRAPE-Bench, which jointly evaluates video reasoning across visual degradation conditions and question difficulty. It combines a synthetic set spanning twelve corruption types across daily-life and driving videos with real-world footage of driving at night or in rain, snow, or fog. Each video is paired with three progressively challenging questions, from fundamental judgments with potential safety implications to demanding reasoning stress tests. Across open-weight and frontier models, we observe ranking reversals between synthetic and real-world conditions and level-specific weaknesses obscured by overall accuracy. Synthetic-data fine-tuning improves Qwen3.5-9B's real-world accuracy from 76.7% to 81.1%, while clean-privileged on-policy distillation reaches 80.4% in a separate reasoning-enabled setting. Through preference steering, we further demonstrate continuous control over a frozen model's trade-off between safety-relevant judgments and challenging reasoning, with little change in overall accuracy. Together, GRAPE-Bench provides a unified testbed for evaluating, improving, and controlling video reasoning under degraded visual conditions.

</details>


### [252] [SHARPnP: Self-Supervised and Plug-and-Play Hyperspectral Super-Resolution from Real Noisy Unaligned Camera Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33032)
*Mohamad Jouni,Brayan Monroy,Jorge Bacca,Mauro Dalla Mura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SHARPnP：一个可解释、灵活的自监督框架，耦合配准与重建，实现无干净参考、含噪且未对齐条件下RGB引导的高光谱超分辨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快照高光谱相机空间分辨率低且有噪声；低成本RGB相机可提供可见光细节用于引导NIR重建，但存在空间变化的跨相机错配。现有监督融合方法依赖大量干净高分辨率高光谱数据，对噪声敏感，跨传感器泛化差。

Method: 以跨视角损失反向传播、经固定plug-and-play融合模块微调预训练配准模型；用时间对与重损坏对在含噪输入上实现无参考自监督；测试时做跨相机辐射定标与对齐精化；融合模块采用顺序三波段去噪。

Result: 在REAL基准上，干净输入下4×/8×/16×及高斯噪声（σ=5/255、10/255）下4×均优于监督融合基线；在实际RGB-HSI快照测量上重建全部51个VIS-NIR波段（超出常规31波段VIS设定），且无需高分辨率参考。

Conclusion: 配准-重建联合优化加无参考自监督可有效摆脱对干净高分辨参考数据的依赖，并提升噪声鲁棒性与跨传感器适应性。

Abstract: Affordable snapshot hyperspectral cameras capture spectral images in a single exposure but suffer from limited spatial resolution and sensor noise. Low-cost RGB cameras provide fine visible details to guide NIR reconstruction, but introduce spatially varying cross-camera misalignment. Supervised fusion methods require relatively large datasets of clean, high-resolution hyperspectral images, are sensitive to sensor noise, and generalize poorly across new sensors. We introduce SHARPnP, an interpretable, flexible framework coupling registration and reconstruction for RGB-guided hyperspectral super-resolution from noisy, unaligned measurements without clean references. We adapt a pretrained registration model via a cross-view loss backpropagated through a fixed plug-and-play fusion module. Temporal and recorrupted pairs enable reference-free self-supervision on noisy inputs. At test time, we calibrate cross-camera radiometry and refine alignment. Plug-and-play fusion employs sequential three-band denoising. On the REAL benchmark, SHARPnP outperforms supervised fusion baselines at $4\times$, $8\times$, and $16\times$ on clean inputs and at $4\times$ under Gaussian noise ($\sigma=5/255, 10/255$). On native RGB-HSI snapshot measurements, it reconstructs all 51 VIS-NIR bands, beyond the conventional 31-band VIS setting, without high-resolution references.

</details>


### [253] [Linear Probes Can Guide: Training-Free Control of Representation Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33037)
*Pengwei Wang,José Morano,Botond Fazekas,Hrvoje Bogunović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LiSM 框架，利用表示空间（RAE/VFM）的线性可读性构建"语义均值空间"，把语义引导转化为贝叶斯线性逆问题，实现免训练条件的后验均值级引导，并在类别/属性/前景/关键点四种控制任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉基础模型不仅加速训练与提升保真度，其表示空间高度组织化且线性可读；利用线性期望性质可消除事后引导的关键障碍——理想去噪器预测经线性探针后恰为探针得分在任意噪声水平下的后验均值，从而无需训练时条件化即可实现语义引导。

Method: 将语义引导表述为探针空间的贝叶斯线性逆问题；提出统一的"语义均值空间线性引导"框架 LiSM，形式化多种求解器并按"探针空间不确定性估计方式"与"校正向表示空间的传播方式"分类；新提出 Fit-Path 求解器以兼顾全局与局部控制。

Result: 在类别、属性、前景、关键点四种控制设定下，LiSM 系列方法（尤其 Fit-Path）提供了有效、轻量的全局与局部控制，优于噪声感知引导与像素空间引导。

Conclusion: 表示空间生成模型的线性可读性是可控生成的可利用结构资源；语义均值空间视角为免训练引导提供了理论清晰且实用的统一框架。

Abstract: Representation-space generative models, such as Representation Autoencoders (RAEs), leverage pretrained vision foundation models (VFMs) primarily to accelerate training and improve sample fidelity. Yet VFMs are more than reconstruction tokenizers, as they feature rich representation spaces that are highly organized and linearly readable. We observe that this removes a key obstacle of post-hoc guidance: by linearity of expectation, a clean probe applied to an ideal denoiser's prediction yields the exact posterior mean of the probe scores at any noise level. This regime, which we call Semantic Mean-Space, turns semantic guidance into a Bayesian linear inverse problem, bypassing the need for training-time conditioning. With this foundation, we propose LiSM, a unified framework for Linear Guidance in Semantic Mean-Space. To validate the framework, we developed and adapted different solvers, and characterized them based on how they estimate probe-space uncertainty and propagate the correction through the representation. In addition, we propose Fit-Path, a solver designed to achieve strong global and local control. Our results in four control setups (class, attributes, foreground, and keypoints) show that LiSM methods, and Fit-Path in particular, provide effective, lightweight control across global and local tasks, outperforming noise-aware and pixel-space guidance.

</details>


### [254] [Spectrify: Framework for Hyperspectral Image Generation Using Computer Graphics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33042)
*Yasin Mamedov,Daniil Vladimirov,Sofiia Dorogova,Bakhtiyar Khalfin,Egor Ershov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spectrify 将现有 3D 场景数据集经 Mitsuba 3 逐波长路径追踪渲染为高光谱图像（HSI），并配套一个按材料类别标注的实测反射率谱库；由此生成的 4,500 张合成 HSI 训练的高光谱重建网络在多数真实数据集上泛化优于用最大真实 HSI 数据集训练的模型，投影得到的 RAW 也可用于颜色恒常性训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实 HSI 采集慢、成本高，最大数据集仅约 1000 张；高层视觉可用 3D 渲染场景替代真实数据，但低层任务需要物理正确的光谱，现有光谱渲染场景极少且成像模型过度简化（缺少逐材料实测光谱）。

Method: 构建材料标注的反射率谱库（来自近距离高光谱数据集与分光光度计测量）；在 Spectrify 中让光谱跟随材质——每个物体按材料类别赋予基于物理的光学模型与实测反射率谱/光学常数，灯具赋予对应类型的发射谱，天空使用建模日光光谱；用 Mitsuba 3 对每个场景做逐波长的完整光传输路径追踪，输出 HSI。

Result: 仅用合成 HSI 训练的高光谱重建网络在多数测试集上对未见真实数据集的泛化优于用最大真实 HSI 数据集训练的基线；按材料类别（而非仅按颜色）分配光谱在全部测试集上均提升迁移效果；从 HSI 投影的 RAW 作为颜色恒常性训练数据与现有合成 RAW 生成器表现相当；发布 4,500 张渲染 HSI、代码与材料标注光谱库。

Conclusion: 光谱正确的物理渲染可作为一种可扩展的合成 HSI 数据来源，弥补真实高光谱数据稀缺，并同时服务低层高光谱重建与相机管线任务；材料级光谱标注是提升域迁移的关键因素。

Abstract: Hyperspectral images (HSIs) record the full spectrum of light at every pixel, so projecting an HSI through a camera's spectral sensitivities gives that camera's RAW image, and one HSI can supply training data for hyperspectral reconstruction and camera-pipeline methods. Real HSIs, however, are slow and expensive to capture, and the largest datasets hold only about 1,000 images. High-level vision commonly replaces scarce real data with images rendered from 3D scenes. Low-level tasks, however, need physically correct spectra, which requires spectral rendering with a measured spectrum for every material in a scene, and existing spectral renderings cover only a handful of scenes, mostly with a simplified image formation model. We present Spectrify, a framework that converts 3D scene datasets into HSIs, together with a library of reflectance spectra labeled by material and compiled from close-range hyperspectral datasets and spectrophotometer measurements. In Spectrify, spectra follow the material: each object receives a physically based optical model and measured reflectance spectra or optical constants from its material category, each lamp an emission spectrum of its type and the sky a modeled daylight spectrum, and Mitsuba 3 path-traces the full light transport of each scene per wavelength. We show that a hyperspectral reconstruction network trained only on our synthetic HSIs generalizes to unseen real datasets better than networks trained on the largest real HSI datasets on most test sets, and that assigning spectra by material category instead of by color alone improves this transfer on every test set. RAW images projected from our HSIs are also useful training data for color constancy, performing on par with existing synthetic RAW generators. We release the 4,500 rendered HSIs, the code of Spectrify and its material-labeled reflectance library.

</details>


### [255] [SUPRA: Synthesizing Amyloid PET from MRI and Plasma Biomarkers with an Explicit Regional SUVR Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33061)
*Hanwen Li,Fadi Deek,Zhi Wei,Ruixiang Tang,Feng Liu,Bharat Biswal,Michal Schnaider Beeri,Auriel Willette,Chenxi Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SUPRA: 用血浆生物标志物（p-tau217 等）回归出区域 SUVR 作为显式先验，贴到 MRI 分割图上，再由 3D 网络做残差校正，实现从 MRI+血液检测合成绝对 SUVR 的淀粉样蛋白 PET。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淀粉样 PET 昂贵且可及性差；结构 MRI 常规可得，血浆 p-tau217 日趋实用。但仅从 MRI 合成 PET 是病态问题，因为解剖结构几乎不含淀粉样负荷信息，需要外部信号补充淀粉样信息。

Method: SUPRA 将任务拆分：回归模型把血浆标志物面板映射为区域级 SUVR 水平，按受试者 MRI 分割标签"涂绘"成显式先验；3D 网络在 MRI 引导下学习残差校正；输出为绝对 SUVR 尺度，也支持仅用 p-tau217。消融验证血浆提供淀粉样信息、网络提供图像质量。

Result: ADNI 111 对测试：PSNR 23.4 dB（对比四个已发表模型最高 20.7 dB）；在复合皮层、早期沉积区与纹状体的真实 SUVR 秩相关 0.56–0.67（对比 ≤0.24）。第二个认知正常队列（其他血浆平台，143 对）：22.8 dB，相关性 0.32–0.61（对比 ≤21.4 dB / 0.20）。淀粉样阳性 AUC 0.91 与 0.82（对比 ≤0.63）。

Conclusion: MRI 加血液检测可生成逼真、区域级追踪淀粉样负荷的合成 PET；核心贡献是"信息源解耦"的先验引导合成范式，而非单纯的图像到图像翻译。

Abstract: Amyloid positron emission tomography (PET) quantifies brain amyloid-$\beta$ as a standardized uptake value ratio (SUVR), but is costly and not widely available. Structural magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is routine, and plasma biomarkers such as phosphorylated tau 217 (p-tau217) are becoming practical. Synthesizing amyloid PET from MRI alone is ill-posed, because brain anatomy reveals little about amyloid burden. We present SUPRA (SUVR prior-guided amyloid synthesis), which divides this task between MRI and plasma biomarkers. A regression maps the plasma panel to regional SUVR levels, painted onto the subject's MRI segmentation as an explicit prior. A 3D network then adds a residual correction guided by MRI. The output is in absolute SUVR, and the model also runs on p-tau217 alone. On the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort (111 test pairs), SUPRA reaches a peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) of 23.4 dB, against at most 20.7 dB for four published models, and rank correlations of 0.56 to 0.67 with true SUVR in the composite cortex, an early-accumulation region and the striatum, against at most 0.24. In a second, cognitively unimpaired cohort with other plasma platforms (143 test pairs), it reaches 22.8 dB and 0.32 to 0.61, against at most 21.4 dB and 0.20. Its area under the curve for amyloid positivity is 0.91 and 0.82, against at most 0.63. Ablations show that plasma supplies the amyloid information and the network the image quality. Combining MRI with a blood test yields synthetic amyloid PET that is realistic and tracks burden across regions.

</details>


### [256] [The Radar-based Activities of Daily Living (R-ADL) Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33088)
*Dominique Regli,Brian C. Williams*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出R-ADL雷达ADL数据集：8小时以上标注、8类高层日常活动、5个采集位置（两室两卫公寓），并给出面向临床显著ADL的选取依据、采集/标注/后处理流程与基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达感知兼顾隐私与穿透能力，但雷达活动识别受限于大规模数据集稀缺；现有数据集规模小、仅限单一房间配置、且多聚焦低层原子动作而非高层ADL。

Method: 先跨学术研究、健康调查工具与临床试验分析ADL优先级以确定临床显著活动；设计8类活动实验方案；在两室两卫公寓5个位置采集超过8小时标注数据；给出从原始数据到数据集的后处理流水线，并在标准机器学习架构上跑基线。

Result: 构建并公开R-ADL数据集（>8小时标注、8类ADL、5种房间位置），附带数据集构建实践指南与标准架构上的基线性能。

Conclusion: R-ADL可作为推进ADL识别系统研究的资源，未来需继续扩展规模与多样性；其构建方法论对未来ADL数据集设计有借鉴价值。

Abstract: Monitoring Activities of Daily Living (ADLs) supports safety and well-being for people living at home, especially older adults, and radar sensing is gaining increasing attention in the field of activity recognition for its ability to ensure privacy in the home while also surpassing technical limitations of other sensing modalities. However, radar-based activity recognition remains constrained by the limited availability of large-scale datasets. We introduce the Radar-based Activities of Daily Living (R-ADL) dataset to addresses the gaps identified in existing work: 1) it expands the scale of available data by providing over eight hours of annotated recordings in which a person performs eight distinct activities; 2) it overcomes room configuration limitations by recording from five different locations in a two-bedroom, two-bathroom apartment; and 3) it extends beyond primitive actions to focus on high-level ADLs. In addition to the dataset, this paper introduces practical guidance for future ADL dataset construction. We conducted a detailed analysis of how ADLs are prioritized across academic research, health survey tools, and clinical trials to identify clinically significant ADLs. Furthermore, we provide our experimental design, data collection and annotation strategies, post-processing pipeline for forming the R-ADL dataset from raw data, and baseline results on standard machine learning architectures. The R-ADL dataset serves as a valuable resource for advancing ADL recognition systems as we continue building larger and more diverse datasets.

</details>


### [257] [EventDiffusion: Learning Native Event Stream Distribution from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33096)
*Karthik Sundar Senthilkumar,Christopher Thirgood,Simon Hadfield*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EventDiffusion：分层生成框架，直接从真实事件相机录制中学习原生异步事件流的条件分布，分为事件活动扩散(EAD，离散计数)、事件空间分配(ESA，保基数像素分配)、事件时序扩散(ETD，连续时间戳)三个概率过程，并提出事件流真实感度量CEFS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机数据稀缺，稀疏RGB帧无法唯一确定中间事件流（同帧可对应不同活动、极性、时序的事件流）；现有模拟器依赖预设传感器动力学与估计亮度轨迹，假设限制生成事件流的多样性与保真度。

Method: 分层生成式建模：EventDiffusion将事件流生成分解为Event Activity Diffusion（学习随机离散事件计数）、Event Spatial Allocation（将计数分配到像素且保持基数不变）、Event Timing Diffusion（学习连续异步时间戳分布）；配套提出CEFS真实感指标，融合事件活动与极性、多尺度时空结构与时间保真度。

Result: 所有数据集上精度为SOTA方法的两倍；对不同相机、任意分辨率均具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 条件扩散式分层建模为事件流生成提供了不依赖预设传感器模型的可行路径，可用于缓解事件相机数据稀缺并提升下游数据驱动任务的多样性；CEFS提供了更全面的真实性评估。

Abstract: Event cameras capture asynchronous brightness changes at high temporal resolution, but scarce recordings constrain data-driven research. Sparse RGB observations cannot uniquely determine intervening events since identical frames may correspond to streams with different activity, polarities, and timing. Existing simulators largely rely on prescribed sensor dynamics and estimated brightness trajectories, whose assumptions limit the diversity and fidelity of plausible event streams. We introduce EventDiffusion, a hierarchical generative framework that directly learns the conditional distribution of native asynchronous event streams from real recordings. EventDiffusion decomposes generation into three probabilistic processes: Event Activity Diffusion (EAD) learns stochastic discrete event counts; Event Spatial Allocation (ESA) distributes these counts to individual pixels while preserving cardinality; and Event Timing Diffusion (ETD) learns continuous asynchronous timestamp distributions. We also introduce CEFS, an event-stream realism metric combining event activity and polarity with multiscale space-time structure and temporal fidelity. Across all datasets, EventDiffusion achieves double the accuracy of SOTA methods and demonstrates robustness across different cameras at any resolution.

</details>


### [258] [RenGen: Generating Images with as Many Tokens as the Scene Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33109)
*Savya Khosla,Jay Mahajan,Akin Tuna Sakalli,Derek Hoiem,David Forsyth,Alex Schwing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RenGen 在预训练区域编码器的隐空间中生成图像，每个 token 对应图像中一个形状、位置可变的区域，用随机采样的锚点表征区域，从而以"场景复杂度"而非"图像质量"换取更少 token，在 ImageNet 256×256 上以平均 88 token 达到 gFID 1.34。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流潜空间生成器将图像表示为固定网格的 patch token，简单图像与复杂图像的计算开销相同；已有内容自适应分词方法在减少 token 时会显著掉画质。作者希望让 token 数与场景复杂度挂钩，而非牺牲真实感。

Method: 基于预训练区域编码器的潜空间做扩散生成，每个 token 编码一个区域（区域编码器特征经池化，并在池化前加入位置嵌入以隐含空间范围）；生成时把每个 token 锚定到一个随机采样点，由生成器推断该区域的形状、大小与内容，再由解码器渲染回像素；采样时锚点数量控制复杂度—算力权衡。

Result: ImageNet 256×256 上平均 88 token 取得 gFID 1.34，在可变 token 数方法中最优；以约 2.5× 更低延迟接近 patch 基线的 SOTA 模型 RAEv2；gFID 在不同场景复杂度下基本持平，而延迟在 2.4× 范围内变化；自编码器以 2.9× 更少 token 达到接近的重建质量（rFID 0.32 vs. 0.30）。

Conclusion: 将 token 的语义从"固定 patch"改为"可变区域 + 锚点"可有效实现按场景复杂度自适应的计算分配，推进了生成质量—计算量的 Pareto 前沿；但实验限于 ImageNet 类图像，尚无关涉 3D/物理/机器人场景的验证。

Abstract: We present RenGen, a latent diffusion model that generates simple scenes from a few tokens and busy scenes from many, saving time and compute on simple scenes without losing realism. Most latent generators represent every image as a fixed grid of patch tokens, so a simple image costs as much to generate as a complex one. Prior methods that vary the token count through content-adaptive tokenization lose image quality as the count shrinks. RenGen instead trades scene complexity, rather than image quality, for fewer tokens. It does so by generating in the latent space of a pretrained region encoder, where each token represents one region of the image, and a decoder renders these region tokens back into pixels. Unlike patch tokens, region tokens cover areas of varying shape and position, so each token must capture both what its region contains and where it lies on the image canvas. We encode this spatial extent by adding positional embeddings to the encoder features before pooling, and we generate it by anchoring each token to a single randomly sampled point, from which the generator infers the region's shape, size, and content. At sampling time, the number of anchor points sets the trade-off between scene complexity and compute. On ImageNet $256{\times}256$, RenGen reaches a gFID of 1.34 with 88 tokens per image on average, the best among methods that vary their token count. It approaches the quality of RAEv2, a state-of-the-art patch-based model, at $2.5{\times}$ lower latency, advancing the Pareto frontier of generation quality versus compute. RenGen's gFID stays flat across scene complexity while latency varies by $2.4{\times}$ between simplest and busiest scenes, and its autoencoder reconstructs nearly as well as RAEv2 (rFID 0.32 vs. 0.30) with $2.9{\times}$ fewer tokens.

</details>


### [259] [BriConstrain: Geometrically Constrained Decoding for Autoregressive Brick Assembly Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33135)
*Nadav Magar,Amir Hertz,Ariel Shamir,Or Patashnik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 BriConstrain 约束解码方法：通过约束"如何连接"而非"连接哪一个"，用预计算几何 oracle 动态过滤碰撞，对下一个 token 分布加偏置，使预训练语言模型生成无碰撞、有效连接的 LEGO 拼装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型生成 LEGO 拼装时常见部件互相穿透或悬空未连接。常用的"拒绝采样+重采样"会丢弃已解码 token 且无法保证最终有效；而砖块放置合法性依赖非预定的当前构建状态，无法编译为固定文法，且合法放置空间组合爆炸无法穷举，因此难以在采样前校验。

Method: BriConstrain 在采样前限制模型下一个 token 的概率。关键思路是把搜索空间从"哪些部件相互连接"收窄为"已选定的部件与父部件之间的相对连接方式"：给定模型预测的当前部件及其要连接的父部件，遍历二者所有可能的连接变换，借助预计算几何 oracle 校验（含碰撞检测），保留合法候选后对模型下一 token 分布施加偏置。在两种格式上验证：BrickNet（显式标注连通性）与作者提出的 ICLF（隐式连通性 LDraw 格式，仅用数值表示放置）。

Result: 无碰撞构建比例 BrickNet 由 31% 提升至 91%，ICLF 由 15% 提升至 65%；在两种格式上均优于拒绝采样，且解码开销约为后者的 60%。

Conclusion: 将结构合法性校验内嵌进解码而非后置重采样，可用几何 oracle 动态约束 token 分布，兼顾有效性与推理效率；该思路可迁移到其他需要动态状态校验的结构化生成任务（如装配、布局生成）。

Abstract: Language models can generate LEGO$^\circledR$ assemblies from text, but their builds often contain parts that collide or attach to nothing. The usual approach of rejecting and resampling invalid placements discards decoded tokens without guaranteeing a valid build. We present BriConstrain, a constrained decoding method that restricts a pretrained language model's next-token probabilities before sampling, steering it towards valid placements. Because the validity of a brick placement depends on the non-predetermined build state, it cannot be compiled into a fixed grammar and must be checked dynamically. However, checking the validity of a brick before sampling is challenging as the space of valid placements is combinatorially large and cannot be enumerated exhaustively. Our key idea is to narrow the search space by constraining how parts attach rather than which parts attach to each other. Concretely, given the model's predictions of the currently placed part, and the previously placed parent it wants to connect to, we constrain the model's choice of how they attach. This allows us to span the space of part attachments, using a precomputed geometric oracle, and to effectively filter out colliding options. With valid attachment candidates in hand, we bias the model's next-token distribution towards the remaining valid candidates. We evaluate our method on two LEGO$^\circledR$ formats and models: BrickNet, which annotates connectivity explicitly, and our proposed Implicit Connectivity LDraw Format (ICLF), which uses numeric values for placements and leaves connectivity implicit. Our method raises collision-free builds from 31% to 91% on BrickNet and from 15% to 65% on ICLF, and outperforms rejection sampling on both formats at about 60% of its decoding cost.

</details>


### [260] [Physicl Scenes: An Artist-created 3D Dataset for Learning Perception and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33137)
*Yueh-Cheng Liu,Snehal Jauhri,Matthias Nießner,Angela Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 发布6,499个艺术家创作、含几何/PBR材质与对象及部件级标注的3D场景数据集，在逆渲染、3D重建、场景理解与移动操作上带来性能提升，并引入带抓取部位评分的移动操作基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉与具身学习缺乏兼具精细几何、PBR材质、场景组成与对象/部件级语义标注的大规模高质量3D场景数据，合成数据替代方案的监督信号不足。

Method: 构建Physicl Scenes数据集：6,499个由美术师创作与策划的3D场景，覆盖家庭房间、公共室内与户外空间，包含精细几何、基于物理的材质、精心布置的环境，并提供对象级与部件级语义标注；在逆渲染、3D重建、场景理解、移动操作四类任务上作为训练数据验证，并新增移动操作基准，评测VLA模型在真实布局场景中的物理抓取放置，利用部件标注评估抓取位置。

Result: 相较其他合成数据集，用该数据训练SOTA方法在上述任务上均取得性能提升；新基准可量化VLA模型抓取部位准确性。

Conclusion: 该高质量、联合标注的3D场景数据集为视觉与具身AI研究提供有价值的训练与评测资源，将公开发布。

Abstract: We present Physicl Scenes, a high-quality artist-created and curated dataset of 6,499 3D scenes spanning household rooms, public interiors, and outdoor spaces. The dataset combines detailed geometry, physically based materials, and carefully composed environments with object- and part-level semantic annotations, providing rich, joint supervision for visual and embodied learning. We demonstrate its value as training data across inverse rendering, 3D reconstruction, scene understanding, and mobile manipulation. Across these tasks, training state-of-the-art methods with our scenes improves performance over alternative synthetic datasets. In addition, we introduce a mobile manipulation benchmark, evaluating vision-language-action models performing physical pick and place in diverse 3D scenes with realistic layouts, and using our part annotations to also score where each object is grasped. Our dataset will be released publicly to facilitate research in visual and embodied AI.

</details>


### [261] [Lost on Campus: Evaluating Embodied Spatial Reasoning of Vision-Language Models in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33145)
*Zehan Zheng,Yanyuan Chen,Deming Li,Yutao Tang,Jianwen Xie,Alan Yuille,Jieneng Chen,Cheng Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Lost on Campus 基准，用3D高斯泼溅重建大规模真实户外校园场景，统一诊断式QA与闭环交互导航，评估VLM具身空间推理的六个能力维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ESR评测局限于静态图像或合成室内环境，语义捷径足以通过，无法忠实衡量真实野外场景中的空间推理能力。

Method: 基于3D Gaussian Splatting重建真实户外3D校园环境；构建"推理-行动"统一框架，结合孤立推理的诊断性QA与多模态指令下的闭环交互导航；按动作落地、空间前瞻、度量感知、目标导向规划、自我-他我定位、时空一致性六维度细分诊断。

Result: 实验显示：(i) 户外真实环境对空间推理要求显著高于室内，现有模型表现差；(ii) VLM细粒度视觉对齐仍不足，空间前瞻与自我-他我定位是主要瓶颈；(iii) 多模态交互与长时程空间推理能力对具身智能至关重要。

Conclusion: 忠实评测ESR需要将感知、推理与行动紧耦合于真实连续环境的基准；当前VLM在户外具身空间推理上能力缺口明显。

Abstract: Embodied Spatial Reasoning (ESR) is central to deploying Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in real-world embodied tasks, yet remains inadequately evaluated. Existing benchmarks primarily assess spatial reasoning in static images or synthetic indoor environments, where semantic shortcuts often suffice, making it difficult to faithfully evaluate spatial reasoning capabilities in the wild. In light of this, we present Lost on Campus, a benchmark for evaluating ESR in large-scale real-world outdoor 3D environments reconstructed by 3D Gaussian Splatting. Our benchmark introduces a unified reasoning-action evaluation framework that seamlessly integrates diagnostic QA for isolated reasoning with closed-loop interactive navigation for active reasoning under multimodal instructions. To enable fine-grained diagnosis, we evaluate six capability aspects of ESR: action grounding, spatial foresight, metric awareness, goal-directed planning, ego-allocentric localization, and spatio-temporal consistency. Extensive experiments reveal that: (i) Compared to indoor settings, real-world outdoor environments impose substantially higher demands on spatial reasoning, where existing models exhibit poor performance; (ii) VLMs still struggle with fine-grained visual alignment, with failures in spatial foresight and ego-allocentric localization emerging as primary bottlenecks; (iii) Improving multimodal interaction capabilities and long-term spatial reasoning is crucial for advancing embodied intelligence. These findings highlight that faithful evaluation of ESR demands benchmarks tightly coupling perception, reasoning and action within realistic and continuous environments.

</details>


### [262] [One Take, Many Cameras: Audio-Grounded Shot Synthesis for Dialogue Re-Cinematography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33147)
*Anthony Chen,Naomi Ken Korem,Matan Ben-Yosef,Tavi Halperin,Urska Jelercic,Ofir Bibi,Daniel Cohen-Or,Or Patashnik,Maneesh Agrawala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出对话再摄影（dialogue re-cinematography）任务：从单段广角对话视频合成新的机位镜头（特写、中景、过肩），利用语音的视角不变性驱动目标视角下的唇动，并以训练无关的说话人感知音频门控（speaker-aware audio gating）消除多人场景中的语音泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影对话场景需多机位覆盖，但多机位拍摄成本高、很多场景无法实现。现有视频生成器仅依赖视觉线索，当源视频中嘴唇几乎不可见时，生成唇动与语音不同步；多人场景中预训练音视频模型还会让所有可见人脸按同一语音说话，产生“静默听者复述说话人台词”的语音泄漏。

Method: 核心观察：语音音频是视角不变的，可在任意目标视角约束唇动。基于此，改编预训练音视频扩散模型，输入目标帧（指定视角）+ 源视频（提供表演内容）+ 语音音频（驱动唇动）来生成新镜头；并引入说话人感知音频门控——一个无需训练的模块，仅让音频驱动目标镜头中真正说话的人脸。

Result: 在覆盖多视角与多景别的电影配对视角基准上，相较最强基线 Wan 3.0：感知误差降低 59%，关键点（landmark）误差降低 56%，语音泄漏降低 97%；在真实野外场景中亦保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 语音作为视角不变条件可有效解决单视角视频重打光式重摄影中的唇动一致性问题，训练无关的音频门控可解决多人语音泄漏；方法具备应用于影视后期制作的潜力。但评测以感知指标为主，缺少电影级主观质量与长时序一致性分析。

Abstract: Creating a dialogue scene typically requires cutting between multiple views (e.g., close-ups, medium shots, and over-the-shoulder shots) of the actors. Yet capturing such coverage is costly and in many settings unavailable. We study dialogue re-cinematography: synthesizing additional shots from a single wide-angle dialogue video. Existing video generators can synthesize new shots, but rely on visual cues alone, so when the source video barely shows the lips, the generated lip motion fails to match the speech. Our key observation is that the speech audio is view-invariant: it constrains lip motion in any target view. Building on this insight, we adapt a pretrained audio-visual diffusion model to generate the new shot from a target frame that specifies the view, a source video that contains the performance, and the speech audio that drives lip motion. In multi-person scenes, however, pretrained audio-visual models drive every visible face with the same speech, causing speech leakage, where silent listeners mouth the speaker's words. We therefore introduce speaker-aware audio gating, a training-free module that lets the audio drive only the faces that speak in the target shot. On a cinematic paired-view benchmark spanning diverse viewpoints and framings, our method lowers perceptual error by 59%, landmark error by 56%, and speech leakage by 97% relative to the strongest baseline, Wan 3.0. Our method also remains robust on in-the-wild scenes, highlighting its potential for film post-production.

</details>


### [263] [BioLoom: Scaling Biomedical Vision-Language Models by Learning from the Structure of Scientific Articles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33151)
*Yuwei Yang,Xiaoxiao Sun,Alejandro Lozano,Yuan-Sen Ting,Yuhui Yuan,Liang Zheng,Serena Yeung-Levy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BioLoom: 利用科学文献结构（图表、分图、标题、正文段落）构建层次化对比监督，发布52.4M图像-文本对数据集Loom52M，训练BioLoom(DFN-2B)生物医学VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学VLM依赖大规模图文对扩容，但现有做法把论文中的图、分图、标题、正文当作独立图文对，忽略了它们之间的结构关系，浪费了文献自身蕴含的监督信号。

Method: 将文献结构注入对比学习：保留精确图文匹配为正样本，对相关但不对等的pair（如分图与兄弟分图的标题）赋予较弱正监督；对同内容类型的跨文章负样本上采样权重以强化细粒度区分；用视觉内容+原图标题+提及段落共同丰富分图描述；分图配丰富描述、整图配原标题，构成52.4M对的Loom52M数据集，训练DFN-2B规模的BioLoom。

Result: 在10个领域43个数据集共67个零样本分类任务上域平衡平均准确率56.82%（MedPMC-CLIP为48.77%）；BioLoomBench双向平均Recall@1为63.20%，BioMedFlickr为10.59%，对应最强基线BiomedCLIP为46.37%、MedPMC-CLIP为7.55%。

Conclusion: 数据"结构"而非单纯"规模"可提供额外监督，层次化弱正样本与难负样本加权显著提升生物医学图文检索与零样本分类；数据集、基准与模型均开源。

Abstract: The development of biomedical vision-language models (VLMs) with contrastive image-text pretraining has been driven by scaling to increasingly large and diverse image-text datasets. Beyond scaling, further progress can come from learning richer representations from existing data. We hypothesize that the structure of scientific literature itself provides an additional source of supervision: figures, panels, captions, and accompanying article text encode rich relationships across modalities. Yet, treating these as independent image-text pairs overlooks their relationships. To address this limitation, we incorporate these structural relationships into contrastive image-text supervision. We preserve exact image-text matches while introducing weaker positive supervision for related pairs, such as a panel and the caption of a sibling panel. We upweight cross-article negatives of the same content type to encourage finer discrimination. We also enrich panel descriptions using visual content, original figure captions, and mentioning paragraphs. Pairing panels with these descriptions and whole figures with their original captions yields Loom52M, a dataset of 52.4 million image-text pairs spanning scientific charts and biomedical images. Using Loom52M, BioLoom (DFN-2B) achieves 56.82% domain-balanced mean accuracy across 67 zero-shot classification tasks from 43 datasets in 10 domains, compared with 48.77% for MedPMC-CLIP. On BioLoomBench with concise captions and BioMedFlickr, it achieves bidirectional mean Recall@1 of 63.20% and 10.59%, versus 46.37% and 7.55% for the respective strongest evaluated baselines, BiomedCLIP and MedPMC-CLIP. Code, datasets, benchmarks, and models are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [264] [DiaCap: Jointly Learning Who Speaks When for Audiovisual Dialogue Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33153)
*Binfei Ji,Zhiwen Cao,Anqi Yang,Prem Seetharaman,Shuo Yang,Ratheesh Kalarot,Yingyan Celine Lin,Mingze Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiaCap：统一音视频对话字幕的LLM，一次性输出每句话的转写文本、时间戳与说话人视觉描述；先用225K合成标注片段训练，再用2.9K人工纠正片段做强化学习微调（奖励直接评估时间戳、说话人分组与绑定），在DiaDemBench上时间戳准确率显著超过Gemini 4 Argon。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有字幕/说话人日志（diarization）方法与语音活动检测、说话人绑定任务割裂，导致逐句时间戳与音视频说话人归属难以同时精确；作者希望以单一多模态LLM统一 utterance timing 与 audiovisual diarization，并用细粒度反馈提升时序精度。

Method: 两阶段训练：(1) 225K 精编视频片段上，用密集标注每句话细节的合成字幕做监督训练；(2) 2.9K 人工纠正片段上做强化学习，反馈信号分别针对语句时序、说话人分组、说话人-话语绑定打分。模型支持 off-the-shelf ASR 转写输入与端到端变体 DiaCap-E2E，输出 transcript + timestamps + speaker 视觉描述。

Result: 在扩展了语句级时间戳与字幕遮挡（subtitle redaction）的 DiaDemBench 上，DiaCap 与 DiaCap-E2E 均表现强劲；音视频说话人日志与 Gemini 4 Argon 持平，时间戳准确率大幅领先（76.59 vs. 42.76 TIME）；在多语言与分钟级长视频上保持稳健，并可迁移至音视频问答。

Conclusion: 将语句时序与音视频说话人日志统一进单个多模态LLM，并结合合成数据监督+针对性强化学习，是提升对话级字幕精度的有效路径；模型具备跨语言、长视频与下游VQA的泛化能力。

Abstract: We present DiaCap, an audiovisual LLM for comprehensive dialogue captioning, which produces the transcript, timestamps, and a visual description of the speaker for every utterance in a video. Considering that utterance timing and audiovisual diarization are tightly coupled, DiaCap unifies the tasks in a single model, trained in two complementary stages. First, we train DiaCap on 225K curated clips, using synthetic captions that densely label every utterance in detail. Second, we further refine the model via reinforcement learning on 2.9K human-corrected clips, using fine-grained feedback that directly evaluates utterance timing, speaker grouping, and speaker binding. On DiaDemBench, which we extend with utterance-level timestamps and subtitle redaction, both DiaCap with off-the-shelf transcripts and its end-to-end variant DiaCap-E2E achieve strong results. Specifically, DiaCap performs on par with Gemini 4 Argon in audiovisual diarization while clearly outperforming it in timestamp accuracy (76.59 vs. 42.76 TIME). It also maintains strong diarization on multilingual and minute-long videos, and transfers to audiovisual question answering.

</details>


### [265] [PointX: Multi-View Consistent Intrinsics by Querying Surface Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33157)
*Miguel Farinha,Sam Sartor,Iliyan Georgiev,Giuseppe Vecchio,Ronald Clark,Michael Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PointX 提出前馈式模型，将场景内蕴属性（albedo、roughness、metallicity、normal、irradiance）从逐像素预测改为逐 3D 表面点预测：多视图 Transformer 编码无位姿图像，对任意查询点跨视图汇聚 token 后一次性解码，从而天然获得跨视图一致的内蕴分解，并支持一致的重光照与编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐像素内蕴分解本质上是病态的，同一表面点在不同视角下会得到不同分解结果，导致跨视图不一致，难以支撑可靠的重光照与编辑。作者希望以 3D 表面点为预测单元，从构造上消除这种视角不一致性。

Method: 1) 采用多视图 Transformer 编码一组未标定位姿的图像；2) 对给定 3D 重建（点云或网格）上的任意表面点，通过几何/对应关系在可见视图间池化该点的 token；3) 解码器对每个点仅输出单次内蕴预测；4) 通过在每像素处查询对应表面点，同一模型亦可输出逐视图内蕴图，单图或多图输入均可。

Result: 跨视图一致性显著优于现有方法；单视图精度与现有方法相当，并随输入视图增加而持续提升；逐视图内蕴图在几何与对应精度的范围内保持一致。

Conclusion: 以 3D 表面点为查询单元的前馈内蕴分解，可在不牺牲单图精度的前提下获得构造级跨视图一致性，为逆向渲染、重光照与场景编辑提供更实用的表示。

Abstract: The appearance of a scene is determined by the albedo, roughness, metallicity and normal of every point on its surface, and by the light that reaches it, its irradiance. Recovering these intrinsics from images is ambiguous, and methods that predict them per pixel give the same surface point a different decomposition in every view. We introduce PointX, a simple feed-forward model that predicts intrinsics per 3D surface point instead. A multi-view transformer encodes a set of unposed images, and for any queried surface point, PointX pools the point's tokens across the views in which it appears and decodes the point's intrinsics once. Each point of a 3D reconstruction of the scene, such as a point cloud or a mesh, thus receives a single prediction, which is exactly consistent across views by construction and enables view-consistent relighting and editing. The same model also predicts per-view maps, from one image or many, by querying the point seen at every pixel. These maps agree across views up to the precision of the estimated geometry and correspondences. PointX is significantly more consistent than existing methods, while remaining competitive in accuracy from a single image and improving as views are added. Project page: this https URL

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [266] [ArEssays: Towards Robust Arabic Handwritten Text Understanding in Educational Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32211)
*Hamdy Mubarak,Anas Madkoor,Ehsaneddin Asgari,Majd Hawasly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ArEssays：面向埃及小学到中学学生手写阿拉伯语作文的大规模数据集（逐字转写＋语言规范化版本），并基准测试34个VLM/OCR模型，揭示真实学生手写体上的显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语手写体因书写系统复杂、儿童书写变异大而难以自动识别；现有资源缺乏真实学生手写作文数据，教育场景下的文档理解与OCR缺少评测基准。

Method: 构建覆盖埃及小学至中学真实作文的数据集，提供逐字转写与语言矫正双版本标注；对34个视觉-语言模型与OCR模型（含微调系统）进行统一基准评测，并开源数据集、训练流程与结果。

Result: 主流VLM/OCR模型在真实学生手写体上表现存在大幅性能落差，微调可部分改善但整体仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 为阿拉伯语OCR、文档理解与教育NLP提供公开基准，呼吁针对儿童手写变异性设计更强的识别与规范化方法。

Abstract: Automated understanding of handwritten Arabic student essays remains challenging due to script complexity and high variability in children's writing. We introduce ArEssays, a large-scale dataset of authentic essays from primary to secondary students across Egypt, with both verbatim transcriptions and linguistically corrected versions. We benchmark 34 vision-language and OCR models, including fine-tuned systems, and find large performance gaps on real student handwriting. We release the dataset, training pipelines, and results to support research in Arabic OCR, document understanding, and educational NLP.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [The Fingerprinting Paradox: Why Sensitivity Undermines Black-Box Model Integrity Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31455)
*Shahinul Hoque,Farhin Farhad Riya,Jinyuan Sun,Fnu Suya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示黑盒模型完整性验证中"敏感指纹"的双刃性：相对真实输入过度敏感的验证查询可被不诚信提供方统计识别并选择性掩盖篡改，证明敏感度超量下界了可区分性，并在5种框架×3架构×3数据集×3知识设定下实证检测AUC≥0.989。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒完整性验证框架普遍以"敏感指纹"（篡改下预测更易翻转的查询）提升验证效率，但忽略了提供方可能主动自适应对抗；若指纹相对真实输入过于敏感，提供方可据此识别验证查询并定向隐藏篡改，从而反转这些框架宣称的效率优势。

Method: 理论侧：建立敏感度与可检测性之间的定量关系，证明指纹相对真实输入的"超额敏感度"下界其统计可区分性（AUC/检测能力）。实证侧：构造指纹检测器，在5个验证框架、3种模型架构、3个数据集、3种对抗知识设定下评估；进一步设计更强隐藏约束下的验证方法，权衡可检测性与指纹有效性。

Result: 框架已知时检测器AUC≥0.989；选择性过滤使全部5个框架所需估计查询预算显著高于真实输入，效率优势被反转；仅在检测迁移性差的少量设定中残余边际效率优势。在更强隐藏约束下重设计的验证方法虽降低可检测性，但敏感度或指纹生成成功率下降。

Conclusion: 敏感度与可检测性存在内在张力：单靠敏感性无法保证对自适应恶意提供方的黑盒验证效率；完整性验证框架的设计需同时考虑指纹的可隐蔽性与有效性。

Abstract: Black-box integrity verification aims to detect unauthorized modifications to outsourced models using only prediction APIs. Existing frameworks seek to improve verification efficiency through sensitive fingerprints: queries whose predictions are more likely to change under tampering than those of genuine inputs. We show that this sensitivity can also help dishonest providers recognize verification queries and selectively conceal tampering. We prove that excess sensitivity relative to genuine inputs lower-bounds statistical distinguishability. We evaluate five integrity verification frameworks across three model architectures, three datasets, and three adversarial knowledge settings. When the deployed framework is known, our fingerprint detectors achieve AUCs at least 0.989, and selective filtering makes all five frameworks require significantly larger estimated query budgets than genuine inputs, reversing their intended efficiency advantage. A query-efficiency advantage remains marginally in a small subset of evaluated settings with poor detection transfer. We further design verification methods under stronger concealment constraints but find that reduced detectability comes with losses in sensitivity or fingerprint-generation success. Our results expose a sensitivity-detectability tension and show that sensitivity alone does not ensure efficient black-box verification against adaptive malicious providers.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [268] [EvoMisty: Building, Optimizing, and Evolving Capabilities on a Social Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31341)
*Xiao Wang,Lu Dong,Ifeoma Nwogu,Srirangaraj Setlur,Venu Govindaraju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EvoMisty：插件式框架，让编码智能体（Claude Code / Codex）从官方文档自动构建并持续优化机器人控制能力；在真机 Misty II 上验证 109/107 个原子能力，完成全部 28 项 AutoMisty 任务，并按用户指标迭代优化（对话延迟降 52%/71%），可将优化后技能蒸馏复用至其他对话任务（降延迟 33%–72%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 方案生成任务程序时仍依赖手工开发的控制接口与预定义能力，且缺乏按用户指定指标优化已完成任务、并跨任务复用性能改进的通用机制。

Method: 插件式框架：编码智能体依据官方文档编写控制程序，在物理机器人上验证候选能力；通过验证的能力存入原子能力库并与既有技能组合解任务；任务完成后采集执行数据、度量用户指定指标、迭代改进任务程序，最终将可复用技能蒸馏进技能库。在真机 Misty II 上用 Claude Code 与 Codex 评估。

Result: 两个智能体分别构建 109 和 107 个经真机验证的原子能力；均完成全部 28 项 AutoMisty 基准任务及 7 项超出 AutoMisty 能力范围的任务；在 5 个任务上七项评估指标全部提升（对话响应延迟分别降低 52%、71%）；跨任务技能复用使另外三个对话任务延迟降低 33%–72%，无需额外优化。

Conclusion: 以"验证—能力库—指标驱动迭代—技能蒸馏复用"闭环替代手工接口开发，为真机社交机器人提供可自演化、可复用的能力构建范式，并开源代码与实验视频。

Abstract: Social robots face diverse and changing tasks in companionship and education. LLM-based methods can generate task programs, but they still rely on manually developed control interfaces and predefined capabilities. They also lack a general mechanism to optimize completed tasks based on user-specified metrics and reuse performance improvements across this http URL present EvoMisty, a plugin-based framework that enables coding agents to build and continually improve robot capabilities. EvoMisty builds a control program from official documentation and verifies candidate capabilities on a physical robot. Verified capabilities are stored in an atomic capability library and combined with existing skills to solve tasks. After task completion, EvoMisty can collect execution data, measure user-specified metrics, and iteratively improve task programs. It then distills reusable skills from final solutions into a skill library for future this http URL evaluate EvoMisty on a physical Misty II robot using Claude Code and Codex. The two agents construct 109 and 107 verified atomic capabilities, respectively. Both complete all 28 AutoMisty benchmark tasks and seven additional tasks beyond AutoMisty's capabilities. Optimization improves all seven evaluated metrics across five tasks. For example, dialogue response latency decreases by 52% and 71%, respectively. Reusing skills from one optimized dialogue task reduces latency by 33% to 72% in three other dialogue tasks without further optimization. All code and experimental videos will be publicly released through the webpage: this https URL

</details>


### [269] [Learning from Human Corrections through Temporal Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31446)
*Anjiabei Wang,Tesca Fitzgerald*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将"纠正前"的时序信号与"纠正后"的行为对比分别构造偏好对，用于学习价值函数，实现目标推断与纠正预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Learning from Corrections 仅比较新轨迹与机器人原意图，忽略了干预发生时机所蕴含的上下文信息。

Method: 构建两类偏好对：后干预偏好（纠正后行为 vs 被替代行为）与前干预偏好（干预前状态序列的时间关系），由此学习近似人类潜在奖励的价值表示，并在目标推断和纠正预测两项下游任务上评估。

Result: 后干预偏好对目标推断尤为有效，前干预偏好显著提升纠正预测；两者融合在两项任务上均表现稳健，且可泛化到训练中未见的参与者。

Conclusion: "何时纠正"与"如何纠正"提供互补信息，应联合用于从纠正交互中学习价值表示。

Abstract: When people intervene to correct a robot, they provide information not only through how they correct its behavior, but also through when they choose to intervene. Existing approaches to Learning from Corrections focus on comparing the new trajectory to the robot's originally intended behavior, thus underutilizing the context of the robot's behavior before the intervention. In this work, we investigate how both pre- and post-intervention behavior can be translated into preference pairs for learning value functions that approximate a human's latent reward. We construct post-intervention preferences by comparing corrected behavior with the behavior it replaces, and pre-intervention preferences from temporal relationships among states leading up to an intervention. We evaluate the learned value representations through two downstream tasks: goal inference and correction forecasting. Our results show that post-intervention preferences are particularly effective for goal inference, while pre-intervention preferences provide strong correction-forecasting performance. Combining both sources of information performs strongly across both tasks, even when generalizing to participants unseen during training. These findings demonstrate that both when and how humans correct provide complementary information for learning value representations from corrective interactions.

</details>


### [270] [Geometric Control Analysis of an Internally Actuated Flying Wing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31463)
*Jonathan Becker,Ella M. Atkins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用李括号与强李代数秩条件分析仅由内部移动质量、动量飞轮与外推力驱动的固定翼UAS的几何可达性与可控性，并用Sussmann条件证明姿态子系统在平衡点的小时间局部可控，配合基于Huygens-Steiner定理的时变惯量处理，最后用非线性MPC在无扰/扰动流场下跟踪轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除传统舵面可大幅减少高保真非定常流场CFD建模的组合爆炸式算例，但需要对这种非常规驱动的几何可控性给出严格保证，以判断该构型是否具备实际飞行可行性。

Method: 基于李括号计算与强Lie Algebra Rank Condition（LARC）证明全非线性动力学的一般性小时间局部可达，并显式刻画奇异构型、说明可通过质量直接驱动逃逸；对姿态子系统在平衡点用Sussmann条件证明小时间局部可控；用Huygens-Steiner定理显式处理时变惯量张量，揭示控制向量场间的非平凡耦合；飞轮提供直接偏航控制权并与移动质量引起的惯量变化共同生成满秩可达李代数；据此推导带飞行包线约束的非线性MPC。

Result: 全非线性动力学被证为一般性小时间局部可达，奇异构型被显式刻画且可立即逃逸；姿态子系统在平衡点满足小时间局部可控；移动质量+飞轮+推力构成的执行器集合产生满秩可达李代数；MPC在无扰动与扰动流场条件下均实现多轨迹跟踪。

Conclusion: 该无舵面、移动质量-飞轮-推力驱动构型在几何上具备可达性与（姿态）小时间局部可控性，为后续高保真建模与MPC实现提供了严格理论依据。

Abstract: This paper presents a rigorous geometric accessibility and controllability analysis for a fixed-wing Uncrewed Aircraft System (UAS) actuated solely by an internal moving mass, a momentum wheel, and external thrust. This configuration eliminates traditional control surfaces, which in turn reduces the combinatorial set of computational fluid dynamics (CFD) studies necessary for high-fidelity modeling of flight through unsteady flow fields. Using Lie bracket computations and the strong Lie Algebra Rank Condition, it is shown that the full nonlinear dynamics are generically small-time locally accessible, with singular configurations explicitly characterized and shown to be immediately escapable by direct mass actuation. The stronger property of small-time local controllability is shown to hold for the attitude subsystem at equilibrium points, using Sussmann's condition. A key contribution is the explicit treatment of the time-varying inertia tensor via the Huygens-Steiner Theorem, which introduces nontrivial coupling between the control vector fields. The momentum wheel provides direct yaw authority that, combined with inertia variation from the moving mass, generates a full-rank accessibility Lie algebra. A nonlinear Model Predictive Controller (MPC) is derived with consideration of flight envelope constraints and is applied to track different trajectories under unperturbed and perturbed flow field conditions.

</details>


### [271] [CRAB-H: Task-Centric Certification for Healthcare Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31470)
*Tord Johan Eide,Einar Holt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRAB H: 以"任务"为一等治理对象的医疗自主系统认证方法，用任务需求画像(TRP-H)把认证定义为"已证实能力 vs 所需能力"的比对，并引入可靠性门控的保证分配机制与基于阈值合取的Capability Margin。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主系统认证（如基于基准分数）只问机器人是否达到足够高的 benchmark 得分，未回答"该能力是否满足特定医疗任务的安全要求"，且对不可复现评估的认知维度往往直接丢弃，导致治理完整性受损。

Method: 将任务提升为一等治理对象，以 Task Requirement Profiles (TRP-H) 形式化认知能力、治理约束、证据要求与运行假设；认证 = 已证实能力与所需能力的比较；引入 reliability-gated assurance allocation，将无法可复现评估的维度路由至 LEA 运行要求；Capability Margin 定义为各维度合规判定的阈值式合取，保持非补偿性治理逻辑。

Result: 抽象提出方法论框架，未给出实验数据或真实部署验证；主要论点为不可靠维度被"治理而非忽略"，并避免标量扣减破坏非补偿逻辑。

Conclusion: 为安全关键医疗部署提供任务中心、非补偿性的认证与保证分配架构，属治理/认证层面的概念性贡献，缺乏物理AI方法学与实证支撑。

Abstract: This paper introduces CRAB H as a task-centric certification methodology for healthcare autonomous systems. CRAB H extends the ARC governance architecture by introducing the task as a first-class governance object, represented through Task Requirement Profiles TRP H. Each profile defines the cognitive capabilities, governance constraints, evidentiary requirements, and operational assumptions necessary for the safe execution of a specific healthcare activity. Rather than asking whether a robot achieves a sufficiently high benchmark score, CRAB H asks whether demonstrated capabilities satisfy the requirements of the task. Certification becomes a comparison between demonstrated capability and required capability. A reliability-gated assurance allocation mechanism routes cognitive dimensions that cannot be reproducibly evaluated to LEA operational requirements rather than discarding them. Governance integrity is preserved: unreliable dimensions are governed, not ignored.
