The Capability Margin is defined as the threshold-based conjunction of per-dimension compliance determinations, not as a subtracted scalar preserving non compensatory governance logic appropriate for safety-critical healthcare deployment

</details>


### [272] [Embodied Neuro-symbolic AI: Making Robot Behavior Auditable, Safe, and Scalable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31488)
*Ruwan Wickramarachchi,Jonathan Francis,Alessandro Oltramari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出“具身神经符号AI”（Embodied NeSy AI）范式：在VLA端到端系统中融合可学习感知/规划/控制与机器可读的背景知识和形式化约束，通过知识引导感知、约束感知符号规划与混合学习，实现可审计、可机器校验的安全合规与更强的泛化及因果推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA端到端系统在分布偏移下泛化脆弱、缺乏物理与因果推理、细粒度运动控制不稳定，且难以表达和审计“机器人不得做什么”的安全约束，限制其在安全关键场景中的可扩展性与可信部署。

Method: 定位/立场类论文，提出面向部署的神经符号范式：将学习式感知、规划与控制同显式机器可读背景知识和形式化约束结合，包含三个组件——知识引导感知、约束感知符号规划、混合学习（神经-符号联合）。摘要未给出具体算法、数据集或实验设计。

Result: 摘要中无定量结果或实证评估，仅给出定性主张：可推理环境、适应新任务、提供可审计且机器可校验的安全与运行合规性。

Conclusion: 将神经符号方法引入VLA/具身智能是提升安全保证与可信部署的合理方向，但目前仅为范式层面的论述，缺乏具体方法细节与真实机器人验证；需待完整方法或实证再评估其实际增益。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) end-to-end systems have driven major progress in robot learning, yet they still face persistent barriers to dependable real-world deployment. In practice, these models often exhibit brittle generalization under distribution shift (i.e., novel objects, layouts, lighting, or contact conditions), limited physical and causal reasoning, and inconsistent fine-grained motor control. Critically, many VLA pipelines offer limited ways to express and audit what a robot must not do, making it difficult to provide assurance in safety-critical settings. These limitations constrain scalability, maintainability, and trust when robots move beyond controlled demonstrations. We propose Embodied Neuro-Symbolic (NeSy) AI: a deployment-oriented paradigm that integrates learned perception, planning and control with explicit, machine-readable background knowledge and formal constraints. By combining knowledge-guided perception, constraint-aware symbolic planning, and hybrid learning, Embodied NeSy AI enables robots to reason about their environment, adapt to new tasks, and provide auditable, machine-checkable compliance with safety and operational requirements, thus supporting scalable, trustworthy robot deployment.

</details>


### [273] [Hi3R-VLA: Grounding Semantics and Actions in 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31535)
*Shizhe Chen,Paul Pacaud,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Hi3R-VLA 将视觉、语言与动作统一锚定在显式 3D 点云几何中，用 3D-RoPE 增强的 action-to-point 注意力预测控制命令，参数量<250M 即在多套机器人操作基准与真机上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型虽引入 3D 几何，但如何有效利用几何实现精确且可泛化的动作预测仍不清晰；作者关注两个关键问题：3D 表征中的语义融合，以及观测与动作之间的空间对齐。

Method: 构建统一 3D 策略：将空间对齐的视觉特征与语言条件注入点云表征，获得任务相关的细粒度几何语义；将低层控制指令锚定到同一 3D 坐标系；在粗到细点特征上使用 3D-RoPE 增强的 action-to-point 注意力建模动作目标与观测点间的几何关系。

Result: 在 RoboCasa、RLBench、RoboTwin2、DexJoCo、DexSuite 上跨单臂/双臂夹爪与灵巧手多种本体达到 SOTA；真机实验验证有效性；可训练参数不足 250M，模型、代码与数据已开源。

Conclusion: 显式 3D 几何与语义融合、动作-观测空间对齐是提升 VLA 精度与泛化的有效路径，且可在小参数规模下实现强性能并具备真机可迁移性。

Abstract: While recent vision-language-action (VLA) models increasingly incorporate 3D geometry, how to effectively exploit it for precise and generalizable action prediction remains a central challenge. We introduce Hi3R-VLA, a 3D policy that grounds heterogeneous modalities - vision, language, and action - in explicit 3D geometry for robotic manipulation. Our model integrates spatially-aligned visual features and language conditioning into point cloud representations, enriching scene geometry with fine-grained, task-relevant semantics. It then anchors low-level control commands in the same 3D coordinate frame and uses 3D-RoPE enhanced action-to-point attention over coarse-to-fine point features to model geometric relationships between action targets and observed points. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of semantic fusion within 3D and spatial alignment between observations and actions. With fewer than 250M trainable parameters, Hi3R-VLA achieves state-of-the-art performance on RoboCasa, RLBench, RoboTwin2, DexJoCo and DexSuite across diverse manipulation tasks and embodiments, from single-arm and dual-arm grippers to dexterous hands. Real-robot evaluations further demonstrate its effectiveness in the physical world. The model, code and dataset are publicly released at this https URL .

</details>


### [274] [Sampling at Scale: Distributed Multi-Agent Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31561)
*Minchan Jung,Dimitrios S. Georgiou,Augustinos D. Saravanos,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分布式采样轨迹优化架构 DS-TO：基于 splitting 机制，让每个智能体仅在自身控制空间采样并与邻居通信，实现完全去中心化并行优化，在最多 1024 智能体的 2D/3D 任务上提升非凸探索与约束满足并具良好可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体轨迹优化需同时应对两个耦合难题：约束诱导的非凸地形探索，以及规模在大团队下的可扩展性。采样类方法擅长探索但随维度膨胀；将其直接嵌入分布式优化又导致采样要么无法处理约束诱导的非凸性，要么搜索空间随邻域规模增长。作者希望弥合这一矛盾。

Method: 引入适配采样的 splitting 机制构建 DS-TO 架构：将耦合问题按智能体拆分，每个智能体只在自己控制空间中采样（与邻域大小无关），同时仍暴露于约束诱导的非凸性与其他智能体决策；算法完全去中心化，仅需邻域通信，各智能体局部更新并行执行。并给出并行 Jacobi 子问题更新与单步约束线性化等高效变体以降低计算量。

Result: 在 2D 与 3D 任务上测试（最多 1024 个智能体），相较最先进的基于导数的分布式优化方法具有更好的探索能力与约束满足性能，相较现有分布式采样方法具有更优的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 采样与分布式优化的张力可通过 splitting 化解，使采样并行化且与邻域规模解耦，为大规模多智能体轨迹优化提供可扩展的去中心化方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent trajectory optimization poses two coupled challenges: effectively exploring non-convex landscapes and scaling favorably to large teams of agents. Sampling-based methods are natural candidates for the exploration challenge but scale poorly with problem dimension. Moreover, their off-the-shelf integration into distributed optimization introduces a fundamental trade-off: sampling either fails to address the constraint-induced non-convexity or its search space grows with neighborhood size. We address this gap by introducing a $\underline{D}\text{istributed}$ $\underline{S}\text{ampling-based}$ $\underline{T}\text{rajectory}$ $\underline{O}\text{ptimization} \ \text{(DS-TO)}$ architecture built on a splitting mechanism tailored to sampling. Under this splitting, each agent samples only in its own control space, independent of neighborhood size, while still remaining exposed to both the constraint-induced non-convexity and the decisions of other agents. The resulting algorithm is fully decentralized, requiring only neighborhood communication, with all local updates executed in parallel across agents. Efficient variations, including parallel Jacobi updates within each agent's subproblem and single-step constraint linearization, further reduce computation. Experiments on 2D and 3D tasks with up to 1024 agents demonstrate improved exploration and constraint satisfaction over state-of-the-art derivative-based distributed optimization, and superior scalability to existing distributed sampling methods.

</details>


### [275] [FabricBench: A Standardized Policy Benchmark for Bimanual Fabric Manipulation with a Contact-Driven Deformable Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31577)
*Wenkang Hu,Haoran Wang,Liu Liu,Wenchao Huang,Yiji Chen,Mengao Zhao,Xincheng Tang,Zhengjie Shu,Lai Jiang,Wei Li,Zhendong Wang,Zhizhong Su,Huamin Wang,Ruigang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FabricBench：一个双臂布料操作基准，含6个任务（衣物/丝绸/包）、10000个可仿真资产、基于Newton开源求解器的布料-刚体双向力耦合接触仿真（无人工附着），并用6个代表策略（含VLA与世界动作模型）评测配置与外观泛化。最优策略平均成功率77%，长程T恤折叠均不超过36%，强形变初始配置下仅6-14%；π0.5零样本真机部署复现任务难度排序（Spearman ρ=0.94）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布料操作存在几何形变、自遮挡与接触等难题，但标准化评测长期集中于刚体物体，缺乏可复现、可控制变量（配置/外观）的双臂布料操作测试平台，也难以判断现有VLA/世界模型策略的真实能力边界。

Method: 构建基于Newton开源求解器的仿真器，重新设计布料-刚体接触与双向力耦合，使双臂能通过接触力完成抓取-保持-释放并支持包内自由刚体传递重量；提供6任务、1万仿真就绪资产及配置与外观泛化的受控测试；对比6类代表策略，并做零样本真机迁移验证。

Result: 求解器在45次抓取-保持-释放试验中全部成功，基线配置全部失败；最优策略平均成功率77%，长程T恤折叠无策略超过36%，最强形变初始配置下降至6-14%，退化幅度大于外观变化；π0.5真机零样本保持任务难度排序（ρ=0.94）。

Conclusion: FabricBench提供了可复现的布料操作评测平台，表明仿真可信度与真机难度排序一致，但策略在长程、强形变配置泛化上仍显著不足，配置泛化是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Fabric manipulation challenges robot policies with changing geometry, self-occlusion, and contact, yet standardized evaluation remains focused on rigid objects. We introduce FabricBench, a benchmark for bimanual fabric manipulation with six tasks spanning garments, silk, and bags, 10,000 simulation-ready assets, and controlled tests of configuration and appearance generalization. Built on an open-source solver in Newton, our simulator enables stable bimanual grasping, holding, and release through contact forces, without artificial attachment. Redesigned cloth-rigid contact and bidirectional force coupling support interactions among robot arms, fabric, and free rigid objects, allowing a bag to support its contents and transmit their weight to the grippers. In a controlled grasp-hold-release evaluation, our solver succeeds in all 45 trials, compared with none for the tested baseline configurations. Evaluating six representative policies, including vision-language-action models and world action models, reveals substantial remaining challenges: the best policy achieves 77% average success, while no policy exceeds 36% on long-horizon T-shirt folding. Under the most deformed initial T-shirt configurations, success falls to 6-14% across policies, with larger drops than under the evaluated appearance shifts. Zero-shot deployment of $\pi_{0.5}$ on a real robot largely preserves task difficulty ordering across six tasks (Spearman $\rho=0.94$). These results establish FabricBench as a reproducible testbed for measuring fabric-manipulation capabilities and exposing limitations in configuration generalization. The project website is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [276] [A Self-Organizing Swarm Control Model for Underwater Robots Based on Turing-Type Reaction-Diffusion Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31637)
*Takeshi Ishida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 无GPS、无绝对坐标的水下集群控制：仅用内外两个通信半径信道的接收信号数，扩展基于图灵斑图的元胞自动机模型到连续空间，通过势场差驱动的趋高势运动实现去中心化集群形成与形态调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境无GPS、无高速无线电通信，带宽与能见度严重受限，Boids等依赖邻居相对位置与速度矢量的传统集群方法难以适用；需要一种仅依赖通信信号计数、无领航者、无绝对坐标的极低信息量集群控制模型。

Method: 将基于图灵斑图形成的元胞自动机模型推广至连续空间；以两信道（内/外邻域，不同传输距离）接收信号数之差计算势场，机器人趋向高势区域。稳定行为由四项机制决定：势值时间平均、基于迟滞的状态判定、基于时间差的步进转向、与外部方向指令的矢量合成。二维仿真验证。

Result: 二维仿真表明可实现自主集群形成，并可通过形态学参数调控集群空间散布程度；在通信成功率降至约0.7的丢包条件下仍能维持集群与高极化度，具备鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 仅靠双信道信号计数的极简信息即可支撑水下去中心化集群编队与散布控制，且对通信丢包具鲁棒性；但验证限于二维仿真，缺乏实物或三维实验及与基线方法的定量对比。

Abstract: In underwater environments, Global Positioning System (GPS) signals and high-speed radio communication are unavailable, and severe constraints exist on communication bandwidth and visibility; consequently, applying conventional swarm formation methods, such as the Boids model, which require the relative positions and velocity vectors of neighboring vehicles, is difficult. This study proposes a decentralized swarm control model that operates without a leader or absolute coordinates, relying solely on the number of received signals from two communication channels with different transmission ranges (inner and outer neighborhoods). The proposed model extends a cellular automaton model based on Turing pattern formation principles to continuous space and is grounded in the fundamental concept that swarms form as robots move toward regions of higher potential calculated from the difference in received signal counts. Specifically, stable behavior is determined through temporal averaging of potential values, hysteresis-based state evaluation, stepwise turning based on temporal differences, and vector composition with an external directional command. Two-dimensional simulation results demonstrated that autonomous swarm formation and control of swarm spatial dispersion via a morphological parameter are achievable, confirming robustness in maintaining swarms and high polarization even under communication dropouts where the communication success rate decreases to approximately 0.7.

</details>


### [277] [Collision Rendering: GPU-Accelerated Trajectory Planning on Sparse Voxel Radiance Fields with Risk-Bounded Collision Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31658)
*Byoungkwon Yoon,Han Gyeom Lee,Hojun Lee,Yuseop Sim,Daniel Sungwoo Bae,Martin Byung-Guk Jun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Collision Rendering 框架，将稀疏体素辐射场（SVR）的渲染管线作为碰撞概率的快速数值近似器，实现 GPU 加速、有风险上界的碰撞检测与可微碰撞代价，用于轨迹规划与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射场能为机器人提供精细场景表征，但如何把学到的空间基元转化为可靠的碰撞判据尚无共识，导致其在轨迹规划中应用不足。作者希望无需辅助几何地图或改动已学表示，直接在 SVR 上完成碰撞检测。

Method: 核心思路是利用 SVR 结构化体积密度的渲染管线近似碰撞概率。用一组碰撞球包络机器人，每个球心放置同尺寸球形相机；在概率碰撞模型下，从渲染管线推导每个球最大方向碰撞概率的上界。该上界可在 GPU 上求值，形成模块化碰撞检测器，可直接接入采样类规划器；同时由渲染不透明度计算碰撞代价及其梯度，扩展到轨迹优化。

Result: 在消费级 GPU 上达到毫秒级碰撞检测时间；所有查询均生成无碰撞轨迹，优于其他基于辐射场的规划器。

Conclusion: 渲染管线可作为碰撞概率的代理，为辐射场规划提供了一种无需显式几何建模、可微且风险有界的统一碰撞检测方案，兼顾采样规划与轨迹优化。

Abstract: Although radiance fields provide detailed scene representations for robotics, they remain underexplored for trajectory planning, without a widely agreed-upon way to translate learned spatial primitives into reliable collision criteria. To bridge this gap, we introduce Collision Rendering, the first trajectory planning framework on sparse voxel radiance fields (SVR), which supports GPU-accelerated, risk-bounded collision checking and a differentiable collision cost. Our key insight is that the structured volumetric density of SVR allows its rendering pipeline to serve as a fast numerical approximator of collision probability. We approximate the robot with collision spheres and place a coinciding spherical camera at each sphere. Under a probabilistic collision model, we derive an upper bound on each sphere's maximum directional collision probability from the camera's rendering pipeline. Evaluating this bound on the GPU yields a modular collision checker that integrates with established sampling-based planners without an auxiliary geometric map or changes to the learned representation. We further compute a collision cost and its gradients from rendered opacities through the same pipeline, extending the framework to trajectory optimization. Experiments demonstrate millisecond collision checking time on a consumer-grade GPU and collision-free trajectories in all queries, outperforming other radiance field-based planners.

</details>


### [278] [Exteroceptive Sensor Faults in Autonomous Vehicles: A Cross-Modal Taxonomy and Fault Injection Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31667)
*Davide Possenti,Daniele Cianca,Stefano Arrigoni,Francesco Braghini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 综述外感知传感器（相机/激光雷达/毫米波雷达）故障，提出跨模态、以故障起因驱动的五类分类体系（硬件、环境、标定、软件、协同故障），并提供开源故障注入工具箱（94个症状级例程、4级严重度、突发调度、逐帧标签）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述与基准多聚焦单一传感器、孤立工况或整车级后果，缺乏统一词汇把三种模态的故障起源关联起来，限制了感知可靠性分析与安全验证的可比性。

Method: 文献综述 + 分类学构建：将故障条目映射到物理/逻辑机理、可观测表现与受影响子系统，并与 ISO 26262、ISO 21448 安全分析对应；同时发布开源故障注入工具包，含94个症状级注入例程（相机41、激光雷达27、雷达26）、4个分级严重度、可复现突发调度与逐帧标签（记录类别、子系统、严重度）。

Result: 在 Boreas 数据集上示例展示了五类故障对相机图像、激光雷达点云与雷达扫描的具体影响，验证了工具包可用性与标签一致性。

Conclusion: 该分类体系与工具包为传感器可靠性研究、故障检测与诊断方法开发评测、以及带故障标注数据集构建提供了共享参考与可控测试床，可支撑感知系统的安全验证。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles rely on cameras, LiDAR, and radar to perceive their surroundings, yet these exteroceptive sensors can be degraded by hardware aging, environmental interference, miscalibration, embedded software errors, and combinations of these causes, with direct consequences for perception quality and safety. Existing surveys and benchmarks mostly address individual sensors, isolated conditions, or vehicle-level consequences, and no common vocabulary relates fault origins across the three modalities. This paper reviews the literature on exteroceptive sensor faults and proposes a cross-modal, origin-driven taxonomy that organizes them into five categories, namely hardware, environmental, calibration, software, and synergistic faults, mapping each entry to its physical or logical mechanism, observable manifestation, and affected subsystem, and relating the categories to the safety analyses of ISO 26262 and ISO 21448. To make the taxonomy operational, we release an open-source fault injection toolkit with 94 symptom-level routines (41 camera, 27 LiDAR, and 26 radar), four graded severity levels, reproducible burst scheduling, and per-frame labels recording the fault category, affected subsystem, and severity. Examples on the Boreas dataset illustrate how each category alters camera images, LiDAR point clouds, and radar scans. Together, the taxonomy and the toolkit provide a shared reference and a controlled testbed for studying sensor reliability, developing and benchmarking fault detection and diagnosis methods, and building fault-annotated datasets for the safety validation of perception systems. The toolkit is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [279] [Exact Collision Gradients for Ellipsoids in Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31676)
*Shamak Dutta,Biswadip Dey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文针对椭球体碰撞的可微仿真，指出固定步长仿真器与线性TOI估计在碰撞时刻的梯度误差随碰撞斜度、接触曲率、步长及是否考虑自旋而增大，提出用Perram-Wertheim接触函数上的连续碰撞检测精确定位碰撞时刻，并对"接触条件"而非搜索过程求导，从而在任意步长下获得精确梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微仿真的梯度在碰撞瞬间最不可靠：固定步长仿真器仅在物体已重叠后才检测到碰撞，部分可微仿真器用碰撞时间(TOI)的线性估计做修正，但该修正的误差来源与规模缺乏分析，尤其在椭球（非球）形状下偏差可能显著。

Method: (1) 理论分析：推导线性TOI估计的梯度误差随碰撞斜度、接触点曲率与时间步长的增长关系，并说明忽略自旋会引入额外误差；(2) 方法：在Perram-Wertheim接触函数上做连续碰撞检测以求解精确TOI，然后对"接触（相切）条件"进行隐式微分，绕开对搜索过程的求导，实现与步长无关的精确梯度。

Result: 在最优控制与系统辨识两个任务上，精确梯度能恢复最优控制序列与真实参数；而线性TOI估计（即便显式考虑自旋）失败。

Conclusion: 椭球碰撞的可微仿真中，应通过连续碰撞检测精确定位接触时刻并对接触条件求导，以获得步长无关的精确梯度，这对接触丰富的学习与控制问题具有实际必要性。

Abstract: Differentiable simulation enables gradient-based optimization in learning and control problems involving collisions, but its gradients are least reliable at the moment of impact, when the velocities jump. A fixed-step simulator detects a collision only after the bodies overlap, and some differentiable simulators correct for this with a linear estimate of the time of impact. In this paper, we study impacts between ellipsoids. We show that the gradient error of this estimate grows with the obliqueness of the collision, the curvature at contact, and the time step, and that ignoring spin adds a further error. Subsequently, we find the time of impact with continuous collision detection on the Perram Wertheim contact function and differentiate through the touching condition rather than the search. This gives exact gradients at any step size. We demonstrate our method on an optimal control problem and a system identification problem. In both, exact gradients recover the optimal control and the true parameters, while the linear estimate does not, even when it accounts for spin.

</details>


### [280] [CATWAY: Cost-sensitive agentic three-way robot decisions with calibrated language-model confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31694)
*Erik Derner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CATWAY：代价敏感的三分类决策门控（执行/询问/拒绝），用四类指令条件构造损失矩阵在概率单纯形上划分区域，概率由十次采样投票与歧义/不可行/风险分级分数经逻辑回归融合；家庭域拟合后冻结，在域内、两个新机器人域与TRUST-NAV上验证，18组家庭比较全部显著更省（0.28 vs 0.57–0.83），未执行142条不安全指令中任何一条。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人语言智能体需判断指令是执行、质询还是拒绝，而"误执行"代价远高于"多问一句"。既有门控方法均未从错误代价推导全部阈值、未基于校准概率行事，也缺乏跨域冻结迁移的评估。

Method: 从四类条件（清晰/歧义/不可行/不安全）的损失矩阵推导最小期望损失规则，在概率单纯形上固定执行、询问（信息采集步）与拒绝三区域；概率输入为十样本投票结果+歧义/不可行/风险的分级评分，经逻辑回归融合；在家庭指令上拟合后冻结，在同一决策文本层面用于三个小模型。

Result: 每条决策11次生成（对比1次）：18组家庭比较全部显著更优（单项平均损失0.28 vs 0.57–0.83），新机器人域9/18、TRUST-NAV 10/18显著更优（Holm校正），从未显著更差；142条不安全测试指令零执行。投票一致时最小期望损失规则将校准降为单一损失导出阈值，导致6个模型配置中2个仅用投票的门控从不执行清晰指令（TRUST-NAV 0.49 vs CATWAY 0.13/0.09），融合估计在所有集合上避免该退化。

Conclusion: 以代价驱动、校准概率为基础的三向决策门控可在跨域冻结条件下稳定降低安全代价，且融合式概率估计优于单一投票门控；主要代价是约11倍推理开销，跨新域收益并不总能达到显著。

Abstract: A language-model robot agent must decide whether to execute, question or refuse an instruction, and an unsafe execution costs far more than an unneeded question. No prior robot gate derives all its thresholds from error costs or acts on calibrated probabilities, and none was fitted in one domain and evaluated unchanged in another. We propose CATWAY, a cost-sensitive three-way decision gate: a loss matrix over four instruction conditions (clear, ambiguous, infeasible, unsafe) fixes execute, ask and refuse regions on the probability simplex, the question being the information-gathering step. Its probabilities fuse a ten-sample vote with graded scores of ambiguity, infeasibility and risk by logistic regression. Fitted on household instructions and frozen, CATWAY was tested on text-level decisions of three small models in-domain, in two new robot domains and on the external TRUST-NAV benchmark. At eleven generations per decision against one, it was significantly cheaper than a direct prompt, a cost-aware prompt and an unguarded agent in all 18 household comparisons (mean loss per item 0.28 against 0.57-0.83), in 9 of 18 in the new domains and in 10 of 18 on TRUST-NAV (Holm per set), and never significantly costlier. It executed none of the 142 unsafe test instructions. On a unanimous vote, the minimum-expected-loss rule reduces calibration to one loss-derived threshold, so for two of six model configurations a gate on the vote alone never executed a clear instruction, costing 0.49 per item on TRUST-NAV against CATWAY's 0.13 and 0.09; the fused estimate avoided this on every set.

</details>


### [281] [FoveaWAM: Role-Aware 4D World-Action Modeling for Dynamic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31705)
*Jiaqi Yin,Baoyue Zhang,Jiyuan Zhang,Wenxuan Liu,Zhaofei Yu,Yue Deng,Tiejun Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FoveaWAM：按角色分配精度的原生4D世界-动作模型，目标位姿用度量SE(3)轨迹、环境用稀疏3D流、静态内容用当前帧；单卡RTX 5090推理15.1ms，比视频世界-动作基线快约10倍；并提出OnTimeBench（计算期间场景持续运动的动态操作基准），成功率82.1%，领先最强基线20.8点，并在DOMINO与真机上领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作运动物体时，策略需预测动作执行瞬间的场景，但预测越久该时刻越远（延迟-漂移矛盾）。场景级预测计算昂贵，目标中心预测又易忽略影响接触的邻近物体。

Method: 关键洞察是预测内容可按角色解耦：仅目标位姿需毫米级精度（决定接触），环境主要通过净空与运动传递影响。据此构建FoveaWAM：(1) 目标过去/未来运动建模为度量SE(3)轨迹；(2) 环境建模为稀疏3D流；(3) 静态部分直接复用当前帧。紧凑表示实现低延迟推理。评测上提出OnTimeBench，让策略按其自身延迟计算，场景在计算期间继续演化。

Result: 单张RTX 5090上动作耗时15.1ms，约为视频世界-动作基线的1/10；OnTimeBench成功率82.1%，超最强基线20.8个点；DOMINO与真实机器人任务上同样领先。

Conclusion: 按角色分配4D预测精度可在保持接触级精度的同时大幅压缩推理延迟，显式以延迟为变量的评测基准更能反映真实动态操作需求。

Abstract: To manipulate a moving object, a policy must predict the scene at the moment its action executes, yet every millisecond spent predicting pushes that moment further away. Scene-level forecasts are computationally expensive, whereas target-centric forecasts can omit nearby objects that affect contact. Our key insight is that what a policy must predict decouples by role: only the target's pose, which determines contact, requires millimeter precision, while the surroundings matter primarily through the clearance they leave and the motion they impart. We present FoveaWAM, a native 4D world-action model that allocates precision according to these roles. Specifically, it models the target's past and future motion as a metric SE(3) trajectory, the surroundings as sparse 3D flow, and relies on the current frame for everything static. This compact representation lets FoveaWAM act in 15.1 ms on one RTX 5090, about 10 times faster than video world-action baselines, leaving substantially less time for the scene to evolve before action execution. To evaluate each policy under its own latency, we introduce OnTimeBench, a dynamic manipulation benchmark in which the scene keeps moving while the policy computes. FoveaWAM reaches 82.1% success on OnTimeBench, 20.8 points above the strongest baseline, and also leads on DOMINO and real-robot tasks. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [282] [Sparks of Weight-Space Programming for Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31715)
*Kyle Coelho,Eugen Solowjow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将"权重空间编程"(WSP)应用于大规模预训练视觉-语言-动作策略pi0.5：由语言模型智能体直接读写神经网络参数，在动作专家(action expert)中复用少数近乎静默的神经元作为"写入位置"，无需额外数据、示教或训练即可纠正重复出现的卡死失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型(RFM)的失败往往不是随机的，而是反复落入相同的可识别"卡死状态"。传统修复需额外数据采集、示教或微调，成本高。作者希望验证：若失败状态在模型内部激活中可辨识，是否可通过极少量参数编辑触发纠正动作，同时保持其余参数不变。

Method: 在pi0.5策略上实施WSP：(1) 由语言模型智能体直接读取并改写参数，而非通过梯度训练；(2) 关键设计是"写入位置"的选择——复用动作专家中少数近乎静默的神经元，使编辑能在与失败情境关联时被激活；(3) 纠正动作（如将夹爪下移至目标）直接指定而非学习得到；(4) 通过反向编辑与随机编辑作为对照，验证增益来源。

Result: 仿真实验中，仅编辑约0.0002%的参数即可提升重复性失败任务的成功率，且对已完成任务微调的策略同样有效（这一点尤为关键，说明编辑可与微调互补而非重复）。反向与随机编辑不产生收益，佐证增益来自于预期的纠正行为。方法在失败情境可识别、且存在简单纠正手段时效果最佳。

Conclusion: 权重空间编程为RFM失效修复提供了一条免数据、免训练的轻量路径，接近"外科手术式"的参数编辑。局限在于适用范围受限于可辨识的失败模式与简单纠正策略，尚缺乏实机验证与复杂多模态失败的处理能力。

Abstract: This paper demonstrates an approach for correcting failures of robot foundation models (RFMs) without additional data collection, demonstrations, or training. We apply weight-space programming (WSP), in which a language-model agent directly reads and writes a neural network's parameters, to a large, pre-trained vision-language-action policy. The idea is simple: many policy failures are not random but repeatedly land in the same recognizable stuck state, so if that state is visible in the model's internal activations, a small edit could trigger a corrective movement while leaving the rest of the model's parameters unchanged. A core design choice is where to write the edit: repurposing a few near-silent neurons in the action expert lets the edit switch on in situations associated with the failure and produce a corrective movement, such as lowering the gripper toward the target, that is specified directly rather than learned through training. Empirically, when applied to the pi0.5 policy in simulation, we find that edits to roughly 0.0002% of the parameters can improve success on recurring failures, including for a policy that was already fine-tuned on its task. Reversed and random edits indicate that the gains come from the intended behavior, and the approach works best when the failure situation is recognizable and a simple correction can address it.

</details>


### [283] [DAA for an Autonomous Delivery Drone: Maneuver Selection Policies for Near-Collision Risk Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31729)
*Franco Callari,Ishay Kamon,Anthony Narkawitz,Pradeep Yarlagadda,Amir Navot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 对比两种无人机自主探测与规避（DAA）机动选择策略：纯几何法（朝远离CPA处入侵者方向转向）与考虑入侵者位置不确定性的方法（远离包含其CPA位置分布的平面），以风险比为指标在大规模仿真近碰撞数据集上评估，后者显著优于前者，二者均可由机载航迹估计闭式求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DAA架构面向有人驾驶飞机，而小型无人机面临感知距离极近（亚公里级）、决策时间极短、CPA及其到达时间预测不确定性大、低空机动包线受限等差异显著条件，需要针对性的机动选择策略。

Method: 提出并比较两种机动选择策略：(1) 纯几何法，在CPA处朝远离入侵者的方向转向；(2) 在几何转向基础上显式建模入侵者位置已知不确定性来源，转向远离一个预期包含入侵者CPA位置分布的平面。两者均可由机载航迹估计闭式计算。

Result: 在大量与有人驾驶入侵航空器仿真的近碰撞遭遇数据集上，以风险比（risk ratio）为评价指标进行评估，结果表明两种策略均可行，且不确定性感知策略显著优于纯几何策略。

Conclusion: 显式考虑CPA位置预测不确定性可显著提升小无人机DAA机动选择的安全性能，为受限感知与机动条件下的自主规避提供了可闭式实现的实用方案；证据限于仿真，尚无实机验证。

Abstract: Detect-and-avoid (DAA) systems for autonomous drones operating in shared airspace must select avoidance maneuvers under conditions that differ markedly from those assumed by conventional DAA architectures designed for crewed aircraft. Limited on-board sensing capabilities restrict detection of collision risk to near distances (sub-km), the time available for decision-making is severely limited, uncertainty in the predicted closest point of approach (CPA) and the time to reach it is substantial, and the maneuver envelope of a small uncrewed aircraft at low altitude is tightly constrained. This paper presents and compares two maneuver-selection strategies that address these challenges: (1) a purely geometric approach, steering in the direction away from the intruder at CPA, and (2) a method that also takes into account the known sources of uncertainty in the intruder's location, and steers away from a plane expected to contain the intruder's location distribution at CPA. Performance is evaluated using the risk ratio metric on a large dataset of simulated near-collision encounters with crewed intruders. Results indicate that both strategies are feasible (i.e., computable in closed form from an onboard track estimate), and that the second strategy substantially outperforms the first.

</details>


### [284] [Many Senses, One World: Learning Hierarchical Multimodal World Representations for Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31782)
*Jin Yang,Youlin Wang,Ping Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiMoDex 提出两阶段框架：先用119万同步多模态时间步预训练层次化世界状态表示网络（视觉+触觉+力/力矩+音频），冻结后以其嵌入条件化共享 world-action transformer 联合学习动作生成与动作条件未来状态预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂灵巧操作需同时利用视觉上下文与非视觉物理交互线索，如何有效融合多模态做策略学习是难点。

Method: Stage 1：大规模同步多模态数据上预训练层次化表示网络，输出统一 world-state embedding；Stage 2：冻结表示网络，用 embeddings 条件化共享 world-action transformer，同时优化动作生成与 action-conditioned 未来状态预测。

Result: 五个真实世界任务平均成功率 78.7%，较最强基线绝对提升 32.0%；消融验证层次化表示与两阶段训练的必要性，并给出失败分析。

Conclusion: 多模态层次化世界状态表示结合世界模型式联合预测，可显著提升真实双臂灵巧操作成功率。

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous manipulation relies on multimodal inputs that capture visual context and non-visual physical interaction cues for precise and coordinated control. To address the challenge of effectively integrating these modalities for policy learning, we propose HiMoDex, a two-stage framework centered on hierarchical multimodal world-state representations. In Stage 1, we pretrain a hierarchical representation network on the collected dataset of 1.19 million synchronized multimodal time steps. The network integrates visual context with tactile, force-torque, and audio cues into unified world-state embeddings. In Stage 2, we freeze the pretrained network and use its embeddings to condition a shared world-action transformer. The transformer jointly learns action generation and action-conditioned future-state prediction. Experiments across five real-world tasks show that HiMoDex achieves an average success rate of 78.7\%, representing an absolute improvement of 32.0\% over the strongest evaluated baseline. Extensive ablations validate the benefits of hierarchical representation learning and two-stage training. Failure analysis helps explain performance differences across variants. Code, data, and demos will be released at this https URL .

</details>


### [285] [Beyond the Benchmark: Localizing Released Autonomous Driving Models to Real Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31814)
*Morui Zhu,Deyuan Qu,Qi Chen,Qing Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Released-to-Real (R2R)部署局部化方法，定位发布模型与源系统（传感/数据/执行）的耦合项并仅修正受影响组件；含HA-RL（修正ego状态上的轨迹级优化与滚动时域执行的错配）与冻结感知权重的时空表征自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源自动驾驶模型在基准上表现强，但迁移到新实车仍困难；传统微调后模型仍残留源传感、数据与执行过程的隐含假设，部署缺口缺乏系统性诊断手段。

Method: R2R：先定位源系统耦合再最小化修正，保留发布模型主体。数据/行为层面用Human-Anchored RL，在记录的实车驾驶数据上以人类轨迹为锚点修正ego状态上的分布偏移，避免生成式闭环RL的高昂rollout；物理传感层面用冻结感知权重定位并校正依赖源的空间/时间表征，无需目标域标注或重训练。

Result: 发布版扩散规划器闭环成功率82.2%→95.2%；在五个相机目标装备上，发布版多相机检测器车辆mAP由0.041提升至0.420，跨多个检测器与三种实车传感配置均有效。

Conclusion: 将部署视为"局部化+最小修正"而非全模型微调，可在无目标标注、无重训练的前提下衔接发布模型与实车，为实车部署前准备提供可落地路径。

Abstract: Released autonomous-driving models achieve strong benchmark performance, yet deploying them on a new real vehicle remains challenging. We study this deployment gap and find that released models can remain coupled to assumptions from their source sensing, data, and execution processes even after conventional adaptation. We introduce Released-to-Real (R2R), a deployment-localization methodology that identifies such source-system coupling and corrects only the affected components while preserving the released model. For data and behavior adaptation, we find that the mismatch between trajectory-level optimization and receding-horizon execution enters through the ego state rather than the observation, and introduce Human-Anchored Reinforcement Learning (HA-RL), which corrects it on recorded target driving without the costly rollouts of generative closed-loop reinforcement learning (RL). For physical sensor adaptation, R2R localizes and corrects source-dependent spatial and temporal representations with frozen perception weights, requiring neither target annotations nor retraining. HA-RL improves the closed-loop success rate of a released diffusion-based planner from 82.2% to 95.2%, while sensor adaptation across multiple released detectors and three real sensor rigs improves the vehicle mean average precision (mAP) of a released multi-camera detector from 0.041 to 0.420 on a five-camera target rig. These results provide a practical path for preparing released models before deployment on new real vehicles. Our project page is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [286] [VG-GP: Uncertainty-Gated Visuo-Geometric Memory for Negative-Transfer-Resistant Grasp Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31826)
*Yonglong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VG-GP：一种即插即用的视-几何高斯过程经验记忆框架，用颜色不变特征与SE(2)残差抓取迁移实现零样本新物体抓取自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练对跖抓取网络在规范形状上泛化好，但对质量分布不均、薄边、陌生把手的物体零样本失败；现有回合制经验记忆的朴素检索存在视觉外观偏置、覆盖原有效抓取造成负迁移、以及闭环采集退化三类问题。

Method: 融合DINOv2表面法向（颜色不变）特征、夹爪对齐的规范SE(2)局部3D高度图与接触几何描述子，构建乘积核GP且ARD长度尺度仅在源物体上学习；对基础抓取与候选SE(2)备选建立联合后验，仅当联合协方差LCB超过风险敏感阈值才覆盖基础策略；用单记录必要性/充分性反事实干预门控隔离负迁移经验；以贝叶斯EI在等预算SE(2)探测上闭环采集。

Result: 在ManiSkill3的42个YCB物体（20源/12开发/10留出测试）上包络GR-ConvNet，22个未见物体物理抬升率从38.6%(34/88)升至53.4%(47/88)，零回退(+13救援/0损失，聚类95%CI [+6.8%,+22.7%]，McNemar p=0.00024)；10个留出物体上EI主动采集达45.0%(18/40，基线32.5%，占oracle余量5/7)，相较被动回退探测差异率55.0%，认知不确定性单调下降。

Conclusion: 将几何/接触感知的表征、风险敏感的联合后验覆盖判据与反事实经验隔离结合，可在冻结基础策略前提下实现零回退的闭环抓取迁移，说明"何时覆盖基础策略"比单纯检索更关键。

Abstract: Pretrained antipodal grasping networks generalize well to canonical shapes but frequently fail zero-shot on novel objects with irregular mass distributions, thin rims, or unfamiliar handles. While episodic experience memory adapts a frozen base policy without retraining, naive retrieval suffers from visual appearance bias, negative transfer from overriding working base grasps, and degenerate closed-loop acquisition. We present VG-GP, a plug-and-play Visuo-Geometric Gaussian Process memory framework for closed-loop $SE(2)$ residual grasp transfer. First, we fuse color-invariant surface-normal DINOv2 features, canonical $SE(2)$ gripper-aligned 3D local heightmaps, and contact-geometry descriptors under a product kernel with Automatic Relevance Determination (ARD) lengthscales learned strictly on source objects. Second, we model the joint posterior over the base grasp and candidate $SE(2)$ alternatives, overriding the base policy only when the joint-covariance Lower Confidence Bound (LCB) clears a risk-sensitive threshold. Third, an online counterfactual curation gate using single-record necessity and sufficiency interventions quarantines source experiences causing negative transfer. Fourth, we close the acquisition loop via Bayesian Expected Improvement (EI) over equal-budget $SE(2)$ probes. Across 42 disjoint YCB objects in ManiSkill3 (20 source, 12 development, 10 held-out test), VG-GP wraps a pretrained GR-ConvNet base policy and raises physical lift rate across all 22 unseen objects from 38.6% (34/88) to 53.4% (47/88) with zero regressions (+13 rescues, 0 losses; object-clustered 95% CI [+6.8%, +22.7%], exact McNemar $p = 0.00024$). On the 10 held-out objects, EI active acquisition reaches 45.0% (18/40 vs. 32.5% base, 5/7 of oracle headroom), achieves 55.0% probe divergence over passive fallback, and monotonically reduces epistemic uncertainty.

</details>


### [287] [POISE: Curvature-Aware Critic Regularization for Smooth Actor-Critic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31835)
*Jeong Woon Lee,Namho Kim,Hyoseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POISE 提出以"贪心漂移的曲率加权能量"作为 critic 平滑正则：基于局部凹性一阶近似贪婪动作在状态转移下的位移，并证明该能量的曲率加权二次形式近似保留旧贪心动作的价值损失，沿时间与空间状态位移同时正则化，actor 不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Actor-critic 训练的 actor 存在高频动作振荡；现有平滑方法多约束 actor，PAVE 类方法分别惩罚动作梯度变化与鼓励负曲率，但二者无法度量"相同梯度变化在平坦区域使 critic 偏好动作移动更远"这一效应，缺乏对位移价值后果的刻画。

Method: 定义 greedy shift（在局部凹性下贪婪动作位移的一阶近似）；推导其曲率加权能量可与"保留上一贪心动作"的价值损失二阶近似挂钩；据此构造 POISE 正则项，沿时间与空间状态位移惩罚该能量，采用 saddle-free 曲率度量与奖励尺度一致的归一化，不修改 actor。

Result: 在 6 个连续控制任务（TD3）上，POISE 使所有任务的平滑度分数平均降低 5.9×（几何均值 4.5×），最强基线为 2.2×（1.9×），并在 5 个任务上取得最低分；配对回报置信区间在 4 个任务上含零，Ant 回报提升 14%，Hopper 下降 11%。

Conclusion: 以 critic 侧曲率加权能量正则可显著改善动作平滑性且基本保持回报，但存在任务相关的回报波动，且缺乏真实机器人验证。

Abstract: Actor-critic methods achieve high returns in robotic control, yet trained actors exhibit high-frequency action oscillations. Most smoothness methods constrain the actor, whereas PAVE regularizes the critic by separately penalizing action-gradient variation and encouraging negative action curvature. The same gradient change, however, moves the critic's preferred action farther where the critic is flat, and separate penalties do not measure this motion. We characterize this motion through the greedy shift, which approximates greedy-action displacement across state transitions to first order under local concavity. Since displacement alone does not reflect its critic-value consequences, the analysis further shows that the shift's curvature-weighted energy approximates the value lost by retaining the previous greedy action to second order. Based on this relationship, POISE regularizes the curvature-weighted energy along temporal and spatial state displacements. It employs a saddle-free curvature metric and reward-scale-consistent scaling while leaving the actor unchanged. On six continuous control tasks with TD3, POISE reduces the smoothness score on every task by $5.9\times$ on average ($4.5\times$ geometric mean), compared with $2.2\times$ ($1.9\times$) for the strongest baseline, and achieves the lowest score on five tasks. Paired return intervals include zero on four tasks, with a 14% increase on Ant and an 11% decrease on Hopper.

</details>


### [288] [Co-DRIS: Physics-Informed Predictive Belief and Variable-Impedance Learning for Reactive Catching under Coupled Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31843)
*Yonglong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Co-DRIS + SuperMimic感知-动作协同设计框架，用惯性世界系物理世界模型、动作条件双流因果GRU置信编码器和相位感知变阻抗软接球，解决感知与执行不确定性耦合下反应式操作性能崩塌问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单帧框架（如DRIS）在孤立不确定性下鲁棒性尚可（86.4%），但感知噪声与执行误差同时出现时因无记忆编码与固定高刚度PD控制而严重退化（10类耦合压力场景平均仅27.6%），需要感知-动作一体化的协同设计。

Method: 三部分耦合：(i) 惯性世界系物理先验世界模型，精确弹道运动学+零初始化接触残差，滚动补全缺失观测，并用零成本反事实dropout一致性加速训练；(ii) 动作条件双流因果GRU信念编码器，化解去噪与滞后两难；(iii) 相位感知变阻抗软接球，在力矩层将7-DoF FR3切换至过阻尼区（zeta/zeta0≈2.26倍）。

Result: 未见抛掷3种子尾部分布评估（每场景15,360次抛掷）：标准基准89.6±0.4%（4σ噪声+10.5%至70.5%），10类耦合/丢帧压力场景66.6±0.5%（较DRIS+39.0%，50%丢帧+30.4%）；并验证阻抗解耦、+0.65 s飞行时间偏移下因果接触触发，以及跨任务迁移至钢板乒乓球弹跳（87.5%→100.0%）与FR3+Hand接球（4倍噪声下+8.4%，峰值冲击力降低6-13%）。

Conclusion: 感知-动作协同设计（物理先验世界模型+因果信念估计+相位变阻抗）显著优于单帧基线，尤其在高噪声、丢帧与耦合压力下；方法的模块化验证与跨任务迁移表明其具备通用性。

Abstract: Reactive robotic manipulation is acutely sensitive to simultaneous sensing noise, measurement dropouts, joint execution errors, and contact parameter variations. While single-frame frameworks like Domain-Randomized Instance Set (DRIS) achieve strong robustness under isolated uncertainties (86.4%), they degrade severely when perception and actuation uncertainties co-occur (27.6% across 10 coupled stress scenarios) due to memoryless encoding and fixed high-stiffness PD control. We propose Co-DRIS + SuperMimic, a perception-action co-design framework coupling: (i) an inertial world-frame physics-informed world model combining exact ballistic kinematics with a zero-initialized contact residual to roll forward missing observations and accelerate training via zero-cost counterfactual dropout consistency; (ii) an action-conditioned dual-stream causal GRU belief encoder resolving the denoising-vs-lag deadlock; and (iii) phase-aware variable-impedance soft-catching that transitions the 7-DoF Franka Research 3 arm into an overdamped regime (zeta/zeta0 ~ 2.26x) at the torque layer. Under unbiased 3-seed tail evaluation on unseen throws (15,360 throws/scenario), our method achieves 89.6 +/- 0.4% on standard benchmarks (+10.5% on 4-sigma noise to 70.5%) and 66.6 +/- 0.5% across 10 coupled/dropout stress scenarios (+39.0% over DRIS; +30.4% on 50% dropout). We further validate impedance decoupling, causal contact triggering under +0.65 s flight-time shifts, and cross-task transfer to steel-plate ping-pong bouncing (87.5% to 100.0%) and FR3+Hand catching (+8.4% under 4x noise with 6-13% lower peak impact force).

</details>


### [289] [BendGS: Observation-Conditioned Gaussian Self-Modeling for Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31844)
*Jeong Woon Lee,Doungjun Kim,Namho Kim,Seungheon Chae,Jongwoo Kim,Hyoseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BendGS 用观测估计的虚拟骨干（串联坐标系）驱动高斯基元形变，从多视角RGB-D图像学习连续体机器人在相对电机指令下的下一状态（3D形状、外观与末端位姿）联合预测，无需关节角或弯曲标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人连续弯曲、无测量关节角且无固定连杆几何，现有自模型仅预测点云、图像曲线或未来图像，无法从观测躯体同时给出下一时刻的3D形状、外观与末端位姿；且铰接高斯自模型依赖关节角驱动形变，不适用于连续体。

Method: 前馈网络由多视角图像重建机器人3D高斯；将坐标帧链拟合到反投影深度点得到虚拟骨干以估计弯曲状态；过渡网络在相对电机指令下预测骨干变化并形变绑定高斯，渲染下一图像与3D形状，远端帧输出末端位姿；损失仅用RGB-D与掩码，无需弯曲/末端标签。

Result: 在仿真与真实机器人上，Chamfer 距离最低、图像质量最佳、末端位姿误差最小，优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 以观测驱动的虚拟骨干作为隐式弯曲状态表示，可将铰接式高斯形变范式推广到无关节角连续体机器人，实现指令条件下的多模态下一状态自模型预测。

Abstract: Visual self-models learn from images how a robot moves under motor commands. Continuum robots need them even more, as they bend continuously without measured joint angles or fixed link geometry. Existing continuum self-models predict point clouds, image-space curves, or future images, but not the next 3D shape, appearance, and tip pose together from an observed body. 3D Gaussians offer a renderable representation of shape and appearance, yet articulated Gaussian self-models rely on joint angles to drive deformation. In this work, we propose BendGS, which uses a virtual backbone estimated from observation to drive command-conditioned Gaussian deformation, enabling next-state prediction for continuum robots without measured joint angles. In Gaussian modeling, a feed-forward network reconstructs the robot as 3D Gaussians from multi-view images, and the backbone, a chain of coordinate frames, is fitted to back-projected depth points to estimate the bending state. In transition, a network predicts its change under a relative motor command to deform the bound Gaussians. The deformed Gaussians render the next images and the 3D shape, and the distal frame gives the tip pose. Training losses use RGB-D images and masks, without bending or tip labels. On simulated and real robots, BendGS achieves the lowest Chamfer distance, best image quality, and lowest tip errors among the compared methods.

</details>


### [290] [Ada-BFM: Adapting Behavior Foundation Models for Humanoid Scene Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31863)
*Furui Xu,Weishuai Zeng,Zirui Wang,Kangning Yin,Jiacheng Bao,Huayi Wang,Yingying Qin,Yufei Xue,Yang Tang,Zenghao Tang,Kailin Li,Jingbo Wang,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Ada-BFM：通过轻量适配器对预训练人形行为基础模型做后训练，使其获得场景交互（地形/物体）能力并保留全身控制泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为基础模型（BFM）在大规模行为数据预训练后具备通用全身协调能力，但其动力学/运动先验与场景几何约束、接触动力学不匹配，难以直接用于场景交互；同时为每个任务单独训练控制器代价高。

Method: 后训练框架：在预训练BFM架构上插入轻量适配器（adapter）做交互特定微调，冻结/复用原控制能力；按交互域（terrain、object）训练共享模型而非逐任务控制器，支持域内多样任务。

Result: 仿真与真机实验显示：可实现多样场景交互行为；相比从头训练收敛更快；基本保留预训练行为技能（通用全身控制能力不退化）。

Conclusion: 适配器式后训练是将BFM能力扩展到预训练分布之外场景交互的高效途径，兼顾效率与能力保持；摘要未量化保持程度与真机任务规模。

Abstract: Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose humanoid control through pretraining on large-scale behavioral data. However, their proficiency in whole-body coordination does not readily extend to scene interaction, which requires adapting motion to scene-dependent geometric constraints and contact dynamics. To bridge this gap, we introduce Ada-BFM, a post-training framework that adapts pretrained BFMs for humanoid scene interaction. Ada-BFM augments the original architecture with a lightweight adapter, enabling interaction-specific adjustments while leveraging pretrained control capabilities. Rather than producing a separate controller for each task, the framework yields a shared model for each interaction domain, supporting diverse tasks involving terrain or object interaction. Extensive experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate that Ada-BFM enables diverse scene interaction behaviors, accelerates learning compared with training from scratch, and largely preserves pretrained behavioral competence. These findings establish Ada-BFM as an effective and efficient approach to extending the capabilities of BFMs beyond their pretraining settings while retaining their strengths in general whole-body control.

</details>


### [291] [CALVIN-MAX: When Does a Mid-Execution Change Hurt a Robot Policy?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31868)
*Sajjad Pakdamansavoji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 CALVIN-MAX 基准：在 CALVIN 五指令链上构造 7,828 个案例，每条链只对一条指令引入一次变化（物体/相机/机器人/指令），并用三条仅在受影响指令开始时才出现差异的对照运行，将"改变世界的成本"与"中途遭遇变化的时序效应"解耦。10 个已发布策略在 1,526 个留出案例上一致下降 4.4–9.9 个百分点，且成本会外溢到后续指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作已表明任务中途的一次变化会使所有被测试策略的成功率下降，但没有把"变化发生的时机效应"与"变化后世界本身的成本"分开，也没有衡量变化在任务结束之后遗留的代价。作者希望提供一个可控制的诊断型基准，量化这两类效应并检验其持续性。

Method: 构建 CALVIN-MAX：基于 CALVIN 的五指令链共 7,828 个案例，每例仅含一次变化，类型涵盖物体、相机、机器人本体与指令。每例设计三条对照组：无变化运行；变化自受影响指令开始即到位；变化在机器人执行该指令的运动过程中才发生。以"第二条 vs 第一条"度量改变世界带来的成本，以"第三条 vs 第二条"度量中途遭遇变化的影响。另设回卷实验（将机器人重置到该指令起点）以检验时序收益是否可复现。在每策略 1,526 个留出案例上评估十个已发布策略，假设预先固定。

Result: 八个核心变化上，十个策略在受影响指令上的成功率下降 4.4–9.9 个百分点。时序效应依变化类型而异：改变机器人视觉输入的变化在运动中途发生时比自始存在少损失 5.4 个百分点，而场景本体或机器人本体的变化无可检测的时序效应。回卷到指令起点会消除所有策略的时序收益；预先设定的更严格检验（回卷运行与第二条运行相匹配）仅三个策略通过。成本具有持续性：处于完成状态但仍保留变化时，策略完成下一条指令的概率下降 5.0–16.8 个百分点。

Conclusion: 世界变化对长程机器人策略的代价既取决于变化类别，也取决于遭遇时机，并在受影响指令结束后继续外溢到后续指令，说明当前策略缺乏对观测过时与场景漂移的鲁棒性修复机制。该基准将"变化成本"与"时序效应"分离的可控对照设计，为诊断而非总体评分式的评测提供了可复用范式。

Abstract: Robots must often keep working after the world changes, as when a block moves or the camera shifts while the robot still acts on what it saw before. Recent work showed that one change during a task lowers success for every policy tested, but did not separate the effect of its timing from the cost of the changed world, or measure what it costs afterwards. We introduce CALVIN-MAX, a benchmark of 7,828 cases on CALVIN's five-instruction chains, each with one change to the objects, the camera, the robot, or the instruction. Each case compares three runs that are identical until the affected instruction begins: one without the change, one with it in place from that moment, and one in which it arrives while the robot is moving. The second run against the first gives the cost of the changed world, and the third against the second the effect of meeting it mid-motion. On 1,526 held-out cases per policy, with hypotheses fixed in advance, each of ten released policies loses 4.4 to 9.9 percentage points on the affected instruction over the eight core changes. Whether timing matters depends on the change: one that alters what the robot sees costs 5.4 points less mid-motion than from the start, whereas a change to the scene itself or to the robot shows no detectable effect. Rewinding the robot to the start of the instruction removes the benefit for every policy; the stricter test we fixed in advance, that the rewound run then matches the second run, passes for three. The cost also outlasts the affected instruction: the change stays in place, and a policy that still completes that instruction is 5.0 to 16.8 points less likely to complete the next one.

</details>


### [292] [RESET: One Demonstration, One Verified World, Self-Improved Skills Across Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31894)
*Zheng Sun,Zhuo Li,Zhihao Li,Hengyi Sim,Ge Wang,Ziyue Zhu,Mingkai Jia,Fei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RESET框架：用单条真实演示重建接触丰富的仿真世界并生成冻结的成功判据，经开环重放与轮廓一致性校验后，自进化白盒任务图与技能代码，最终零LLM调用部署到真实机器人并迁移至异构机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人任务缺乏真值成功评分器与可复位练习环境，LLM编码代理的自我进化能力被限制在仿真基准中。

Method: 单演示同时提供场景几何、终止成功判据与物理参考轨迹；流程为 Rebuild（重建世界+冻结判据）、Examine（开环重放+轮廓一致性校验）、Self-improve（基于逐节点失败证据进化模块化技能图代码）、Execute（冻结代码零LLM调用部署）、Transfer（共享世界与判据给无演示的新机器人）。

Result: 8个任务仿真新布局平均成功率97%，对比零样本代码27%、pi0.5微调31%、Diffusion Policy 50%；故意注入重建缺陷表明练习可容忍多数重建误差，几何误差仍会误导，检查能标记大部分、物理随机化覆盖其余。3个异构机器人无目标演示获得技能，相似臂机器人95%技能函数原样复用；4台真实机器人280次试验成功率80%。

Conclusion: 单条真实演示可同时充当世界重建源、判据与保真度校验参照，实现可校验、可共享、零运行时LLM调用的真实机器人技能自进化与跨本体迁移。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents can iteratively self-improve robot manipulation skills in simulation benchmarks, yet real-world tasks provide neither ground-truth success scorers nor resettable practice environments. Our key observation is that a single real demonstration simultaneously specifies scene geometry, defines the terminal success criterion, and serves as a physical reference trajectory for verifying world fidelity---all decoupled from the source robot's kinematics. We present {RESET}: we reconstruct a contact-rich world and derive a frozen read-only criterion from one demonstration ({R}ebuild); check that world against the demonstration by open-loop replay and silhouette consistency ({E}xamine); evolve modular white-box task graphs and skill code from per-node failure evidence ({S}elf-improve); deploy frozen code on real robots with zero runtime LLM calls ({E}xecute); and share the verified world and criterion with unseen robots without new demonstrations ({T}ransfer). On 8 manipulation tasks, each rebuilt from one real demonstration, RESET achieves 97% average success on unseen layouts in simulation, whereas zero-shot code reaches 27%, per-task fine-tuned pi0.5 policies 31%, and Diffusion Policy 50%; injecting reconstruction defects shows that practice tolerates many reconstruction errors, that geometric errors can still stall or mislead it, and that the check flags most of them while physical randomization covers the rest. Without any target demonstrations, 3 heterogeneous robots acquire the same task skills in the shared world: a robot with similar arms copies 95% of its skill functions unchanged from the source robot, while robots with different arms and hands write their own. In real-robot experiments, the resulting programs run on 4 robots and succeed in 80% of 280 trials.

</details>


### [293] [PreWAM: Exploring the Design of Latent Future Prediction for World-Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31901)
*Wan Chi,Sen Li,Zihang Cui,Chuqin Huang,Yunlong Wang,Nanshan Deng,Yanjun Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PreWAM 将世界-动作模型的未来预测监督组织在预训练 DINO 特征空间中，用"残差世界预测"和"时间尺度预测"两个目标共同驱动共享视觉-语言主干，在 LIBERO、SimplerEnv、RoboTwin 2.0 和真机上验证了操作性能与分布偏移下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 world-action model 用视觉过渡作为策略学习的监督，但"预测空间"的选择缺乏系统研究。作者发现：空间结构化的语义特征（如 DINO）在视觉扰动下泛化更好，且学习到的预测表示更易恢复运动信息，因此希望把未来监督对齐到这一空间。

Method: 1) 受控实验比较不同预测空间（像素 vs. 预训练语义特征）对泛化性与运动可恢复性的影响；2) Residual World Prediction：联合学习源帧相对变化量与其推导出的未来状态，突出动态信息同时保留未来状态语义；3) Temporal-Scale Prediction：以时间距离为条件调节 world query，兼顾短时局部运动与长时程状态演化；4) 上述预测目标与动作监督共同在预训练与下游策略学习中塑造共享 VLM 主干。

Result: 在 LIBERO 系列基准、SimplerEnv、RoboTwin 2.0 以及真实机器人操作上均取得较强的操作性能，并在多种分布偏移下表现出更好的泛化性与鲁棒性。摘要未给出具体数值提升。

Conclusion: 预测空间的设计是 world-action model 的关键自由度；以 DINO 语义特征为空间、以状态变化为监督核心，可在不改变主干架构的前提下提升泛化与鲁棒性。局限在于缺少定量消融与真机规模细节。

Abstract: World-action models combine action generation with future prediction, allowing visual transitions to serve as supervision for policy learning. Our controlled study of prediction spaces shows that spatially structured semantic features support better overall generalization under visual perturbations and make motion information more readily recoverable from learned predictive representations. Building on these observations, we introduce PreWAM, a world-action model that organizes future supervision around state changes in a prediction space defined by pretrained DINO features. Specifically, Residual World Prediction jointly learns source-relative changes and their resulting future states to emphasize dynamic information while preserving future-state semantics. Temporal-Scale Prediction conditions world queries on temporal distance to capture both short-term local motion and longer-horizon state evolution. These predictive objectives jointly shape a shared vision-language backbone with action supervision during pretraining and downstream policy learning. Experiments across the LIBERO benchmark family, SimplerEnv, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world robotic manipulation demonstrate strong manipulation performance and improved generalization and robustness under diverse distribution shifts.

</details>


### [294] [Learning When a Robot Should Ask For Help: Adaptive Intervention Requests in Human-Robot Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31925)
*Yanran Jiang,Cécile Paris,Dana Kulić,Leimin Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于离线强化学习的“求助策略”（help-seeking policy），机器人依据历史干预有效性与自主推进情况，在干预更可能有益时才请求人类介入，从而自适应生成求助时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂环境中自主导航失败难以避免，及时的人类干预对防故障与恢复很关键；但频繁或不当时机的求助会加重操作员负担、降低团队效率，延迟求助又会导致长时间停机或不可恢复失败，且人-机器人-任务上下文共同影响干预效果。

Method: 用离线强化学习训练求助策略，奖励/信号来自过去干预的有用性与自主导航进展；策略整合人、机器人、任务上下文决定是否及何时发出求助，使机器人选择性地请求人类帮助。

Result: 在多任务环境与不同用户专业度下进行仿真评估，并开展真实用户研究：学习策略提升探索效率、减少不必要告警，在不损害专家表现的前提下改善对新手用户的支持。

Conclusion: 自适应学习型求助策略可在人机协同任务中平衡干预收益与操作员负担，兼顾新手支持与专家效率。

Abstract: Robots operating in complex environments occasionally encounter situations where autonomous navigation fails. Requesting and receiving a timely intervention from their human supervisor can be critical for failure prevention or recovery. However, requesting help too frequently or at inappropriate times can increase operator workload and reduce team efficiency, while delayed requests may lead to prolonged downtime or unrecoverable failures. In addition to alert timing, the human, robot and task context also influences the intervention outcomes. This paper proposes an algorithm for learning to generate adaptive human intervention requests during human-robot teaming. A help-seeking policy is trained via offline reinforcement learning based on past intervention helpfulness and autonomous progress. This learned alert policy enables a robot to selectively request human help when the intervention outcome is likely to be beneficial. We evaluate the proposed policy in different task environments and user expertise levels in simulation, followed by evaluation in a physical user study. Our findings demonstrate that the learned policy improves exploration efficiency, reduces unnecessary alerts, and enhances novice user support without compromising expert performance in real-time human-robot teaming missions.

</details>


### [295] [RoboHarness-RL: Agentic Reinforcement Learning for VLM-Based Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32016)
*Liyang Li,Mingyu Liu,Xiaoman Li,Zhiyue Zhao,Jin-Chuan Shi,Canyu Zhao,Haokai Zhang,Jinyuan Tian,Linhao Zhong,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboHarness-RL：让紧凑开源 VLM（Qwen3.5-9B）通过"工具调用型 agent harness"操作机器人，用任务成败奖励做多轮 RL；配合 LiteHarness 分层记忆压缩多轮上下文，无需 VLA/WAM 即可显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿 VLM 借助 agentic harness（感知、规划、控制工具）已能操作机器人，但紧凑的开源权重 VLM 难以达到同等水平；希望在不训练 VLA 或世界动作模型的前提下，用强化学习把 agent 式工具调用能力"奖励出来"。

Method: 两阶段：先在成功教师示范上做监督微调（SFT）；再用任务结果奖励（task-outcome reward）做多轮 RL，对工具调用轨迹进行优化。为支撑高效多轮训练提出 LiteHarness——用分层记忆（hierarchical memory）保持交互上下文紧凑，同时维持任务性能。整体是纯 agentic 框架，不依赖 VLA 或 world action model。

Result: Qwen3.5-9B 上 RoboTwin 2.0 成功率 31.2%→44.0%，LIBERO-Pro 57.8%→73.3%；零样本跨场景（训练未见随机化 RoboTwin）26.4%→38.0%。行为分析：重复调用率最多降低 4×、成功执行更短、对负面反馈的恢复能力提升。

Conclusion: 表明对紧凑开源 VLM 而言，agentic RL + 高效上下文管理是替代 VLA/WAM 路线的可行方案；但全部证据限于仿真基准（RoboTwin/LIBERO-Pro），无真实机器人验证，绝对成功率仍偏低（约 44%），方法细节在摘要层面较薄弱。

Abstract: Agentic harnesses enable frontier vision-language models (VLMs) to manipulate robots through perception, planning, and control tools, yet achieving comparable performance with compact open-weight VLMs remains challenging. We present RoboHarness-RL, an agentic reinforcement learning framework for improving VLM-based robot manipulation through tool calls. Starting from supervised fine-tuning on successful teacher demonstrations, the VLM further improves through multi-turn reinforcement learning with task-outcome rewards, without relying on vision-language-action models or world action models. To support efficient multi-turn training, we develop LiteHarness, which uses hierarchical memory to keep interaction contexts compact while preserving task performance. With Qwen3.5-9B, RL raises success from 31.2% to 44.0% on RoboTwin 2.0 and from 57.8% to 73.3% on LIBERO-Pro; these gains extend to randomized RoboTwin scenes unseen during training (26.4% to 38.0%). Behavioral analysis further shows that RL reduces repetitive-call rates by up to $4\times$, shortens successful executions, and improves recovery from negative feedback. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [296] [KeyMemVLA: Subtask-Guided Key Memory for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32081)
*Yongwei Zhang,Tao Chen,Sicheng Ma,Yuanzhe Xing,Pengwei Zhang,Yongen Zhao,Yaoqing Hu,Zhikun She,Ce Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KeyMemVLA 提出由子任务引导的稀疏关键帧记忆框架，通过进度指示与决策证据两级标注选择关键帧，监督在线记忆写入，并联合学习子任务、动作与关键帧预测，同时构建 KMBench 评测长时程主动记忆保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆依赖型长时程操作要求 VLA 策略推断任务进度并回忆当前观测中不存在的证据，而记忆预算有限，需保留能消除阶段歧义、支撑后续决策的历史。

Method: 两级标注（进度指示 / 决策证据）筛选子任务并将其末帧标为关键帧作为在线记忆写入监督；共享视觉-语言骨干联合训练子任务预测、动作预测与关键帧预测；推理时先用指令、视觉上下文与记忆预测当前子任务，再以该语义条件预测动作与未来记忆写入时刻，执行时在选定时刻保留真实观测。另构建 KMBench 基准。

Result: 在 RMBench-M(n)、RoboTwin-MeM、KMBench 及真实机器人操作任务上平均成功率高于基线；消融与表征分析支持关键记忆与显式任务进度语义的作用。

Conclusion: 稀疏关键记忆与在线进度推断相结合对历史依赖控制具有明确价值，可作为长时程 VLA 记忆机制的可复用设计。

Abstract: Memory-dependent long-horizon manipulation requires vision-language-action (VLA) policies to infer task progress and recall evidence absent from current observations. Under a limited memory budget, the challenge is to retain history that resolves stage ambiguity and supports subsequent decisions. We present KeyMemVLA, a subtask-guided key memory framework based on progress indication and decision-evidence retention. A two-level annotation process selects subtasks satisfying either criterion and labels their final frames as keyframes to supervise online memory writes. A shared vision-language backbone jointly learns subtask, action, and keyframe prediction. At inference, the policy first predicts the current subtask from the instruction, visual context, and memory, then uses this semantic condition to predict actions and future memory-write positions. Actual observations are retained at the selected times during execution. We introduce KMBench to evaluate active memory retention for decisions across subtasks. Evaluations on RMBench-$M(n)$, RoboTwin-MeM, KMBench, and real-world manipulation tasks show higher average success rates than the compared baselines. Ablation studies and representation analysis support the roles of key memory and explicit task-progress semantics. These results highlight the value of coupling sparse key memory with online progress inference for history-dependent control.

</details>


### [297] [MARGIN: A Risk-Aware Agent for Robot Program Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32093)
*Hongbo Duan,Fan Du,Haipeng Luo,Bin Qian,Zichao Nie,Yilin Mi,Yaopeng Tian,Jiake Tian,Siyuan Liu,Yan Gao,Fangming Liu,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MARGIN 是一个风险感知的机器人编程智能体，将成功 rollout 转化为"近失裕度"证据，用于程序修复与候选选择，提升未见场景下的执行安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人编程智能体只优化二元成功率，成功率饱和后仍无法反映执行风险；在未见场景/种子上仍大量失败，缺乏可量化的稳健性信号。

Method: 定义 near-miss margin：最慢成功开发 rollout 中剩余执行预算的比例。以此信号优先修复脆弱程序、指导定向修复、在保持开发成功率前提下挑选更大裕度的修订版本；并提出精确 safe-racing 规则加速基于成功的候选评估，配合成对执行探针识别隐藏回归。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Pro 80 任务复现中，78 个程序开发集成功率满分但未见种子大量失败；margin 预测留出集失败 AUC=0.774，低裕度程序失败风险高 2.97 倍。两轮独立评估（37 个 LIBERO-Pro 任务）执行步数降低 10.7%/15.0%，LIBERO-90 与 Robosuite 降低 18.0–20.0%；safe racing 减少 53.5% 候选评估回合且选择结果一致。

Conclusion: 近失裕度可作为风险感知、预算高效的机器人程序精炼有效依据，弥补二元成功率饱和的评估盲区。

Abstract: Robot-programming agents improve executable control policies through repeated testing and repair, yet binary success saturates while execution risk persists across unseen scenes. We introduce MARGIN, a risk-aware agent that converts successful robot rollouts into actionable evidence for program refinement. Its near-miss margin measures the fraction of the execution budget remaining in the slowest successful development rollout. The agent uses this signal to prioritize fragile programs, guide targeted repairs, and select revisions that preserve development success while increasing execution headroom. An exact safe-racing rule accelerates success-based candidate evaluation. We evaluate MARGIN across LIBERO-Pro, LIBERO-90, and Robosuite. In our 80-task LIBERO-Pro reproduction, 78 programs attain perfect development success while accounting for substantial failures on unseen seeds. The near-miss margin predicts held-out failures with an AUC of 0.774, and low-margin programs exhibit 2.97 times higher failure risk. Across two independent evaluation rounds spanning 37 unique LIBERO-Pro tasks, MARGIN reduces executed control steps by 10.7% and 15.0%, with reductions of 18.0-20.0% on LIBERO-90 and Robosuite. Safe racing reduces fixed-sequence candidate-evaluation episodes by 53.5% while retaining identical selections, and paired execution probes improve acceptance decisions by identifying hidden regressions. Together, these results establish near-miss evidence as an effective basis for risk-aware, budget-efficient robot program refinement.

</details>


### [298] [ActiveBridge: Scaling Active Prior for Robots through Egocentric Perception-Interaction Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32098)
*Zixiao Gu,Qiren Qin,Yu He,Zhengyang Liu,Da Huang,Hongquan Zhang,Simo Wu,Peng Zhou,Guangnan Ye,Yunbo Wang,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ActiveBridge 训练框架，从第一人称（egocentric）人类视频中学习"主动感知"先验，通过 world-action modeling 将头部主动视角控制能力迁移到机器人，使单头部相机、无腕部相机的机器人能主动调整视角完成操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型对被赋予的固定观测做被动响应，忽略了 egocentric 视频中人类"主动寻求信息"的先验，导致非结构化场景性能受限；同时现有方案依赖腕部相机获取近距离细节。

Method: 设计具备头部主动视角控制能力的机器人（仅单头部相机）；训练阶段用指令条件模型联合预测未来 egocentric 视频与同步的 head-wrist 动作轨迹，头部动作提供主动感知的显式监督；通过 world-action modeling 将人类主动先验迁移到机器人。

Result: 在主动操作任务上优于基线；消融实验表明 head-wrist 联合动作监督带来增益。

Conclusion: 将人类主动感知先验与世界-动作联合建模结合，无需腕部相机即可实现主动视角操作，验证了联合头-腕动作监督的有效性。

Abstract: Robotics foundation models are now widely used for manipulation, and egocentric videos offer rich data for scaling. However, existing methods overlook the human active priors captured in egocentric videos, which limits performance in unstructured scenes. They act on given observations instead of actively seeking information as humans do. We propose ActiveBridge, a training framework that learns these human active priors and transfers them to robots through world-action modeling. We design a robot that actively controls its viewpoint through head actions to acquire the visual information needed for manipulation using a single head camera, without wrist cameras. During human training, an instruction-conditioned model jointly predicts future egocentric videos and synchronized head-wrist action trajectories, with head actions providing explicit supervision for active perception. Experiments show that ActiveBridge outperforms baselines on active manipulation tasks, while ablations demonstrate the benefit of joint head-wrist action supervision.

</details>


### [299] [Ego2Real: Learning Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation from Egocentric Visuo-Tactile Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32101)
*Ange Bao,Haishan Zhang,Lingzi Xie,Shuang Li,Pei Zhao,Ling Shi,Bidan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Ego2Real 用第一视角人类示范加触觉手套，将双手灵巧操作技能迁移到仿人手：在两阶段引入触觉监督——触觉引导的手-物体交互重建与接触先验强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯视觉示范缺少接触力信息，导致手-物交互解释存在歧义，人类到机器人灵巧技能迁移不可靠；需引入触觉传感提升示范质量与可迁移性。

Method: 框架包含两部分：(1) 触觉引导的手-物体交互重建，用测得的触觉信号修正手部姿态与接触几何；(2) 接触引导强化学习，将人类接触模式转化为机器人的接触奖励。训练时结合精修后的动作参考与物理接触奖励，使仿人手在自身形态与驱动约束下复现示范接触行为。

Result: 实验表明，在重建与策略学习两个阶段都加入触觉监督可提升操作成功率，验证触觉观测对第一视角示范质量与可迁移性的作用。

Conclusion: 将触觉监督同时注入交互重建与策略学习，是提升人类示范到灵巧机器人操作迁移效果的有效途径。摘要未给出基线对比数值、任务数量与真机验证细节。

Abstract: Learning dexterous manipulation from egocentric human demonstrations offers a scalable path toward robot skill acquisition. Vision alone, however, provides limited information about physical contacts and the demonstrator's force intent, leading to ambiguities in interpreting hand-object interactions. Capturing human touch via tactile sensing is therefore crucial for reliable human-to-robot dexterous skill transfer. We present Ego2Real, a framework that leverages egocentric demonstrations and tactile gloves to transfer bimanual dexterous manipulation skills to robotic hands. Ego2Real incorporates tactile supervision at two complementary stages: (1) tactile-guided hand-object interaction reconstruction, which uses measured tactile signals to refine hand poses and contact geometry; and (2) contact-informed reinforcement learning, which translates human contact patterns into robotic contact rewards. By combining refined motion references with physical contact rewards during training, Ego2Real guides anthropomorphic robotic hands to emulate demonstrated contact behaviors while adapting to their own morphology and actuation. Experiments show that incorporating tactile supervision into both reconstruction and policy learning yields higher manipulation success rates, underscoring the importance of enriching egocentric demonstrations with tactile observations to improve their quality and transferability.

</details>


### [300] [Duo: Towards Spatiotemporal Action Coordination in Bimanual Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32108)
*Zhimu Zhou,Yuqi Yang,Yixin Lin,Junsen Zhang,Jian Zhao,Jiayu Zhong,Jiaxun Sun,Bingxin Wang,Binhua Zuo,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Duo 将双臂在流匹配策略中拆分为每时间步两个动作 token，通过跨臂注意力建模空间与时间依赖，在 RoboTwin 2.0、RoboDojo 与真机任务上显著提升成功率与执行速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 flow-based 机器人策略把双臂动作合并为每个未来时间步的单一 token，注意力只能在时间维度上区分位置，无法在臂维度上区分，导致双臂协调能力受限。

Method: 在单一共享 action head 内，将每步动作为左右臂拆成两个 token，共享注意力主干，并在 flow matching 过程中引入跨臂注意力（cross-arm attention）实现臂间交互；辅助以系统性消融实验，分析臂间协调、动作分组与 token 粒度。

Result: RoboTwin 2.0 上使四个基线策略整体成功率全部提升（0.45B–3.62B 参数），FLOWER(0.89B) 提升 12.55 个百分点、π0.5(3.62B) 提升 9.02 个百分点；FLOWER-Duo 在 RoboDojo 的随机泛化、精度与内存指标上取得最佳。真机任务中成功率与执行速度快于 π0.5，并随训练数据规模有效扩展。

Conclusion: 跨臂注意力是性能提升的主因，臂级交互在精度与计算开销之间给出最佳权衡；Duo 用极简改动即可通用增强现有双臂流策略。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation requires each arm to perform distinct motions while coordinating with the other. Common flow-based robotic policies combine both arms' actions into one token per future timestep, giving attention distinct positions across time but not across arms. We introduce Duo, a simple but effective design that models spatial and temporal dependencies within one shared action head. Specifically, Duo separates both arms into two action tokens per timestep, allowing each arm's motion to interact via cross-arm attention during flow matching. On RoboTwin 2.0, Duo improves the overall success of all four base policies, from 0.45B to 3.62B parameters, with gains of 12.55 and 9.02 percentage points for FLOWER (0.89B) and $\pi_{0.5}$ (3.62B), respectively. Despite its compact size, FLOWER-Duo achieves the best randomized-generalization, precision, and memory scores on RoboDojo. In real-world tasks, it outperforms $\pi_{0.5}$ in both success rate and execution speed, while scaling effectively with increasing training data. To provide deeper insight into the design of Duo, we conduct systematic ablations on inter-arm coordination, action grouping, and token granularity, confirming that cross-arm attention drives the improvement while arm-wise interaction offers the best accuracy-compute trade-off.

</details>


### [301] [Reading Geometric Anchors: Viewpoint Generalization in Imitation Learning for Robotic Manipulation with RGB-Only Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32162)
*Chengyu Tao,Chunxi Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReGeoAnch：在机器人基座系建全局锚点、在夹爪系建局部锚点作为跨视角共享的物理参考，配合PAA与解耦的Anchor-to-Policy Readout及跨视角读出一致性约束，提升模仿学习的视角泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机移动会改变物体在图像中的位置，RGB模仿学习难以泛化；现有"合成视角配对同一动作"的做法忽略了跨视角的物理对应关系，而基于标定预测深度的3D策略又受观测点集变化（点集不匹配、非稳定）困扰。

Method: 1) 基座系全局锚点 + 夹爪系局部锚点，提供跨视角共享物理参考；2) Decoupled Appearance-Guided Geometric Readout，用Point-to-Anchor Aggregation在锚点邻域聚合，得到可比且稳定的几何表征而非逐点对应；3) Anchor-to-Policy Readout：用视角不变的机器人状态查询视角依赖的外观以选取任务相关锚点，仅由几何值形成最终读出，形成可控解耦的信息流；4) Cross-View Readout Consistency，对齐源视角与合成目标视角的策略读出方向。

Result: 在MetaWorld与ManiSkill3共12个操作任务上，相比基线策略在视角泛化上取得提升（抽象未给出具体数值与真机实验）。

Conclusion: 以锚点为中心的几何锚定可作为RGB模仿学习的通用视角泛化机制；解耦外观/几何信息流与跨视角一致性是可复用设计原则，但证据限于仿真基准。

Abstract: Moving the camera changes where objects appear in an image, challenging RGB-based imitation learning. A common strategy pairs synthesized views with the same demonstrated action, but ignores supporting physical correspondence across views. By predicting depth with camera calibrations, 3D policies may handle it yet suffer the issue of varied observed point sets. To address these limitations, we propose ReGeoAnch, a geometry-anchored imitation learning framework for viewpoint generalization. Specifically, we build global anchors in the robot base frame and local anchors in the gripper frame, providing shared physical references across viewpoints. Furthermore, Decoupled Appearance-Guided Geometric Readout uses Point-to-Anchor Aggregation (PAA) to obtain comparable and stable geometry around anchors beyond pointwise correspondence. A controllable and decoupled information flow, Anchor-to-Policy Readout (APR), leverages complementary visual and geometric information and supports viewpoint generalization: view-dependent appearances are queried by view-invariant robot state to select task-relevant anchors, with geometric values alone to form the final readout. We further introduce Cross-View Readout Consistency to align the directions of policy readouts from paired source and synthesized target views. Experiments on 12 manipulation tasks across MetaWorld and ManiSkill3 demonstrate improved viewpoint generalization over baseline policies.

</details>


### [302] [Wind-Aware Trajectory Planning for Deformable Aerial Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32184)
*Haozhe Wang,Xun Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Family-Air，一种风感知的轨迹族规划器：将无人机编队共享安全飞行空间建模为虚拟管道，用共享仿射变换表示连续轨迹族，联合优化编队中心运动、形变与时间分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集城市环境中的协同空中运输需要无人机编队穿越狭窄空间并适应建筑周围空间变化的风场。编队形变虽能通过狭窄通道，却会改变单机速度及所遇局部风，使编队运动与气动效应强耦合，现有规划方法难以处理这种耦合。

Method: 以虚拟管道作为共享无碰撞飞行空间；用单一共享仿射变换参数化连续轨迹族，将空间变化风与形变诱导速度统一写入"对空速度"目标函数；优化中施加虚拟管道约束、机间分离约束与运动学可行性约束，同时优化编队中心轨迹、形变与时间协调。

Result: 在四类城市风场场景的仿真中验证了局部风-形变耦合的重要性；飞行仿真显示轨迹跟踪精度提升。

Conclusion: 风感知的轨迹族规划在复杂城市环境的协同空中运输中具有潜力；但证据以仿真为主，缺乏实飞验证与开源代码。

Abstract: Coordinated aerial transport in dense urban environments requires drone formations to navigate confined spaces and adapt to spatially varying winds around buildings. Formation deformation enables passage through narrow spaces but changes individual drone velocities and the local winds they encounter, coupling formation motion with aerodynamic effects. We propose Family-Air, a wind-aware trajectory-family planner that constructs a virtual tube as shared collision-free flight space and jointly optimizes formation-center motion, deformation, and timing. A shared affine transformation represents a continuous trajectory family, incorporating spatially varying wind and deformation-induced velocities into a unified air-relative motion objective. The optimization enforces virtual-tube containment, inter-drone separation, and kinematic feasibility. Simulations across four urban wind-field scenarios demonstrate the importance of local wind-deformation coupling. Flight simulations further show improvements in trajectory tracking accuracy. These results demonstrate the potential of wind-aware trajectory-family planning for coordinated aerial transport in complex urban environments.

</details>


### [303] [OVOK: A Recursive Self-Improving Agentic Framework for Video-Based Articulated Robot State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32185)
*Cheng Dai,Renkai Luo,Guanfeng Tang,Siming Li,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OVOK:由智能体自改进框架从单段视频递归恢复机器人关节状态与相机参数,通过 proposer–executor–verifier 闭环演化工具与可执行工作流,验证通过即冻结用于推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视频恢复铰接机器人状态对操作、场景重建与动作规划有重要价值,但该问题病态(内参、相机-机器人位姿、关节角耦合)。已有方法或假设内参已知、单机器人专用且需大量数据采集、跨场景迁移差;基础模型虽零样本通用但误差大。因此需要一个机器人无关、自改进、可验证的智能体框架。

Method: 智能体化框架:(1) 在人工撰写协议下由 agentic initializer 构建机器人专属 OVOK-4k 数据集;(2) proposer–executor–verifier 循环,基于反馈联合演化工具(code tools)与可执行工作流;(3) 仅当 verifier 判定正向改进时才接受更新,保留的最优工作流冻结用于精确推理。

Result: 在 DREAM-V 与 RoboKeyGen-V 两个基准上,演化得到的工作流取得 SOTA 精度,估计稳健且准确。

Conclusion: 将"工具+工作流"的共同演化与验证门控机制用于视频到运动学的估计,可突破单机器人专用回归器与通用基础模型之间的精度—泛化权衡;为机器人状态感知提供可复用的自改进范式。

Abstract: Recovering articulated robot state from video supports metric spatial sensing for manipulation, scene reconstruction, and action planning. Yet the problem is ill-posed: different intrinsics, camera-to-robot poses, and joint angles can explain similar observations. Prior task-specific regressors typically assume known intrinsics, fit one robot through laborious data collection and robot-specific training, and still transfer poorly across scenes. Foundation models apply to any robot zero-shot, but their estimates have large errors. We present OVOK (One Video to Kinetics), a recursive self-improving agentic framework for this task. Under a human-authored protocol, an agentic initializer constructs robot-specific OVOK-4k; a proposer--executor--verifier loop then jointly evolves tools and executable workflows from feedback. Updated tools and workflows are accepted only when the verifier judges a positive improvement. The retained workflow is frozen for accurate inference. Experiments demonstrate that the evolved workflow achieves state-of-the-art results on DREAM-V and RoboKeyGen-V, delivering robust and accurate estimates.

</details>


### [304] [SkillHarness: Execution-Aware Coordination with Plug-and-Play Skills for Long-Horizon Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32186)
*Bailin He,Xiting Zhao,Kang Wang,Xinyu Zhou,Yongxing Cui,Haojie Liu,Qiao Sun,Hongyan Zhang,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkillHarness：执行感知的“插拔式”运动技能库 + VLM 引导的事件触发 Harness（几何可行性校验+执行阶段约束），实现跨地形技能组合与扩展，无需重训运动策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多地形运动多依赖固定策略，适应性差、技能不可扩展；感知扰动与不可靠的技能切换削弱长时程通过的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出执行感知协调框架 SkillHarness，集成可插拔运动技能库；设计保留任务相关环境参考、持续更新本体感知反馈的感知RL策略以降低感知抖动敏感性；用视觉-语言引导的 Harness 通过事件触发推理、几何可行性验证与执行阶段约束协调导航与技能执行，并引入执行反馈与回溯性决策修正以改进技能选择、抑制错误切换。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果；声称在多种地形配置、感知扰动与多障碍长时程穿越任务中评估其适应性与可靠性，可在不重训运动策略的情况下组合/扩展技能应对未见地形与任务组合。

Conclusion: 以“技能库+高层协调器”的解耦架构替代固定端到端运动策略，是提升长时程复杂地形导航可扩展性与鲁棒性的可行路线，但缺乏量化验证与实机细节披露。

Abstract: Traversing complex terrain requires robots to coordinate diverse locomotion skills while adapting to unseen obstacles and changing environments. Although perceptive reinforcement learning enables agile locomotion, conventional multi-terrain locomotion rely on fixed policy, limiting adaptability and skill extensibility. Perceptual disturbances and unreliable skill transitions further challenge robust execution. To address these challenges, we propose SkillHarness, an execution-aware coordination framework that integrates a flexible, plug-and-play locomotion skill library. To enhance traversal robustness, we introduce a perceptive reinforcement learning approach that preserves task-relevant environmental references while continuously updating proprioceptive feedback, reducing sensitivity to perceptual fluctuations. A vision-language-guided Harness coordinates navigation and skill execution through event-triggered reasoning, geometric feasibility verification, and execution-phase constraints. By flexibly composing and extending available skills, SkillHarness adapts to unseen terrain and task combinations without retraining the locomotion policies. The framework further incorporates execution feedback and retrospective decision revision to improve skill selection and avoid inappropriate skill switching. We evaluate SkillHarness across diverse terrain configurations, perceptual disturbances, and multi-obstacle traversal tasks to assess its adaptability and reliability in long-horizon traversal.

</details>


### [305] [Disentangling Visual Dynamics for World Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32228)
*Yuhong Shi,Yihan Guo,Jie Wei,Jianyi Liu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FactorWorld提出解耦视觉动力学世界模型框架：用双向反事实对引导的CCRF模块分离智能体与外部动力学，并用语义路由将因子翻译为规划决策，在MiniGrid、MetaDrive、CARLA上提升因子稳定性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型中智能体动力学与外部环境动力学相互纠缠，会掩盖任务进展；仅靠因子稳定性无法保证规划距离有意义，需同时解决跨因子干扰抑制与因子-任务信号关联两个问题。

Method: 提出FactorWorld框架：1）Counterfactual Consensus Residual Factorizer（CCRF），以双向反事实对为引导，将共享上下文与动态残差分离，结合平均约束与最差配对约束抑制干扰，并用状态与运动监督保留有用信息；2）语义路由机制，基于因子refine全表征任务预测，用于状态估计、轨迹打分与任务自适应规划。

Result: 在MiniGrid、MetaDrive、CARLA上，两种骨干网络均提升双向因子稳定性；MiniGrid上因子路由将平均成功率从78.80%提升至82.40%；在含Push-T的四个连续控制任务上平均成功率91.67%~100%。

Conclusion: 将解耦的视觉动力学与显式任务结合可显著提升世界模型的规划能力，解耦本身需配合任务语义路由才有规划价值。

Abstract: Visual world models enable planning through predictive latent states. However, entangled agent and exogenous dynamics can obscure task progress. Disentangling visual dynamics for planning requires addressing two challenges: 1) suppressing cross-factor interference while retaining useful information; and 2) linking factors to task-relevant signals, since stability alone does not ensure meaningful planning distances. Therefore, we introduce FactorWorld, a disentangled visual dynamics framework for world model planning. To separate agent and exogenous dynamics, we propose a Counterfactual Consensus Residual Factorizer (CCRF) guided by bidirectional counterfactual pairs. CCRF separates shared context from dynamic residuals, combining average and worst-partner constraints to suppress interference with state and motion supervision to retain useful information. To translate these factors into planning decisions, we introduce semantic routing to refine full-representation task predictions for state estimation, trajectory scoring, and task-adapted planning. Experiments on MiniGrid, MetaDrive, and CARLA show improved bidirectional factor stability across two backbones. On MiniGrid, factor routing raises mean SR from 78.80% to 82.40%. Across four continuous control tasks, including Push-T, FactorWorld achieves mean success rates ranging from 91.67% to 100% across tasks. These results demonstrate that combining decoupled visual dynamics with explicit tasks can significantly improve the planning capabilities of world models.

</details>


### [306] [Roboclock: A Unified Benchmark for Time-Aware Robotic Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32233)
*Yujie Zhao,Anthony L. Hu,Zimeng Huang,Zhijing Wu,Luobin Wang,Richard Li,Shrivas Sudharsan,Henrik I. Christensen,Jishen Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboClock是面向时间感知机器人操作的统一基准，涵盖5个域60个任务、三类时间约束（时间窗、事件响应、吞吐率），并引入推理与控制延迟期间持续推进环境时间的Latency-Aligned评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准主要关注空间正确性与逻辑任务完成，忽视真实应用中的时间约束，导致动作物理正确却因错过时间窗、响应过慢或吞吐不足而失败。

Method: 构建60个任务、5个应用领域的时间感知操作基准，按三种时间约束类型（Temporal Window、Event-Reactive、Throughput）组织；提出Latency-Aligned评测协议，在模型推理与控制接口延迟期间持续推进环境时间，并配以时间敏感指标；评测30余种方法。

Result: 最佳方法为训练过的VLA，但在Easy级别任务上时间成功率仅8.3%，泛化与迁移仍存持续挑战。

Conclusion: 当前机器人系统在时间推理与执行延迟方面存在根本性局限，需联合优化物理能力、时间推理与执行延迟。

Abstract: Time is a fundamental dimension of robotic intelligence: a robot may fail despite physically correct actions if it acts outside a valid time window, responds too slowly to an event, or fails to meet required throughput. Yet existing robotic benchmarks primarily emphasize spatial correctness and logical task completion, largely overlooking the temporal constraints of real-world applications. We present RoboClock, a unified benchmark for time-aware robotic manipulation featuring 60 tasks across five application domains. Mirroring these failure modes, RoboClock structures tasks around three types of temporal constraints: Temporal Window, Event-Reactive, and Throughput.
