Furthermore, RoboClock incorporates a Latency-Aligned evaluation protocol that continuously advances environment time during model inference and control-interface delays, paired with time-sensitive metrics. Evaluating over 30 methods, we find that the best-performing approach is the trained VLA, yet it achieves only 8.3% temporal success even on Easy-level tasks, with persistent challenges in generalization and transfer. These findings reveal fundamental limitations in current robotic systems and highlight the need to jointly optimize physical competence, temporal reasoning, and execution latency.

</details>


### [307] [Boundary-Aware Policy Learning for Agent-Orchestrated Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32238)
*Kaipeng Fang,Yuhao Wang,Ji Zhang,Yunsong Zhou,Jingkuan Song,Lianli Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出边界感知策略学习（BAPL）：先通过边界显式训练在多样起始位姿的接触段上微调视觉运动策略，得到宽且可刻画的“交接边界”；再用边界扩展自改进，在筛选后的成功系统轨迹上重训策略，把边界前移，使智能体能更早、更少调用LLM地交接控制权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体+视觉运动策略的分层系统常在“交接时刻”失败：策略只在有限起始状态集合内可靠，而多模态智能体可能在区域外调用它。现有整任务策略的交接边界狭窄且难以被智能体刻画，导致协调不可靠。

Method: 1) Boundary-Explicit Training：在带多样起始位姿的接触片段上微调策略，塑造宽且定义清晰的边界，并通过简单交接协议告知智能体；2) Boundary-Expanding Self-Improvement：用筛选的成功系统rollout重训策略，将边界扩展到任务更早阶段，使智能体更早交接、减少语言模型调用。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0与RoboDojo上比最强基线高26+个百分点，随机化RoboTwin 2.0场景成功率98.4%；API成本与执行时间分别降低41.3%和37.5%。

Conclusion: 稳健的智能体-策略编排不仅取决于单个组件能力，更关键在于显式地塑造二者之间的“交接边界”；边界既是可学习对象，也是可通信的接口。

Abstract: Agent-orchestrated robot systems combine the general reasoning capability of multimodal agents with the precise physical control of learned visuomotor policies. Yet such systems often fail at the moment of handoff: a policy is reliable only from a limited set of start states, while the agent may invoke it outside this region. We call this region the policy's handoff boundary. Existing whole-task policies typically have handoff boundaries that are narrow and difficult for an agent to characterize, making reliable coordination challenging. We propose Boundary-Aware Policy Learning, which explicitly shapes and then expands the handoff boundary of a visuomotor policy. First, Boundary-Explicit Training fine-tunes the policy on contact segments with diverse start poses, yielding a wide and well-defined boundary that can be communicated to the agent through a simple handoff protocol. Second, Boundary-Expanding Self-Improvement retrains the policy on curated successful system rollouts, extending the boundary toward earlier stages of each task and allowing the agent to hand over sooner with fewer language-model calls. Across RoboTwin 2.0 and RoboDojo, our system outperforms the strongest baseline by over 26 percentage points, reaching 98.4% success in randomized RoboTwin 2.0 scenes, while further reducing its API cost and execution time by 41.3% and 37.5%, respectively. These results suggest that the key to robust agent-policy orchestration lies not only in improving either component individually, but also in explicitly shaping the boundary between them.

</details>


### [308] [Motion Chain of Thought: Disentangling Motion Semantics from Visual Appearance for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32241)
*Xiangkai Ma,Xinyi Wang,Junjie Wang,Sheng Xu,Mingyang Li,Yue Ma,Han Zhang,Yuzheng Zhuang,Wenzhong Li,Zhihao Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PILOT框架，通过"表征推演"(RD)让动作分支显式建模潜在状态转移token，并以运动链式思维(CoT)形式保留于推理空间，缓解WAM中高层物理状态演化与低层动作轨迹的表征纠缠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有World Action Model的视觉分支仅预测静态视觉观测，未体现运动交互下世界状态演化的潜在转移信息，导致Action Model内部高层物理条件演化与低层动作轨迹生成出现表征纠缠，形成结构性瓶颈，并削弱世界演化建模对动作生成的预测作用。

Method: PILOT以表征推演(RD)为核心机制，将运动链式思维(CoT)引导作为模型原生能力，驱使动作分支显式生成潜在状态转移token，并在推理空间中保留为CoT以引导细粒度运动轨迹，从而解耦高层运动语义与低层轨迹细节；RD引入的丰富状态转移监督信号用于缓解动作生成的稀疏监督。

Result: 在复杂机器人操作任务上，RD提升了WAM的成功率与泛化能力；通过解耦高层运动语义与低层轨迹细节增强了物理可解释性；额外的状态转移监督信号有效缓解稀疏监督，支持以更少示教数据实现真机数据高效适配。

Conclusion: 以潜在状态转移token作为推理空间内的运动CoT，可有效弥合世界演化建模与动作生成之间的结构鸿沟，作为WAM的通用增强模块兼具性能、可解释性与数据效率收益。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) aim to construct a unified architecture capable of understanding world state evolution and guiding action generation. However, existing visual branches focus on predicting static visual observation, rather than reflecting potential transition information that captures the evolution of world states under motion interactions. This leads to representational entanglement between high-level physical condition evolution and low-level action trajectory generation within the Action Model, creating a structural bottleneck while weakening the predictive capability of world evolution modeling for action generation. We propose PILOT (Physical Inference for Latent Optimized Trajectories), whose core Representational Deduction (RD) bridges this gap by integrating motion chain-of-thought (CoT) guidance as a native model capability. Specifically, RD aims to encourage the action branch to explicitly model potential state transition tokens, which are retained as CoT in the reasoning space to guide fine-grained motion trajectory. Experiments demonstrate that RD not only improves the success rate and generalization ability of WAMs in complex robotic manipulation tasks but also enhances the model's physical interpretability by decoupling high-level motion semantics from low-level trajectory details. Furthermore, the abundant state transition supervision signals introduced by RD effectively alleviate the sparse supervision in action generation, supporting data-efficient real-robot adaptation with reduced demonstration sets.

</details>


### [309] [VLAs Execute, Code Recovers: 3D Skill Envelopes Bridge the Compositional Gap in Agentic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32245)
*Jiajie Zhang,Yankai Xiang,Changhao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过因果干预发现冻结VLA技能在拼接处失败源于其刚性复现既有轨迹并碰撞场景残留物；提出Skill Envelope——策略实测的3D几何前置条件，将其暴露为CHECK/FREE两个经典3D工具，用代码修复越界状态，在LIBERO-Goal上大幅提升长程组合成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合冻结VLA技能可实现规模化长程操作，但即使规划正确，原子技能在"接缝"处仍常失败；常规做法（重置位姿、语言描述入口条件）忽略了物理根因：冻结策略会刚性重放习惯轨迹并撞上被移动的场景残留物，无论是否"看得见"。

Method: 因果干预揭示失败机理；提出Skill Envelope：在成功执行中测量的3D几何扫掠体（策略可测的前置条件）。利用"刚性→轨迹可预测"的洞见，将每个包络实例化为两个经典3D工具：CHECK（检测阻挡残留）与FREE（定位安全放置区）。形成分工：VLA负责分布内操作，代码负责修复分布外状态。

Result: 在LIBERO-Goal＋冻结π0.5上：零失败包络以0.946 AUROC预测碰撞（单RGB-D视角0.944），接缝就绪度0.902优于前沿VLM的0.793；用于技能排序与代码修复后，两步接缝成功率82.8%→92.3%，含抽屉的3–4步链29.7%→93.8%，与特权世界重置相当。

Conclusion: 可靠的VLA组合取决于对策略物理工作空间的测量，而非用语言猜测前置条件；后续将接入LLM自主编排、自由物体恢复合成与跨策略/真机评测。

Abstract: Composing frozen Vision-Language-Action (VLA) skills under an agentic planner promises scalable long-horizon manipulation without long-horizon demonstrations, yet strong atomic skills routinely fail at seams even under a correct plan. Standard remedies reset the robot's pose or draft entry conditions in language, missing the physical cause we uncover via causal interventions: even after re-homing, a frozen VLA rigidly retraces its habitual trajectory and collides with displaced scene residue, whether or not it can see it. We propose the Skill Envelope, a policy-measured 3D geometric precondition capturing the volume a frozen skill sweeps during successful executions. Exploiting the insight that the rigidity making a frozen skill fragile at seams also makes its trajectory predictable in advance, we expose each envelope as two classical 3D tools (CHECK to detect blocking residue and FREE to locate safe placement regions), establishing a division of labor where VLAs execute in-distribution manipulation and code repairs out-of-distribution states. On LIBERO-Goal with frozen $\pi_{0.5}$, our zero-failure envelope predicts collisions at 0.946 AUROC (0.944 from one RGB-D view) and beats frontier VLMs on seam readiness (0.902 vs. 0.793); using it for skill ordering and code repair lifts success from 82.8% to 92.3% on two-step seams and from 29.7% to 93.8% on drawer-bearing 3-4 step chains, matching privileged world reset. Together, these results show that reliable VLA composition hinges on measuring a policy's physical workspace rather than guessing preconditions in language. Ongoing work closes the loop with autonomous LLM orchestration, synthesized free-object recovery, and cross-policy evaluation on cluttered benchmarks and real hardware.

</details>


### [310] [RoboDemo: Reconciling Demonstration Intent and Execution Progress for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32256)
*Xiangkai Ma,Zhihao Yuan,Linbo Zhai,Xinyi Wang,Junjie Wang,Sheng Xu,Guiliang Liu,Han Zhang,Chuyi Chen,Jifeng Gao,Wenzhong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboDemo 提出基于双重级联测试时训练（Dual-Cascaded TTT）的示教增强操作框架，以参考视频（人类演示或仿真机器人视频）构建意图记忆、以在线交互构建进度记忆，通过快权重独立更新与级联读取实现长时序机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序操作需要全局程序先验（子任务阶段期望进度）与实时交互进度追踪并存；现有策略仅从当前观测预测下一动作，缺乏持久的过程性意图记忆，且人类演示与机器人执行间存在语义-运动差异。

Method: 将参考视频（人类演示或语义兼容的仿真机器人视频）的任务语义与物理运动信息蒸馏进持久"示教意图记忆"；部署时将在线观测、机器人状态与已执行动作因果压缩为"交互进度记忆"。两类视频 token 分别以快权重独立更新状态，避免在进度演化中覆盖意图；动作专家执行级联记忆读取，先取参考引导再用实际进度精修，从而在偏差下自适应而不破坏存储意图。

Result: Imitator Game 成功率 90.7%，三项真实家庭任务平均成功率 73.3%；含真实世界视频、代码与 HF 权重开源。

Conclusion: 以双流记忆解耦"意图稳定"与"进度演化"是长时序示教增强 VLA 的有效设计，且支持有人类配对演示与仅有仿真参考两种设定，具备真实机器人验证与开源可复现性。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation requires more than predicting the next action from the current observation. The policy must maintain a global procedural prior that specifies the expected progress of each subtask stage, while also tracking the realized progress produced by its interactions. To this end, we propose RoboDemo, a demonstration-augmented manipulation framework built on Dual-Cascaded Test-Time-Training. RoboDemo accepts either a task-relevant human demonstration video or a semantically compatible robot video retrieved from simulation corpus as reference, allowing us to evaluate its applicability with and without paired human demonstrations. Task semantics and physical motion information from the reference video are then transferred into persistent demonstration-intent memory. During deployment, we causally compress online observations, robot states, and executed actions into interaction-progress memory. To keep reference intent stable while interaction progress continually evolves, reference and execution video tokens independently update their respective fast-weight states. The action expert performs cascaded memory reads, first obtaining reference guidance and then refining it using realized progress, allowing the policy to adapt to execution deviations without overwriting the stored intent. RoboDemo achieves 90.7% success on Imitator Game and 73.3% success across three real-world household tasks. Real-world videos, code and hf-weights are available in our project website.

</details>


### [311] [EgoLoAF: Learning Humanoid Loco-Manipulation from Egocentric Demonstrations with Hand-Object Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32260)
*Yiming Zeng,Jiangyuxuan Dai,Yuheng Na,Tan Zhu Xuan,Zihan Lin,Xinfang Qi,Shijian Zhang,Chengqi Shi,Yihan Kuang,Zhide Zhong,Junjie He,Junfeng Li,Zimu Han,Naipeng Li,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoLoAF：无需穿戴动捕设备，直接从第一人称人类演示中导出人形loco-manipulation的全部训练数据（躯干运动由相机位姿估计、双手轨迹由视频重建），以手-物相对位姿作为显式affordance经cross-attention注入action expert，仅用转换数据后训练VLA即可接近遥操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形VLA训练依赖昂贵的真机遥操作；现有ego-video转人形数据的方法多数仍需穿戴式动捕，手-物交互线索隐式，且常混入真机遥操作数据，限制数据规模化与接触精度。

Method: 从第一人称录制中估计相机/躯干运动并重建双手轨迹得到人形动作目标；用每只手相对物体的当前位姿表示hand-object affordance，在后训练阶段通过cross-attention注入action expert（VLM backbone冻结）；纯转换数据后训练，无任何真机遥操作数据；同时发布128,400条演示、1,000小时、1,284任务的ego数据集及转换动作轨迹与渲染的机器人视角观测。

Result: 四个真实世界任务上平均Task Progress Score 73.1 vs 遥操作74.4，数据采集效率平均提升4.03倍。

Conclusion: 无需穿戴动捕与真机遥操作的ego演示可直接后训练人形VLA，显式affordance注入是弥补人-机具身差距、接近遥操作性能的关键，且具备规模化数据管线。

Abstract: Training humanoid vision-language-action (VLA) models relies heavily on costly real-robot teleoperation. Recent efforts convert egocentric human demonstrations into humanoid training data. However, most methods still require wearable motion trackers and leave important hand-object interaction cues implicit. Many also mix in real-robot teleoperation data for VLA post-training. These dependencies limit scalable data collection, while implicit interaction cues make precise contact harder to learn. To overcome these limitations, we propose EgoLoAF, the first paradigm to derive all training data for humanoid loco-manipulation directly from egocentric demonstrations without wearable motion trackers. We post-train the VLA on the converted data alone, without real-robot teleoperation demonstrations, and use explicit hand-object interaction cues to guide action generation. Specifically, to obtain humanoid action targets, we estimate torso motion from recorded camera poses and reconstruct both hand trajectories from video. From the same recording, we use each hand's current pose relative to the object to represent hand-object affordances. During post-training, we inject these affordances into the action expert through cross-attention while keeping the vision-language backbone frozen. Additionally, we release 128,400 egocentric demonstration episodes spanning 1,000 hours and 1,284 tasks, together with converted humanoid action trajectories and rendered robot-view observations. Across four real-world tasks, EgoLoAF nearly achieves teleoperation performance (73.1 vs. 74.4 in mean Task Progress Score), while achieving a mean 4.03 times improvement in data-collection efficiency.

</details>


### [312] [Putting a Robot on a Phone Call: Layered Signaling, Bounded Occupancy and Turn Arbitration over WebRTC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32308)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Zaide Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将 Thor 机器人接入浏览器语音通话:分离 HTTPS 信令、WebRTC 中继媒体与语音生成三条可观测路径,配合会话槽、非阻塞扬声器锁与 2 s 抑制门,16 次首拨中 15 次达到可说话状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人语音接入(浏览器↔机器人扬声器)涉及信令、实时媒体、语音生成与本地语音抢占等多路并发资源,需要可观测的端到端工程化编排与冲突控制机制。

Method: 浏览器预取中继凭证并在首个 relay candidate 后留 500 ms 窗口;后端在 55 s 截止内并发启动语音合成与 WebRTC answer 准备;用会话槽 + 非阻塞扬声器锁协调通话与本地语音访问;2 s 门让控制器在播放前抑制对话回复。基于应用层事件日志做 40 次受控重放的时序分析。

Result: 16 次首拨中 15 次 ready-to-speak(Wi-Fi 7/8,中位 5.57 s;蜂窝 8/8,中位 6.76 s);20 个占用/恢复场景均允许后续呼叫;5 次 60 s 断网时后端在手机离线期间释放线路;2 s 门在 1 s 决策时成功抑制聊天,3 s 决策时按序播放且无重叠输出;另暴露两处边界缺陷(回执命名错位、新呼叫返回前任反馈)。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一套可观测、可调节的机器人语音通话时序编排与冲突抑制机制,但仅为单平台应用层事件与模拟控制器决策的测量,声学输出与任务正确性未纳入评估。

Abstract: We put a Thor robot on a browser voice call, with speech sent from Safari, answers played through the robot's loudspeaker and transcripts returned to the phone. We separate HTTPS signaling, relayed WebRTC media and speech generation into independently observable routes. The browser prefetches relay credentials and allows a 500 ms window after its first relay candidate. The backend starts speech and WebRTC-answer preparation concurrently within a 55 s deadline. A session slot and a non-blocking speaker lock coordinate calls with local voice access. A 2 s gate gives the controller an opportunity to suppress chat before playback. Fifteen of sixteen first dials reached ready-to-speak, comprising seven of eight Wi-Fi calls with a median of 5.57 s and eight of eight cellular calls with a median of 6.76 s. All twenty scored occupancy and recovery scenarios admitted a later call. During five 60 s outages, the backend released the line while the phone remained offline. Across 40 controlled replays, enabling the gate suppressed chat when the controller decided at 1 s. At 3 s, chat and controller feedback both played in sequence, with no overlapping output in any trace. Two additional boundary traces exposed a receipt naming a later feedback item and a new call's query returning its predecessor's feedback. These measurements cover application events on one platform and simulated controller decisions, with acoustic output and task correctness outside their scope.

</details>


### [313] [Any2Act: Scaling In-Context Robot Learning from Heterogeneous Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32353)
*Ziang Ren,Zifan Wang,Zhuoyang Li,Benhao Qin,Haoyang Li,Qingtao Liu,Xuchuan Chen,Zekun Qi,He Wang,Yanchao Yang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Any2Act：以"执行者本体/相机布局/动作空间"作为统一示范表示，一次性地从120万机器人-机器人轨迹学"跟随"，再将人类视频、仿真、UMI、异构机器人示范翻译进该表示，配合少量对的路由适配器（冻结主干）实现免微调的单样本机器人ICL，未见任务成功率57.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人上下文学习可让策略通过上下文示范完成未见任务而无需任务微调，适合"低频LLM规划+高频执行策略"的系统。但人类视频示范受限于稀缺的人-机配对，机器人示范方法又只支持一两种来源且规模有限。核心痛点是：不同示范来源在相机视角与动作空间上不一致，导致每新增一种来源就要重新学习如何"跟随"。

Method: 关键洞察：示范应以执行者（executor）而非示范者（demonstrator）的相机布局与动作空间表达。1）用120万条真实轨迹、1万任务中天然满足该形式的机器人-机器人配对，一次性预训练"跟随能力"；2）人类视频、仿真、UMI、其他机器人数据被翻译成语义统一的本体grounded表示；3）每种来源训练仅需数百对数据的source-routed适配器吸收残余域差，预训练主干保持冻结；4）长程执行采用免训练tracker，仅喂入执行进度附近窗口的示范片段以限定上下文长度，失败步骤可回滚重试。

Result: 单条示范、无微调条件下，未见任务成功率57.5%，优于按每任务50条示范微调的VLA；使用其他示范来源时仍保留执行者自身示范成功率的74%–91%。

Conclusion: 统一"执行者grounded"示范表示可把"学会跟随"与"接纳新来源"解耦，使跨本体、跨模态示范近乎即插即用地扩展机器人ICL，并支持长程分窗执行与失败回滚。

Abstract: Robot in-context learning (ICL) enables a policy to perform unseen tasks by following contextual demonstrations, without task-specific fine-tuning. This is increasingly valuable as large language models reasoning at low frequency can specify tasks through demonstrations and delegate high-frequency execution to such a policy. However, methods conditioned on human videos are bounded by scarce human-robot pairs, while those conditioned on robot demonstrations accept only one or two source types at limited scale. We present Any2Act, a foundation model for robot ICL. Our key insight is that a demonstration should be expressed in the camera layout and action space of the robot that performs it (the executor), not of whoever provided it (the demonstrator). This unified embodiment-grounded representation decouples learning to follow from admitting a new source. Any2Act learns to follow once, from robot-to-robot pairs that already take this form in 1.2M real trajectories spanning 10K tasks. Human videos, simulation, UMI, and other robots are then translated into this representation, and source-routed adapters trained on only a few hundred pairs each absorb the residual gap while the pretrained model stays frozen. For long horizons, a training-free tracker feeds only the demonstration window around the executor's progress, bounding the context and rolling back to redo failed steps. From a single demonstration, Any2Act achieves 57.5% success on unseen tasks without fine-tuning, exceeding VLAs fine-tuned on 50 demonstrations per task; other demonstrators retain 74-91% of the success achieved with the executor's own demonstrations.

</details>


### [314] [Does Research-Paper Context Improve LLM-Generated Quadruped Navigation? An Exploratory Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32381)
*Barnabas Juhasz,Rolf Pfister*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在LLM与运动策略均冻结的条件下，比较提供/不提供特定研究论文作为上下文时，Claude Opus 5 为 Go2 四足机器人编写导航控制器的差异。提供"增量重规划"论文后，8个控制器中仅2个能完成全部四段行程，而未提供时为7/8（Fisher精确检验 p=0.041），且差异仅在物理仿真中出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 已能为机器人编写可用的导航代码，但随着领域文献增长，模型难以在任务中召回并正确应用相关研究成果。作者希望验证以论文作为上下文是否真能提升生成代码质量。

Method: 受控对照实验：固定 LLM（Claude Opus 5）与运动策略，仅在上下文中增删特定论文；涵盖三种代码形态（行为树、基于共享基座的导航器编辑、从零编写），并在 Isaac Sim 物理仿真与理想运动学仿真两种环境下评测，含迷宫中途路径被阻断的鲁棒性场景。

Result: 路径阻断场景下，提供增量重规划论文的控制器仅 2/8 完成全部行程，对照组 7/8（双侧 Fisher 精确检验 p=0.041）；该差距在理想运动学仿真中消失，说明物理真实性与鲁棒性差异无法被理想化指标捕获。

Conclusion: LLM 可能对作为上下文的论文内容及其目标过度加权（此处为增量重规划的效率），从而损害主任务完成度并产出更脆弱的代码；论文上下文并非无害的增益手段。

Abstract: Large language models can write competent robot navigation code, but not only does the research on the topic advance, a model may also, due to the volume of findings, fail to recall relevant findings and apply them to the task at hand. In this work, we test whether providing particular research papers as context improves the LLM-written navigation code for the Go2 quadruped, with the LLM (Claude Opus 5) and locomotion policy frozen. Specifically, we compare the navigation controllers the LLM writes when provided with and without particular papers across three settings: behavior trees, navigators edited from a shared base, and controllers written from scratch. When a maze's shortest route is blocked mid-episode, 2 of 8 controllers written with the context of an incremental-replanning paper complete all four trips under Isaac Sim physics, against 7 of 8 without it (two-sided Fisher's exact test, p = 0.041). This difference appears only under physics: in an idealized kinematic simulator, the controllers written with and without the paper are indistinguishable. We hypothesize that an LLM provided research papers as context tends to overweight both their content and their goals -- in this case, the efficiency of incremental replanning -- negatively affecting the fulfillment of the main task and leading to less robust code.

</details>


### [315] [Delay-Tolerant Formation Tracking for Multi-AGV Systems via Reference-Shift Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32409)
*Yunfei Bi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出级联式编队控制：邻居的延迟数据只驱动二阶滤波器平移本车参考轨迹，饱和保证可行性，增益调度跟踪律保持不变且前馈精确，从而跟踪环与网络解耦。证明任意有界延迟与有向/切换图下有界收敛，构造严格Lyapunov函数并给出显式收敛与耦合衰减因子，高保真AGV仿真4,894次运行验证鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多AGV协同搬运需在无线链路时变、跃变且链路相关延迟下保持编队并跟踪各自参考；直接延迟耦合一旦增益饱和驱动即失效，缺乏对任意延迟的理论保证。

Method: 面向运动学独轮车的差速AGV级联设计：延迟邻居数据仅进入二阶参考偏移滤波器，饱和限制参考偏移可行性，未修改的增益调度跟踪律跟踪平移后参考，前馈精确使跟踪环不受网络影响；证明有界/收敛性，构造带显式常数的严格Lyapunov函数，给出指数收敛、延迟无关增益的局部ISS估计及无向图上编队误差衰减因子λ/(λ+Λ·λ₂(L))；事件驱动误差可控仿真逐条验证不等式，并在含驱动动力学、打滑、定位误差、时钟偏移、丢包Wi-Fi的高保真模型上做4,894次运行与调参基线配对比较。

Result: 4,894次运行中级联方案从未失败，可承受至4 s延迟、90%丢包、12车规模；直接延迟耦合在增益使驱动饱和时失败。耦合使牵引失效后编队误差降低21–32%、安全停车后降低至47%，代价是跟踪精度；定位偏差与时钟偏移成为精度上限。

Conclusion: 延迟解耦的级联架构在任意网络条件下提供严格理论保证与实证鲁棒性，是网络化编队控制的可迁移范式；定位偏差与时钟偏移是不可观测的残余误差源。

Abstract: Automated guided vehicles (AGVs) that transport a load together must keep a formation while each vehicle tracks its own reference, and they coordinate over wireless links with link-dependent, time-varying and jumping delays. We propose a cascade design for differential-drive AGVs modelled as kinematic unicycles: delayed data from the neighbours drive only a second-order filter that shifts each vehicle's own reference, and an unmodified gain-scheduled tracking law follows the shifted reference. A saturation keeps the shifted reference feasible, and the feed-forward is exact, so the tracking loop never sees the network. We prove that tracking and formation errors remain bounded for arbitrary delays and converge for every bounded delay and every directed, possibly switching, graph. We also construct a strict Lyapunov function with explicit constants and prove exponential convergence and a local input-to-state estimate with delay-independent gains. On undirected graphs, the coupling attenuates the formation error caused by persistent local errors by at least the factor lambda/(lambda+Lambda*lambda_2(L)) (leak lambda, coupling gain Lambda, algebraic connectivity lambda_2(L)), for any delay. Every inequality is verified with an event-driven, error-controlled simulation. A robustness study on a high-fidelity AGV model (drive dynamics, slip, localisation errors, clock offsets, lossy Wi-Fi) comprises 4,894 runs, including paired comparisons against tuned baselines. The cascade never failed, with latencies up to 4 s, 90% packet loss and up to 12 vehicles, whereas a direct delayed coupling failed when its gain saturated the drives. Coupling reduced the formation error after traction losses by 21-32% and after safety stops by up to 47%, at the price of tracking accuracy. Localisation bias and clock offsets, which no coupling law can observe, limit the achievable accuracy.

</details>


### [316] [A Face Robot which Imitates Human Musculoskeletal Skin System and Its Evaluation on Basic Movement of Facial Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32413)
*Shun Kaneko,Shunnosuke Yoshimura,Akihiro Miki,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于人体面部骨骼-肌肉-皮肤分层解剖结构的面部机器人平台，用三维解剖数据确定几何与附着位置，通过PTFE管布线的线驱动柔性人工肌肉实现47个FACS动作单元，无皮肤时可复现40个，贴附硅胶皮肤后复现30个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面部表情机器人需要大量协同的小幅度驱动，在紧凑的面部空间内容纳这些驱动十分困难；现有面部机器人多采用集中式刚性驱动，难以复现人体面部肌肉的分层组织方式与细腻动作。

Method: ①依据三维解剖数据构建骨骼、肌肉、皮肤三层结构，几何形状与附着点均来自解剖学测量；②设计65条人工肌肉（34种类型）以对应FACS中的47个目标AU，肌肉为线驱动柔性元件，经PTFE管布线，使驱动器可置于面部之外；③分两阶段评测AU复现性：先无皮肤驱动，再贴附硅胶皮肤后驱动。

Result: 仅靠肌肉组件可复现47个目标AU中的40个（约85%）；贴附硅胶皮肤后复现30个（约64%），表明在解剖学启发的肌骨结构下可复现较广的FACS表情范围。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了解剖启发式肌骨结构复现FACS表情的可行性，为面部机器人的系统化设计与评估提供了平台基础；但皮肤附着后性能明显下降，说明皮肤力学与肌肉-皮肤耦合仍是待解决的关键问题，且缺乏真实表情识别或人类感知层面的定量评估。

Abstract: Facial robots require many coordinated, smallamplitude motions, but accommodating these motions in a compact face remains difficult. This paper presents an anatomically inspired facial-robot platform that reproduces the layered organization of the human facial musculoskeletal system. The platform comprises bone, muscle, and skin components whose geometry, shape, and attachment locations are derived from three-dimensional anatomical data. Wire-driven, compliant muscle components are routed through PTFE tubes, allowing actuation to be placed away from the face. We describe the selection and fabrication of 65 muscles (34 muscle types) required to reproduce the 47 target action units (AUs) in the Facial Action Coding System (FACS). We then evaluate the reproducibility of individual AUs in two stages: muscle actuation without skin and actuation after the silicone skin is attached. Forty of the 47 target AUs were reproduced with the muscle components, and 30 were reproduced after skin attachment. These results demonstrate the feasibility of reproducing a broad range of FACS facial expressions using an anatomically inspired musculoskeletal structure. The proposed platform provides a foundation for the systematic development and evaluation of future anatomically inspired facial robots

</details>


### [317] [ROVO: Robust Rotation-Only Visual Odometry for UAVs at Low and Moderate Altitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32475)
*Xiaokai Song,Li Zhang,Xichao Teng,Erting Pan,Yibin Ye,Kun Wang,Leqi Liu,Guangcheng Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出仅旋转视觉里程计ROVO，利用长距离视觉的角线索估计成对旋转并在滑窗内鲁棒平均，仅保留姿态状态以避免不确定深度传播，在低空/窄视场/高空条件下显著降低旋转误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机低中空单目视觉导航中，图像噪声会污染旋转与平移估计，微小扰动即可导致平移方向大误差；受远距离视觉感知中角度线索的启发，希望用纯旋转估计规避这一问题。

Method: ROVO：从匹配图像估计成对相对旋转，在重叠窗口内做鲁棒平均，并通过共享帧对齐相邻窗口；轨迹状态中只保留朝向，切断不确定地标深度向后续估计的传播；配套轨迹仿真工具。

Result: 轨迹仿真中滑窗ROVO平均朝向误差仅为传统SLAM基线的10.7%；真实Bell412飞行数据下与ORB-SLAM3、DPVO旋转精度相当；在更窄FoV或更高飞行高度下，相对旋转RMSE较次优方法降低19.7%–69.4%。

Conclusion: 仅保留旋转状态的轻量里程计方案在朝向估计上具备与完整SLAM/VO系统相当或更优的鲁棒性，代码与仿真工具开放，可作为无人机姿态稳定与导航的实用模块。

Abstract: Image noise can confound rotation and translation estimates in monocular visual navigation for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) at low and moderate altitudes, where even small perturbations may cause large errors in translation direction. Inspired by angular cues in long-range visual perception, we propose rotation-only visual odometry (ROVO) for monocular aerial imagery. ROVO estimates pairwise rotations from matched images, robustly averages them over overlapping windows, and aligns consecutive windows through shared frames. By retaining only orientation in the trajectory state, it prevents uncertain landmark depth from propagating to later estimates. In trajectory simulation, sliding-window ROVO achieved a mean orientation error just 10.7% as large as that of the conventional SLAM baseline. On real flight data, ROVO achieved rotation accuracy comparable to ORB-SLAM3 and DPVO under the original Bell412 settings. With a narrower FoV or at higher flight altitude, ROVO reduced relative rotation RMSE by 19.7--69.4% compared with the next-best method in each comparison. Code and simulation tools are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [318] [ViTacLab: Physically Consistent Visuo-Tactile Simulation, Benchmarking, and Representation Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32476)
*Linjing You,Zeming Yang,Zhenhao Shen,Shengqiang Xu,Guanda Li,Wenxuan Li,Jiyu Guo,Jasper Lu,Kyle Xu,Shawn Xie,Junjie Zhu,Siyu Wu,Jian Liu,Changhao Zhang,Rongchong Li,Zeyi Li,Hengshuang Zhao,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViTacLab提出ViTacSim/ViTacBench/ViTacPolicy一体化框架：基于PhysX接触求解器的三模态触觉基元（稀疏全局接触力格点、稠密局部分力阵列、触觉图像），通过局部刚度对齐修正法向不一致、解耦黏滑重建切向力并保持GPU并行；策略侧用空间位姿嵌入+Transformer聚合异构触觉，并用双流VQ-VAE共享码本对齐力阵列与触觉图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视触觉仿真器在物理保真度与可扩展性之间取舍，且触觉基准多局限于两指夹爪；多指灵巧手与双臂的富接触操作缺乏高保真、可并行、多模态统一且策略可消费的触觉仿真与评测体系。

Method: ViTacSim：三模态触觉基元锚定PhysX接触求解器输出，局部刚度对齐修正法向、解耦滑-黏机制重建切向、联合投影保持稀疏力矩并施加局部摩擦锥约束，同时维持GPU并行；ViTacBench：覆盖平行夹爪、灵巧手、双臂，任务从视觉主导到触觉主导；ViTacPolicy：空间位姿嵌入+Transformer聚合变数量异构触觉输入，双流VQ-VAE共享码本对齐力阵列与触觉图像。

Result: Hertzian参照压入与真实传感器滑动测试中，法向与切向力RMSE由2.31/1.85 N降至0.42/0.53 N；256个并行环境下触觉管线吞吐达2,482.9 FPS；仿真与真机实验在多项富接触任务上取得增益，但触觉主导任务绝对成功率仍低。

Conclusion: ViTacLab为多形态机器人提供高保真且可扩展的触觉仿真-基准-策略闭环，显著提升力重建精度与吞吐；纯触觉控制仍是开放难题，需更强触觉表征与闭环策略。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is essential for contact-rich robotic manipulation, but existing visuo-tactile simulators trade physical fidelity for scalability, and benchmarks largely focus on two-finger grippers. We present ViTacLab, a framework comprising ViTacSim, ViTacBench, and ViTacPolicy. ViTacSim uses a tri-modal tactile primitive: a global sparse contact-force lattice, a local dense force array, and tactile images. Anchored to PhysX contact-solver outputs, it corrects normal inconsistency through local stiffness alignment and reconstructs tangential forces under decoupled slip-stick regimes while preserving GPU parallelism. A joint projection preserves the sparse wrench and enforces local friction cones. ViTacBench spans parallel grippers, dexterous hands, and bimanual systems, with tasks ranging from vision-dominant to tactile-dominant regimes. ViTacPolicy aggregates variable numbers of heterogeneous tactile inputs using spatial-pose embeddings and a Transformer, and aligns force-array and tactile-image modalities through a two-stream VQ-VAE with a shared codebook. Controlled Hertzian-reference indentation and real-sensor sliding tests reduce normal and tangential force RMSE from 2.31 and 1.85 N to 0.42 and 0.53 N, respectively. Throughput reaches 2,482.9 tactile-pipeline FPS at 256 parallel environments. Simulation and real-world experiments show gains on several contact-rich tasks, while low absolute success on tactile-dominant tasks highlights the remaining difficulty of purely tactile control.

</details>


### [319] [CodeWAM: A Code World Action Model for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32483)
*Zhisheng Chen,Jinpeng Wang,Beicheng Lou,Zhengwei Xie,Hao Wu,Rui Qian,Kun Wang,Vivian W. H. Wong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CodeWAM将"可执行代码"作为驾驶世界模型表示的一部分，用代码刻画自车在候选规划下的状态演化，用学习到的视觉模型刻画场景后果，二者联合表示候选未来并用于规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶世界模型需要在候选规划下同时表示周围场景与自车运动，但纯学习的视觉预测对自车动力学/执行过程刻画不足，缺少"车辆如何过渡到该未来"的可解释、可校验表示。

Method: 混合表示世界模型：以可执行代码表达自车在规划执行下的状态演化（ego dynamics），以学习式视觉模型建模场景相关后果；执行代码得到候选条件化的运动序列，并与先前规划及历史记录运动对比评估；执行后果与场景预测联合构成候选未来，供规划器评估"会导致什么"与"如何过渡到该未来"；被选规划进入下一次决策的执行上下文，串联连续决策。

Result: NAVSIM v1 navtest 93.6 PDMS，v2 navtest 92.1 EPDMS，NAVHARD 50.6 EPDMS；成对执行一致性提升，且在Hydra-MDP与DrivoR上免训练即获增益。

Conclusion: 可执行代码可作为驾驶世界模型的有效互补表示，兼顾自车动力学可解释建模与场景后果学习；主要价值在自动驾驶闭环规划，属相邻/背景性参考，非具身操作或3D重建核心议题。

Abstract: Driving world models anticipate action consequences through representations of how the world evolves. For autonomous driving, these representations must capture both the surrounding scene and the ego vehicle's motion under a candidate plan. We propose CodeWAM, a driving world model that incorporates executable code as part of its world representation. Code represents ego dynamics by expressing how the vehicle state evolves under plan execution, while a learned visual model captures scene-dependent consequences. Executing the code produces a candidate-conditioned motion sequence, which is evaluated relative to the prior plan and recorded motion history. The resulting execution consequences and learned scene outcomes jointly represent the candidate's future, enabling the planner to assess both what a plan may lead to and how the vehicle transitions into that future. The selected plan becomes part of the next decision's execution context, linking successive choices within the model. CodeWAM achieves 93.6 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 navtest, 92.1 EPDMS on v2 navtest, and 50.6 EPDMS on NAVHARD. Further experiments demonstrate improved pairwise execution consistency and gains on Hydra-MDP and DrivoR without additional training, supporting executable code as a complementary representation for driving world models.

</details>


### [320] [CoFiNav: VLM-Guided Coarse-to-Fine Semantic Navigation for Open-Ended Last-Mile UAV Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32493)
*Xuanpu Wang,Shun Hu,Bing Li,Rongqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoFiNav：VLM 引导的粗到细无人机语义导航框架，通过层级语义搜索与主动3D观测，将开放式语义描述的地点（含空间关系）逐步落地为目标点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机配送依赖精确终端坐标、预定义起降点或任务专用定位流程，限制了适用范围与灵活性；开放式语义描述终端成为新范式，但传统空中导航难以泛化到开放词表终端导航场景。

Method: 给定任务描述、OSM 粗先验地图与累积观测，VLM 决定下一步应验证哪个未解析实体或空间关系；几何感知模块利用 RGB 与 LiDAR 将语义观测请求转化为可行视点；再由常规运动规划保障安全执行，形成"粗到细"的层级语义搜索闭环。

Result: 在多个城市场景实验中，CoFiNav 成功率优于代表性适配基线，同时飞行路径更短、所需观测次数更少。

Conclusion: 将 VLM 语义推理与几何可行视点生成、经典规划解耦组合，可有效支撑开放词表、关系定义的无人机终端导航，是低空物流语义化终端指定的可行范式。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) delivery is an important application of the low-altitude economy, enhancing the flexibility and accessibility of last-mile logistics. Existing UAV delivery methods generally rely on precise terminal coordinates, predefined terminal specifications, or task-specific localization procedures, limiting the scope and flexibility of delivery. As UAV logistics becomes more intelligent, open-ended semantic descriptions offer a new paradigm for specifying terminal destinations. However, conventional aerial navigation methods have limited ability to generalize to delivery scenarios that require open-vocabulary terminal navigation. To enable this broader delivery paradigm, we propose CoFiNav, a VLM-guided coarse-to-fine UAV semantic navigation framework that progressively grounds relation-defined terminal destinations through hierarchical semantic search and active 3D observation. Given a task description, a coarse prior map from OpenStreetMap (OSM), and accumulated observations, CoFiNav uses a vision-language model (VLM) to determine which unresolved entity or spatial relation should be verified next. A geometry-aware module converts the semantic observation request into feasible viewpoints using RGB images and LiDAR, while conventional motion planning handles safe execution. Experiments across multiple urban environments show that CoFiNav outperforms representative adapted baselines in success rate while requiring shorter flight paths and fewer observations.

</details>


### [321] [MRCT: An Object-Centric Two-Layer Control Architecture for Heavy-Payload Multi-Robot Transportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32502)
*Ning Wang,Yoshihiko Nakamura,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MRCT: a two-layer object-centric control architecture for heterogeneous mobile manipulators cooperatively transporting heavy payloads, combining object-level MPC contact-force generation with robot-level whole-body impedance control and confidence-based blending of velocity-tracking and impedance torques.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cooperative transport of heavy payloads by heterogeneous mobile manipulators demands simultaneous coordinated payload motion, contact-force distribution, and compliant physical interaction; this coupling is hard to satisfy with a single monolithic controller, and abrupt reference changes or disturbances can destabilize rigid tracking.

Method: Two-layer, object-centric hierarchy. Object level: MPC computes a coordinated contact-force reference for the payload. Robot level: per-robot whole-body impedance control (WBIC) realizes the reference via confidence-based blending of velocity-tracking and impedance torques, biasing toward tracking under small joint-velocity error and toward compliance when tracking degrades.

Result: Simulation and hardware experiments with a heterogeneous team (one single-arm and one dual-arm mobile manipulator) transporting a near-rated table payload show accurate object tracking, regulated contact forces, and stable payload orientation. Parameter sensitivity studies, obstacle-avoidance transport, and step-load disturbance tests further validate the approach.

Conclusion: MRCT offers a practical, modular scheme for reliable cooperative transport under the evaluated conditions, with the blending mechanism trading tracking for compliance adaptively; scope remains moderate payload heterogeneity and limited demonstration scale.

Abstract: Cooperative transportation of heavy payloads by heterogeneous mobile manipulators requires coordinated payload motion, force distribution, and compliant physical interaction. This paper presents MRCT, an object-centric two-layer control architecture for multi-robot transportation. At the object level, a model predictive controller (MPC) generates a coordinated contact-force reference for the payload. At the robot level, whole- body impedance control (WBIC) modules on individual robots realise the reference through a confidence-based blending of velocity-tracking and impedance torques. The blending mechanism emphasises tracking when the joint-velocity error is small and increases compliance when tracking degrades because of abrupt reference changes or physical disturbances. Simulation and hardware experiments with a heterogeneous team comprising a single-arm and a dual-arm mobile manipulator transporting a near-rated table payload demonstrate accurate object tracking, regulated contact forces, and stable payload orientation. Parameter sensitivity studies, obstacle-avoidance transportation, and step-load disturbance experiments further demonstrate the effectiveness of MRCT for reliable cooperative transportation under the evaluated conditions

</details>


### [322] [AirLeg: A Robust and Accurate Self-Supervised Visual-Inertial-Leg SLAM System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32523)
*Kuan Xu,Tianxin Hu,Ruofei Bai,Ruimeng Liu,Yuefan Hao,Zeyu Jiang,Zitong Zhan,Shenghai Yuan,Chen Wang,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AirLeg：自监督视觉-惯性-腿部（VIL）SLAM，通过仿真预训练+imperative learning（多传感器BA用腿式里程计精化视觉地图，地图反过来监督网络）实现无真值域适配，部署时融合估计在线更新网络；双窗估计器结合形变感知足-地形约束与方差分量加权。17个GrandTour序列平均误差0.19 m（19法中最低），低成本四足0.5 h无标签数据达0.6%漂移，嵌入式实时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人上VIL估计只需在IMU与关节编码器基础上加一个相机，成本低；但学习型腿式里程计受监督信号制约：仿真训练存在sim-to-real gap，真实标签依赖昂贵传感器或继承视觉估计误差。因此需要一个让视觉与腿部本体感知在训练与部署中互相促进的自监督框架。

Method: 1) 仿真预训练腿式里程计网络；2) 用imperative learning在真机上无真值适配：多传感器bundle adjustment用腿式里程计精化视觉地图，精化后的地图再监督网络；3) 部署阶段，融合估计结果在线适配网络；4) 双窗口估计器融合视觉、IMU、腿式里程计；5) 通过形变感知约束把承重足与视觉重建地形关联；6) 方差分量估计对约束加权。

Result: 在全部17个GrandTour序列上完成，平均误差0.19 m，为19种方法中最低；在低成本四足平台上仅用0.5 h无标签数据即取得0.6%的最低漂移，并在嵌入式计算机上实时运行。

Conclusion: 视觉与腿部本体感知的闭环互监督可有效绕过真值标注瓶颈，实现低成本的腿式机器人自监督VIL SLAM；形变感知足-地形约束与方差分量加权同时改善了定位精度与监督质量。

Abstract: Visual-inertial-leg (VIL) state estimation offers cost-effective localization for legged robots, adding only a camera to the onboard IMU and joint encoders. However, learning-based leg odometry is limited by its supervision: networks trained in simulation suffer from the sim-to-real gap, while real-world labels require costly sensors or inherit the errors of visual estimates. To address this, we present AirLeg, a self-supervised VIL SLAM system in which vision and leg proprioception improve each other from training to deployment. We pre-train the leg odometry network in simulation and adapt it to the robot without ground truth through imperative learning, where multi-sensor bundle adjustment uses the leg odometry to refine the visual map, which in turn supervises the network. During deployment, the fused estimates adapt the network online. To improve both localization and this supervision, a dual-window estimator fuses vision, IMU, and leg odometry, relates loaded feet to the visually reconstructed terrain through a deformation-aware constraint, and weights the constraints by variance component estimation. AirLeg completes all 17 GrandTour sequences with the lowest average error (0.19 m) among 19 methods. On a low-cost quadruped, it achieves the lowest drift (0.6%) with only 0.5 h of unlabeled data, in real time on an embedded computer.

</details>


### [323] [ContinualDrive: A Deployment-Anchored, Multi-Axis Benchmark for Continual Updates of Learned Driving Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32529)
*Zirui Li,Zhiqi Mao,Yangang Ren,Heye Huang,Haohan Yang,Qihang Huang,Zhan Chen,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ContinualDrive——面向驾驶规划器部署后持续学习的"部署锚定"基准，在 nuPlan 真实日志上按场景类型分组增量更新，显式化四条测量轴（评估闭环、参照检查点、时间聚合、数据流协议），并用三值发布裁决对照已部署模型；发现开环 ADE 与闭环分数、BWT 与最终表现之间结论可反转，全量/适配器更新均未在闭环上超越已部署模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习评估几乎不回答"更新是否真的优于已部署模型"这一问题，且结论依赖未明示的评估选择（开环/闭环、参照点、聚合方式、数据流协议），导致发布决策缺乏可靠依据。

Method: 构建 ContinualDrive 基准：以 nuPlan 实车日志按场景类型分组构成任务流；对 PlanTF 与 Diffusion Planner 两种规划器，采用全量微调与 Adapter/LoRA 更新，配以 replay 或梯度投影，测试三种任务顺序；同一批检查点在开环与闭环下评估，将四轴显式化，并将发布裁决（改进/无差异/退化）锚定到部署检查点。

Result: 结论对测量轴高度敏感：开环 ADE 在一半核心套件运行中将最终 PlanTF 排在部署模型之上，而所有闭环指标均排在其下；BWT 未能捕捉该性能损失（各组刚学完时损失已存在）；制度排序对随机种子不敏感、对任务顺序敏感；核心套件中没有任何更新制度在闭环上可测地优于部署模型，十倍规模面板上所有最终检查点均落后；全部组联合训练可恢复大部分损失但不超过部署模型的 nuPlan 闭环分数。

Conclusion: 持续学习结论的"改进/退化"判定必须锚定部署模型并同时声明四条测量轴，否则裁决可能反转或消失；据此提出报告契约并开源代码与全部检查点。

Abstract: Learned driving planners keep being updated after deployment as new logs arrive, yet continual learning evaluations rarely ask whether an update improves on the deployed model, and their verdicts rest on unstated evaluation choices. We present ContinualDrive, a deployment-anchored benchmark for post-deployment continual learning of driving planners. It updates a deployed planner on real-world nuPlan logs one scenario-type group at a time, evaluates the same checkpoints in open and closed loop, makes four measurement axes explicit (evaluation loop, reference checkpoint, temporal aggregation and stream protocol), and anchors a three-valued release verdict to the deployed checkpoint. We study two planners (PlanTF and Diffusion Planner) under full fine-tuning and adapter or LoRA updates, with and without replay or gradient projection, in three task orders. Verdicts can reverse or vanish when one axis changes: open-loop ADE ranks the final PlanTF model above the deployed one in half of its core-suite runs, whereas every closed-loop score ranks it below; backward transfer misses this loss, which on average is already present when each group has just been learned; and regime rankings vary far less across training seeds than across task orders. In the core suite of full and adapter updates, no regime measurably improved on the deployed planner in closed loop; on a panel more than ten times larger every evaluated final checkpoint trails it, and joint training on all groups recovers much of the loss without exceeding its nuPlan closed-loop score. We distill these findings into a reporting contract and will release the code and all checkpoints.

</details>


### [324] [Next-Manip: On-Demand Agent-Policy Collaboration for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32533)
*Jiayi Liu,Ke Ye,Jiaming Zhou,Yufei Huang,Haotian Liang,Kun-Yu Lin,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Next-Manip 提出按需的 agent–policy 协作框架：用轻量分类器 Agent Invocation Controller (AIC) 根据当前执行需求在策略与多模态 agent 之间分配控制权，并用 Task Dynamics Context (TDC) 将上下文示例中的关键操作变化压缩为任务参考，兼顾推理/恢复能力与细粒度控制效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一策略难以同时具备任务推理、失败恢复与精确控制，而操作数据有限；多模态 agent 推理强但控制粗糙、推理开销大，学习型策略执行高效但泛化受限于已学技能。二者互补，但关键问题是需反复判断当前应由哪个执行器接管。

Method: 解耦执行器分配与昂贵的 agent 推理：(1) Agent Invocation Controller (AIC)——轻量分类器判断当前执行需求，动态在 policy 与 agent 间切换控制权，避免每步都调用大模型；(2) Task Dynamics Context (TDC)——从 in-context 示例中抽取有信息量的操作变化，打包为紧凑任务参考，同时服务于 agent 推理与执行器分配决策。

Result: 在两个仿真基准与真实世界实验中取得 SOTA 性能，同时降低 agent 推理成本。

Conclusion: 按需调用 agent 的协作机制可在保留 agent 推理与恢复能力的同时获得策略的细粒度执行效率，为低数据条件下的通用操作提供了一种可落地的分层执行方案；真实机器人实验与开销降低提供了较强的实践证据。

Abstract: General-purpose robotic manipulation requires task reasoning, failure recovery, and precise control, yet limited manipulation data make these abilities difficult to obtain from a single policy. Recent multimodal agents show promise in task reasoning and recovery but lack reliable fine-grained control and incur high inference cost, whereas learned policies provide efficient fine-grained execution but struggle beyond their learned skills. Although their complementary strengths suggest combining the two, doing so requires repeatedly deciding which executor should act as task demands change. Instead of relying on the agent for each decision, we present Next-Manip, an on-demand agent--policy collaboration framework that decouples executor allocation from costly agent inference. We propose an Agent Invocation Controller (AIC) that allocates control between the policy and the agent through a lightweight classifier of current execution demands. We further introduce Task Dynamics Context (TDC), which packages informative manipulation changes from in-context examples into compact task references to support both agent reasoning and executor allocation. Across two simulation benchmarks and real-world experiments, Next-Manip achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing agent inference costs.

</details>


### [325] [EmbodiedStudio: A Task-Driven Simulation Generation Platform for Embodied Learning and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32534)
*Yang Zhao,Xubo Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EmbodiedStudio：任务驱动的仿真环境生成平台，联合编写场景、任务、机器人行为与评分（rubric）程序，并在仿真闭环反馈与独立评审下迭代调试；闭环同时产出“如何搭建环境”的前向构建经验与可用于策略学习的物理执行经验，且环境版本冻结以保证评测可重复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人跨任务训练与评测需要大量适配任务的仿真环境。现有场景/任务生成器虽能扩充供给，但存在两个实用缺口：(1) 外观合理但任务交互不可行或物理不稳定；(2) 生成的任务评分程序常常"试图"检查条件而未忠实实现。因此需要场景-任务-行为-评分协同生成并验证的平台。

Method: 提出 EmbodiedStudio 平台：统一协同生成 scene / task / robot-behavior / rubric 四类程序；由编码智能体在仿真中迭代调试并配合独立评审；环境版本冻结用于可重复评测；闭环过程自然产生两类经验数据——前向构建经验（学习构建环境）与物理执行经验（策略学习）。

Result: 在场景生成、rubric 评测、消融与学习研究中，任务就绪度与 rubric 忠实度显著提升，物理稳定性改善，同时为布局编写与策略适配保留了可用的监督信号。

Conclusion: 将场景/任务/行为/评分视为协同程序化设计，并以闭环仿真反馈加独立评审作为验证手段，是提升仿真任务可用性的有效路径；该框架兼具环境生成与数据生成的双重价值。摘要未给出与真实机器人的量化对比与公开代码信息，证据仍以仿真内评估为主。

Abstract: Training and evaluating robots across diverse tasks requires a supply of simulation environments suited to those tasks. While recent scene and task generators offer a promising way to expand this supply, delivering a usable task environment still faces two practical gaps: generated scenes that look plausible in appearance often fail to support the intended task interactions or remain physically stable in simulation, and generated scoring programs frequently attempt task conditions without implementing them faithfully. We present EmbodiedStudio, a task-driven simulation generation platform that bridges these gaps by jointly authoring scene, task, robot-behavior, and rubric programs under closed-loop simulation feedback and independent review. Crucially, as the coding agent iteratively debugs scenes, behaviors, and stateful rubrics in simulation, this closed-loop process natively yields both forward construction experience for learning to build environments and physical execution experience for policy learning, all bound to frozen environment revisions for repeatable evaluation. Across scene generation, rubric evaluation, ablations, and learning studies, EmbodiedStudio substantially improves task readiness and rubric faithfulness while improving physical stability and retaining actionable supervision signals for layout authoring and policy adaptation.

</details>


### [326] [Self-supervised quality evaluation of grid maps without ground truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32550)
*Unal Artan,Manuel Castellano-Quero,Johannes A. Stork,Martin Magnusson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自监督自编码器方法，对2D占据栅格地图图像块计算重建损失，实现无需真值标签的地图质量自评估，定位需进一步检查的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大环境地图的人工纠错耗时，需辅助地图编辑的工具；现有地图质量评估方法依赖真值或标注数据训练分类器，难以获取，且希望方法独立于具体建图算法。

Method: 仅以地图为输入，从占据栅格地图中提取patch，用自编码器学习地图的主导结构；以重建损失作为异常结构度量，判断该区域是否结构异常、需要进一步调查。自监督，无需标签。

Result: 在基准数据集上对SOTA栅格建图方法输出，以及仓库环境地图上评测，显示能识别稀疏数据、杂乱（clutter）与配准错误（misregistration）区域。

Conclusion: 自编码器重建损失可作为与建图算法解耦的地图质量自评估信号，为地图编辑提供区域级线索，适用于复杂真实环境。

Abstract: Maps are widely used in the robotics community for localization and path planning and the ability to identify incorrect or inaccurate regions of a map is an area of active research interest. For large environments, manual edits of the map can be very time consuming and a tool which can assist a map editor is needed. With this paper, we contribute to robot introspection through self-assessment of map quality, using only a given map as input to be independent of the underlying mapping algorithm. Current methods for map quality assessment rely on ground-truth or labelled information for training classifiers, both of which can be difficult to obtain. In this paper, we propose a self-supervised learning approach to map quality assessment of 2D occupancy grid maps which identifies regions of a map that require further investigation. We leverage an autoencoder for patches extracted from the map to capture the dominant structure of the map. By evaluating the reconstruction loss of a patch reconstructed with the learned model, we can evaluate whether it contains an unusual structure, which indicates the need of further investigation. We evaluate our method on the outcome of state-of-the-art grid mapping methods using benchmark datasets as well as maps from warehouse environments, highlighting the application to more complex environments. We show that the method identifies regions with sparse data, clutter, and misregistrations.

</details>


### [327] [MXI-MemICL: Towards In-Context Robot Manipulation through Self-Retrieved Interaction Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32564)
*Jialong Peng,Shihao Zhou,Feng Yan,Wen Xiao,Haozheng Wang,Wenqi Ren,Wenyuan Sheng,Jian Qiao,Jiaxu Miao,Lin Ma,Chao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MXI-MemICL：将演示转成可检索记忆，用视觉-动作因果编码器+意图预测器检索经验，通过经验token前缀与门控交叉注意力条件化VLA语言主干，实现免参数更新的上下文操作；RoboCasa365/RoboTwin 2.0提升+8.2%/+14.4%，真机+9.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人ICL方法要么依赖受限于稀缺昂贵交互数据的大规模预训练，要么需要额外标注且跨任务泛化差，故希望在VLA模型上实现无需额外预训练与专用标注的记忆引导ICL。

Method: (1) 视觉-动作因果编码器把演示与已完成交互中的动作及其观测效应编码为可复用记忆；(2) 意图预测器预测下一阶段意图并以此检索记忆；(3) 以经验token前缀与门控交叉注意力将检索经验注入语言主干，门控学习调节影响强度以引导动作生成。

Result: RoboCasa365 +8.2%、RoboTwin 2.0 +14.4%；真机+9.0%，其中演示带来已见任务+5.0%、未见任务+16.7%。

Conclusion: 以记忆检索+经验条件化的方式为VLA提供免训练ICL是可行且有效的，对未见任务增益显著；后续可关注其公开代码与记忆容量、检索鲁棒性。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) offers robots a way to acquire new skills from a single demonstration without parameter updates. However, existing robotic ICL methods either rely on large-scale pretraining constrained by scarce, costly robot interaction data or require additional annotations yet struggle to generalize across tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose MXI-MemICL, a framework for memory-guided ICL in vision-language-action (VLA) models. By treating demonstrations as retrievable memory, MXI-MemICL enables in-context manipulation without additional large-scale pretraining or specialized annotations. Specifically, a visual-action causal encoder turns actions and their observed effects in demonstrations and completed interactions into reusable memory to guide execution. To retrieve experience suited to upcoming execution, an intention predictor forecasts the next stage's intent and uses it to query memory. The retrieved experience conditions the language backbone through experience-token prefixes and gated cross-attention, guiding action generation with a learned gate that regulates its influence. MXI-MemICL improves success over the baseline by +8.2% and +14.4% on RoboCasa365 and RoboTwin 2.0. Real-robot experiments show a +9.0% gain, with demonstrations adding +5.0% on seen and +16.7% on unseen tasks. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [328] [Follow-Bench 2.0: An End-to-End 3D Benchmark for Socially-Aware Robot Person Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32579)
*Hanjing Ye,Tianle Zeng,Jianwei Peng,Yanci Wen,Yuchen Zhou,Yonggen Ling,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Follow-Bench 2.0 是面向社会感知机器人跟随(RPF)的端到端 3D 闭环基准，统一评测模块化感知-规划管线、端到端视觉策略与基础模型主动跟踪器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准各自割裂：具身视觉跟踪关注目标可见性但社会交互弱，re-ID 只做感知无闭环控制，运动规划常假设目标与行人状态真值。缺乏同时耦合感知与规划的社会化 RPF 评测平台。

Method: 基于 Unreal Engine 构建多难度场景（人流、光照天气、拥挤布局、瓶颈、排队、干扰者、目标临时遮挡）；统一闭环协议支持模块化管线、端到端视觉策略与基础模型主动跟踪器；评估背随与侧随两种构型，并报告任务成功、目标可见性、目标找回、社会空间侵入、跟随队形、运动平滑度等安全-舒适指标。

Result: RPF 专用模块化管线在近距离、社会舒适跟随上显著优于现有端到端视觉策略；但社会感知 RPF 在交互密集环境尤其是侧随场景仍远未解决。

Conclusion: Follow-Bench 2.0 提供了诊断性平台，用于分析感知误差、遮挡、人群交互与跟随构型如何共同影响机器人人员跟随。

Abstract: Socially-aware robot person following (RPF) requires a mobile robot to follow a designated person in dynamic human environments while maintaining target identity, avoiding obstacles and pedestrians, and preserving socially comfortable formations. Existing benchmarks only partially evaluate this coupled problem: embodied visual tracking benchmarks emphasize target visibility with limited social interaction, person re-identification benchmarks study perception without closed-loop control, and motion-planning benchmarks often assume ground-truth target and pedestrian states. We introduce Follow-Bench 2.0, an end-to-end 3D benchmark for evaluating socially-aware RPF under coupled perception--planning challenges. Built in Unreal Engine, Follow-Bench 2.0 provides difficulty-leveled scenarios with diverse pedestrian flows, lighting and weather conditions, cluttered layouts, bottlenecks, queueing behaviors, distractors, and temporary target occlusions. The benchmark supports modular perception--planning pipelines, end-to-end visual policies, and foundation-model-based active trackers under a unified closed-loop protocol. It further evaluates both back- and side-following configurations and reports safety--comfort metrics covering task success, target visibility, target recovery, social-space intrusion, following formation, and motion smoothness. Experiments show that RPF-oriented modular pipelines substantially outperform current end-to-end visual policies on close and socially comfortable following, but also reveal that socially-aware RPF remains far from solved in interaction-heavy environments and especially in side-following. Follow-Bench 2.0 therefore provides a diagnostic platform for studying how perception errors, occlusions, crowd interactions, and following configurations jointly affect robot person following.

</details>


### [329] [Design, Control, and Teleoperation of a Dexterous Robotic Hand with Soft-Rigid Hybrid Fingers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32582)
*Chao Liu,Wil J. Norton,Brandon Romero,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出五指软-刚混合腱驱动灵巧手：12个主动自由度，刚性骨架提供结构柔顺性，使线性控制即可实现通用软抓取；实现1432°/s高带宽，覆盖人体抓取姿态的84.8%，并给出位置/任务空间控制器与遥操作框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性手抓取质量与环境接触鲁棒性依赖柔顺性，而纯软手虽天然柔顺但建模与控制复杂；因此希望在五指手上结合软-刚结构，获得柔顺性的同时保持可控性与线性控制的可实现性。

Method: 将已有软-刚混合手指扩展为五指腱驱动整手（12个主动自由度、刚性骨架+软体外层），设计位置与任务空间控制器（线性控制策略实现软抓取通用性），并搭建遥操作框架，在多种抓取与操作任务（含薄/可变形物体与软/易损目标）上评估。

Result: 完成人体抓取姿态范围中33个姿态的28个（84.8%），关节伸展速度最高1432°/s（约为人体等效关节700°/s的两倍）；遥操作下成功完成抓取、捏取薄变形物体（如钞票、折叠布料）及与软/易损目标交互的任务。

Conclusion: 软-刚混合架构配合线性控制可在五指灵巧手上同时获得高柔顺性、高带宽与抓取泛化能力，适合遥操作及非刚性、精细操作任务，为灵巧手设计提供可行范式。

Abstract: Adding compliance to robot hands results in higher grasp quality and robust contact with the environment. Soft hands are inherently compliant, but are typically more complex to control and model than rigid equivalents. We extend prior work on soft-rigid hybrid robot fingers into a five-fingered dexterous gripper --- an integrated tendon-driven hand that is highly compliant and controllable. Structural compliance combined with a rigid skeleton, when realized in a five-fingered hand, allow us to use linear control to achieve soft-grasping generality. We evaluate the use of this hand in a variety of teleoperated manipulation tasks, demonstrating the effectiveness of the overall mechanical design and control strategy. Our contributions include: (1) Mechanical design of the a five-fingered Soft-Rigid Hybrid Hand, composed of 12 active degrees of freedom, demonstrating a high degree on generalization enabled by the soft-rigid architecture, with our gripper, able to complete 28 of the 33 (84.8\%) grasp postures in the human range. (2) Precise, high bandwidth (up to 1432deg/s extension speed, compared with 700deg/s for the equivalent human joint). (3) Position and task space controllers for the soft-rigid hybrid finger and whole hand. (4) A teleoperation framework for control of this hand, as well as demonstrations of its efficacy in a variety of grasping and manipulation tasks Further tests illustrate the hand's advantages in completing non-rigid manipulation tasks, such as picking up thin deformable objects (such as a dollar bill or folding cloth) as well as in interfacing with soft or delicate targets.

</details>


### [330] [An XR-Based System for Enhanced Spatial Awareness in Mobile Robot Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32584)
*Subhransu Sourav Priyadarshan,Valentina Pericu,Federico Rollo,Gianni Viardo Vercelli,Navvab Kashiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 多模态XR机器人遥操作系统：固定前/后双摄像头替代360°视频以缓解晕动症，16线LiDAR生成实时2D占据栅格并转译为手柄振动触觉提示，20人用户实验显示触觉反馈显著降低避障认知负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危险环境下移动机器人遥操作需要良好态势感知，但现有可视化与操控接口不足；HMD中的360°相机视频易引发晕动症，且受限/低能见度场景中视觉信息不可靠。

Method: 系统集成沉浸式视觉与触觉反馈：前视相机主导航、后视相机补态势感知（固定双相机规避晕动症）；16通道Velodyne LiDAR实时构建2D占据栅格，映射为手持控制器的振动强度/分布触觉反馈实现非视觉障碍检测。评估为20名被试的用户研究。

Result: 用户评估（20人）表明触觉模态显著降低了遥操作避障中的认知负荷。摘要未报告操作性能、任务完成时间或误差率等客观指标。

Conclusion: 双相机+LiDAR-触觉融合是缓解HMD晕动症并提供非视觉避障提示的可行工程方案；但证据以主观认知负荷为主，缺乏实机定量性能与大规模验证。

Abstract: Teleoperation of mobile robots represents a critical capability for operations in hazardous environments, yet maintaining adequate situational awareness remains challenging without effective visualization and control interfaces. This work presents a multi-modal extended reality (XR) system for robot teleoperation and scene exploration that integrates immersive visual and haptic feedback modalities. To address motion sickness commonly induced by 360-degree camera feeds in head-mounted displays (HMDs), the system employs a fixed dual-camera configuration: a forward-facing camera for primary navigation and a rear-facing camera for enhanced situational awareness without operator discomfort. For navigation in confined or low-visibility conditions, a 16-channel Velodyne LiDAR generates real-time 2D occupancy grid maps, which are converted into vibrational haptic feedback on handheld controllers to provide intuitive, non-visual obstacle detection. User evaluation with 20 participants demonstrates that this haptic modality significantly reduces the cognitive load associated with obstacle avoidance during teleoperation.

</details>


### [331] [RoboRecast: Intent-Preserving Demonstration Augmentation via Diagnosis-Driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32622)
*Yushi Long,Juzhan Xu,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Yin Chen,Sida Peng,Ruizhen Hu,Xiaowei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboRecast：智能体式优化框架，在保持源任务所需交互语义的前提下，联合优化仿真场景与机器人运动，使源示范可迁移到新布局、新物体、新本体，用于示范增广与策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界操作示范采集成本高，仿真增广是降低数据成本的途径；但直接迁移源示范的运动到物体、场景布局或机器人本体变化的目标设定时，会产生语义不合理或物理不可行的行为。

Method: 以智能体（agentic）优化循环为核心：给定源示范及其关键交互约束，在仿真中联合优化场景参数与运动参数；当交互执行失败时，利用仿真反馈诊断失败原因，针对性修改场景或运动参数并回灌测试，迭代直至交互可执行。支持人类示范与机器人示范两类输入。

Result: 在有目标示范的真实单臂任务与仿真双臂任务中，加入 RoboRecast 生成数据可提升策略成功率；在目标配置无示范时，也能通过适配源示范支撑新物体/新本体下的策略学习。

Conclusion: 显式保留交互语义并闭环利用仿真反馈进行场景—运动协同修正，是示范迁移与数据增广的有效范式；局限在于依赖仿真反馈质量与交互约束的可参数化程度，真实场景验证规模相对有限。

Abstract: Collecting diverse real-world manipulation demonstrations is costly, motivating the use of simulation to augment existing demonstrations for robot policy learning. When objects, layouts, or robots change, transferring motions from a source demonstration can produce behavior that is semantically implausible or physically infeasible in the new setting. We introduce RoboRecast, an agentic optimization framework that preserves the interactions required by the source task while jointly optimizing the simulated scene and robot motion to make those interactions executable in the target setting. When an interaction fails, the agent uses simulation feedback to diagnose the cause, revise the relevant scene or motion parameters, and test whether the revision resolves the failure. This flexibility allows RoboRecast to generate synthetic data from both human and robot demonstrations and to adapt them to new layouts, objects, and robot embodiments. When target demonstrations are available, adding RoboRecast data improves policy success on both real-world single-arm and simulated bimanual tasks. Even without demonstrations in the target configuration, RoboRecast adapts source demonstrations to support policy learning with new objects or robot embodiments.

</details>


### [332] [ReFocus: Progress-Guided Adaptive Practice for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32639)
*Haoyuan Li,Wei Huang,Jiehong Lin,Shengrong Zhang,Chenzhi Liu,Shengchao Zhou,Hehe Fan,Xiaojuan Qi,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReFocus：进度引导的多轮 RL 框架，交替进行全局 RL 与局部 RL，用 DAG 子任务进度奖励诊断长程操作瓶颈，并自适应分配额外交互练习到瓶颈片段；LIBERO-Long 达 97.8%，RoboCasa365 达 51.3%，真机 BreadBaker 达 95%（基线 30%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程操作中稀疏 0/1 成功信号几乎不提供"卡在哪里"的信息；而反复完整轨迹采样既浪费交互预算，又使瓶颈环节得不到充分练习和分析。需要一种能定位瓶颈并把算力/交互集中投放到瓶颈段的训练机制。

Method: 1) Global RL 阶段将任务分解为子任务 DAG，并构造基于 DAG 的 Progress Reward，既用于策略训练也用作瓶颈诊断；2) 依据诊断结果选出 Adaptive Practice Windows（从入口阶段到目标阶段的任务片段）；3) Local RL 阶段对窗口中片段追加交互、同时更新整条策略；4) 再在全任务上评估，进入下一轮，形成 Global–Local 迭代。基座为 π0.5 类 VLA 策略的在线 RL 微调。

Result: LIBERO-Long 97.8% vs π0.5 90.6%；RoboCasa365 51.3% vs 39.3%；真机三个长程任务中 BreadBaker 成功率达 95%，基线仅 30%。

Conclusion: 在稀疏奖励下，以进度信号（子任务 DAG）驱动的瓶颈诊断 + 局部重采样练习，是提升 VLA 长程操作在线 RL 样本效率与最终成功率的有效且可迁移（sim→real）的配方；分阶段结构分解取代整体稀疏奖励是关键设计。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) enables vision-language-action policies to improve through environmental interaction. However, in long-horizon manipulation, sparse success/failure feedback provides little information about where policies encounter bottlenecks. Moreover, repeated full-task rollouts also leave these bottlenecks weakly analyzed and insufficiently practiced. To this end, we introduce ReFocus, a progress-guided multi-round RL framework that alternates between Global and Local RL to identify task-specific bottlenecks and allocate additional practice to them. Specifically, during Global RL, we decompose each task into a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) of subtasks. Then a DAG-based Progress Reward is leveraged for both policy training and bottleneck diagnosis. Based on the identified bottlenecks, ReFocus selects Adaptive Practice Windows, defining task segments from an entry stage to a target stage. It allocates additional interaction to these segments during Local RL while updating the entire policy. After that, it evaluates the policy to perform the full task for the next round of RL, forming an iteration between Global and Local RL. Experiments on LIBERO-Long and RoboCasa365 show that ReFocus achieves success rates of 97.8% and 51.3%, respectively, compared with 90.6% and 39.3% for the $\pi_{0.5}$ baseline. Furthermore, we evaluate a real-world adaptation on three long-horizon tasks. On BreadBaker, ReFocus achieves 95% success, compared with 30% for the $\pi_{0.5}$. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [333] [BARE: Body-Aware Reflexive Evasion for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32642)
*Zihao Li,Tong Chen,Hua Song,Zitong Ding,Zihao Xu,Ce Hao,Weibang Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BARE框架：以人类避让动作先验为引导，通过风险引导运动适配器选择并时间相位对齐参考动作，再用强化学习训练"局部避让+全身稳定"的反射式躲避策略，在仿真与真机人形机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在真实环境中面临快速逼近物体时需即时躲避。现有反射式避让研究多针对四足机器人，依赖大规模全身机动；人形可利用丰富关节自由度做小范围局部调整，从而减少执行时间与所需净空。但局部调整会改变质量分布与支撑条件，难以在保持全身稳定性的同时快速躲避。

Method: 1) 构建重定向的人形避让动作库；2) 风险引导运动适配器：依据受威胁身体部位从库中检索参考动作，并按碰撞紧迫度调整时间相位，使机器人在潜在接触前到达避让姿态；3) 以适配参考动作和连杆级碰撞风险为条件，用强化学习学习反射避让策略，协同局部身体调整与全身稳定控制。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中验证BARE能够执行针对性的局部躲避，同时保持全身稳定性。

Conclusion: 人类运动先验与RL结合可用于人形机器人快速、局部化且稳定可控的反射式动态避障；动作检索+时间相位适配是满足紧迫性约束的关键机制。

Abstract: Dynamic obstacle avoidance is essential for safely deploying humanoid robots in real-world environments, where rapidly approaching objects require immediate evasive responses. Recent advances in reflexive evasion have focused primarily on quadrupeds, which typically rely on large-scale whole-body maneuvers. In contrast, humanoids can exploit their rich body articulation to evade through small, localized body adjustments. By moving threatened body regions without repositioning the entire body, these responses may require less execution time and less surrounding free space. However, local adjustments can alter mass distribution or support conditions, making it challenging to achieve rapid evasion while maintaining whole-body stability. To this end, we propose BARE (Body-Aware Reflexive Evasion), a humanoid reflexive evasion framework guided by human motion priors that enables rapid local evasion while maintaining whole-body stability. A risk-guided motion adapter is designed to select reference motions from a retargeted human evasive motion library based on the threatened body region and to adjust their temporal phases based on collision urgency, allowing the robot to reach evasive postures before potential contact. Conditioned on the adapted reference and link-level collision risk, a reflexive evasion policy learns through reinforcement learning to coordinate local body adjustments with whole-body stabilization. We validate BARE in simulation and real-world experiments, demonstrating its ability to perform targeted local evasion while maintaining whole-body stability.

</details>


### [334] [DexWAC: A Unified World-Actor-Critic Model for Dexterous Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32654)
*Hongjia Liu,Dacheng Zhou,Minghao Fu,Chuanjun Guo,Tianze Wang,Qingtao Liu,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexWAC提出统一的世界-演员-评论家模型，在单阶段机器人预训练中联合学习actor、动作条件世界模型和自举critic；世界模型基于图像编辑骨干，直接预测动作块终点的视觉潜变量与本体感受及奖励/继续概率，无需生成中间视频帧即可高效得到完整想象转移，配合PPO或critic引导的best-of-N提升策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型(WAM)仅将未来观测预测作为策略学习的辅助损失；而多数策略改进方法将动力学与价值分开建模，训练开销大且表征无法共享。作者认为视觉预测鼓励控制相关特征、价值监督促进任务相关表征，二者应联合塑造更适合策略学习的表征。

Method: 单阶段预训练联合学习actor、动作条件世界模型与bootstrapped critic；基于图像编辑骨干，对每个动作块预测终点视觉潜变量、本体感受、奖励与继续标志，使世界模型单独即可生成完整想象转移（不做逐帧视频生成）。预训练后在真实rollout上适配世界模型与critic，再通过PPO在想象rollout上微调actor，或用critic引导的best-of-N选择改进策略。

Result: 仿真中超过大规模预训练VLA与WAM基线；两个真实世界任务平均成功率75%，π0.5为45%；世界模型比基于视频的基线更准确且更快；消融表明联合学习动力学与价值可提升策略性能。

Conclusion: 将世界建模、价值估计与策略学习统一于单一模型和单阶段预训练，可共享表征并降低训练开销，且以终点潜变量预测替代视频生成的想象方式在精度与速度上更具优势。

Abstract: Robot demonstrations provide more than actions to imitate: they reveal how actions change the world and how those changes contribute to task success. Recent world-action models (WAMs) extend vision-language-action (VLA) models with the first signal by jointly predicting actions and future observations, yet this predictive ability serves only as an auxiliary loss for policy learning. Meanwhile, most policy improvement methods learn dynamics and values in separate models, increasing training overhead and limiting representation sharing between these complementary objectives. We introduce DexWAC, a unified world-actor-critic model that jointly learns an actor, an action-conditioned world model, and a bootstrapped critic in a single robot pretraining stage. Our key insight is that visual prediction encourages control-relevant features, while value supervision promotes task-relevant representations; together, they shape a representation suited to policy learning. Built on an image-editing backbone, DexWAC predicts, for each action chunk, the visual latent and proprioception at its endpoint together with its reward and continuation, so the world model alone efficiently yields complete imagined transitions without generating intermediate video frames. After adapting the world model and critic on real rollouts, we improve the pretrained actor through either PPO on imagined rollouts or critic-guided best-of-$N$ selection. DexWAC surpasses the large-scale pretrained VLA and WAM baselines in simulation and achieves 75% average success on two real-world tasks, compared with 45% for $\pi_{0.5}$. Its world model is more accurate and faster than video-based baselines, and ablations confirm that learning dynamics and value together improves the policy.

</details>


### [335] [DiffGR00T: Execution-Aware Differentiable Training for Humanoid Whole-Body Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32665)
*Sichen Wu,Qian Sun,Mengfan Liu,Weikai Qin,Bike Zhu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiffGR00T：用可学习的动作条件世界模型替代不可微仿真器，使人形全身执行损失可反传穿过冻结的全身控制器与flow-matching采样器，直达VLA动作头，实现端到端可微训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形分层VLA将高层运动生成与底层物理执行解耦，跟踪误差与平衡误差无法直接优化VLA生成器，导致生成与闭环动力学不对齐。

Method: 将GR00T N1.7与预训练全身行为基础模型SONIC耦合；用动作条件学习的world model替代不可微仿真器，实现多步身体执行损失经冻结WBC控制器和flow-matching采样器反传至VLA动作头。

Result: 在4个HumanoidArena仿真任务上，平均成功率由监督基线59.2%提升至71.7%（+12.5个百分点）。

Conclusion: 执行感知梯度能有效对齐高层运动生成与闭环全身动力学；但仅在仿真验证，缺乏真机部署与开放代码证据。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong results in arm-centric manipulation, yet humanoid whole-body control (WBC) requires dynamic balance and tightly coupled full-body coordination. Existing hierarchical humanoid VLAs typically decouple motion generation from physical execution, leaving tracking and balance errors unable to directly optimize the VLA generator. We propose DiffGR00T, an end-to-end differentiable training framework that couples GR00T N1.7 with SONIC, a pretrained whole-body behavior foundation model. To enable execution-aware differentiable training, we replace the non-differentiable simulator with a learned, action-conditioned world model, allowing multi-step body-execution losses to backpropagate through the frozen whole-body controller and the flow-matching sampler directly into the VLA action head. On four HumanoidArena simulation tasks, DiffGR00T improves average task success by 12.5 percentage points over the supervised base VLA (59.2% to 71.7%), demonstrating that execution-aware gradients effectively align high-level motion generation with closed-loop whole-body dynamics.

</details>


### [336] [DeCoNav-Act: Benchmarking Collective Physical Capability in Multi-Robot Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32683)
*Yunzi Wu,Sunyao Zhou,Xiaotao Zhang,Yucheng Deng,Yuehao Huang,Lin Qian,Xuanrui Cui,Xinhai Li,Chenjia Bai,Hongqing Chu,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DeCoNav-Act：语言引导多机器人移动操作共享物理仿真基准，含350同构+150异构任务，覆盖并行分工与物理依赖交接两类协调机制，并给出路径效率、交接覆盖率/成功率与预交接状态诊断指标；现有方法（ASPIRE、RAX、CoMoMa）在强模型下成功率极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构/多机器人基准侧重任务分配与技能排序，缺乏对"队友能否接手可用物理状态"这一集体物理能力的检验，即交接（handoff）后的状态可用性从未被系统评测。

Method: 构建共享物理仿真中的语言引导多机器人移动操作基准：任务经联合参考执行验证；分两类协调机制——共享空间并行负载与物理依赖（一台机器人为队友建立可接续条件）。指标除团队成功率外，新增路径效率、交接覆盖率与交接成功率，并利用已验证的预交接状态进行诊断式评测（给定良好初始交接状态检验能否完成）。

Result: 在传感器落地版 ASPIRE、RAX 与自研 CoMoMa 上评测：使用 GPT-5.6-luna 时无任何方法完成任务，最多8.8%的依赖被满足，即便从预交接状态出发也无法完成交接；使用 GPT-6.1-sol 时 CoMoMa 在26.5%试次中建立前提条件，但仅4.1%成功。

Conclusion: 多机器人集体物理能力（尤其是交接与状态可用性）是当前 VLA/agent 方法的显著短板；该基准提供细粒度诊断口径，可作为多机器人移动操作研究的标准测试平台。

Abstract: Robot teams can split workloads or combine complementary bodies, but only if each robot leaves the shared world in a state its teammate can use. Existing multi-robot benchmarks emphasize task allocation and skill sequencing, rarely testing whether the resulting physical states are usable. To evaluate collective physical capability, we introduce DeCoNav-Act, a benchmark for language-guided multi-robot mobile manipulation in a shared physics simulation. Its 350 homogeneous and 150 heterogeneous tasks, each verified by joint reference execution, span two coordination mechanisms: parallel workloads in shared space and physical dependencies in which one robot establishes a condition for a teammate to use. Beyond team success, DeCoNav-Act measures path efficiency and handoff coverage and success, and adds a diagnostic from verified pre-handoff states. Evaluating sensor-grounded adaptations of ASPIRE and RAX and our agent CoMoMa, we find that with GPT-5.6-luna no method completes a task, at most 8.8% of dependencies are reached, and even from pre-handoff states no handoff is completed; with GPT-6.1-sol, CoMoMa establishes the condition in 26.5% of trials, but only 4.1% succeed.

</details>


### [337] [Semidefinite Programming for Cold-Start GNSS Doppler-Based Alignment: A Comparison with Nonlinear Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32715)
*Baoshan Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文在统一的最大似然GNSS Doppler目标下系统比较流形NLS与SDP松弛，并提出将模块化对偶SDP认证器嫁接到标准NLS求解器上，实现对局部候选全局最优性的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多传感器融合（GNSS/惯性/里程计）在联合处理全局与局部量测前，需要完成局部导航系与ECEF系的初始姿态对齐。基于GNSS Doppler的方法免去载波相位模糊度固定与多天线硬件，但非凸问题的局部NLS易陷局部极小，而直接SDP松弛延迟高且不输出局部协方差。

Method: 建立统一的ML Doppler目标函数；在Monte Carlo仿真与三个真实驾驶数据集上对比NLS与SDP；提出"候选驱动的双重SDP认证器"——不替换局部估计器，而是用拉格朗日对偶验证固定NLS候选是否达到全局下界。理论分析表明SDP松弛在保持提升代价的同时扩大可行域，使对偶验证可行。

Result: 认证后的NLS精度与直接SDP相差0.001°以内；真实数据95分位偏航误差由>86°降至2.88°–4.70°；中位求解时间最多降低一个数量级，并保留原生的Jacobian协方差；可直接插入现有导航管线。

Conclusion: 候选驱动的认证兼顾局部估计的低延迟/协方差输出与全局优化的可靠性，是连接二者的实用桥梁，具有明确的工程落地价值。

Abstract: Initial attitude alignment between local navigation frames and the Earth-centered Earth-fixed (ECEF) frame is a critical prerequisite for multi-sensor integrated navigation, such as Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS)/inertial/odometry fusion, before global and local measurements can be jointly processed. While GNSS Doppler-based alignment eliminates the need for carrier-phase ambiguity resolution or multi-antenna hardware, solving this non-convex problem via local nonlinear least squares (NLS) suffers from local minima, whereas direct semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxation incurs high latency and omits local covariance outputs. This study systematically compares NLS and SDP on a unified maximum-likelihood Doppler objective across Monte Carlo simulations and three real-world driving datasets. We demonstrate that attaching a modular dual SDP certifier to a standard manifold NLS solver recovers the global accuracy of direct SDP within 0.001 degrees, reduces real-world 95th-percentile yaw errors from over 86 degrees down to 2.88 degrees to 4.70 degrees, and cuts median solve times by up to an order of magnitude while preserving native Jacobian covariances. We conclude that candidate-driven certification bridges local estimation and global optimization due to two structural advantages: (1) our theoretical comparison reveals that SDP relaxation enlarges the feasible region while preserving the lifted cost, allowing Lagrangian duality to verify whether a fixed NLS candidate attains the global lower bound without invoking a full SDP solver, and (2) the candidate-driven architecture acts as a non-intrusive module that plugs directly into existing navigation pipelines without replacing the local estimator, preserving native tangent-space covariance outputs and low typical latency.

</details>


### [338] [Seeing to Drive on a Single Board: Attention-Free Depth for Onboard Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32718)
*Itai Mizlish,Boaz Ben-Moshe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在Raspberry Pi 5 + Hailo-8L（全整型推理，含attention）上部署单目深度基础模型：量化Depth Anything V2会因softmax权重过小（91%低于1个int8步长，却承载17-33%注意力质量）而丢失大块场景；改用蒸馏得到无attention的ResNet-34 U-Net学生模型（24.5M参数，27.1 FPS，int8与float Pearson 0.99993），在仿真场地AbsRel 0.269领先芯片上其他深度网络，真实户外行驶上与厂商DAS V2-Small无可分辨差异但更快；配合仅需相机高度的地面交点测距，实现Pi CPU上的建图、hybrid-A*与SLAM，硬件在环高斯泼溅仿真中10场景成功7.9±1.1次（精确障碍9.2±0.4）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可用于测距的深度基础模型基本是面向GPU的ViT，小型地面机器人只有单目相机与数瓦算力；直接用数据自由整型加速器（Hailo-8L）量化ViT会导致显著精度崩塌，需要给出可行的替代路径并量化失败原因。

Method: 1) 诊断int8量化失效机理：统计attention softmax权重分布与量化步长的关系及其承载的注意力质量；2) 用蒸馏代替量化：以Depth Anything V2-Large为教师，训练无attention的ResNet-34 U-Net学生（24.5M参数），全整型部署；3) 仅需相机高度的“障碍物与地面交线”测距，使建图、hybrid-A*规划与SLAM在Pi CPU上运行；4) 在仿真场地、1000个立体校验真实点及Gaussian-splat真场硬件在环环境中评测。

Result: 449 tokens时91% softmax权重低于1个int8步长却携带17-33%注意力质量；学生模型27.1 FPS，int8输出与float版Pearson 0.99993；留出仿真场景AbsRel 0.269 vs 其他芯片上网络0.354-0.530；真实户外行驶与DAS V2-Small无可分辨差异且更快（27.1 vs 21.6 FPS）；HIL：7.9±1.1/10（自估计位姿+仿真轮速里程计7.7±0.7；插入真实Pi+Hailo 8.2±0.4），精确障碍上界9.2±0.4。

Conclusion: 面向低成本机器人的深度基础模型应以“任务定制蒸馏+整型友好架构”而非“直接量化ViT”落地；单目地面几何测距足以支撑端侧建图、规划与SLAM闭环，并给出可复现的开源栈与量化失效的可解释证据。

Abstract: A small ground robot can carry one camera and a few watts of compute, but the depth foundation models that could turn that camera into a range sensor are vision transformers sized for GPUs. We bring one to a Raspberry Pi 5 with the Hailo-8L, an accelerator that runs every layer in integer arithmetic, attention included. Quantised for it, Depth Anything V2 loses large parts of the scene, and we measure why: at 449 tokens, 91% of softmax weights fall below one int8 step while carrying 17-33% of the attention mass. Our answer is to distil, not quantise. An attention-free ResNet-34 U-Net student of Depth Anything V2-Large (24.5M parameters) runs at 27.1 FPS on the chip, its int8 output matches its float twin (Pearson 0.99993), and on held-out scenes of its simulated yard it is the most accurate depth network on the chip (AbsRel 0.269 against 0.354-0.530). On 1,000 stereo-verified points of a real outdoor drive it is not resolvably different from the vendor's Depth Anything V2-Small build, while running faster (27.1 vs 21.6 FPS). Ranging obstacles by where they meet the floor needs only the camera height, so mapping, hybrid-A* planning and SLAM run on the Pi's own CPU. In real-time hardware-in-the-loop tests in a Gaussian-splat reconstruction of a real yard, the stack reaches the goal without contact in 7.9$\pm$1.1 of 10 scenes, against 9.2$\pm$0.4 with exact obstacles, and in 7.7$\pm$0.7 on its own pose with simulated wheel odometry (ten seeds each; 8.2$\pm$0.4 with the real Pi and Hailo-8L in the loop, five seeds). The source code, pre-trained models and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [339] [AG-WAM: Attention-Guided World Action Model for Accurate and Efficient Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32722)
*Hongcheng Zhang,Lei Sun,Songxuan Liu,Tengteng Pan,Yuqiang Zhai,Xianglong Xu,Jianya Guo,Xiao Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AG-WAM——以显式注意力引导为统一原则的端到端World Action Model框架，含内容自适应预测注意力、引导点视觉注意力、状态条件文本注意力三个即插即用模块，在LIBERO-X、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin-2.0上刷新SOTA并提升训练/推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM虽联合预测未来观测与机器人动作，但注意力分配为隐式学习，缺乏显式的动作相关注意力引导，限制了性能上限。

Method: 将“显式注意力引导”作为统一设计原则，在WAM内部注意力范式中直接重构三个即插即用模块：(1) Content-Adaptive Predictive Attention，内容自适应地分配预测相关的注意力；(2) Guidance-Point Visual Attention，引入引导点约束视觉注意力；(3) State-Conditioned Text Attention，以机器人状态调制文本注意力。整体为无外部插件的端到端框架。

Result: 在多个公开机器人操作基准（LIBERO-X、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin-2.0）上取得新SOTA，显著优于其他方法，包括使用大规模私有具身数据的方法；同时明显提升训练与推理效率；未使用任何私有具身数据。

Conclusion: 显式注意力引导是释放WAM能力的有效统一原则，可在无私有数据、无外部插件条件下同时提升精度与效率。

Abstract: Recently developed World Action Models (WAMs) jointly predict future observations and robot actions, providing predictive context for control. However, existing WAMs typically learn implicit attention allocation with limited explicit action-relevant attention guidance, leading to limited performance.
