To unleash strong capabilities of WAMs, we introduce explicit attention guidance as a unifying principle and propose our Attention-Guided WAM (AG-WAM), an end-to-end framework with three plug-and-play attention guidance modules that directly reformulate internal attention paradigm in WAMs: the Content-Adaptive Predictive Attention module, the Guidance-Point Visual Attention module, and the State-Conditioned Text Attention module. Without any external plugin module adopted, our end-to-end framework with the unifying principle of explicit attention guidance boosts the accuracy and efficiency of WAMs. Without using any private embodied training data, our AG-WAM establishes new state-of-the-art performance on multiple public robotic manipulation benchmarks including LIBERO-X, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin-2.0, significantly outperforming other methods, even those using massive private embodied training data by a notable margin, meanwhile notably improving training and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [340] [Support2Sim: Visually Faithful Real2Sim with Stable Object Supports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32727)
*Samarth Chopra,Ankit Prabhu,Akshayan Nagarajah,Sumer Murthy,Pratik Chaudhari,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Support2Sim 是一个从单张 RGB 图像重建可仿真场景的 Real2Sim 系统：推断支撑图、接触引导的位姿初始化（多支撑物体候选择优）、以及粗到细的物理 rollout 精化，在 10 秒预算内最大化保真度、支撑实现率与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实图像转仿真需要同时恢复空间布局与物体间支撑关系，而现有 Real2Sim 流程常产生物理不稳定、支撑悬空或与源图像偏离的场景；同时需要在实用的生成时间预算内完成。

Method: 1) 从单图推断支撑图（support graph）；2) 接触引导位姿初始化：为多支撑物体生成候选位姿，按几何契合度与源图像一致性排序；3) 粗到细精化器：在受支撑物体间分配固定搜索预算，用物理 rollout 评估位姿更新，接受更新后在新位姿处以更细的平移/旋转尺度继续搜索。

Result: 在覆盖四个数据集的 50 个场景基准上，相比最强基线，10 秒后源掩码 IoU 提升 21.4 个百分点，预期支撑实现率 87.1% vs 73.2%，平均物体漂移从 79.6 mm 降至 45.3 mm。

Conclusion: 显式建模支撑关系并用物理 rollout 驱动的粗到细搜索，可在短时间预算内显著提升单图 Real2Sim 的视觉保真度与物理稳定性，为下游物理仿真与机器人任务提供可用场景。

Abstract: Reconstructing a scene for physics simulation requires recovering its spatial layout and the support relationships that govern its behavior. We present Support2Sim, a Real2Sim system that reconstructs simulation-ready scenes from a single RGB image. Our design objective is to maximize source fidelity, support realization, and stability within a practical time budget for scene generation. Support2Sim infers a support graph, which a contact-guided pose initializer uses to propose poses for objects with multiple supports. The initializer generates and ranks candidates by geometric fit and source-image agreement. A coarse-to-fine refiner allocates a fixed search budget across supported objects and evaluates pose updates with physics rollouts. After an accepted update, it searches from the new pose at a finer translation and rotation scale. We benchmark our method on 50 scenes spanning four datasets. Compared with the strongest baseline, Support2Sim improves source-mask IoU after 10 seconds by 21.4 pp, realizes 87.1% versus 73.2% of intended supports, and reduces mean object drift from 79.6 to 45.3 mm. See our project page at this https URL for more details.

</details>


### [341] [Risk-Informed Path Planning: Collision and Grounding Avoidance Application for Autonomous Surface Vessels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32760)
*Emir Cem Gezer,Ozan Kaya,Ingrid B. Utne,Roger Skjetne*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出融合贝叶斯信念网络(BBN)在线风险模型的RRT*路径规划器，用于自主水面船舶(MASS)的风险感知决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MASS在运营前后均需证明安全性，需要在线风险评估以指导海上决策；现有路径规划器缺乏可解释、可量化的在线风险建模。

Method: RRT*在候选边采样点上评估BBN给出的风险指数；BBN输出危险事件概率与严重度分布并聚合为有界标量风险指数；代价函数由路径长度、航向变化惩罚与累积风险组成，并施加风险可接受上限硬约束；每个树节点存储BBN后验，使搜索树成为针对各危险的可解释风险地图；给出BBN作为风险模型的设计要求，并提出将重规划截止时间转化为单次风险计算时间上限的运行时模型。

Result: 在含碰撞与搁浅危险的模拟港口进港场景中演示；实测运行时间落在运行时模型预测的单次重规划计算边界内。

Conclusion: 证明了将BBN在线风险模型嵌入RRT*的可行性与计算实时性，风险约束与可解释风险地图可支撑MASS在线安全决策，但仅在仿真中验证。

Abstract: Maritime Autonomous Surface Ships (MASS) must demonstrate safety before and during operations, necessitating online risk assessment to guide decision-making at sea. This paper presents a risk-informed Rapidly Exploring Random Tree Star (RRT*) path planner that integrates a Bayesian Belief Network (BBN) as an online risk model. The BBN evaluates the probability and severity distribution of hazardous events, which are aggregated into a bounded scalar risk index. During the search, this risk index is evaluated at sample points along candidate edges. The planner optimizes a composite cost function comprising path distance, heading change penalties, and accumulated risk, subject to a hard constraint enforcing a risk acceptance limit. Each search tree vertex stores the BBN posterior at its state, enabling the tree to function as an explainable risk map for individual hazards. We define design requirements for the BBN to serve as the risk model in this context and introduce a runtime model that translates replanning deadlines into strict limits on single risk calculation times. The planner is demonstrated in a simulated harbor entry scenario involving collision and grounding hazards. Measured run times validate that the planner operates within the computational bounds predicted by the runtime model for a single replanning cycle.

</details>


### [342] [Simulator-as-Proxy World Model for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32786)
*Tingting Liao,Mingjia Huo,Hao Li,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric P. Xing,Guangyi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimPWM 用"仿真器作为代理"为机器人视频世界模型提供显式、可扰动的状态：从首帧构建机械臂3D模型与被操作物体的3D点云，由无参数规则仿真器在动作下驱动，再由视频模型渲染，从而可检验视频是否真的遵循模拟状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人视频世界模型普遍以仿真/渲染代理场景为条件，隐含假设"视频会呈现代理所呈现的内容"，但该假设缺乏可验证性，因为代理状态不显式、不可扰动。作者需要一个状态显式且可控扰动的代理来检验这一假设。

Method: SimPWM：给定标定相机，仅从首帧用机械臂3D模型表示机器人，把夹爪可能接触的物体表示为3D点；用无训练参数的规则仿真器在动作序列下推进状态，再由基座视频模型渲染得到写实视频，同时输出仿真状态。评估采用"冻结/扰动"实验：冻结仿真物体而保留记录的手臂运动，或扰动动作，检验视频中的抬升率是否随代理状态变化；在DROID未见场景上对比三种基座视频模型与端到端模型。

Result: 机械臂对代理的跟随度高（robot IoU 0.75–0.78，端到端模型≤0.69），且仅渲染机器人即可达到；仿真物体主要贡献物体状态，使漏检抓取从54%降至38%；但对物体的遵循依赖基座模型：冻结刚性物体后MiniMax-H3抬升率从42%降至10%（在仿真与真实视频一致的片段上微调），LTX-2.3仅从57%降至49%，似从手臂运动推断抓取；MiniMax-H3剩余抬升中过半为布料与绳索（仿真器按刚性处理）。

Conclusion: 视频世界模型对"手臂代理"高度忠实，但对"物体状态"的忠实度因基座模型而异，且异质软物体是最薄弱环节；该框架提供了一种用状态一致性诊断视频模型是否真正使用模拟状态的方法，并可暴露模型从运动学"推测"交互的伪忠实行为。

Abstract: Video world models for robots are increasingly conditioned on a simulated or rendered proxy of the scene, on the assumption that the video will show what the proxy shows. Testing this assumption requires a proxy whose state is explicit and can be perturbed. We present SimPWM (Simulator-as-Proxy World Model): given a calibrated camera, it represents, from the first frame alone, the robot by its 3D model and the objects the gripper may touch as 3D points; a rule-based simulator with no trained parameters moves them under the actions, and a video model renders the result. SimPWM returns the realistic video together with the simulated state. We then freeze the simulated objects while the arm moves as recorded and check whether the video still shows the object lifted. On DROID episodes from unseen setups, the answer differs for the arm and for the objects. With each of three base video models, the arm follows the proxy (robot IoU 0.75-0.78, against at most 0.69 for end-to-end models); rendering the robot alone already gives this. What the simulated objects add is the object state: against a robot-only proxy, they cut missed pick-ups from 54% to 38%, and the video follows them only with some base models: on rigid objects, freezing them lowers the lift rate of MiniMax-H3, fine-tuned on episodes whose simulation agrees with the real video, from 42% to 10%, but that of LTX-2.3, trained on the same episodes, only from 57% to 49%, as if it inferred the grasp from the arm motion; more than half of the remaining lifts of MiniMax-H3 are of cloth and rope, which the simulator carries rigidly. With any base model, the simulated state records what the simulation did; under perturbed actions, a video that disagrees with it reveals that the video ignores the simulated state.

</details>


### [343] [Keypose or Dense? A Controlled Comparison of Action Representations for Stage-Structured Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32788)
*Haizhou Ge,Mengshuo Wang,Zhixing Chen,Lei Han,Chen Wang,Lu Shi,Guyue Zhou,Ruqi Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 比较稠密动作表征与关键姿(keypose)动作表征在stage-structured操作模仿学习中的鲁棒性与执行敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块策略通常用稠密表征(每步一个控制目标),而在可分阶段、端点可定义的操作任务中,keypose表征(每步设为所属阶段端点,由限速伺服产生运动)可能更具优势,但两者在示范变异与执行偏差下的权衡尚无系统比较。

Method: 在多个仿真操作任务与真实机器人上对比两种表征,统一分块长度、匹配示范、观测、策略架构与更新预算;仿真中用调参场景为各自选择执行规则;测试示范被减速、暂停、改道以及物体尺寸/位置偏移下的表现;覆盖三种策略架构,端点从记录示范中提取。

Result: 示范被扰动(减速/暂停/改道)时,dense成功率骤降而keypose基本稳定;物体尺寸与位置偏移下keypose亦领先。干净示范下keypose仅在示范稀缺时占优,其余接近或落后于dense;若示范与执行规则不适配其执行器,keypose大幅落后。真实机器人开抽屉任务中dense成功率更高,keypose仅在执行器的旋转与前进速度调整后才显著提升。

Conclusion: keypose以对示范变异的低敏感性换取对执行偏离示范的高敏感性;其性能强依赖于执行器与示范的匹配程度,不构成对稠密表征的普适替代。

Abstract: Most imitation policies that predict chunks of future actions use the dense action representation, in which the chunk holds one control target per step. In stage-structured manipulation, where a task can be divided into stages with definable endpoints, the keypose action representation instead sets each step of the chunk to the endpoint of the stage that step belongs to, and a speed-capped servo supplies the motion. We compare the two in several simulated manipulation tasks and on a real robot, with the same chunk length and matched demonstrations, observations, policy architectures and update budgets; in simulation, each one's execution rule is selected on tuning scenes. When the simulated demonstrations are slowed, paused and rerouted, dense success falls sharply while keypose stays roughly level, across three policy architectures and with endpoints extracted from the recorded demonstrations; keypose also leads under object-size and position shift. With clean demonstrations, keypose leads only when they are scarce and is otherwise close to or behind dense across the simulated tasks, provided that the demonstrations and execution rule suit its executor; when they do not, it falls far below dense. The same dependence on the executor appeared on a real robot learning to open a drawer from human demonstrations: dense succeeded more often, and keypose improved substantially only after its executor's rotation and forward speed were adjusted to the demonstrations. These results suggest that keypose trades sensitivity to such variation in the demonstrations for sensitivity to execution that departs from them.

</details>


### [344] [Supply Geometry, Learn Execution: Screw Attention for Zero-Shot Policy Transfer in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32797)
*Aly Magassouba*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Screw Attention Policy：显式提供物体位姿、相对 SE(3) 变换和关节螺旋轴等任务几何，只学习执行策略，仅需 30K 学习参数，仿真单臂训练即可零样本跨具身迁移并直接部署真机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规摸操作策略学到的很多内容其实是几何结构，而这些几何可从感知与机器人模型中获得，无需端到端隐式学习，从而降低数据与参数需求、提升泛化与跨具身迁移。

Method: 以物体位姿、相对 SE(3) 变换与关节螺旋轴（screw axes）作为结构化输入，构建几何中心的螺旋注意力策略，仅学习执行部分，参数量约 30K；纯仿真、单机械臂训练。

Result: LIBERO 上 96% 成功率，布局变化下 85.8%，五个未见机械臂上 80.2%，真机 24 次试验成功 21 次，均无微调或适配。

Conclusion: 显式注入几何先验可仅用极小参数量实现强泛化与零样本跨具身部署，提示"规模化学习"中相当部分可被几何供给替代。

Abstract: We present the Screw Attention Policy, a geometry-centric manipulation framework that supplies task geometry explicitly and learns only execution. The policy operates on object poses, relative SE(3) transforms, and joint screw axes provided by perception and robot models. The policy requires only 30K learned parameters. Trained entirely in simulation on a single robot arm, the policy generalises to new scene layouts, transfers zero-shot across robot embodiments, and deploys directly on a real robot, without adaptation or fine-tuning. It achieves 96% success on LIBERO, 85.8% under layout changes, 80.2% across five unseen robot arms, and 21 of 24 trials on a real robot. These results suggest that much of what manipulation policies learn at scale is geometric structure that can instead be supplied.

</details>


### [345] [MRBench: Benchmarking Physical Necessity Reasoning in Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32803)
*Yanzhe Chen,Zhijun Cao,Ruihe An,Chang Gong,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MRBench: 异构多机器人操作中的"物理必要性推理"基准（8任务×5种资源配置），以及 MRScissors——分离任务需求与实测物理能力、通过约束搜索求最小可行团队，团队选择准确率85.4%/91.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人操作中"成功协同"并不等于"真正需要这些机器人"。机器人能力变化、资源不可用、被动物体（如支架）替代机器人支撑时，所需团队会变。现有协同agent在有参考团队时表现良好，但难以判断哪些资源在物理上真正必需。

Method: 提出 MRBench 基准：8个具有不同并发与可达性约束的任务，每个任务在5种配置下评估（改变所需资源但保持任务目标）。诊断实验显示现有协调智能体在机器人形态变化或被动物体可替代支撑时性能下降。为此提出 MRScissors：将任务需求与实测物理能力解耦，通过约束搜索识别最小可行团队。

Result: 在两个模型骨干上，MRScissors 的团队选择准确率达85.4%和91.2%，分别超过最强基线10.4和9.5个百分点；基准实验中现有agent严重依赖参考团队。

Conclusion: 揭示了"协同执行机器人"与"理解机器人物理必要性"之间的关键缺口，并提出需求-能力分离+约束搜索的最小团队选择方案；属基准与任务规划层面工作，缺乏真实机器人验证。

Abstract: Recent advances in multi-robot manipulation have shown how robots can collaborate to accomplish complex tasks. However, successful coordination does not reveal which robots are actually needed. The same task may require different teams as robot capabilities change, resources become unavailable, or objects replace robotic support. We argue that reliable multi-robot manipulation requires physical necessity reasoning: understanding what a task demands, which resources can fulfill those demands, and which are truly necessary. We introduce MRBench, a benchmark for physical necessity reasoning in heterogeneous multi-robot manipulation. It comprises eight tasks with distinct concurrency and reach constraints, each evaluated under five configurations that change the required resources while preserving the task goal. Our evaluation reveals that existing coordination agents perform well with reference teams but struggle when robot embodiments change or passive objects can replace robotic support. To address this gap, we propose MRScissors, which separates task requirements from measured physical capabilities and identifies minimal feasible teams through constrained search. Across two model backbones, MRScissors achieves 85.4% and 91.2% team-selection accuracy, outperforming the strongest baselines by 10.4 and 9.5 points, respectively. Together, these findings expose a critical gap between coordinating robots and understanding their physical necessity.

</details>


### [346] [Atomic Policy: Composing Atomic Manipulation Skills with Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32813)
*Kaidi Zhang,Pengyuan Guo,Zhengtong Xu,Caiyi Zhang,Huayi Gou,Guanlin Huang,Aaron Lim,Anas Rafei,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Atomic Policy 框架：VLM 估计符号状态与目标，PDDL 求解器生成技能计划，agentic harness 通过语言/视觉指向引导原子策略执行并验证结果以驱动阶段切换与重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程操作中目标语义模糊、动作间依赖复杂，VLM 的语义推理与低层视觉运动策略的精细控制难以有效结合。

Method: VLM 做符号状态/目标估计 → PDDL 规划出技能序列 → 统一的阶段级接口驱动专用策略或通用动作模型 → agentic harness 以语言、视觉指点和二者组合进行 grounded steering → 结果验证器判断成功并触发下一阶段或重规划。

Result: 仿真与真机实验显示任务跟随能力提升，并能在复杂长程操作中实现失败恢复。摘要未给出量化指标。

Conclusion: 符号规划 + 原子技能 + 闭环验证的层次化组合是衔接 VLM 推理与低层控制的有效路径，通用阶段接口使可插拔策略与通用动作模型可复用。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation requires robots to translate task intent into physical interactions as the scene changes. Vision-language models (VLMs) offer semantic and spatial reasoning, while visuomotor policies provide fine-grained control. Yet ambiguous goals and dependencies between actions make their integration challenging. We introduce Atomic Policy, a framework that composes learned manipulation skills through grounded steering and execution feedback. A VLM estimates the symbolic state and goal, and a PDDL solver constructs a skill plan. An agentic harness steers each skill through language, visual pointing, or both, and verifies its outcome to guide phase transitions and replanning. A common phase-level interface supports specialized policies and generalist action models. Experiments in simulation and on a real robot show how Atomic Policy improves task following and supports recovery in complex, long-horizon manipulation. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [347] [What Does a Skill Vocabulary Cost? A Closed-Loop Decomposition over Demonstrated Motions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32858)
*Maximilian Xiling Li,Nils Blank,Paul Mattes,Pankhuri Vanjani,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MPQ 将“运动原型库”置于策略之外：把策略预测的动作块量化到最近库元素并在界内修正，无需训练参数，从而可分离诊断选择器、技能选择与执行三者的失效来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散技能策略把技能词汇表作为与选择器、解码器联合学习的隐码本，闭环失败时无法定位是词汇表、技能选择还是执行环节的问题，缺乏归因能力与基线。

Method: 提出 Movement Prototype Quantization：在训练好的策略之后挂接示教运动库，用最近邻替换每个预测动作块，并以有界修正向预测靠拢；不训练任何参数。在 6 种架构（含 7B VLA 模型）上验证策略等价性；以参考策略通过该库执行作为基线，逐个替换选择器组件做消融归因；并在 LIBERO-90、三个骨干上评测。

Result: 包装后的策略与原策略统计等价；LIBERO-90 上量化几乎零代价，技能选择误差至多 2 分，而选择器自身的“放置与修正”占据几乎全部剩余差距（最高 12 分）；离线重构指标无法预测上述差异，并会误判库规模、设计与跨基准迁移。

Conclusion: 将技能词汇表外置为可解释的运动原型库，可获得正交可归因的基线与模块化诊断流程；同时表明离线重构类指标不能作为离散技能策略的代理评价，需以闭环性能为准。

Abstract: Discrete skill policies execute motions drawn from a finite vocabulary. In most current methods, this vocabulary is a latent codebook learned jointly with a selector and a decoder. When such a policy fails in closed loop, the failure cannot be traced to the vocabulary, to the choice of skill or to its execution. We present Movement Prototype Quantization (MPQ), which places a library of demonstrated motions after a trained policy instead of inside it. MPQ replaces each predicted action chunk with its nearest library element, corrects that element toward the prediction within a bound, and trains no parameters. Across six architectures, including a 7B vision-language-action model, the wrapped policy is statistically equivalent to the original. A reference policy executed through the library is therefore a baseline for any selector over the same library. Substituting a selector's components one at a time attributes its shortfall to them. On LIBERO-90, with selectors on three backbones, quantization costs nothing and the choice of skill costs at most 2 points under the reference's placement and correction. The selectors' own placement and correction account for nearly all of the remaining gap, up to 12 points. Offline reconstruction predicts none of these differences and misjudges library size, design and transfer across benchmark suites. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [348] [Self-Sensing Compliant Anthropomorphic Hand for Speed-Dictated Shared Force Control in Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32937)
*Huijiang Wang,Yu Zhang,Rui Chen,Tristan Bonato,Jaemin Eom,Kai Junge,Cheng Pan,Benhui Dai,Yue Xie,Josie Hughes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用柔性腱驱动拟人手串联弹簧的弹性传动作为本体感知传感器，无外部触觉传感器即可估计关节状态与指尖力，并据此构建VR遥操作中的接触感知共享控制框架，提升易碎/重物抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉遥操作缺乏力反馈，操作者难以调节抓取力；加装指尖触觉传感器带来布线、成本与集成复杂度问题。作者希望把柔性手的机械柔顺性从"被动安全/自适应特性"转化为可用的内在感知信息源。

Method: 在腱绳走线中串入弹簧，由前臂电机盒内的电位器读取弹簧形变，据此估计关节状态与手指受力（自感知，无外部传感器）。在此基础上设计接触感知共享控制：接触前按操作者手部运动学映射驱动；本体感知检测到接触后切换为指级力控制器，设定点由操作者抓取速度决定，并支持控制权分配到单指。

Result: 实验验证了力估计的有效性；与直接遥操作相比，对易碎物体与重物抓取成功率提高。

Conclusion: 机械柔顺性不仅可用于被动适应，还能作为人机物理协作的内在本征感知资源，为无触觉传感器的力觉共享遥操作提供低成本方案。

Abstract: In vision-based teleoperation of robotic hands, the operator receives no haptic feedback and therefore struggles to regulate the grasp force, while adding tactile sensors to the fingers increases wiring, cost and integration complexity. Compliant hands offer an alternative, as their mechanical compliance, usually treated only as a passive property for safe and adaptive grasping, can also serve as a source of information. Here, we show that the elastic transmission of a compliant, tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand can itself act as the sensor for shared-control telemanipulation. Series springs in the tendon routing are read by potentiometers in a forearm-mounted motor box, from which the hand estimates its joint states and finger forces without any external sensors. Building on this self-sensing, we develop a contact-aware shared-control framework for VR-based teleoperation. The robot hand is controlled through a mapping from the operator's hand kinematics until a contact is detected proprioceptively. Control then switches to a finger-level force controller whose set point is determined by the operator's grasping speed, and control authority can be allocated to individual fingers. Experiments validate the system's force estimation and increased grasp success rate compared with direct teleoperation for fragile and heavy objects. These results show that compliance can serve not only for passive adaptation but also as an intrinsic sensing resource for physical human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [349] [DynaMimic: A Physics-Informed World Model for Humanoid Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32944)
*Chaoyue Xing,Linyi Huang,Yiting He,Yixuan Yin,Siqi Hou,Yi Zhang,Liyuan Pan,Miaomiao Liu,Daqi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynaMimic 将共享物理显式嵌入可微世界模型：网络预测各候选支撑连杆的接触力旋量与残余广义力，再经拉格朗日闭式前向动力学计算下一状态，据此训练全身运动跟踪策略，降低误差并支持真实机器人多技能执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 model-free RL 方法要么把多个运动专家蒸馏为单策略而损失性能，要么用集群级算力直接训练通用策略；SuperMimic 用可微世界模型降低开销，但隐式状态预测难以完整刻画物理过程，尤其在支撑切换附近误差会在想象的 rollout 中累积。

Method: 提出 DynaMimic 可微世界模型：网络为每个候选支撑连杆预测接触力旋量，并预测残余广义力；两者与闭式拉格朗日前向动力学结合，显式地由当前状态与动作计算下一状态，用其想象的 rollout 训练全身运动跟踪策略。

Result: 在 60 条完整 LAFAN1 运动上，MPJPE 相比 SuperMimic 降低 18.6%。地形跟踪中使用相同局部高度图，相比地形条件 SuperMimic，A3 Parkour 上 MPJPE 从 66.15 mm 降至 56.28 mm（−14.9%），GRAIL 地形片段从 141.88 mm 降至 126.57 mm（−10.8%）。在真实 A3 人形机器人上，所学策略完成倒地恢复、侧手翻与箱体翻越等多样化技能。

Conclusion: 显式物理结构（接触力旋量 + 残余广义力 + 解析前向动力学）优于纯隐式可微世界模型，能减少支撑切换处的误差累积，并在仿真指标与真实人形机器人多技能执行上同时取得收益，为降低全身运动跟踪训练成本提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Whole-body motion tracking enables humanoid robots to reproduce diverse skills from human demonstrations. Existing model-free reinforcement learning methods either distill motion specialists into a single policy at the cost of performance or train a generalist policy directly using cluster-scale computation. SuperMimic reduces this computational cost through a differentiable world model that implicitly learns the physics shared across motions by predicting the next state from the current state and action. However, this implicit formulation may not fully capture the underlying physical processes. Errors around changes of support can accumulate over imagined rollouts used to train the tracking policy. To address this challenge, we introduce DynaMimic, a differentiable world model that explicitly incorporates shared physics into state prediction. Specifically, its network predicts a contact wrench at each candidate support link and a residual generalized force. DynaMimic then uses these predictions to compute the next state through closed-form Lagrangian forward dynamics. Across 60 full LAFAN1 motions, DynaMimic reduces mean per-joint position error (MPJPE) by 18.6% relative to SuperMimic. For terrain tracking, we give both methods the same local height maps. Relative to terrain-conditioned SuperMimic, DynaMimic reduces MPJPE by 14.9% on A3 Parkour (66.15 to 56.28~mm) and by 10.8% on GRAIL terrain clips (141.88 to 126.57~mm). On a real A3 humanoid, the learned policies can perform diverse skills, such as floor recovery, cartwheel and a box vault.

</details>


### [350] [CAPER: PartGround and PlanRefine for Reliable Manual-Guided Robotic Assembly Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32986)
*Hanzhao Xi,Zhonghao Li,Suxuan Liu,Shanghan Zhou,Zesheng Chen,Qiufeng Wang,Yaran Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAPER: 通过 PartGround（将手册步骤证据与产品库存/零件参考对齐，输出精确 Part ID 与数量到共享 Assembly IR）与 PlanRefine（按置信度裁剪、审计有限库存），提升 VLM 生成装配流程的零件可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 用于机器人装配任务规划时，虽能正确理解单个装配动作，却常错认零件身份、预测不存在的 Part ID、或跨步骤零件不一致；自由形式的硬件预测缺乏库存约束与证据支撑。

Method: 提出 CAPER 框架，含两个模块：(1) PartGround 将步骤级手册证据与产品特定库存及零件参考信息对齐，把自由形式预测转化为证据引导的显式赋值，并可标注不确定身份待人工复核；(2) PlanRefine 依据有限库存审计赋值，移除置信度最低的分配以消解库存冲突。所有结果统一表示在 Assembly IR 中。

Result: 在 IKEA Assembly in the Wild (IAW) 数据集上，有限库存一致性达 83.33%，越界 Part ID 出现次数为 0；计划层面库存超用对由 9 降至 0、多余单元由 51 降至 0，局部预测精度亦有提升。

Conclusion: 零件接地与库存审计可显著提升 VLM 装配规划的零件级可靠性与一致性；但评测限于单一数据集、离线指标，缺乏真实机器人执行闭环，通用性仍待验证。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Models (VLMs) used in robot task planning to derive assembly procedures from existing manuals may correctly interpret individual assembly actions yet confuse exact part identities, predict unsupported Part IDs, or produce inconsistent parts across assembly steps. Thus, we propose CAPER (Capacity-Aware Part-grounding and Evidence Refinement), a manual-guided robotic assembly framework that enables VLMs to generate assembly procedures with two modules--PartGround and PlanRefine. PartGround aligns step-level manual evidence with product-specific inventory and part-reference information to identify exact Part IDs and quantities, transforming free-form hardware prediction into evidence-guided assignments represented in a shared Assembly Intermediate Representation (Assembly IR). PlanRefine audits PartGround assignments against finite inventory by removing the lowest-confidence assignments. Uncertain identity cases remain marked for review in the Assembly IR. We evaluate CAPER on the IKEA Assembly in the Wild (IAW) dataset. Experiment results show that CAPER reaches a Finite-inventory Consistency Rate of 83.33% and produces zero observed out-of-inventory Part-ID occurrences. At the plan level, PlanRefine removes all observed inventory-overuse pairs (9 -> 0) and excess units (51 -> 0). Overall, the results indicate that PartGround and PlanRefine improve part reliability, while also enhancing finite-inventory consistency and local prediction accuracy. Project page and video: this https URL

</details>


### [351] [At the Limit: Autonomous Vehicle Drifting under Generative Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33040)
*Jie He,Meixun Qu,Cheng Hu,Lei Xie,Ezio Bartocci,Radu Grosu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在线强化学习框架，将SDE采样探索与基于价值的策略优化结合，用于训练/微调流策略，实现F1TENTH数字孪生平台上的自主漂移控制（圆形与八字形），实时性好、跟踪更紧、成功率更高、噪声鲁棒性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主漂移场景下轮胎力饱和、车辆动力学高度非线性，传统控制与离线策略难以同时兼顾敏捷性与鲁棒性；同时希望利用专家演示加速学习。

Method: 在线RL训练流匹配（flow）策略：用SDE采样做探索，配合基于价值的策略优化；支持从零训练与基于专家演示预训练后的微调；部署时可用SDE或ODE采样生成动作。

Result: 在F1TENTH数字孪生平台上，圆形与八字形漂移实验表明两种训练策略均能实现有效实时漂移控制，跟踪误差更小、成功率更高、噪声条件下鲁棒性更好。

Conclusion: SDE采样+价值优化的流策略在线RL是移动机器人高动态控制的有效方案，兼具从零学习与演示微调两种可行路径，但验证仅限数字孪生仿真，缺少实车部署。

Abstract: Autonomous drifting requires agile control despite tire-force saturation and highly nonlinear vehicle dynamics. We propose an online reinforcement learning method for flow policies that combines stochastic differential equation (SDE) sampling for exploration with value-based policy optimization. This method supports both training from scratch and fine-tuning a policy pretrained on expert demonstrations. After training, control actions can be generated using either SDEs or ordinary differential equations (ODEs). Experiments on circular and eight-shaped drifting in an F1TENTH digital twin platform show that both training strategies can achieve effective real-time drift control, with tighter tracking, higher success rates, and better robustness under noisy conditions.

</details>


### [352] [EmbodiSafety: Benchmarking Risk Understanding and Response in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33054)
*Erfei Cui,Shengjia Zhao,Yan Qin,Kaixuan Wang,Junming Chen,Ping Luo,Tianxing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EmbodiSafety 具身安全评测基准：844 个视觉场景下的 1,092 个快照任务与 540 条轨迹任务，定义 6 种响应模式（直接执行/拒绝/澄清/受限执行/自主恢复/人工升级），并按模式正确性、风险识别、可操作性处置三项全部通过计算"完全决策准确率"；另构建 40k 安全对齐数据管线，微调 Qwen3.5-2B/4B 后指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身基础模型评测以任务成功率为主，物理安全关注不足；而以规则遵循和二元判断为中心的安全评测，无法检验模型是否理解环境危害并做出安全决策，也缺少短/长时程统一的评估框架与安全对齐数据。

Method: 构建 EmbodiSafety 基准：844 个视觉场景，1,092 快照任务（短时程）+540 轨迹任务（长时程）；定义 6 种响应模式并区分"自主恢复"与"人工升级"，以显式刻画安全自主性边界；评测协议含模式正确性、风险识别、可操作处置三项，全部适用检查通过才算完全决策正确；同时设计数据构建流水线与筛选流程，得到 40k 安全对齐样本，对 Qwen3.5-2B/4B 进行监督微调。

Result: 参考模型中 Claude-Opus-5.5 完全决策准确率最高：快照 56.1%、轨迹 61.5%，说明当前模型在长时程安全决策上仍有大量失败；在 40k 自建语料上监督微调可提升 Qwen3.5-2B 与 Qwen3.5-4B 的完全决策准确率（摘要未给出具体数值与基线对比）。

Conclusion: 该工作把具身安全评估从"是否违规"的二元判断推进到"是否理解风险并做出分级可操作决策"，并将自主恢复与人工介入的边界纳入量化目标；其数据管线为安全对齐提供了可复用的构建范式，但缺乏真机验证与策略层面的动作安全后果评估。

Abstract: Progress in embodied foundation models is largely measured by task success, while the physical safety of their decisions remains underexamined. Safety evaluations centered on rule following and binary judgments do not adequately assess whether embodied models understand environmental hazards and make safe decisions. We introduce EmbodiSafety, comprising 1,092 snapshot tasks and 540 trajectory tasks across 844 visual scenarios to assess safety over short and long horizons. Six response modes cover direct execution, refusal, clarification, constrained execution, autonomous recovery, and human escalation. Distinguishing recovery from human intervention makes the boundary of safe autonomy an explicit evaluation target. Our protocol assesses mode correctness, risk identification, and actionable handling, with complete decision accuracy requiring all applicable checks to pass. Claude-Opus-5.5 achieves the highest complete decision accuracy among reference models, reaching 56.1% on snapshots and 61.5% on trajectories. We further develop a data construction pipeline and curate 40k examples for safety alignment. Supervised training on this corpus raises the complete decision accuracy of Qwen3.5-2B and Qwen3.5-4B.

</details>


### [353] [Neural Implicit Representation for Bathymetry Estimation using Sidescan Sonar Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33118)
*Tianxiang Lin,Sacchin Sundar,Fenghao Liu,Onur Bagoren,Katherine A. Skinner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可微分神经渲染框架，结合基于侧扫声呐(SSS)几何的观测模型，显式建模其宽俯仰孔径与测量歧义，从SSS数据中估计水下地形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多波束测深(MBES)受限于水面船只布放，深水区空间分辨率低；SSS可搭载AUV贴近海底获得高分辨率声学影像，但从SSS反演地形存在测量歧义与覆盖受限问题，现有SOTA性能不佳。

Method: 可微分神经渲染 + 物理一致的SSS测量模型：依据声呐几何构建前向成像/强度模型，显式处理宽俯仰孔径带来的方位-俯仰耦合与歧义，通过渲染损失反传优化地形高度场。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上验证，鲁棒性与精度均优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 将神经渲染与声呐成像物理模型结合是从SSS高效估计测深的可行路径，可弥补MBES深水分辨率不足。

Abstract: Bathymetry measurement and estimation is critical for understanding the structure of underwater environments. While multibeam echosounders (MBES) provide accurate depth measurements, their deployment on surface vessels limits spatial resolution in deep water. Sidescan sonar (SSS), in contrast, can be deployed on autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to operate closer to the seafloor, enabling high-resolution acoustic imaging. However, the inherent ambiguity in measurements and the limited observation coverage of sonar datasets degrade the performance of state-of-the-art methods for underwater bathymetry estimation from SSS. In this paper, we propose a differentiable neural rendering framework for SSS that enables effective estimation of bathymetry from SSS measurements. Our approach incorporates a measurement model based on SSS geometry, explicitly accounting for its wide elevation aperture and associated ambiguities. We evaluate our method on both synthetic and real-world datasets, demonstrating improved robustness and accuracy compared to alternative approaches.

</details>


### [354] [Exploratory Midtraining for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33130)
*Kshitiz,Zheyuan Hu,Robyn Wu,Yizhou Li,Yunchu Zhang,Vattanary Tevy,Kavya Tayal,Shawn Yin,Aykut Onol,Kushal Arora,Dhruv Shah,Abhishek Gupta,Zackory Erickson,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 $\beta$-Data 数据集（对抗式遥操作采集：人类对手可逆地扰动环境，操作员不重置并持续完成任务），用自然语言局部结果重标注轨迹片段，对预训练策略做以近期动作历史为条件的中期训练，提升低层行为覆盖度并支持下线适配与在线改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略主要从"成功/熟练行为"的人类数据中学习，低层行为（从困难状态恢复到可继续推进状态）覆盖不足，制约自主改进能力。作者希望通过中期训练构建"连通的行为覆盖"（connected behavioral coverage）。

Method: 1) 对抗式遥操作采集：人类对手可逆扰动环境，操作员不重置、持续朝目标推进，得到覆盖扰动-恢复过程的轨迹；2) 用自然语言标注局部结果（local outcomes）对轨迹片段重标注；3) 对预训练策略做条件于近期动作历史的微调（midtraining）。

Result: 仿真与三个真机平台上的操作任务中：数据扩展律更优、零样本泛化更好、额外 rollout 时间利用更高效；在线微调时所需 rollout 更少即可收集成功经验并达到更高成功率。

Conclusion: 中期训练（面向连通低层行为覆盖的数据构造与微调）既能提升任务执行鲁棒性，也能为持续自主改进做准备；数据采集范式从"展示熟练行为"转向"刻意构造扰动-恢复经验"具有价值。

Abstract: Robot policies predominantly learn from human-collected data emphasizing competent behavior, limiting coverage of the low-level behaviors needed to support autonomous improvement. We study how a form of midtraining can be used to build coverage over diverse low-level behaviors that connect challenging states of the robot and its environment to states from which further progress is possible ("connected behavioral coverage"). We also examine how this coverage supports downstream adaptation and online improvement. To this end, we introduce $\beta$-Data, a dataset collected via adversarial teleoperation, where a human adversary reversibly perturbs the environment while a human operator continues toward completion without resetting. We relabel trajectory segments with local outcomes in natural language and finetune a pretrained policy conditioned on recent action history. The resulting policy exhibits sustained use of more low-level behaviors. Across manipulation tasks in simulation and on three real-robot platforms, our approach yields more favorable data scaling laws, better zero-shot generalization, and more effective use of additional rollout time than training on standard demonstrations. During online finetuning, these policies need fewer rollouts to collect successful experience and reach higher success rates, suggesting that midtraining can prepare robots for both more robust task execution and continued autonomous improvement. Our project page and robot videos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [355] [iWAM: Efficient Action Prediction from World Models without Explicit Future Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33141)
*Vansh Kapoor,Max Sobol Mark,Sreyas Venkataraman,Max Simchowitz,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: iWAM 提出用流匹配视频模型单次前向的中间特征作为动作策略条件，通过双向流映射摊销未来生成计算，在仿真与真机任务上保留 WAM 泛化优势同时大幅降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World-action models 依赖预训练视频生成模型预测场景演化以辅助动作决策，但逐帧去噪生成未来帧开销大，难以满足实时控制。作者认为代理式"未来想象"无需高分辨率帧，只需足以区分不同未来的信息。

Method: 利用 flow-matching 模型的可逆动力学，将训练时的未来帧反演为对应潜噪声；由于在潜噪声处单次速度评估产生的特征未必信息量充足，学习一个双向 flow map 来摊销未来生成计算，使单次模型评估即可提供有效条件特征。推理时从先验采样潜噪声，仅用一次视频骨干前向即可条件化策略。

Result: 在多个仿真基准与真实机器人上，iWAM 保留了 WAM 的泛化收益，同时显著降低推理开销。

Conclusion: 将视频世界模型的未来预计算重定义为高效特征条件问题，单次前向的中间特征配合学习式双向流映射可作为高质量未来代理，为实时 world-action 策略提供可行路径。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) build policies on pretrained video-generation models, on the premise that predicting how a scene evolves helps determine how to act. However, generating future scenes is expensive for real-time control. This motivates efficient proxies for future imagination, but how to train such proxies without sacrificing action-prediction quality remains unclear. An effective proxy need not generate a high-resolution frame; it only needs to provide enough information to distinguish between possible futures. This leads to the design principle behind iWAM: rather than denoising an entire future frame, can we condition the action policy on intermediate features from a single video-model evaluation? We show that the answer is yes. Exploiting the reversible dynamics of flow-matching models, we invert each training future into the corresponding latent noise vector. However, a single velocity evaluation at this latent does not necessarily yield informative features for action prediction. We therefore learn a bidirectional flow map that amortizes future-generation computation, enabling a single model evaluation to provide effective conditioning features. At inference, we sample a latent noise vector from the prior and condition the policy using just one video-backbone forward pass. Across simulated benchmarks and real robots, iWAM recovers the generalization benefits of WAMs while substantially reducing its inference cost.

</details>


### [356] [ROAM-Bench: Evaluating Embodied Active Spatial Intelligence through Evidence Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33143)
*Xingjian Ran,Xiaoye Mo,Sihao Liu,Li Luo,Taiqiang Wu,Ji Zhao,Wenqi Shao,Hongyang Li,Jiaqi Wang,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROAM-Bench: 一个在机器人运动与碰撞约束下评测具身空间智能的基准，用仿真状态与episode记录对"结果正确性"与"任务要求完成度"分别做独立检查，覆盖250个人工验证任务、11类任务、27个室内场景；人类严格成功率84%，7个前沿VLM暴露两类互补失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身空间智能基准无法区分"结果正确"与"真正满足任务要求（探索、覆盖、几何/放置合规）"，导致模型可能以不合规流程或侥幸正确收尾却被判为成功，也无法识别"结果错但过程满足更强检查"的诊断性失败。

Method: 构建ROAM-Bench：在机器人运动与碰撞约束下评测探索-推理-行动闭环；采用仿真状态与episode记录做独立、分离式的双轨检查——结果(outcome)检查与任务要求(task requirement)检查，并对几何、覆盖、放置施加强于最终答案的strict checks；任务集含5组11类共250个手工验证任务，分布于27个室内环境，涵盖物体/房间搜索、计数、几何判断、方向推理与重定位；另有oracle-input诊断实验。

Result: 100个抽样任务上人类评估者严格任务成功率为84%，且凡结果正确者均满足全部要求检查；7个前沿VLM呈现两类互补失败：(1)结果正确但任务要求未达成；(2)结果错误但通过了更强检查。Oracle-input诊断显示更完整的目标观测与校准反馈可改善最终空间判断。

Conclusion: 该工作指出仅看最终输出的评测会系统性高估具身空间智能，主张解耦"结果"与"过程合规"的细粒度诊断式评测；其发现也为提升VLM空间推理指明方向（观测完整性与校准反馈）。局限在于以仿真+离线检查为主，未涉及真实机器人部署与实际执行鲁棒性。

Abstract: Embodied spatial intelligence requires agents to gather information through exploration and interaction, then use it to reason and act. Existing benchmarks insufficiently distinguish correct outcomes from completion of these task requirements. We introduce ROAM-Bench to evaluate these abilities under robot motion and collision constraints. Independent checks use simulation state and episode records to assess outcomes and task requirements, with stronger checks of geometry, coverage, or placement. It comprises 250 manually validated tasks across 11 types in five groups and 27 indoor environments, covering object and room search, counting, geometric judgments, direction reasoning, and relocation. Human evaluators achieve 84% strict task success on 100 sampled tasks, with every correct outcome satisfying the required checks. Across seven frontier vision-language models, we identify two complementary failure modes: correct outcomes with unmet task requirements, and incorrect outcomes despite passing stronger checks. Oracle-input diagnostics show that more complete target observations and calibrated feedback improve final spatial judgments.

</details>


### [357] [PyRUA-Dex: Learning Programmatic Policies for Dexterous Bimanual Manipulation with Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33144)
*Feixiang Ruan,Guodong Lv,Kewei Zhang,Wenbo Huang,Yintianrun Zhang,Qiang Wang,Ruiyang Si,Shunyu Yang,Shulong Jiang,Weicheng Duan,Rui Ni,Jianxin Bi,Lekai Wang,Yuze Fan,Haobo Li,Zeliang Fu,Jiankun Zhang,Duomin Wang,Enze Xie,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PyRUA-Dex：用编码智能体以编程式策略开发灵巧操作技能，无需人类演示；两阶段（并行探索+蒸馏成任务库/单程序；随机布局精炼提鲁棒与效率）。Bench2Dex 72.9 SOTA，DexJoCo 全随机化 81.0% 成功率，双臂 22-DoF 真机上学会弹钢琴、书法，新曲目学习约 30 分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作学习受限于高质量演示稀缺：视频重定向不精确、遥操作无力反馈、外骨骼采集难扩展，因此需要一种不依赖演示的策略获取路线。

Method: 以编码智能体生成可执行的编程式策略，分两阶段：探索阶段多个智能体并行搜索任务技能及其组合方式，由蒸馏智能体把成功尝试压缩为任务库与单一程序；精炼阶段通过随机化布局迭代改进该程序，提升鲁棒性与执行效率。

Result: Bench2Dex 得分 72.9 达 SOTA；DexJoCo 全随机化下成功率 81.0%，两基准均优于全部已训练策略。真实双臂 22-DoF 平台完成弹钢琴、书法等任务，单曲目学习最短约 30 分钟。

Conclusion: 在缺乏高质量灵巧操作演示的条件下，编码智能体可有效开发灵巧操作策略，兼具仿真 SOTA 与真机高效学习能力，为演示稀缺问题提供可编程替代路径。

Abstract: Learning for dexterous manipulation is limited by the scarcity of high-quality demonstrations: retargeting from human video is imprecise, teleoperation gives no force feedback to the operator, and exoskeleton-based collection is hard to scale up. We present PyRUA-Dex, a framework that enables coding agents to develop dexterous manipulation through programmatic policy, without the need of demonstrations. The programmatic policy is developed in two stages: during exploration, multiple agents explore in parallel for task skills and ways to compose them, and a distilling agent condenses the successful attempts into a task library and one program. During refinement, this program is improved through randomized layouts for enhanced robustness and execution efficiency. In simulated environments, PyRUA-Dex achieves SOTA on Bench2Dex with a score of 72.9 and reaches 81.0% success on DexJoCo under full randomization, ahead of every trained policy on both benchmarks. In real world experiments on a bimanual platform with two 22-DoF hands, PyRUA-Dex learns to play piano, write calligraphy, and other tasks efficiently: learning a new piece from sheet music takes as little as 30 minutes. These results suggest that PyRUA-Dex is an effective framework for developing dexterous manipulation policies with coding agents in the absence of high quality dexterous manipulation demonstrations.

</details>


### [358] [OmniAct: One Policy, One Million Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33158)
*Xiang Fan,Yuheng Wang,Jiayuan Yan,Bohan Fang,Wisdom Oluchi Ikezogwo,Ainaz Eftekhar,Max Argus,Jason Ren,Jiafei Duan,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniAct：以机器人运动学图为直接条件输入的跨本体操作策略，用流匹配预测各关节节点的相对运动，配合百万级程序化生成的可行本体与仿真轨迹数据实现规模化训练，在留出评估与真实新机器人上显著超过基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨本体迁移多依赖共享动作表征，这类表征未必能体现形态差异如何塑造操作策略，导致单一策略难以同时适配不同机器人能力。

Method: 构建与视觉观测对齐的运动学图（关节位置、运动轴、连通性），策略直接以其为条件；采用 flow matching 预测每个节点的相对关节运动，从而兼容不同自由度与构型；程序化设计并筛选 100 万个物理可行本体，配套算法在仿真中为全部本体生成轨迹，并在多样仿真操作任务上联合训练。

Result: 2000 个本体-场景留出评估回合成功率 63.0%（pi 0.5 为 17.8%，最强基线 57.9%）；在特定任务族上 64.7% 对比 41.1%；在未见过的真实机器人上成功率约为次优方法 MolmoAct2 的两倍；并表现出针对不同形态能力与限制的差异化操作策略。

Conclusion: 以运动学图为条件、以相对关节运动为动作空间的跨本体策略，配合大规模程序化本体与轨迹生成，可有效实现单一策略对多形态机器人的泛化，并在sim-to-real新本体上取得明显增益。

Abstract: A single policy that controls diverse robots must not only transfer task knowledge across embodiments, but also adapt its behavior to each robot's capabilities. Existing approaches facilitate transfer through shared action representations, but these representations do not necessarily capture how differences in robot morphology should shape manipulation strategies. We introduce OmniAct, a cross-embodiment manipulation policy that conditions directly on each robot's kinematics in a specific scene to produce embodiment-specific behavior. This kinematic graph represents the robot's joint positions, motion axes, and connectivity to its visual observation, allowing the policy to account for how its embodiment related to objects and surfaces in the scene. A flow-matching policy predicts relative joint motions for each node, enabling control across robots with different degrees of freedom and kinematic configurations. To learn how physical structure shapes behavior at scale, we procedurally design and filter one million physically feasible robot embodiments; next, we develop algorithms to generate robot trajectories in simulation for all embodiments; finally, we train across diverse simulated manipulation tasks. On 2,000 embodiment-scene-held-out evaluation episodes, OmniAct achieves 63.0% success, compared to 17.8% for pi 0.5 and 57.9% for the strongest baseline; on the task families GPT-6 Astra was evaluated on, it achieves 64.7% against GPT-6 Astra's 41.1%. On a previously unseen real-world robot, it achieves nearly twice the success rate of the next best method, MolmoAct2. Beyond task success, we observe that OmniAct learns distinct manipulation strategies that exploit the capabilities and accommodate the limitations of different morphologies. Project: this https URL

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [359] [Read My Lips: Context-aware Lip Reading Using Language and Audio-Visual Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33117)
*Amir Razlighi,Zhuo Ning,Naomi Ken Korem,Nisarg Patel,Yiorgos Chrysanthou,Daniel Cohen-Or,Ali Mahdavi Amiri*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: RML combines a lip reader, an LLM, and an audio-visual generative model to reconstruct speech under missing/corrupted audio, using multi-hypothesis generation plus evidence-based selection without hand-tuned thresholds.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Real-world speech is only partially observable: audio may be missing or masked by noise and competing speakers while lips remain visible; robust recovery needs to fuse heard, seen, and inferred (contextual) cues.

Method: A collaborative framework of three modules: (1) lip reader producing articulation hypotheses, (2) LLM using linguistic context to disambiguate or fill missing words, (3) audio-visual generative model reconstructing synchronized speech while preserving speaker identity; source reliability is handled by generating multiple reconstructions and scoring/favoring them by available evidence (no manual thresholds).

Result: Quantitative and qualitative experiments across missing-audio, strong-interference, and degraded-visual conditions show gains from combining visual, acoustic, and linguistic signals; the selection scheme adapts to varying source reliability.

Conclusion: Evidence-weighted multi-hypothesis fusion of articulatory, linguistic, and acoustic cues is an effective, threshold-free route to robust speech reconstruction from partially observable multimodal input.

Abstract: Real-world speech is often partially observable: audio may be missing or corrupted by noise and competing speakers, while the speaker's lips remain visible. Recovering speech requires combining what can be heard, seen, and inferred from context. We introduce RML (Read My Lips), a collaborative method combining a lip reader, a language model, and an audio-visual generative model. The lip reader provides articulation hypotheses, the language model uses context to resolve ambiguous or missing words, and the audio-visual generator reconstructs synchronized speech while preserving speaker identity. Since source reliability varies, RML generates multiple reconstructions and uses the available evidence to favor the most reliable ones. This allows RML to handle missing audio, strong interference, and poor visual evidence without manually defined thresholds. Quantitative and qualitative experiments across these conditions show the benefit of combining visual, acoustic, and linguistic signals.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.QM'></div>

# q-bio.QM [[Back]](#toc)

### [360] [Proxy Metrics Can Hide Downstream Collapse: A Case Study on Compressed Cellular Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31243)
*Sebastián A. Cruz Romero*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 系统评估 Cellpose-SAM 后训练量化的输出级代理指标（NRMSE、余弦相似度、光流角度误差）能否预测下游实例分割掩码保留率，发现这些指标失效，应按部署相关影像条件分层用下游保留率验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉模型后训练压缩通常仅用输出级代理指标（NRMSE、余弦相似度、角度误差）作为验收标准，但这类 FP32 参考代理能否反映 promptless 密集预测模型的下游任务性能尚不清楚，论文旨在检验其可靠性。

Method: 在 245 幅分层图像（BBBC038、BBBC039、NIST iPSC，覆盖不同密度区间）上对 Cellpose-SAM 做后训练量化；对比输出代理指标（NRMSE、余弦相似度、光流角度误差）与下游掩码保留率，并按模态-密度分层做秩一致性分析；同时测试 W8A8-cal-QDQ、A8 以下激活量化及三元权重量化。

Result: 代理指标失效：角度误差从 A8 到 A2 饱和于 90°；W8A8-cal-QDQ 下余弦 0.944 却有 89/245 空掩码；BBBC039 为 0/47 空掩码而 NIST iPSC 为 63/99；五个分层中余弦与空掩码率秩相关仅约 0.3，最高余弦分层下游表现第二差；A8 以下全部塌缩为 245/245 空场；三元权重量化 12.08 倍压缩被代理正确判为灾难性。

Conclusion: 以 FP32 为参考的输出代理不足以作为 promptless 密集预测模型的独立验收标准，压缩评估应在部署相关成像条件下按下游保留率分层验证。

Abstract: Post-training compression of vision models is commonly screened using output-level proxies such as normalized RMSE (NRMSE), cosine similarity, and flow angular error. We evaluated post-training quantization of Cellpose-SAM, a promptless Segment-Anything model for cellular instance segmentation, on a stratified 245-field panel spanning BBBC038, BBBC039, and NIST iPSC images across density regimes. These proxies failed to reliably predict downstream mask retention under activation quantization. Angular error saturated at 90° from A8 through A2, while cosine and NRMSE preserved rank-order information without providing an interpretable acceptance threshold. At W8A8-cal-QDQ, cosine similarity was 0.944 despite 89/245 empty masks; moreover, performance varied sharply by modality, with BBBC039 yielding 0/47 empty masks versus 63/99 for NIST iPSC fields at the same proxy footprint. Across five modality-density strata, cosine similarity and empty-mask rate showed weak rank agreement (\r{ho}=~0.3), with the highest-cosine stratum producing the second-worst downstream outcome. Below A8, all modalities collapsed to 245/245 empty fields. By contrast, ternary weight quantization produced a 12.08x storage reduction and was correctly identified as catastrophic by the proxies. These results show that FP32-referenced output proxies are insufficient as standalone acceptance criteria for promptless dense-prediction models; compression should instead be validated against downstream retention stratified by deployment-relevant imaging conditions.

</details>


### [361] [A multimodal foundation model for tissue proteomics and morphology in cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31451)
*Benedikt von Querfurth,Lukas Klein,Cédric Vincent-Cuaz,Eeshaan Jain,Yexiang Cheng,Johann Wenckstern,Phil F. Cheng,Petros Liakopoulos,Olivier Michielin,Martina Haberecker,Andreas Wicki,Pascal Frossard,Charlotte Bunne*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: VirTues-M2 是一个联合 H&E 组织形态与多重空间蛋白组学的多模态基础模型，通过在预训练中编码 marker 身份实现对缺失模态、弱配准与可变 panel 的鲁棒性，支持组织表型、全景细胞分割、虚拟染色、无标注生物标志物发现与 RL 自适应 panel 选择；在 1,053 例 NSCLC 队列中发现并独立验证了可分层总生存的组织风险签名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床诊断与治疗决策需要形态学与分子信息的整合，但 H&E 易得、空间蛋白组学昂贵且 panel 不一致，造成转化瓶颈：多数患者缺高深度分子谱，现有模型很少在单一框架中融合形态与空间分子数据。

Method: 基于 ViT 的多模态基础模型；预训练时显式编码 marker 身份表征，使模型可容忍缺失模态、不完美配对/配准及可变 marker 组合；统一推理接口覆盖组织分型、panoptic 细胞分割、虚拟染色、无标注生物标志物发现，以及基于强化学习的自适应 marker panel 选择以减少检测负担。

Result: 在 1,053 例初治 NSCLC 患者配对空间蛋白组与 H&E 队列中，发现高/低风险组织签名，在发现队列与独立验证队列均能分层总生存，优于现有 SOTA 分层方案。

Conclusion: 多模态联合建模可在单一模型内同时利用形态与空间分子信息，缓解分子 profiling 覆盖不足的瓶颈，并在真实临床队列中展现出优于既有方案的预后分层能力。

Abstract: Cancer diagnosis, prognosis, and treatment decisions increasingly rely on integrating tissue morphology with specific molecular readouts of the tumor and its microenvironment. H&E histopathology provides scalable morphology, whereas multiplex spatial proteomics quantifies dozens of proteins in situ with spatial context, but remains selectively deployed, often with different marker panels across cohorts. This creates a translational bottleneck: most patients lack high-depth molecular profiling, and existing models rarely integrate morphology and spatial molecular data in a single framework. We present VirTues-M2, a multimodal foundation model that jointly learns from H&E and spatial proteomics using a novel vision transformer-based architecture. By encoding marker identity during pretraining, VirTues-M2 is robust to missing modalities, imperfect modality pairing or registration, and variable marker panels, enabling unified inference for tissue phenotyping, panoptic cell segmentation, virtual staining, annotation-free biomarker discovery, and reinforcement learning-based adaptive marker panel selection to reduce assay burden. As a concrete demonstration of translational utility, in a newly collected cohort of 1,053 treatment-naive non-small-cell lung cancer (NSCLC) patients with paired spatial proteomics and H&E, VirTues-M2 discovers high-risk and low-risk tissue signatures that stratify overall survival in both the discovery cohort and an independent validation cohort, outperforming current state-of-the-art stratification schemes; all enabled by VirTues-M2's ability to jointly analyze both modalities within a single model.

</details>


### [362] [Tempering and few-shot calibration of pathology foundation models improve digital biomarker reproducibility across slide scanners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31536)
*Frank T Wen,Kyler Rosen,Siddhi Ramesh,Michael Koo,Sara Kochanny,Sasha Jain-Liu,Emma Dyer,Alexander T Pearson,James M Dolezal*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 提出"tempering"微调方法,利用仅公开数据降低病理基础模型对扫描仪图像特征的敏感性,在HPV、MSI、前列腺分级与乳腺表型四类任务上大幅减小跨扫描仪预测差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型被广泛用于组织学癌症生物标志物,但其对扫描仪特异性成像"签名"敏感,导致跨中心可靠性与可重复性下降;现有的事后特征校准(post hoc feature calibration)需要目标站点数据,部署成本高。

Method: 一种基础模型微调(fine-tuning)层面的"tempering"方法,在训练阶段直接抑制扫描仪相关特征通道,而非事后做特征校准;仅在公开数据上训练,即可匹配或超过需现场数据的on-site校准。论文还在训练集存在完全"扫描仪-HPV"混杂(full confounding)的极端设定下评估AUROC退化幅度。

Result: 跨扫描仪预测差异降低:HPV 67.5%(95% CI 59.0–73.9%)、MSI 69.3%(51.9–76.5%)、乳腺特征 63.4%–81.2%;前列腺分级跨扫描仪一致率由42.5%升至92.7%。全混杂设定下AUROC:原始特征0.80→0.68,tempering 0.86→0.84;且仅用公开数据即匹敌或优于on-site校准。

Conclusion: 在微调阶段施加tempering可显著削弱病理基础模型对扫描仪的依赖,提升跨中心可复现性,且无需目标站点数据即可部署,支持作为病理基础模型的通用鲁棒化步骤。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models underpin a growing number of histology-based cancer biomarkers, but sensitivity to slide scanner-specific image signatures degrades cross-center reliability and reproducibility. We developed a foundation model fine-tuning method that improved consistency in head-and-neck cancer human papillomavirus (HPV) prediction, colorectal cancer microsatellite instability (MSI) identification, prostate cancer grading and breast cancer phenotyping. This tempering method decreased cross-scanner prediction differences by 67.5% for HPV (95% CI 59.0-73.9%), 69.3% for MSI (95% CI 51.9-76.5%) and 63.4%-81.2% for breast signatures. Cross-scanner prostate grade agreement rose from 42.5% to 92.7%. Under full scanner-HPV confounding during training, AUROC fell from 0.80 to 0.68 with original model features but only from 0.86 to 0.84 with tempering. Unlike post hoc feature calibration, tempering used only public data yet matched or exceeded on-site calibration. These findings support tempering to reduce scanner dependence of pathology foundation models.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [363] [Residual Guidance for Real-Time Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32820)
*NaHyeon Park,Kyeong-Hwan Moon,Youjin Jeon,Hyunjung Shim*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 Residual Guidance (RG)，通过在新 prompt 下无缓存地对上一 chunk 去噪一次并取残差，注入下一 chunk 早期去噪步，缓解流式视频生成中的 context inertia，显著提升 prompt 切换能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时交互式视频生成允许用户在生成过程中切换 prompt 以引导视频演化，但现有的分块流式模型在切换后仍持续跟随旧 prompt。作者将该失败归因于 context inertia：上一 chunk 作为强先验抑制了新 prompt；且即使缓存仅保留上一个 chunk，惯性依然存在，而直接丢弃上下文又会破坏视觉连续性，因此缓存编辑类方法效果受限。

Method: 提出 Residual Guidance（RG），一种无需训练的推理期修正方法。具体做法：对重新加噪的上一 chunk 在新 prompt 下、不使用缓存进行一次去噪，得到残差；将该残差加到下一 chunk 的早期去噪步（此时内容基本确定、新 prompt 受抑制最强）。方法不编辑缓存，每 chunk 仅增加一次前向计算，无需训练或逐模型调参。

Result: 在从单次切换到长程多次切换的多种设置下，RG 在现有流式模型（包括已具备专门切换机制的模型）上大幅提升 prompt 切换能力，同时基本保持视觉质量。

Conclusion: context inertia 是流式视频生成 prompt 切换失败的核心原因，基于缓存外部测量的残差式引导是一种简洁有效的对策；RG 无训练、无调参、开销小，便于集成进现有实时视频生成管线。

Abstract: Real-time interactive video generation allows users to change the prompt during generation to steer the evolving video. However, existing streaming models, which generate videos chunk by chunk, often keep following the previous prompt after a switch. We trace this failure to context inertia, where the previous chunk acts as a strong prior that suppresses the new prompt. Furthermore, we find that this inertia persists even when the cache retains only the previous chunk, yet discarding the context breaks visual continuity, which limits approaches that edit the cache. Rather than editing the cache, we propose Residual Guidance (RG), a simple, training-free method that counteracts context inertia with a correction measured outside the cache. Specifically, RG denoises the re-noised previous chunk once under the new prompt without the cache and adds the resulting residual to the early denoising steps of the next chunk, where its content is largely decided and the new prompt is most suppressed. Across settings ranging from a single switch to long multi-switch generation, RG substantially improves prompt switching capability on existing streaming models, including those with dedicated switching mechanisms, while largely retaining visual quality. As it requires no training or per-model tuning and adds only one forward pass per chunk, RG can be easily integrated into existing real-time video generation pipelines.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [364] [IncrementDET: Continual AI-Generated Music Detection via Enrollment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32023)
*Runbao Du,Wei Li,Qile He*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: IncrementDET：面向持续出现的AI音乐生成器的检测器，每遇到新生成器就"enroll"一个线性"needle"分类器（与固定人类参考曲目做二分类），旧needle不变，并用Šidák校正控制整体误报率；配套定义continual AI-music detection任务与基准及EvoMusicFake-17K数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成音乐已泛滥且听众难以分辨；现有检测器基于固定生成器集合训练，对未见生成器泛化差。频繁出现新生成器时，更新"通用检测器"需大量数据与算力，且会导致对旧生成器的性能退化（灾难性遗忘）。

Method: 提出IncrementDET：为每个生成器训练一个线性分类器（needle），区分该生成器样本与一组固定人类参考曲目；新增生成器只需追加一个needle，已有needle保持不变，实现无需联合重训的增量检测。采用Šidák校正在needle数量增加时维持目标总误报率。同时定义continual AI-generated music detection任务，构建生成器逐个到来的基准（测量每次更新前后的准确率与更新成本），并构建EvoMusicFake-17K数据集（6个近期生成器+4个人类音乐平台的器乐曲）。

Result: 相较SOTA通用检测器SpecTTTra，IncrementDET仅用少量样本即可更准确地学习新生成器，对未见生成器泛化更好，对已学生成器的性能保持更佳，且更新成本显著更低。其结构可迁移至图像与视频生成检测。

Conclusion: 以"线性needle+统计校正"的增量式架构替代联合重训的通用检测器，可在低更新成本下兼顾新旧生成器的检测性能，并提供了持续检测任务、基准与数据集；方法思路对图像/视频生成内容检测具通用性。

Abstract: AI-generated music has become common on music streaming platforms, and listeners often cannot distinguish it from music made by humans. AI-music detection is therefore needed, but detectors trained on a fixed set of generators often fail on unseen generators. As new generators emerge frequently, updating a universal detector, a single model for all generators, typically requires substantial training data and computation, while risking performance degradation on previously learned generators. We therefore introduce IncrementDET, a detector that learns each new generator by a process called enrollment. For each generator, IncrementDET trains one linear classifier, which we call a needle, to separate samples of that generator from a fixed set of human reference tracks. Enrolling a new generator adds one needle while the previously constructed needles remain unchanged. A Sidak correction keeps the overall false-positive rate at its target as needles are added. To compare detectors under these conditions, we define the task of continual AI-generated music detection and introduce a benchmark in which generators arrive one at a time. The benchmark measures the accuracy of a detector before and after each update and the cost of each update. For this benchmark, we also construct EvoMusicFake-17K, a dataset of instrumental music from six recent generators and four human music platforms. Compared with SpecTTTra, a state-of-the-art universal detector, IncrementDET learns a new generator more accurately from only a few samples, generalizes better to unseen generators and better preserves detection performance on previously learned generators, at a substantially lower update cost. The structure of IncrementDET can also be applied to the detection of generated images and video, where new generators are released just as frequently.

</details>


### [365] [Cif-query bridging for audio-native asr transfer to visual speech recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32697)
*Chang Sun,Dongliang Xie,Wanpeng Xie,Bo Qin,Hong Yang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出CIF-Query Bridge：用CIF预测器从Paraformer VSR编码器产生自适应数量的token级query，经两层交叉注意力桥接后线性adapter接入Qwen3-ASR，实现视觉语音识别迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频原生ASR模型具备强语音-语言先验，但其紧凑声学接口与长帧级视觉特征不匹配；直接对数百帧视频自回归处理代价过高。

Method: Paraformer VSR编码器上的连续积分-发放(CIF)预测器生成输入依赖的token率query；两层交叉注意力桥在完整帧序列上精炼query以保留未压缩视觉上下文；线性adapter接预训练ASR projector与解码器。做了桥深度、projector初始化、adapter容量、CTC监督的消融。

Result: CMLR纯视觉迁移系统CER 7.97%，原生Paraformer同划分同评分下11.78%，绝对降3.81点、相对降32.3%。

Conclusion: CIF导出的自适应query可作为紧凑接口，把音频原生ASR能力迁移到VSR；桥接设计避免了对长帧序列的直接自回归处理。

Abstract: Audio-native automatic speech recognition (ASR) models provide strong speech-language priors, but their compact acoustic interfaces are mismatched with long, frame-level visual features. We propose CIF-Query Bridge, a lightweight connection from a Paraformer visual speech recognition (VSR) encoder to Qwen3-ASR. The existing visual continuous integrate-and-fire (CIF) predictor produces an input-dependent number of token-rate queries; a two-layer cross-attention bridge refines them against the complete frame sequence, and a linear adapter feeds the pretrained ASR projector and decoder. This design preserves access to uncompressed visual context while avoiding direct autoregressive processing of hundreds of video frames. On CMLR, the complete visual-only transfer system achieves 7.97% character error rate, compared with 11.78% for native Paraformer under the same test split and scoring protocol---a 3.81-point absolute and 32.3% relative reduction. Controlled ablations characterize bridge depth, projector initialization, adapter capacity, and CTC supervision. The results show that CIF-derived adaptive queries provide a compact interface for transferring audio-native ASR capability to VSR.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [366] [Reliability-Aware and Anatomy-Preserving Replay for Continual Echocardiographic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31270)
*Zahid Ullah,Sieun Choi,Jihie Kim*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出可靠度感知回放网络RAR-Net，用于连续超声心动图分割，通过多因子样本优先排序、受保护的任务感知记忆、遗忘条件自适应回放与结构感知监督，缓解灾难性遗忘和域偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续医学图像分割面临灾难性遗忘、域偏移与解剖标注不完整三重挑战；现有连续学习方法在有限回放缓存和不完整标注下难以兼顾旧知识保持与新分布适应。

Method: RAR-Net整合四部分：(1)多因子样例优先排序，结合类别、边界、图像、解剖与时间可靠度筛选历史样本；(2)受保护的任务感知记忆库；(3)遗忘条件自适应回放，动态调节历史样本贡献；(4)结构感知监督，在后续数据仅有部分标注时强化已学解剖结构。

Result: 在CAMUS、CardiacNet、EchoNet-Dynamic上，最终平均精度在对比的连续学习方法中最高，旧知识保持具竞争力，分割性能接近离线联合训练上界。

Conclusion: 协同设计可靠度引导的记忆管理、遗忘条件回放与解剖特定监督，可在异构超声心动图序列上兼顾知识保持与适应。

Abstract: Continual echocardiographic segmentation remains challenging due to catastrophic forgetting, domain shifts, and heterogeneous anatomical supervision. Existing continual-learning methods often struggle to preserve previously acquired knowledge while adapting to new imaging distributions, particularly under limited replay memory and incomplete anatomical annotations. To address these challenges, we propose the Reliability-Aware Replay Network (RAR-Net), a continual segmentation framework integrating multi-factor exemplar prioritization, protected task-aware memory, forgetting-conditioned adaptive replay, and structure-aware supervision. RAR-Net combines class, boundary, image, anatomical, and temporal reliability criteria to prioritize informative historical examples, while adaptive replay regulates their contribution during sequential training. Structure-aware supervision further reinforces previously learned anatomical knowledge when subsequent datasets provide incomplete annotations. Extensive experiments performed on CAMUS, CardiacNet, and EchoNet-Dynamic demonstrate that our RAR-Net achieves the highest final average accuracy among the evaluated continual-learning methods, with competitive historical knowledge retention and segmentation performance approaching the offline joint-training reference. These findings indicate that coordinating reliability-guided memory management, forgetting-conditioned replay, and anatomy-specific supervision can support knowledge retention and adaptation under the evaluated sequence of heterogeneous echocardiographic datasets.

</details>


### [367] [Exact Volume-Preserving Diffeomorphic Shear Splitting: Continuous-Discrete Geometric Bounds, Reversible Memory Backpropagation, and Expressivity Frontiers in Volumetric Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31343)
*Alvaro Garcia-Casas*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出基于级联坐标剪切（coordinate shear）的精确保体积三维微分同胚配准框架：将无散李代数分解为六个对流非线性恒为零的剪切生成元，每级解析积分为det(DS)=1的闭式三角剪切并具机器精度逆；给出全局微分同胚、边界约束、H^2谱预算与可逆反传等证明，实现零折叠、零体积漂移且显存降低78.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVF、LDDMM等速度场方法只能通过数值ODE积分或软惩罚近似不可压缩性，存在数值体积漂移（~1e-4至1e-1）、需迭代求逆、反向传播需存储O(L·N_vox)整条轨迹等问题。作者希望建立一个结构保持的精确框架，从根本上保证det(DΦ)=1且无拓扑折叠，同时获得解析逆与常数内存反传。

Method: 把无散李代数sdiff(Ω)分解为六个坐标剪切生成元，使对流项恒为零，每级剪切可解析积分成闭式三角映射，det(DS_k)=1，逆为精确闭式、无需迭代与空间插值；多级级联生成SDiff_0(Ω)连通分量。证明部分包括：(i) 全局微分同胚性（填补Hadamard–Caccioppoli单射性缺口）；(ii) Dirichlet边界约束引理；(iii) 各向异性黎曼相似不变性；(iv) Sobolev H^2谱预算以抑制混沌混合；(v) 可逆反传引擎消除O(L)轨迹存储。数值上采用palindromic Strang分裂实现二阶精度。

Result: 在16^3–64^3网格上验证O(h^2)亚体素收敛；Strang分裂二阶轨迹精度，误差比Lie–Trotter低468.1倍，漂移|det(DΦ)-1|≤2.22e-16；中间激活0.00 MB；相比SVF速度快2.1–4.0倍、GPU显存降78.4%。心脏cine-MRI（ACDC，N=100）Dice 0.884，脑MRI（OASIS-1，N=414）Dice 0.841，折叠率0.0000%、体积漂移为零。

Conclusion: 该工作以群论+数值分析的方式实现"精确"不可压缩配准，把不可压缩性从软约束变为结构性保证，兼顾解析逆、常数内存反传与效率优势，在医学图像与一般三维形状配准中具普适意义。

Abstract: Incompressible diffeomorphic registration requires continuous spatial mappings that preserve local tissue volume (det(D\Phi) = 1) without topological folding (det(D\Phi) > 0). Standard velocity-field methods (SVF, LDDMM) approximate incompressibility via numerical ODE integration or soft penalties, exhibiting numerical volume drift (~10^-4 to 10^-1), iterative grid inversions, and linear backpropagation memory O(L * N_vox). We formulate an exact structure-preserving framework for 3D registration based on cascaded coordinate shears. By decomposing the divergence-free Lie algebra sdiff(\Omega) into six coordinate-shear generators whose convective non-linearities vanish identically, each elementary stage integrates analytically to a closed-form triangular shear with det(DS_k) = 1 and exact machine-precision inverse requiring zero iterations and zero spatial interpolations. Multi-stage compositions generate the connected volume-preserving group SDiff_0(\Omega). We establish: (i) an unconditional global diffeomorphism proof closing the Hadamard-Caccioppoli injectivity gap; (ii) a Dirichlet boundary confinement lemma; (iii) an anisotropic Riemannian similarity invariance; (iv) a Sobolev H^2 spectral budget preventing chaotic mixing; and (v) an exact reversible backpropagation engine eliminating O(L) trajectory storage (0.00 MB intermediate activations). We prove second-order O(h^2) sub-voxel convergence across 16^3 to 64^3 lattices and second-order trajectory accuracy for palindromic Strang splitting (468.1x error reduction vs. Lie-Trotter; drift |det(D\Phi)-1| <= 2.22e-16). Execution is 2.1x-4.0x faster than SVF with 78.4% GPU memory reduction. On cardiac cine-MRI (ACDC, N=100) and brain MRI (OASIS-1, N=414), our model achieves competitive Dice (0.884 and 0.841) while strictly maintaining 0.0000% folding and zero volume drift.

</details>


### [368] [Characterizing Datasets to Anticipate Unsupervised Domain Adaptation Performance for Radiographic Pneumonia Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32360)
*Iftakhar Hossain,Md. Imran Hasan,Salahuddin Ahmed,Eric D. McCollum,Abdullah H. Baqui,Taufiq Hasan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该研究表明无监督域适应(UDA)性能取决于源域和目标域数据集的结构性质且方向不对称：类可分性(Silhouette Score)高的目标域更易取得高AUC(r=0.934)，而高可分性的源域反而带来更差AUC(r=-0.922)；可分性差值可在适配前预测更好的迁移方向(10/10对)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸片肺炎检测的深度学习模型跨机构应用时精度显著下降，需UDA；但UDA效果依数据集对而异且增益不对称，作者希望找到可在适配前测量的结构性质来预测UDA性能，从而指导低资源场景的数据集选择。

Method: 用冻结DenseNet-121单次前向得到特征，以Silhouette Score（Fisher Ratio辅助）刻画5个胸片数据集的类可分性；对全部20个有向源-目标对用DANN进行UDA，并与直接迁移对比；在同一表示上计算7个已发表的可迁移性估计器作对照，做相关性/方向预测分析。

Result: 目标域可分性与AUC正相关(r=0.934, p=0.042)，源域可分性与AUC负相关(r=-0.922, p=0.017)；可分性符号差与AUC符号差相关(r=-0.932, p=0.017)，在10/10对中正确指出更优迁移方向(p=0.001)；所提描述子在目标域评估上与最佳已发表估计器相当。

Conclusion: 源与目标数据集在域适应中承担相反的结构角色：高类可分性使数据集更适合作为测试集而非训练源。该框架可在不运行完整适配实验的情况下指导数据集选择，适用于低资源医疗场景。

Abstract: Deep learning models for pneumonia detection from chest radiographs exhibit degraded accuracy when applied across institutions, which motivates Unsupervised Domain Adaptation (UDA). We observe that UDA performance depends on the properties of both the source and target datasets, and that performance gains are asymmetric with respect to dataset pairs. We investigate which structural properties of chest X-ray datasets are the most important predictors of UDA performance, and whether they can be measured before adaptation is attempted. Five chest radiograph datasets were characterized by the class separability of their features, obtained from one forward pass of a frozen DenseNet-121 and summarized by the Silhouette Score, with the Fisher Ratio as a supporting measure. All twenty directed source-target pairs were adapted with a Domain-Adversarial Neural Network (DANN), while direct transfer without adaptation was also evaluated. Seven published transferability estimators were computed on the same representation for comparison. We found that adaptation performance depended on both endpoints in opposite directions. More separable target datasets attained higher AUC scores ($r=0.934$, $p=0.042$), whereas more separable sources produced lower AUC scores ($r=-0.922$, $p=0.017$). The signed difference in separability tracked the signed difference in AUC between the two directions of a pair ($r=-0.932$, $p=0.017$) and identified the better direction in 10 of 10 pairs ($p=0.001$). The proposed descriptor was comparable to the best published estimators on target evaluation. Source and target datasets play opposite structural roles in adaptation: higher class separability makes a dataset easier as a test set but not as a training source. This framework can guide dataset selection for adaptation in low-resource settings without running full experiments.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [369] [UniCon: Unified Framework for Efficient Contrastive Alignment via Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16678)
*Hangke Sui,Yuqing Wang,Minh N Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniCon 提出统一对比对齐框架，用对比相似度权重矩阵 S(γ) 给出线性/非线性编码器、一对一/多对多对齐的闭式全局解，用 RKHS 视角统一对比对齐并与谱方法建立联系，从而替代 minibatch 反向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态对比目标训练依赖长时间随机优化、收敛慢，作者希望用核方法的闭式解加速对比对齐训练。

Method: 引入对比相似度权重矩阵 S(γ)，在 RKHS 框架下推导覆盖线性与非线性编码器、一对一与多对多对齐的闭式全局最优解，以精确更新替代小批量梯度反传，并揭示其与谱方法的关系。

Result: 在合成、单模态、多模态与零样本任务上验证理论，报告显著效率提升且保持通用性与较强实证性能；但摘要未给出具体加速倍数或精度数字。

Conclusion: UniCon 把对比对齐统一为核视角下的闭式求解问题，为多模态对比训练提供了一种可证明等价的高效替代路径。

Abstract: Contrastive objectives power state-of-the-art multimodal models, but their training remains slow, relying on long stochastic optimization. We propose a Unified Framework for Efficient Contrastive Alignment via Kernels (UniCon), which spans linear and nonlinear encoders as well as one-to-one and many-to-many alignments. At its core, UniCon introduces the contrastive similarity weight matrix $S(\gamma)$, which enables closed-form global solutions that provably replace minibatch back-propagation with exact updates. Through the lens of reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHS), UniCon provides a kernelized perspective that unifies contrastive alignment and reveals its connection to spectral methods. To validate the theory, we conduct experiments on synthetic, unimodal, multimodal, and zero-shot tasks, demonstrating that UniCon achieves substantial efficiency gains while preserving generality and strong empirical performance.

</details>


### [370] [Hidden Clues in WM-811K: Uncovering the Spatial Signals Behind Wafer Map Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31292)
*Kabir Tolani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过构造 Original、Layout-only、Global-shuffle、Radial-shuffle 四种晶圆图变体并保持同一数据划分与 CNN 结构，量化 WM-811K 数据集中不同信息层级对分类性能的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WM-811K 上 CNN 分类精度很高，但不清楚性能究竟由哪些数据特征驱动，需要做受控的消融以理解基准有效性与潜在捷径。

Method: 清洗数据（去重、剔除同图冲突标签、按批次/同图隔离训练验证测试）后得到 25,513 张八类缺陷晶圆图；构造四种变体：原始图、仅保留非背景 die 的 Layout-only、保持失效 die 数量但随机打乱位置的 Global-shuffle、仅在 8 个径向分箱内随机化位置的 Radial-shuffle；四种变体共用同一划分、预处理与 CNN 架构。

Result: Macro-F1：Layout-only 0.3829，Global-shuffle 0.5803，Radial-shuffle 0.7439，Original 0.8563，呈清晰的单调递增信息层级。

Conclusion: WM-811K 的信息分布在多个层级：晶圆布局、失效数量、给定半径内的失效分布、失效 die 的精确二维位置；因此高基准性能并非只来自学习精确缺陷构型，benchmark 的解释需谨慎。

Abstract: WM-811K (also known as LSWMD) is a wafer map dataset first made publicly available in a 2015 paper by Wu et al. Convolutional neural networks have been used to achieve high classification accuracy on this dataset, yet it's not easy to decipher which features of the dataset are driving that performance. This study attempts to answer this specifically for WM-811K: how much of the predictive performance results from the wafer layout, the count of failed dies, their distance from the center and their exact 2-D location. After analyzing duplicate wafer maps, removing same wafer maps with conflicting labels and ensuring that the same manufacturing lot or exact same map doesn't appear in training, validation and test data, we were left with 25,513 defect-labeled wafer maps from eight classes. We then constructed four variations of each wafer map. The Original was the actual wafer map present in the dataset. The Layout-only kept only the layout of the non-background dies. The Global-shuffle kept the count of failed dies while shuffling them around. The Radial-shuffle randomized the locations of failed dies only in their radial bin, which was chosen out of 8 radial bins based on distance from the center. The data split, preprocessing and CNN architecture remained the same for all four of them. Macro-F1 progressed like this: 0.3829 (Layout-only), 0.5803 (Global-shuffle), 0.7439 (Radial-shuffle) and 0.8563 (Original). These results show that WM-811K contains useful information at several levels: wafer layout, number of failures, distribution of failed dies in a given radius, and exact 2-D locations of the failed dies. Therefore, high classification performance on this benchmark does not come only from learning the exact defect configuration.

</details>


### [371] [Near-Zero Forgetting Is Not Enough: Out-of-Distribution Robustness in Continual Learning of Pretrained VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31511)
*Adrià Ruiz,Bartłomiej Twardowski,Tinne Tuytelaars,Sergi Masip*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Pi0经经验回放持续学习后，域内近零遗忘，但分布外(OOD)旧任务能力迅速崩溃，且表征漂移集中于多模态与动作特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作声称VLA在经验回放的持续学习中几乎不遗忘，但该结论仅在域内设定下成立；对已学任务在域偏移下的鲁棒性尚无人研究。

Method: 在LIBERO与LIBERO-Plus上对Pi0做经验回放微调，分别评测ID与OOD性能；跨域偏移做表征分析（特征漂移定位）与线性探测（检验OOD特征是否仍保留任务相关信息）。

Result: ID设定下遗忘近零；OOD下旧任务性能快速崩塌，且退化呈现跨域偏移共享的表征模式，在两个较简单套件的行为层面亦可观察到。漂移集中在多模态与动作特征；线性探测显示OOD特征仍含任务相关信息但“不可读”，符合表征错位。

Conclusion: ID稳定性完好不等于鲁棒：严重OOD遗忘可伴随持续学习中的表征漂移同时发生，需在持续学习评测中显式纳入OOD协议。

Abstract: Recent work shows that pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exhibit near-zero forgetting in specific continual learning settings with experience replay. However, robustness to domain shift of learned tasks remains unexplored. In this work, we study the robustness of Pi0 fine-tuned with experience replay under out-of-distribution (OOD) data using the LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus benchmarks. While the in-domain (ID) setup achieves near-zero catastrophic forgetting, OOD performance on previous tasks quickly collapses. Specifically, we show that during the continual learning process, the OOD capabilities of the model degrade, following a shared representational pattern across domain shifts, which is also visible behaviorally in the two simpler suites. Representational analysis shows that drift concentrates in the multimodal and action features. Linear probes indicate that OOD features still retain task-relevant information, but appear unreadable, consistent with representation misalignment. These findings suggest that even when ID stability remains intact, severe OOD forgetting can still occur, accompanied by representational drift during continual learning.

</details>


### [372] [Denoising Diffusion with Progressive Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31558)
*Zeyu Luo,Shuai Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出渐进后验采样（PPS）：在反向扩散中闭式构造高斯近似后验，自动估计测量噪声方差、无需SVD，并将引导样本投影到近似局部噪声图像流形的球面水平集上以保证先验局部几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型后验采样求解含噪线性逆问题时，依赖测量算子的奇异值分解或需预先知道噪声方差；仅用局部梯度引导对后验几何的刻画有限。

Method: 反向扩散每一步解析构造后验的高斯近似，将局部先验采样点朝近似后验均值引导，再投影到球面水平集以逼近局部噪声图像流形；噪声方差由采样过程自动估计，避免SVD。

Result: 在FFHQ与ImageNet上，覆盖4类复原任务与3档测量噪声水平，PPS在重建精度与感知质量上均有竞争力。

Conclusion: 闭式高斯后验近似加流形投影是一种无需SVD、无需已知噪声方差的实用后验采样增强方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide effective generative priors for solving noisy linear inverse problems. However, existing posterior sampling methods may require singular value decomposition (SVD) of the measurement operator or prior knowledge of the measurement noise variance, while guidance based on local gradients provides only a limited view of posterior geometry. We propose progressive posterior sampling (PPS) with manifold projection, which automatically estimates the measurement noise variance and avoids SVD. During reverse diffusion, PPS constructs a Gaussian approximation to the posterior distribution in closed form and guides a sample drawn from the local prior toward the approximate posterior mean. We then project the guided sample onto a spherical level set that approximates the local noisy image manifold, promoting consistency with the local geometry of the diffusion prior. Experiments on FFHQ and ImageNet across four restoration tasks and three measurement noise levels demonstrate competitive reconstruction accuracy and perceptual quality by PPS.

</details>


### [373] [One Phase Is Enough: Sim-to-Real Transfer with Direct Visual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31588)
*John Marangola,Antonia Bronars,Younghyo Park,Stephen Andrews,Kushal Kedia,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT 用冻结的预训练视觉基础编码器做单阶段视觉强化学习，避免两阶段蒸馏的性能损失，真实世界成功率相对提升 58.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉 sim-to-real 中，"特权状态训练教师 + 模仿蒸馏学生"的两阶段流程存在学生显著弱于教师的问题；而直接端到端视觉 RL 从零学习任务相关表征样本效率低，单阶段难以收敛。作者希望在不牺牲样本效率的前提下直接训练可部署的视觉策略。

Method: 提出 DRIFT 配方：使用冻结的预训练基础视觉编码器（foundation vision encoder）提取图像特征，RL 只在冻结表征上学习控制策略。冻结编码器免去视觉主干的反向传播开销，降低显存与计算；同时预训练表征的泛化性使策略可从快速栅格化仿真渲染迁移到真实世界。实验在架构、观测与计算预算对齐的条件下与两阶段蒸馏对比，覆盖三个操作任务，并做分布内/分布外受控仿真评估。

Result: 在匹配架构、观测与算力预算下，DRIFT 在三个真实操作任务的平均成功率上相对两阶段蒸馏提升 58.8%；仿真中相对优势从分布内约 9% 扩大到分布外超过 61%，表明更强泛化性贡献了更优的真实迁移。

Conclusion: 简单、有效的单阶段视觉 RL 配方优于教师-学生蒸馏范式，且"冻结预训练视觉编码器"是关键设计选择；但其仍依赖域随机化栅格化渲染与真实环境评测，未涉及触觉、动态场景或开放代码/机位泛化等方向。

Abstract: Sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) has enabled impressive robotic behaviors, yet deploying policies directly from vision remains challenging. A common approach first trains a teacher policy with RL using privileged state observations, then distills its behavior into a vision-based student via imitation. However, the resulting student can substantially underperform its teacher. Directly training visual policies with RL offers a promising alternative, but learning task-relevant visual representations from scratch can be sample-inefficient, making one-phase visual RL intractable. We propose DRIFT, a simple recipe for efficient one-phase visual RL using image observations available at test time. Our key insight is to use frozen, pretrained foundation vision encoders, allowing RL to focus on learning control from existing visual representations. Freezing the encoder avoids costly backpropagation through the vision backbone, while its pretrained representations enable real-world transfer from fast, rasterized simulation rendering. Under matched architectures, observations, and compute budgets, DRIFT outperforms two-phase distillation and achieves a 58.8% relative improvement in average real-world success rate across three manipulation tasks. Controlled simulation evaluations demonstrate that DRIFT's relative advantage grows from roughly 9% in-distribution to over 61% under out-of-distribution shifts, suggesting that stronger generalization contributes to superior real-world transfer. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [374] [Start Where the Data Are: Statistical Priors for One-Step Generation on Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31856)
*Yuyao Wang,Haoliang Sun,Xiankai Lu,Xinxin Zhang,Yongshun Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在流形上用切空间高斯混合建模数据统计先验并结合"后验耦合"采样，长链用隐马尔可夫先验，可插入现有一步生成框架，在球面/环面/SO(3)及蛋白扭转角生成上大幅降低分布误差与离群率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流形上的一步生成器常用均匀噪声配对集中分布的数据，导致在两个模态之间低密度区域产生虚假样本；作者希望用捕获数据粗结构的统计先验替代均匀噪声。

Method: 先用几何感知的矩匹配拟合切空间高斯混合作为先验；针对独立噪声-数据配对会跨模态连接的问题，引入后验耦合——以每个数据点为条件采样潜在分量，再从该分量采样配对噪声；对长扭转角链用共享参数的隐马尔可夫先验建模序列依赖，避免全协方差混合的高成本。框架不改动生成器结构、训练目标，推理仍是单次网络评估。

Result: 在球面、环面、SO(3)上实验，多种生成器的分布误差最多降低125倍；蛋白Ramachandran离群率从12.1%降至0.55%。

Conclusion: 数据相关的统计先验加后验耦合是一步流形生成的有效、低侵入式改进，可扩展到高维序贯分子结构。
