Abstract: Fast generation on manifolds is important for scientific applications such as protein design, yet existing one-step generators often produce spurious samples in low-density gaps between data modes. Our analysis highlights the mismatch between uniform noise and concentrated data, motivating a statistical prior that captures the data's coarse structure. We first estimate this prior by fitting mixtures of tangent-space Gaussians through geometry-aware moment matching. However, independent noise-data pairing can still connect different modes and weaken the benefit of the fitted prior. We therefore introduce posterior coupling, which samples a latent component conditioned on each data point and draws its paired noise from that component. To extend this approach to long torsion-angle chains, where full-covariance mixtures become costly, we use a hidden Markov prior to model sequential dependence with shared parameters. The resulting framework plugs into existing one-step training pipelines while retaining the generator architecture, training objective, and single network evaluation at inference. Experiments on spheres, tori, and SO(3) demonstrate substantial improvements across multiple generators, reducing distributional error by up to $125\times$ and the protein Ramachandran outlier rate from 12.1% to 0.55%.

</details>


### [375] [Subliminal Learning Crosses Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31870)
*Saraswathy Amjith,Kimia Hamidieh,Antonio Torralba,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 文本式潜隐学习可跨模态迁移：通过数字序列训练学生模型后，其偏好（如动物主题）会传递到以其为文本编码器的扩散模型生成图像中，并改变图像表征；该效应由学生模型权重中的一个常量分量驱动，可用"weight lens"从单一模型权重中识别潜隐特质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜隐学习（subliminal learning）此前仅在语言模型同模态内被观察到，其是否能跨模态（文本→视觉生成与表征）迁移、迁移哪些概念、以及机制为何，均不清楚。

Method: 1) 先用带偏好（如喜欢某类动物）的教师模型向学生模型以看似无意义的数字序列进行蒸馏训练；2) 将学生模型作为扩散模型的文本编码器，比较生成图像中目标主题的出现频率；3) 机制分析：分离学生表征变化中的常量分量，并将其均匀加回，检验对生成图像的影响；4) 提出 weight lens，仅从单一已训练模型的权重中识别潜隐特质。

Result: 使用学生作为文本编码器的扩散模型比使用原始模型更频繁地生成学生的目标动物，但不影响颜色或风格；学生学习变化中的常量分量可解释图像表征向该特质的偏移，均匀加入该分量可改变生成动物（依赖完整提示），而颜色、风格这类由单一 token 编码的概念不受影响；weight lens 能识别几乎所有学生的潜隐特质，包括未在生成图像中显现的特质。

Conclusion: 潜隐学习可跨模态迁移，且其机制可分解为与提示无关的常量偏移；对该现象的权重级检测（weight lens）可行，提示跨模态训练数据存在隐蔽的偏好/行为传导风险。

Abstract: Subliminal learning refers to a surprising phenomenon in language models wherein a prompt given to one model (e.g., preference for cats) can be subliminally distilled to another model via training on data that is ostensibly meaningless (e.g., number sequences). This paper shows that text-based subliminal learning transfers cross-modally, in particular, a topic/preference (e.g., animals) transferred through number sequences across vision-language models also changes how images are generated and represented. First, we show that a diffusion model using the student as its text encoder generates the student's animal more often than with the original model as its text encoder, but not its color or style. We then investigate the mechanism behind this phenomenon and which concepts transfer across modalities. A constant component of the student's change accounts for much of the shift of image representations toward the trait. Adding this component uniformly can change the generated animal, which depends on the whole prompt, but not concepts such as color or style, which are encoded in a single token. Finally, we introduce weight lens as an approach to identify a subliminal trait from the weights of a single trained model, and show that it identifies the trait of nearly every student, including traits that do not surface in generated images.

</details>


### [376] [PhysRF-WorldJEPA: Transforming Heterogeneous Large Language Models into Manifold-Decoupled Millimeter-Wave Radar World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32113)
*Yicheng Huang,Feng Ke,Linhan Chen,Ziyi Jiang,Xiu Yin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PhysRF-WorldJEPA，将连续电磁物理状态预测与跨模态语言对齐解耦的流形解耦 JEPA 架构，用于毫米波雷达-语言模型适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准指令微调将连续雷达流当作静态类文本 token，导致雷达-文本全局检索近乎随机，且传感器中断时出现严重物理漂移；而将成对对比损失（如 SigLIP）直接并入共享主干虽提升检索，却注入 O(B²) 排斥梯度并撕裂连续坐标流形（R²<0）。

Method: 未衰减 1.0x 时钟锚 + 并行双尺度 RF 编码器驱动约 3% LoRA 的轻量 LLM；采用两步状态空间 EMA-JEPA（去噪→推演）与样本轴 Batch-VICReg 预测 +3.0 s 物理状态；随后用 stop-gradient 对齐头将冻结的提示状态映射到文本超球面，并配合假阴性掩码。

Result: 在 4,541 窗口三频段（77/60.25/60 GHz）数据集上对比 10 个基线：仅训练解耦对齐头即将 R→T R@10 从 5.95% 提升至 15.01%，MedRank 从 107.0 降至 60.0，同时保持物理读出完全一致：中断后 +3.0 s 躯干倾角 3.37°（R²=0.961）、杂乱环境无人机高度 1.89 m（R²=0.679；RF 前端 1.19 m/0.863）、穿墙 NLOS 距离 2.04 m（R²=0.461）、虚拟 RIS 不变性 0.9976、跌倒召回 100%。跨 5 个 0.5B–1.8B LLM 家族（15 个零开销模型）在 10/10 次运行中同时改善检索与 75% 丢帧补全；在中断倾角上以 Span-Euc 计 4/5、Scatter 2/5、各族最优 5/5 优于 SFT。

Conclusion: 通过流形解耦设计可同时获得跨模态检索提升与物理状态预测精度的无损保持，验证了"预测与对齐分离"对连续传感模态适配 LLM 的有效性，并具跨模型族可迁移性。

Abstract: Adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar enables privacy-preserving, all-weather physical scene understanding across darkness, smoke, and walls. However, standard instruction tuning treats continuous radar streams as static text-like tokens, causing near-random global radar-to-text retrieval and severe physical drift under sensor blackouts. Conversely, coupling pairwise contrastive losses (e.g., SigLIP) directly into the shared backbone improves retrieval but injects $O(B^2)$ repulsive gradients that fracture continuous coordinate manifolds ($R^2 < 0$). We present PhysRF-WorldJEPA, a manifold-decoupled Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) that separates continuous electromagnetic state prediction from cross-modal language alignment. An unattenuated 1.0x clock anchor and parallel dual-scale RF encoder feed a lean ~3% LoRA LLM trained with two-step state-space EMA-JEPA (Denoise -> Rollout) and sample-axis Batch-VICReg to predict +3.0 s physical states; a stop-gradient alignment head then maps frozen prompt states onto the text hypersphere with false-negative masking. On a 4,541-window tri-domain suite (77/60.25/60 GHz) against 10 baselines, freezing the physical backbone and training only the decoupled head lifts R->T R@10 from 5.95% to 15.01% and improves MedRank from 107.0 to 60.0 while preserving identical physical readout: 3.37 deg ($R^2=+0.961$) blackout +3.0 s torso tilt, 1.89 m ($R^2=+0.679$; 1.19 m/+0.863 at the RF front-end) cluttered UAV altitude, 2.04 m ($R^2=+0.461$) behind-wall NLOS range, 0.9976 virtual-RIS invariance, and 100% fall recall. Across five 0.5B-1.8B LLM families (15 zero-overhead models), World-JEPA improves retrieval and 75%-drop completion in 10/10 runs and blackout tilt in 4/5 (Span-Euc), 2/5 (Scatter), and 5/5 (per-family best) over SFT.

</details>


### [377] [Multi-view Random-set Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32170)
*Mengchi Zuo,Tianyang Wang,Cai Xu,Ziyu Guan,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多视图随机集学习(MRSL)范式,用随机集(候选类集合)保留各视图的部分判别信息,通过跨视图集合交集做集合级证据融合以缩小候选类,避免证据扭曲与"先投影到单类再融合"的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图数据存在"视图退化":单视图仅含部分判别信息,无法独立确定实例标签。强制单视图输出明确类证据会扭曲证据学习并误导融合;现有方法要么把证据分配给单一类而不显式表示未定类组,要么在融合前就把复合置信投影到单类,阻碍跨视图集合交集的直接精化。

Method: MRSL:1) 以类重叠度量筛选每个视图的紧凑候选集合集(避免枚举全部类子集的指数代价);2) 集合级融合,为集合交集分配联合支持度,使互补视图在概率转换前收窄候选类。

Result: 合成数据验证了重叠度量有效性;在8个真实数据集上MRSL全部取得最高准确率,相较最强基线平均精度提升4.71%,平均期望校准误差降低6.33%。

Conclusion: 以随机集/集合级融合替代单类证据投影,是处理视图退化的有效且校准良好的多视图可信学习方案。

Abstract: Trusted multi-view learning aims to integrate heterogeneous views for accurate and reliable predictions. However, multi-view data often exhibit the view degradation phenomenon: individual views contain only partial discriminative information and cannot independently determine the instance-level label. Requiring such views to produce unambiguous class evidence can distort evidence learning and mislead subsequent fusion. Existing methods addressing this problem have at least one of the following drawbacks: (1) assigning evidence to individual classes without explicitly representing unresolved class groups; (2) projecting composite beliefs onto individual classes before fusion, preventing direct refinement of candidate groups through cross-view set intersections. To address these limitations, we propose a new Multi-view Random-Set Learning (MRSL) paradigm, which preserves view-specific support for candidate class sets and refines candidate classes through cross-view fusion. Specifically, we quantify class overlap to select a compact collection of sets for each view, avoiding the exponential cost of modeling all class subsets. We then perform set-level fusion by assigning joint support to set intersections, allowing complementary views to narrow the candidate classes before probability conversion. Experiments on synthetic and eight real-world datasets validate the proposed overlap measure and demonstrate that MRSL achieves the highest accuracy on all eight datasets. Compared with the strongest baselines, MRSL improves average accuracy by 4.71% and reduces average expected calibration error by 6.33%. All datasets and code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [378] [See the Answer, Learn to Embed: Learning Universal Multimodal Embeddings from Hindsight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32439)
*Yoonki Cho,Youngju Na,Taegeun Yang,Jinhwan Seo,Sung-Eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HEME 在训练时以"匹配候选"作为特权上下文，通过带 hindsight 注意力掩码与错配候选条件的教师模型学习 query-grounded 后见表示，再蒸馏到独立学生嵌入器，推理时无需候选条件或推理链，兼顾效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理驱动的通用多模态嵌入（UME）方法需要生成中间推理步骤来提供额外上下文，推理开销大；作者希望验证能否在训练时"看到答案"，把匹配候选本身当作廉价上下文来源，但直接用候选条件会导致泄漏候选特有信息、并使 query 表示向候选嵌入塌缩。

Method: 提出 Hindsight-Enhanced Multimodal Embeddings（HEME）：1）先训练后见教师（hindsight teacher），采用后见注意力掩码、错配候选条件（mismatched-candidate conditioning）与 query-token 对齐，抑制对候选的过度依赖；2）冻结教师，将其后见表示与相似度分布蒸馏到独立的学生嵌入器；3）推理时学生仅独立编码 query 与候选，无候选条件、无教师、无显式推理。

Result: 在 MMEB-V2 以及 MMEB-V3 的 agent 与文本任务上验证了 HEME 的有效性，并显示相较显式推理驱动方法具有更高的推理效率。

Conclusion: 候选条件可作为推理链的替代上下文来源，配合指令微调式蒸馏可把后见知识内化到无条件的双模态编码器中，为多模态检索嵌入提供低成本增强路径；但其价值局限于嵌入/检索任务，与具身与三维重建主线关系较弱。

Abstract: Universal Multimodal Embedding (UME) models map heterogeneous multimodal inputs into a shared embedding space, where pairwise similarity supports diverse downstream tasks. Recent reasoning-driven UME approaches use intermediate reasoning to generate additional context for embedding, but often incur extra computation at inference. In this paper, we explore the matched candidate (i.e., the paired target) as an alternative source of context, asking whether a model can "see the answer" during training and "learn to embed" without it at inference. However, direct conditioning on this privileged context can introduce candidate-specific information unavailable from the query alone or cause the candidate-conditioned query representation to collapse toward the candidate embedding. To address this, we introduce Hindsight-Enhanced Multimodal Embeddings (HEME), which learns query-grounded hindsight representations from such candidate context. HEME first trains a hindsight teacher with a hindsight attention mask, mismatched-candidate conditioning, and query-token alignment to limit excessive reliance on the candidate. It then freezes the teacher and distills its hindsight representation and similarity distribution into a standalone student embedder. At inference, the student independently encodes queries and candidates without candidate conditioning or the hindsight teacher. Experiments on MMEB-V2 and the agent and text tasks of MMEB-V3 demonstrate the effectiveness of HEME and show that it enables more efficient inference than explicit reasoning-driven methods.

</details>


### [379] [AVQ-Attention-2: Adaptive Vector-Quantized Attention with Exact Local Blocks and Linear-Cost Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32989)
*Winfried van den Dool,Patrick Forré,Amir Habibian,Yuki M. Asano,Max Welling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AVQ-Attention-2：将自适应矢量量化注意力与精确局部注意力结合，并以簇内键均值替代码字，训练与推理均为 O(NM)，可直接嵌入预训练Transformer且质量接近或优于稠密注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力复杂度 O(N^2)，矢量量化注意力通过聚类键压缩到 O(NM)，但其训练仍需 O(N^2)，且量化码字带来的近似误差使其难以替代精确注意力。

Method: 在AVQ-attention基础上融合精确局部注意力（近邻窗口精确计算）与远距离簇级近似；将每个簇的表示由单一码字改为该簇键向量的均值，降低量化误差；并改进训练过程使前向/反向均为 O(NM)。

Result: 三点改进：同成本下质量一致更好；训练成本降为 O(NM)；近似精度足以无需微调直接替换预训练Transformer的注意力层。多种序列长度下与精确稠密注意力竞争力相当，且速度超过FlashAttention而质量损失很小。

Conclusion: 簇均值+局部精确注意力的混合设计使近似注意力在效率与精度上同时逼近甚至匹配稠密注意力，具备作为即插即用高效注意力模块的潜力。

Abstract: Attention costs $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ in the number of tokens. Vector-quantized attention reduces this to $\mathcal{O}(NM)$ by clustering the $N$ keys, attending to the $M$ codewords instead, and its adaptive form, AVQ-attention, concentrates codebook resolution where a query's attention mass actually falls. We present AVQ-Attention-2, a better design that merges it with exact local attention and attends to the mean of each cluster's keys rather than to a codeword. We show three improvements: consistently better quality at comparable cost; training that now also costs $\mathcal{O}(NM)$ rather than $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$; and an approximation accurate enough that the layer can be dropped into a pretrained transformer without fine-tuning. Across a variety of sequence lengths the result is competitive not merely with other approximate attention but with exact dense attention, running faster than FlashAttention at little to no loss in quality.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [380] [From Safety-Driven Redundancy to Value-Driven Architecture: An ODD-Based Rationalization Framework for Level 4 Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32960)
*Tiago Simão Ferreira,Farzad Fallahi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种基于ODD的安全约束系统工程框架，用于L4自动驾驶架构合理化：将ODD能力、物理故障假设、故障后运行策略与传感空间需求通过安全-可行性闸门传播，仅在此可行设计空间内评估删除/迁移/功能重分配/合并等架构变换，并进行生命周期成本敏感性与多目标Pareto分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L4自动驾驶需要容错架构以在感知、计算、通信、电源、执行故障下保持关键驾驶功能，但面向工业规模化时架构复杂度、验证负担、集成工作量与全生命周期成本成为关键瓶颈，缺乏系统化的架构合理化方法。

Method: 构建ODD驱动的安全约束系统工程框架：将ODD特定能力、物理故障假设、故障后运行策略、空间感知需求经集成的安全-可行性闸门传播，形成安全可行设计空间；在此空间内评估删除、重定位、功能再分配、合并等架构变换，再做生命周期成本敏感性分析与鲁棒多目标Pareto分析；以一个通用、合成参数化的L4架构作为演示性计算案例。

Result: 直接消融的合理化潜力有限；方向性重分配与功能合并产生更多安全可行候选。示例性生命周期成本模型下，最佳名义候选实现5.55%归一化成本降低，且在指定不确定性域内5000个采样点上均保持降本。识别出15个非支配架构-策略解，覆盖7种物理变换。

Conclusion: 结果支持在给定假设下以能力为基础、策略条件化、面向生命周期的架构合理化，安全性作为非补偿性可行性约束保留；所报告的变换属于设计候选而非经过验证的量产架构。

Abstract: Level 4 autonomous vehicles require fault-tolerant architectures that preserve critical driving functions under sensing, computing, communication, power, and actuation failures. As these systems progress toward industrial scalability, architectural complexity, validation burden, integration effort, and lifecycle cost become increasingly relevant. This study proposes an Operational Design Domain (ODD)-based, safety-constrained systems-engineering framework for architecture rationalization. ODD-specific capabilities, physical fault assumptions, post-fault operational policies, and spatial sensing requirements are propagated through an integrated safety-feasibility gate. Architecture transformations, including removal, relocation, functional reassignment, and consolidation, are evaluated only within the resulting safety-feasible design space, followed by lifecycle-cost sensitivity and robust multiobjective Pareto analysis. A generic, synthetically parameterized Level 4 architecture is used as a demonstrative computational case study. Within this modeled design space, direct ablation showed limited rationalization potential, whereas directional redistribution and functional consolidation generated a broader set of safety-feasible candidates. Under the illustrative lifecycle-cost model, the best nominal candidate achieved a 5.55% normalized cost reduction and remained cost-reducing across all 5,000 sampled points in the prescribed uncertainty domain. The analysis identified 15 non-dominated architecture-policy solutions representing seven physical transformations. The results support capability-based, policy-conditioned, and lifecycle-aware architecture rationalization under the stated assumptions, with safety retained as a non-compensatory feasibility constraint. The reported transformations are design candidates rather than validated production architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [381] [MOZAIC: An Audio-Video Deepfake Benchmark for Telling What Is Fake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31301)
*Shaina Raza,Mohamed Hafez,Ahmed Y. Radwan,Khalid Elgazzar,Kathryn Hume*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOZAIC 是一个成对音视频深度伪造基准，每个源片段同时发布四种真/假视频×真/假音频组合，含137,745片段、15,014源对，支持真实性、操纵类型、身份验证与解释四项任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频深度伪造基准多只做二分类（真/假），无法揭示检测器究竟依赖画面、语音还是编码痕迹，也难以量化各模态贡献。

Method: 构建四路标签（视频真/假 × 音频真/假）的配对基准，假样本涵盖内脸替换、整帧视频扩散与声音转换；评测12个音视频多模态大模型（MLLM）、训练4个检测器，并用LoRA微调MLLM。

Result: 最佳MLLM在四路T1仅35.6%，对仅视频造假识别43.6%、仅语音造假仅7.5%；训练检测器T1达98.4%；LoRA将解释分数提升（OmniVinci 4.17→7.40，Qwen2.5-Omni 4.42→6.98）；最佳T1检测器在留出换脸生成器时降至78.3%。

Conclusion: 配对四路设计可显式度量模态贡献，但跨生成器泛化与可解释性仍是开放问题；作者开源数据集、MOZAIC-SMALL子集、代码与提示词。

Abstract: Audio-video (AV) deepfakes can fake the face, the voice, or both. Most benchmarks only ask whether a clip is real or fake, so they cannot show whether a detector relies on the picture, the voice, or encoding traces. We present MOZAIC, a paired benchmark with a 4-way label in which every source clip is released in all four combinations of real or fake video and real or fake audio so each modality contribution can be measured directly. Fakes cover inner-face swapping, whole-frame video diffusion and voice conversion. MOZAIC has 137,745 clips from 15,014 source pairs and four tasks: T1 AV authenticity, T2 manipulation type, T3 identity verification and T4 explanation. We evaluate 12 AV multimodal LLMs (MLLMs), train four detectors and fine-tune MLLMs with LoRA. Overall, the best MLLM reaches only 35.6% on the four-way T1 and catches far more video-only fakes than voice-only fakes (43.6% vs 7.5%). Trained detectors reach 98.4% on T1, and LoRA fine-tuning raises explanation scores (OmniVinci 4.17 to 7.40, Qwen2.5-Omni 4.42 to 6.98), but the best T1 detector falls to 78.3% when the face-swap generator is held out of training, so generalization and explanation remain open. We release the dataset, the compact MOZAIC-SMALL split, the code and the prompts. this https URL

</details>


### [382] [Beyond Static Charts: Benchmarking Interactive Chart-to-Code Generation for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31345)
*Yuhan Bian,Haoyu Liu,Siyu Li,Hanpei Guo,Jingrong Gao,Zhixu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChartInt是首个面向交互式图表到代码生成（interactive chart-to-code）的基准，含2,779条标注样本，并提出预设状态快照协议将动态交互行为转化为可复现评估目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM图表到代码基准只覆盖静态图表，强调视觉一致性；而交互式图表评估的是"行为一致性"——图表状态需在用户交互序列下正确演化，传统单步评估范式失效，故需要新基准与协议。

Method: 构建ChartInt基准，覆盖hover、highlight、tooltip等常见交互模式，并加入数据更新、样式调整、跨图表风格迁移等编辑任务；提出预设状态快照协议（predefined target states + 确定性触发交互事件）使动态行为可复现；据此设计语义与风格一致性的多维度评估框架。

Result: 实验显示当前MLLM在静态图表上表现良好，但在交互任务上性能显著下降，说明现有模型缺乏交互感知能力，凸显该类基准的必要性。

Conclusion: 交互式图表生成需要面向行为一致性的评估范式；ChartInt提供了可复现的协议与多维评估框架，揭示了MLLM在交互任务上的明显短板。

Abstract: In recent years, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved significant progress in chart-to-code generation. However, existing benchmarks mainly focus on static charts, overlooking interactive charts that enable richer and more expressive data exploration. Unlike static chart-to-code generation, which emphasizes visual consistency, interactive chart-to-code evaluation targets behavioral consistency, requiring chart states to evolve correctly under sequences of user interactions, thereby rendering traditional one-step evaluation paradigms ineffective. To address this gap, we introduce ChartInt, the first benchmark specifically designed for interactive chart-to-code generation. ChartInt consists of 2,779 carefully annotated examples covering common interaction patterns such as hover, highlight, and tooltips. Additionally, ChartInt incorporates editing tasks such as data updates, style adjustments, and cross-chart style transfer to expand real-world application scenarios. To enable reliable evaluation of interaction behavior, we propose a preset state snapshot protocol that predefines target interaction states and deterministically triggers interaction events, transforming dynamic behaviors into reproducible evaluation targets. Based on this protocol, we design a multi-dimensional evaluation framework measuring semantic and stylistic consistency. Experiments show that while current MLLMs perform well on static charts, their performance drops significantly on interactive tasks, highlighting the need for interaction-aware benchmarks.

</details>


### [383] [Compressible Representations: Functional Spines in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31817)
*Niranjan Rajesh,Meenakshi Khosla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于隐藏表示Fisher型矩阵的"任务敏感子空间"识别框架：逐层按方向对下游计算的影响排序并投影，发现预训练视觉网络高度可压缩（少量维度保留大部分性能），压缩后表示对抗鲁棒性更高；子空间分解可分离形状/纹理功能方向；约束梯度更新正交于旧任务敏感子空间优于方差型梯度投影；子空间内比较显示跨模型对齐更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深层网络在高维表示中编码输入，但不清楚其中有多少容量真正决定行为。作者希望显式刻画每一层对任务功能敏感的表示子空间，从而量化表示的功能冗余度，并为鲁棒性、可解释分解、持续学习与模型比较提供一个统一的分析工具。

Method: 定义类似Fisher信息、但作用于隐藏表示而非参数的矩阵，用以度量各方向对下游计算的敏感度；据此对方向排序并投影到最任务敏感的子空间进行压缩评估。在此基础上：(1) 在压缩子空间上评估对抗攻击鲁棒性；(2) 在形状-纹理冲突刺激上检验子空间是否功能分离，并用消融验证选择性损伤；(3) 将持续学习的梯度更新约束在与旧任务敏感子空间正交的方向，与基于方差（如EWC/GPM类）的梯度投影对比；(4) 在任务敏感子空间内做跨模型对齐（CKA类）比较。

Result: 预训练视觉网络表示高度可压缩：仅需少量维度即保留大部分精度，维度需求显著低于几何替代方法；压缩子空间对全网络生成的对抗样本更鲁棒；形状敏感与纹理敏感方向基本不重叠，消融其一可选择性破坏对应任务；正交于先前任务敏感子空间的更新优于标准方差型梯度投影；晚期层任务敏感方向上的模型间对齐高于任务不敏感方向，且同一刺激在不同任务条件下暴露全空间相似度无法检测的不对称性。

Conclusion: 深度网络实际通过嵌入在大规模表示空间中的一个低维"功能脊柱"进行计算；任务敏感子空间可作为统一的、原则化的分析单元，贯穿压缩、鲁棒性、机制分解、持续学习与跨模型比较。

Abstract: Deep networks embed inputs in high-dimensional representations, but how much of this capacity actually drives behavior? We introduce a framework for identifying, at each layer, the subspace of the representation that is functionally sensitive to the task. Using a matrix analogous to the Fisher information but defined over the hidden representation, we rank directions by their influence on downstream computation and project onto the most task-sensitive subspace. Across pretrained vision networks, representations are remarkably compressible: a small fraction of the task-sensitive subspace preserves most performance, often requiring far fewer dimensions than geometric alternatives. We find that this compressed representation is also more robust to adversarial attacks generated on the full network. We additionally show that this decomposition recovers functionally distinct subspaces within the same representation: on cue-conflict stimuli, shape-sensitive and texture-sensitive directions are largely non-overlapping, and ablating one selectively impairs the corresponding task while leaving the other intact. Task-sensitive subspaces also provide a principled foundation for continual learning: constraining gradient updates to lie orthogonal to the task-sensitive subspaces of prior tasks outperforms standard variance-based gradient projection methods. Finally, restricting representational comparisons to task-sensitive subspaces reveals that inter-model alignment is higher in task-sensitive directions than in task-insensitive directions at later layers, and that conditioning on different tasks over the same stimuli uncovers asymmetries that full-space similarity measures cannot detect. Together, these results establish that deep networks compute through a low-dimensional functional spine embedded within a much larger representational space.

</details>


### [384] [Perception Is Enough, at the Right Scale: Scale-Adaptive Token Pruning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31954)
*Yifeng Lin,Liuxiang Qiu,Yunfeng Chen,Tiesong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TauPrune-VLA 是一种免训练 VLA 推理加速方法：用单一"感知温度"τ 控制保留 token 的语义尺度，并依据感知语义的变化趋势在线调整 τ，配合异步 token 选择，在多个 VLA 模型与仿真基准上实现最高 1.86 倍加速且成功率接近基线，并在两个真实机器人平台上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型需持续处理视觉流，计算开销限制实时控制；现有 token 剪枝采用固定语义尺度，难以适配操作不同阶段所需的信息；且常见方法依赖内部 attention，带来额外开销。

Method: 免训练框架：(1) 以感知温度 τ 控制保留感知表示的语义尺度；(2) 利用感知语义演变趋势在线更新 τ，并由机器人状态变化与动作幅度调节调整强度；(3) 利用相邻帧语义连续性，将当前观测算得的保留索引用于下一次策略调用，使 token 选择与推理异步执行以降低剪枝开销。

Result: 在多种 VLA 模型和仿真基准上取得最高 1.86× 推理加速，任务成功率接近基线；并在两个真实机器人平台上验证有效性。

Conclusion: 自适应语义尺度的 token 剪枝可在不训练、不依赖 attention 的前提下显著提升 VLA 实时性，且跨模型与真实平台有效；但绝对成功率是否保持仍是核心问题，异步索引复用对快速场景变化的鲁棒性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models must process continuous streams of visual observations, and the resulting computational cost limits real-time robotic control. Token pruning reduces this cost, but a fixed semantic scale of perceptual representation can overlook information needed at different stages of manipulation. We show that perceptual semantics can guide effective token selection without relying on internal attention when the selection scale adapts to the scene's evolving semantic structure. Based on this observation, we introduce TauPrune-VLA, a training-free method that controls the scale of the retained perceptual representation through a single perception temperature $\tau$. To adapt this scale as manipulation proceeds, TauPrune-VLA updates $\tau$ online using trends in perceptual semantics, with robot-state changes and action magnitude regulating the adjustment strength. While these semantics evolve over the course of manipulation, they remain relatively stable between consecutive frames. This continuity allows retention indices computed from the current observation to be applied at the next policy invocation, enabling token selection to run asynchronously with inference and reducing pruning overhead. Evaluated across diverse VLA models and simulation benchmarks, TauPrune-VLA achieves up to a $1.86\times$ inference speedup while maintaining near-baseline task success rates. We further validate its effectiveness on two real-world robotic platforms.

</details>


<div id='physics.bio-ph'></div>

# physics.bio-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [385] [The cyborg fly: reflexes survive engineered actuators, coordination needs stand-ins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31644)
*Mauricio Muñoz Arias*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该工作报告将成年果蝇全中枢神经系统两套连接组（BANC 雌性、MaleCNS 雄性）作为约16万神经元LIF脉冲网络，通过port-Hamiltonian执行器与仿真传感器/身体闭环驱动行走与飞行，并用五级打乱零模型检验结果对真实布线的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验连接组本身作为网络能否"驱动身体"，并在替换生物肌肉为工程执行器时判断哪些功能由布线决定、哪些依赖外挂控制律，从而厘清全CNS连接组在具身条件下可实现功能的边界。

Method: 将BANC与MaleCNS建模为约16万神经元的leaky integrate-and-fire网络；执行器采用port-Hamiltonian建模以逐步审计能量，替换执行器仅改配置文件；构建五级打乱（从整体神经系统到突触计数）的null network对照；闭环测试唇瓣糖刺激—喙运动神经元、腿关节阻力反射、腿部振荡、looming诱发起飞、下行神经元油门/转向飞行，并跨20种执行器设置（含Hill型肌对与齿轮直流电机）比较BANC与MaleCNS；另重建已发表的嗅觉导航闭环以检验"表观布线依赖"的来源。

Result: 布线产生明确特异功能：唇瓣糖驱动喙运动神经元、腿关节抵抗外力、腿回路振荡、MaleCNS中looming触发同侧起飞、下行神经元调节并操纵飞行方向。阻力反射在MaleCNS 20/20、BANC 19/20执行器设置下保持，说明反射来自布线而非执行器；执行器仅决定行走速度与能耗。六足协调行走、飞行稳定与扰动校正未自发涌现，需标注的工程替代模块补足；此类替代模块可制造虚假的布线依赖（如重建中嗅觉导航实际源于手写规则，网络仅通过一个效价数值介入）。两种性别在部分功能上存在差异并可追溯至布线（如MaleCNS特有的haltere驱动飞行动力肌）。

Conclusion: 具身的全CNS连接组可提供反射、节律与指令信号，但不能提供将其组合成行为的协调机制；具身连接组仿真的价值在于区分"布线承载的功能"与"工程替代模块承载的功能"。

Abstract: Two wiring diagrams now cover the whole central nervous system of an adult fruit fly: one female, one male. Can such a diagram, run as a network of spiking neurons, drive a body? And if we swap the body's muscles for engineered actuators, what survives? We ran both connectomes, BANC and MaleCNS, as leaky integrate-and-fire networks of about 160,000 neurons each, and closed the loop through simulated sensors, actuators and bodies for walking and flight. The actuators are port-Hamiltonian, so energy is audited at every step, and swapping one for another changes only a configuration file. Null networks shuffled at five levels, from the whole nervous system to synapse counts, test whether a result depends on the wiring. The wiring produced specific functions: sugar on the labellum drove the proboscis motor neuron, leg joints resisted imposed movement, leg circuits oscillated, a looming disc drove a take-off signal on its side (in MaleCNS), and descending neurons throttled and steered flight. The resistance reflex survived 20 of 20 actuator settings in MaleCNS and 19 of 20 in BANC, including a Hill-type muscle pair and a geared DC motor; the actuator set walking speed and energy cost instead. Coordinated six-leg walking, flight stability and the correction of perturbations did not emerge. Each needed a labelled engineered stand-in, which the connectome could command. Such stand-ins can also manufacture an apparent dependence on wiring: in a rebuild of a published closed loop, odour navigation came from a hand-written rule, and the network entered only through one valence number. The two animals differed on some functions, and the differences traced to wiring, such as a haltere drive of the flight power muscles found only in MaleCNS. A whole-CNS connectome, embodied, gives reflexes, rhythms and command signals, but not the coordination that turns them into behaviour.

</details>
